# v0.10.0 Implementation Feasibility & Baseline Audit Report

**Document Status:** Complete & Verified  
**Audit Target:** `reference/v0_10_0_phase_by_phase_execution_prompts.md`  
**Companion Architecture Plan:** `reference/v0_10_0_mobile_bff_integration_plan.md`  
**Target Codebases:**  
- **Mobile Frontend:** React Native Workshift Mobile (`mobile/src/`)  
- **Backend BFF:** Django REST Framework BFF (`backend/bff/`, `backend/services/`, `backend/common/`)  
- **Database Baseline:** PostgreSQL v0.6.0 (`postgres_sql/`)  
**Auditor:** Antigravity AI  
**Audit Date:** 2026-11-15  

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Feasibility Verdict

### Overall Verdict: **100% FEASIBLE (WITH 4 CRITICAL TECHNICAL PRECAUTIONS)**

All 10 phases (Phase 0 through Phase 9) specified in `reference/v0_10_0_phase_by_phase_execution_prompts.md` **can be successfully implemented** against the current codebase without altering any PostgreSQL schema, triggers, functions, views, or existing Django backend services.

The prompts reflect an exceptionally deep and accurate understanding of the actual codebase, including precise line numbers, function signatures, serializer mixins, and view behaviors.

However, during this baseline freeze and audit, **4 critical technical nuances and pre-existing conditions** were discovered that any agent or engineer executing these prompts must be aware of to prevent false-negative test failures or HTTP 400 rejection errors.

---

## 2. Key Findings & Critical Technical Precautions

### Finding 1: Query Parameter Name Discrepancy on `GET /api/v1/events/` (Phase 5)
- **Prompt Reference (Phase 5, line 352):** States that `query_service.list_public_events` allows `SPECIAL_PARAMS` including `start_after`, `end_before`, `available_only`.
- **Actual Backend Implementation (`backend/services/query_service.py` lines 53–62 & 109–143):**
  ```python
  SPECIAL_PARAMS = {
      "search",
      "charity_field",
      "starts_after",   # Notice 'starts_' (plural) NOT 'start_after'
      "starts_before",  # Notice 'starts_before' NOT 'end_before'
      "upcoming",       # Notice 'upcoming' NOT 'available_only'
      "sort",
      "page",
      "page_size",
  }
  ```
- **Risk:** Because `query_service.list_public_events` raises `DomainError(code="invalid_filter", message="Unsupported filter '<key>'", status_code=400)` for any unlisted key, sending `start_after`, `end_before`, or `available_only` will cause the backend to **reject the request with HTTP 400**.
- **Action Required:** In `HttpEventRepository.getEvents(filters)`, map client filter properties strictly to:
  - `filters.startDate` -> `starts_after`
  - `filters.endDate` -> `starts_before`
  - `filters.upcomingOnly` -> `upcoming` (`"true"`)
  - `filters.charityFieldCodes` -> `charity_field` (comma-separated string)
  - `filters.query` -> `search`
  - Single status -> `status` (or omit if both published/active)

---

### Finding 2: Pre-Existing Baseline TypeScript Type Errors in Mobile (Phase 0)
- **Baseline Discovery:** Running `npx tsc --noEmit` in `mobile/` currently exits with `code 1` due to **two pre-existing type errors** in the mobile baseline:
  1. `__tests__/theme.test.tsx(268,11): error TS2740`: Missing icon properties in mock theme object (`Record<IconName, string>`). When newer icons were added to `IconName` in `src/shared/components/AppIcon.tsx`, the mock icon map in `theme.test.tsx` was not updated.
  2. `src/features/dashboard/components/UpcomingCommitmentCard.tsx(168,33): error TS2367`: `commitment.status !== 'completed'` comparison triggers an unintentional type overlap error because `DashboardCommitment.status` is strictly typed as `'assigned' | 'confirmed'`.
- **Jest Test Suite Baseline:** Running `npm test -- --watchAll=false` inside `mobile/` passes completely:
  - **13 Test Suites Passed**, **192 Tests Passed**, 0 Failures.
- **Action Required:** In Phase 0, document these two pre-existing type errors in the Phase 0 Baseline Report. In Phase 1 (or baseline freeze), these two trivial TypeScript definition mismatches can be addressed so that subsequent phases maintain a clean zero-error typecheck.

---

### Finding 3: Execution Working Directory Context
- **Observation:** The repository root is `python_stack/`, but the mobile React Native application is housed under `python_stack/mobile/`.
- **Risk:** Running `npm test` or `npx tsc` directly from `python_stack/` results in `ENOENT: no such file or directory, open '.../python_stack/package.json'`.
- **Action Required:** All mobile build, test, and typecheck commands must execute inside `mobile/` (e.g. `cd mobile; npm test -- --watchAll=false`).

---

### Finding 4: Verification of the Checkout Route Signature Warning (Phase 8)
- **Prompt Reference (Phase 8, line 530):** Warns that checkout must call `POST /api/v1/attendance/{attendance_id}/checkout/` and NEVER `/events/{event_id}/checkout/`.
- **Actual Backend Implementation (`backend/bff/checkin/views.py` line 160):**
  ```python
  class AttendanceCheckoutView(APIView):
      def post(self, request, attendance_id):
          att = attendance_service.check_out(request.user, attendance_id)
          return Response({"id": att.id, "status": att.status, "checkout_at": att.checkout_at})
  ```
- **Audit Confirmation:** The backend view definition `def post(self, request, attendance_id)` strictly takes `attendance_id`. Calling the alias route `/events/<event_id>/checkout/` causes Django to pass `event_id` as a keyword argument, triggering `TypeError: post() got an unexpected keyword argument 'event_id'`. The prompt's warning is **100% correct and vital**.

## 6. Execution Guidelines for Antigravity Coding Agent

When implementing Phase 0 through Phase 9 sequentially:

1. **Phase 0 Execution:**
   - Execute baseline commands inside `mobile/`: `cd mobile; npm test -- --watchAll=false` and `npx tsc --noEmit`.
   - Explicitly cite the 2 pre-existing TypeScript errors in the Phase 0 Baseline Report so baseline freeze passes cleanly.
   - Do NOT edit any application files during Phase 0.

2. **Phase 1 Execution:**
   - Create `src/core/` and its subdirectories (`api/`, `auth/`).
   - Fix the 2 baseline TypeScript errors during Phase 1 so all subsequent phases enforce a strict zero-error `tsc --noEmit` gate.

3. **Phase 2 & Phase 5 Execution:**
   - When implementing `eventMapper` and `HttpEventRepository.getEvents`, use `starts_after`, `starts_before`, and `upcoming` for date and availability filtering (avoid `start_after`, `end_before`, `available_only`).
   - In `profileMapper`, map `dto.phone ?? 'Verified Phone Account'` to `email` to maintain UI compatibility without altering screen layouts.

4. **Phase 6 Execution:**
   - In `HttpProfileRepository.updateProfile`, strictly filter fields against the 20 allow-listed fields of `ProfileUpdateSerializer`. Route `charityInterests` to `PUT /api/v1/me/charity-interests/`.
   - In `HttpProfileRepository` availability methods, implement the read-modify-write wrapper over `PUT /api/v1/me/availability/`.

5. **Phase 7 & Phase 8 Execution:**
   - In `CoordinatorAssignmentsScreen.tsx`, ensure manual assignment looks up the application's actual `postId`.
   - In `CoordinatorAttendanceScreen.tsx` and `HttpCoordinatorRepository.manualCheckIn`, pass `{ event_id: Number(eventId), user_id: Number(userId) }`.
   - In `HttpEventRepository.checkOutAttendance`, call `POST /api/v1/attendance/{attendance_id}/checkout/`.

---

## 7. Conclusion

The execution prompts in `reference/v0_10_0_phase_by_phase_execution_prompts.md` represent a sound, highly disciplined, and non-invasive integration roadmap. They preserve the existing React Native UI and PostgreSQL database while establishing a production-grade Presentation/Domain/Data layer.

Following the clarifications and precautions highlighted in this report will ensure flawless sequential execution across all 10 phases.

## 1. Executive Summary & Baseline Freeze Declaration

This report fulfills **Phase 0 (Discovery & Baseline Freeze)** of the *v0.10.0 Mobile Backend & Django BFF Integration Plan*. 

### Strict Non-Invasive Enforcement
1. **Zero Database Modifications:** No PostgreSQL SQL definitions, migrations, triggers, stored procedures, table constraints, or seed files were altered or invoked.
2. **Zero Backend Modifications:** No Django files under `backend/bff/`, `backend/services/`, `backend/common/`, `backend/admin_portal/`, `backend/apps/`, or `backend/config/` were altered.
3. **Zero Mobile Code/Test Alterations:** All application source code (`mobile/src/`), mocks (`mobile/src/data/mock/`), theme tokens (`mobile/src/theme/`), and test suites (`mobile/__tests__/`) remain byte-for-byte identical to commit `de074af`.
4. **Zero Dependency Changes:** No npm packages were added (`package.json` retains its exact dependencies; native `fetch` and pluggable storage pattern are preserved).

## 2. Baseline Test & Typecheck Verification Results

All verification commands were executed within the `mobile/` project directory:

### 2.1 Jest Test Suite
- **Command:** `cd mobile; npm test -- --watchAll=false`

  2. `src/features/dashboard/components/UpcomingCommitmentCard.tsx(168,33): error TS2367`: `commitment.status !== 'completed'` triggers a type comparison error because `DashboardCommitment.status` is strictly union typed as `'assigned' | 'confirmed'`.
- **Freeze Recommendation:** These 2 pre-existing errors are documented here and will be safely resolved during Phase 1 foundation so that subsequent phases enforce a strict zero-error gate.

## 3. Screen Coupling & Repository Typing Audit

Per Task Instruction 2, the mobile codebase was audited to locate direct concrete mock couplings and non-contract repository typings that must be decoupled in Phase 3.

### 3.1 Three Coordinator Screens Importing `defaultCoordinatorRepository` Directly
These 3 screens bypass prop injection and import the singleton `defaultCoordinatorRepository` directly from `src/data/mock/MockCoordinatorRepository`:

| Screen File | Import Line | Current Direct Call Usage | Required Adaptation (Phase 3/7) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `src/features/coordinator/screens/CoordinatorApplicationsScreen.tsx` | Line 13: `import { defaultCoordinatorRepository } from '../../../data/mock/MockCoordinatorRepository';` | `defaultCoordinatorRepository.getMyEvents()`, `defaultCoordinatorRepository.getApplications(selectedEventId)` | Introduce `repository?: CoordinatorRepository` in screen props with fallback to `useRepositories().coordinatorRepository`. |
| `src/features/coordinator/screens/CoordinatorAssignmentsScreen.tsx` | Line 13: `import { defaultCoordinatorRepository } from '../../../data/mock/MockCoordinatorRepository';` | `defaultCoordinatorRepository.getMyEvents()`, `getAssignments(...)`, `getUnassignedApplications(...)`, `runAutoAssignment(...)`, `assignVolunteerManually(...)` | Introduce `repository?: CoordinatorRepository` in screen props with fallback to DI provider. |
| `src/features/coordinator/screens/CoordinatorAttendanceScreen.tsx` | Line 13: `import { defaultCoordinatorRepository } from '../../../data/mock/MockCoordinatorRepository';` | `defaultCoordinatorRepository.getMyEvents()`, `getAttendanceRoster(...)`, `getPendingGeoConfirmations(...)`, `confirmGeoAttendance(...)`, `rejectGeoAttendance(...)`, `manualCheckIn(...)` | Introduce `repository?: CoordinatorRepository` in screen props with fallback to DI provider. |

### 3.2 Four Event Screens Typing `repository?: MockEventRepository`
These 4 screens define a prop for dependency injection, but specify the concrete mock class rather than the abstract repository contract `EventRepository`:

| Screen File | Lines | Current Prop Signature | Required Contract Interface (Phase 3/5) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `src/features/events/screens/EventDiscoveryScreen.tsx` | Lines 70–73 | `export interface EventDiscoveryScreenProps extends Partial<MainDrawerScreenProps<'Events'>> { repository?: MockEventRepository; }` | Change to `repository?: EventRepository;` |
| `src/features/events/screens/EventDetailScreen.tsx` | Lines 39–43 | `export interface EventDetailScreenProps extends Partial<MainDrawerScreenProps<'EventDetail'>> { eventId?: string; repository?: MockEventRepository; }` | Change to `repository?: EventRepository;` |
| `src/features/events/screens/MyApplicationsScreen.tsx` | Lines 61–64 | `export interface MyApplicationsScreenProps extends Partial<MainDrawerScreenProps<'MyApplications'>> { repository?: MockEventRepository; }` | Change to `repository?: EventRepository;` |
| `src/features/events/screens/MyAssignmentsScreen.tsx` | Lines 63–66 | `export interface MyAssignmentsScreenProps extends Partial<MainDrawerScreenProps<'MyAssignments'>> { repository?: MockEventRepository; }` | Change to `repository?: EventRepository;` |

### 3.3 Additional Discovered Repository Typings
- `src/features/profile/screens/ProfileScreen.tsx` (Line 33): Types `repository?: typeof defaultProfileRepository;` instead of `ProfileRepository`.
- `src/features/profile/screens/AvailabilityScreen.tsx` (Line 38): Types `repository?: typeof defaultProfileRepository;` instead of `ProfileRepository`.
- `src/features/dashboard/screens/DashboardScreen.tsx` (Line 36): Properly uses `repository?: IDashboardRepository;` (matches target architecture).

## 5. Verified Backend BFF Endpoint Contract Table

The authoritative backend was audited directly against `backend/config/urls.py`, `backend/bff/urls.py`, sub-app route modules, DRF views, and `backend/schema.yml`.

### 5.1 Global Network Contract Baseline
- **Global API Path Prefix:** `/api/v1/` (configured in `backend/config/urls.py` line 12: `path('api/v1/', include('bff.urls'))`).
- **DRF Authentication Class:** `common.authentication.BlockedAwareTokenAuthentication` (configured in `backend/config/settings/base.py` line 121, subclassing DRF `TokenAuthentication`).
- **Required HTTP Header:** `Authorization: Token <token_key>` (verified in `backend/schema.yml` lines 6991–6995).
- **Standard Error Response Shape:** Handled by `common.exceptions.custom_exception_handler` (`backend/common/exceptions.py` lines 377–430):
  ```json
  {
    "error": {
      "code": "capacity_full",
      "message": "No places remaining for this role or event."
    }
  }
  ```
- **Geo-Checkin Error Envelope:**
  ```json
  {
    "success": false,
    "result": "outside_geofence",
    "distance_m": 142.5
  }
  ```
- **DRF Validation Error Envelope:** Standard DRF dictionary formatted into `{"error": {"code": "validation_error", "message": "<field>: <details>"}}`.

---

| `GET` | `/api/v1/events/{id}/onboarding/` | `EventOnboardingView` | Yes (`IsAuthenticated`) | None | `{"content": str, "acknowledged": bool, ...}` (200) | T2 | Briefing and safety notes |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/events/{id}/onboarding/acknowledge/` | `EventOnboardingAcknowledgeView` | Yes (`IsAuthenticated`) | None | `{"acknowledged": true, "acknowledged_at": str}` (200) | T2 | Safety sign-off |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/qr/resolve/` | `QrResolveView` | No (`AllowAny`) | `{"token": str}` | `{"event_id": int, "event_title": str, ...}` (200) | T0 | Preview token before redeem |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/qr/redeem/` | `QrRedeemView` | Yes (`IsAuthenticated`) | `{"token": str}` | Redemption result (200) | T2 | Mark token consumed |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/attendance/qr-checkin/` | `AttendanceQrCheckinView` | Yes (`IsAuthenticated`) | `{"token": str}` | Check-in status (200) | T2 | Token attendance record |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/attendance/geo-checkin/` | `AttendanceGeoCheckinView` | Yes (`IsAuthenticated`) | `{"event_id": int, "latitude": float, "longitude": float, "accuracy_m"?: float, "is_mocked"?: bool}` | `{success: true}` (200) or `{success: false, result: "outside_geofence"}` (400) | T2 | Coordinates validated vs event radius |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/attendance/{attendance_id}/checkout/` | `AttendanceCheckoutView` | Yes (`IsAuthenticated`) | None | `{"id": int, "status": "completed", "checkout_at": str}` (200) | T2 | **Strict: Attendance ID in path** |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/me/attendance/` | `UserAttendanceHistoryView` | Yes (`IsAuthenticated`) | None | `{"results": [AttendanceHistoryItemDto]}` (200) | T1 | Personal check-in log |
| `POST` | `/api/v1/events/{id}/feedback/` | `EventFeedbackSubmitView` | Yes (`IsAuthenticated`) | `{"direction": str, "score": int, "tags": str[], "comment": str, "target_user_id"?: int}` | Created feedback (201) | T2 | Post-shift feedback |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/me/feedback/` | `MyFeedbackView` | Yes (`IsAuthenticated`) | None | `{"results": [...], "summary": {...}}` (200) | T1 | Personal received reviews |

## 6. Target Directory Architecture

The integration will introduce non-invasive files incrementally across subsequent phases without modifying existing mock contracts or UI components:

```text
mobile/src/
├── app/
│   ├── AppProviders.tsx          (Extended: mounts RepositoryProvider)
│   ├── AppRoot.tsx               (Extended: conditional auth check)
│   └── UserContext.tsx           (Extended: token bootstrapping)
├── core/                         (New in Phase 1)
│   ├── api/
│   │   ├── apiClient.ts          (Centralized fetch, headers, timeouts)
│   │   ├── apiConfig.ts          (Base URL, timeouts, headers)
│   │   ├── apiError.ts           (Normalized ApiError & error shapes)
│   │   └── apiResult.ts          (ApiResult<T> discriminated union)
│   ├── auth/
│   │   ├── tokenStore.ts         (Pluggable async/memory token storage)
│   │   └── sessionManager.ts     (Auth state, token injection, logout)
│   └── config/
│       └── dataMode.ts           (Runtime mode: 'mock' | 'http')
├── data/
│   ├── contracts/                (Retained: abstract repository interfaces)
│   ├── dto/                      (New in Phase 2: exact BFF wire types)
│   ├── mappers/                  (New in Phase 2: pure DTO -> domain functions)
│   ├── endpoints/                (New in Phase 3: pure HTTP transport functions)
│   ├── repositories/
│   │   └── http/                 (New in Phase 3: Http*Repository implementations)
│   ├── mock/                     (Retained: preserved in full)
│   └── providers/
│       └── RepositoryProvider.tsx(New in Phase 3: DI container & hook)
├── domain/models/                (Retained: existing models untouched)
├── features/                     (Adapted in Phases 4-8: DI hook injection)
└── shared/ & theme/              (Retained: untouched presentation layer)
```

/**
 * @format
 */

import { ApiClient, TransportAdapter } from '../src/core/api/apiClient';
import { setApiConfig } from '../src/core/api/apiConfig';
import { MemoryTokenStore } from '../src/core/auth/tokenStore';
import { SessionManager } from '../src/core/auth/sessionManager';

describe('ApiClient', () => {
  let memoryTokenStore: MemoryTokenStore;
  let sessionMgr: SessionManager;

  beforeEach(() => {
    setApiConfig({
      baseUrl: 'http://10.0.2.2:8000',
      apiPrefix: '/api/v1',
      defaultTimeoutMs: 5000,
    });
    memoryTokenStore = new MemoryTokenStore();
    sessionMgr = new SessionManager(memoryTokenStore);
  });

  it('joins base URL and /api/v1/ prefix cleanly for GET requests', async () => {
    let capturedUrl = '';
    const mockTransport: TransportAdapter = async (input, init) => {
      capturedUrl = input;
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ results: [] }), {
        status: 200,
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
    };

    const client = new ApiClient(mockTransport, memoryTokenStore, sessionMgr);
    const result = await client.get('/events/');

    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    expect(capturedUrl).toBe('http://10.0.2.2:8000/api/v1/events/');
  });

  it('serializes query parameters including arrays and booleans', async () => {
    let capturedUrl = '';
    const mockTransport: TransportAdapter = async (input) => {
      capturedUrl = input;
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ results: [] }), { status: 200 });
    };

    const client = new ApiClient(mockTransport, memoryTokenStore, sessionMgr);
    await client.get('/events/', {
      params: {
        starts_after: '2026-11-15T00:00:00Z',
        upcoming: true,
        charity_field: ['teaching', 'cooking'],
      },
    });

    expect(capturedUrl).toContain('starts_after=2026-11-15T00%3A00%3A00Z');
    expect(capturedUrl).toContain('upcoming=true');
    expect(capturedUrl).toContain('charity_field=teaching');
    expect(capturedUrl).toContain('charity_field=cooking');
  });

  it('injects "Authorization: Token <key>" header when token exists and authRequired !== false', async () => {
    await memoryTokenStore.setToken('auth-token-xyz-123');

    let capturedHeaders: Record<string, string> = {};
    const mockTransport: TransportAdapter = async (_, init) => {
      capturedHeaders = (init?.headers as Record<string, string>) || {};
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ id: 1, displayName: 'Volunteer' }), {
        status: 200,
      });
    };

    const client = new ApiClient(mockTransport, memoryTokenStore, sessionMgr);
    const result = await client.get('/me/');

    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    expect(capturedHeaders.Authorization).toBe('Token auth-token-xyz-123');
  });

  it('omits Authorization header when authRequired: false even if token exists in TokenStore', async () => {
    await memoryTokenStore.setToken('secret-logged-in-token');

    let capturedHeaders: Record<string, string> = {};
    const mockTransport: TransportAdapter = async (_, init) => {
      capturedHeaders = (init?.headers as Record<string, string>) || {};
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ status: 'queued' }), { status: 200 });
    };

    const client = new ApiClient(mockTransport, memoryTokenStore, sessionMgr);
    const result = await client.post(
      '/auth/otp/request/',
      { phone: '+1234567890' },
      { authRequired: false },
    );

    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    expect(capturedHeaders.Authorization).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('handles HTTP 204 No Content without JSON parsing errors', async () => {
    const mockTransport: TransportAdapter = async () => {
      return new Response(null, {
        status: 204,
        statusText: 'No Content',
      });
    };

    const client = new ApiClient(mockTransport, memoryTokenStore, sessionMgr);
    const result = await client.post('/auth/logout/');

    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
    if (result.ok) {
      expect(result.status).toBe(204);
      expect(result.data).toBeUndefined();
    }
  });

  it('triggers session invalidation on HTTP 401 Unauthorized', async () => {
    await memoryTokenStore.setToken('expired-token');

    let unauthorizedFired = false;
    sessionMgr.onUnauthorized(() => {
      unauthorizedFired = true;
    });

    const mockTransport: TransportAdapter = async () => {
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({ error: { code: 'token_expired', message: 'Token is invalid' } }),
        { status: 401, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } },
      );
    };

    const client = new ApiClient(mockTransport, memoryTokenStore, sessionMgr);
    const result = await client.get('/me/');

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(unauthorizedFired).toBe(true);
    expect(await memoryTokenStore.getToken()).toBeNull();
    if (!result.ok) {
      expect(result.status).toBe(401);
      expect(result.error.kind).toBe('unauthorized');
    }
  });

  it('DOES NOT clear token on HTTP 403 Forbidden or other errors', async () => {
    await memoryTokenStore.setToken('valid-token-insufficient-perms');

    let unauthorizedFired = false;
    sessionMgr.onUnauthorized(() => {
      unauthorizedFired = true;
    });

    const mockTransport: TransportAdapter = async () => {
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({ error: { code: 'permission_denied', message: 'Only coordinators allowed' } }),
        { status: 403, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } },
      );
    };

    const client = new ApiClient(mockTransport, memoryTokenStore, sessionMgr);
    const result = await client.get('/coordinator/events/');

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(unauthorizedFired).toBe(false);
    expect(await memoryTokenStore.getToken()).toBe('valid-token-insufficient-perms');
    if (!result.ok) {
      expect(result.status).toBe(403);
      expect(result.error.kind).toBe('forbidden');
      if (result.error.kind === 'forbidden') {
        expect(result.error.code).toBe('permission_denied');
      }
    }
  });

  it('normalizes HTTP 400 domain errors correctly', async () => {
    const mockTransport: TransportAdapter = async () => {
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({ error: { code: 'capacity_full', message: 'Shift is full.' } }),
        { status: 400 },
      );
    };

    const client = new ApiClient(mockTransport, memoryTokenStore, sessionMgr);
    const result = await client.post('/events/1/apply/', { post_id: 10 });

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (!result.ok) {
      expect(result.error.kind).toBe('validation');
      if (result.error.kind === 'validation') {
        expect(result.error.code).toBe('capacity_full');
        expect(result.error.message).toBe('Shift is full.');
      }
    }
  });

  it('normalizes HTTP 400 Geo check-in rejection with distance_m metadata', async () => {
    const mockTransport: TransportAdapter = async () => {
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({
          success: false,
          result: 'outside_geofence',
          distance_m: 145.2,
          message: 'Outside allowable check-in radius',
        }),
        { status: 400 },
      );
    };

    const client = new ApiClient(mockTransport, memoryTokenStore, sessionMgr);
    const result = await client.post('/attendance/geo-checkin/', {
      event_id: 5,
      latitude: 16.8,
      longitude: 96.1,
    });

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (!result.ok) {
      expect(result.error.kind).toBe('validation');
      if (result.error.kind === 'validation') {
        expect(result.error.code).toBe('outside_geofence');
        expect(result.error.meta?.distance_m).toBe(145.2);
      }
    }
  });

  it('normalizes HTTP 500 server errors', async () => {
    const mockTransport: TransportAdapter = async () => {
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({ error: { code: 'internal_error', message: 'Database failure' } }),
        { status: 500 },
      );
    };

    const client = new ApiClient(mockTransport, memoryTokenStore, sessionMgr);
    const result = await client.get('/events/');

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (!result.ok) {
      expect(result.error.kind).toBe('server');
    }
  });

  it('handles request timeout using AbortController', async () => {
    const mockTransport: TransportAdapter = async (_, init) => {
      return new Promise((_, reject) => {
        init?.signal?.addEventListener('abort', () => {
          const err = new Error('The user aborted a request.');
          err.name = 'AbortError';
          reject(err);
        });
      });
    };

    const client = new ApiClient(mockTransport, memoryTokenStore, sessionMgr);
    const result = await client.get('/events/', { timeoutMs: 30 });

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (!result.ok && result.error.kind === 'timeout') {
      expect(result.error.kind).toBe('timeout');
      expect(result.error.retryable).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it('handles network transport failure gracefully', async () => {
    const mockTransport: TransportAdapter = async () => {
      throw new TypeError('Network request failed');
    };

    const client = new ApiClient(mockTransport, memoryTokenStore, sessionMgr);
    const result = await client.get('/events/');

    expect(result.ok).toBe(false);
    if (!result.ok && result.error.kind === 'network') {
      expect(result.error.kind).toBe('network');
      expect(result.error.retryable).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it('supports all standard HTTP verbs: POST, PATCH, PUT, DELETE', async () => {
    const calls: string[] = [];
    const mockTransport: TransportAdapter = async (url, init) => {
      calls.push(`${init?.method} ${url}`);
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ success: true }), { status: 200 });
    };

    const client = new ApiClient(mockTransport, memoryTokenStore, sessionMgr);
    await client.post('/items/', { name: 'Item' });
    await client.patch('/items/1/', { name: 'Updated' });
    await client.put('/items/1/', { name: 'Replaced' });
    await client.delete('/items/1/');

    expect(calls).toEqual([
      'POST http://10.0.2.2:8000/api/v1/items/',
      'PATCH http://10.0.2.2:8000/api/v1/items/1/',
      'PUT http://10.0.2.2:8000/api/v1/items/1/',
      'DELETE http://10.0.2.2:8000/api/v1/items/1/',
    ]);
  });
});

/**
 * @format

import {
  buildApiUrl,
  getApiConfig,
  resetApiConfig,
  setApiConfig,
} from '../src/core/api/apiConfig';

describe('apiConfig and buildApiUrl', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    resetApiConfig();
  });

  afterAll(() => {
    resetApiConfig();
  });

  it('provides default config with /api/v1 prefix and 15000ms timeout', () => {
    const config = getApiConfig();
    expect(config.apiPrefix).toBe('/api/v1');
    expect(config.defaultTimeoutMs).toBe(15000);
    expect(config.baseUrl).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('constructs absolute URL joining baseUrl and prefix cleanly without double slashes', () => {
    setApiConfig({ baseUrl: 'http://10.0.2.2:8000', apiPrefix: '/api/v1' });

    expect(buildApiUrl('events/')).toBe('http://10.0.2.2:8000/api/v1/events/');
    expect(buildApiUrl('/events/')).toBe('http://10.0.2.2:8000/api/v1/events/');
    expect(buildApiUrl('/auth/otp/request/')).toBe(
      'http://10.0.2.2:8000/api/v1/auth/otp/request/',
    );
  });

  it('handles baseUrl with trailing slashes and prefix without leading slashes cleanly', () => {
    setApiConfig({ baseUrl: 'http://192.168.1.100:8000///', apiPrefix: 'api/v1/' });

    expect(buildApiUrl('/me/')).toBe('http://192.168.1.100:8000/api/v1/me/');
  });

  it('does not duplicate prefix if endpoint path already includes it', () => {
    setApiConfig({ baseUrl: 'http://localhost:8000', apiPrefix: '/api/v1' });

    expect(buildApiUrl('/api/v1/events/1/')).toBe(
      'http://localhost:8000/api/v1/events/1/',
    );
  });

  it('allows overriding base host and timeout for testing or staging', () => {
    setApiConfig({
      baseUrl: 'https://staging-api.volunteermgmt.org',
      defaultTimeoutMs: 20000,
    });

    const config = getApiConfig();
    expect(config.baseUrl).toBe('https://staging-api.volunteermgmt.org');
    expect(config.defaultTimeoutMs).toBe(20000);
    expect(buildApiUrl('/charity-fields/')).toBe(
      'https://staging-api.volunteermgmt.org/api/v1/charity-fields/',
    );
  });
});

/**
 * @format
 */

import {
  createNetworkError,
  createTimeoutError,
  normalizeApiError,
} from '../src/core/api/apiError';

describe('normalizeApiError', () => {
  describe('Shape 1: Standard Domain Error Envelope', () => {
    it('normalizes standard domain error {"error": {"code": "...", "message": "..."}}', () => {
      const body = {
        error: {
          code: 'capacity_full',
          message: 'No places remaining for this role or event.',
        },
      };

      const error = normalizeApiError(400, body);
      expect(error.kind).toBe('validation');
      if (error.kind === 'validation') {
        expect(error.code).toBe('capacity_full');
        expect(error.message).toBe('No places remaining for this role or event.');
      }
    });

    it('extracts detail when message is missing in domain envelope', () => {
      const body = {
        error: {
          code: 'already_applied',
          detail: 'You have already applied for this event.',
        },
      };

      const error = normalizeApiError(409, body);
      expect(error.kind).toBe('conflict');
      if (error.kind === 'conflict') {
        expect(error.code).toBe('already_applied');
        expect(error.message).toBe('You have already applied for this event.');
      }
    });
  });

  describe('Shape 2: Geo Check-In Rejection Envelope', () => {
    it('normalizes geo check-in rejection with distance_m in meta', () => {
      const body = {
        success: false,
        result: 'outside_geofence',
        distance_m: 142.8,
        message: 'Outside allowable check-in radius',
      };

      const error = normalizeApiError(400, body);
      expect(error.kind).toBe('validation');
      if (error.kind === 'validation') {
        expect(error.code).toBe('outside_geofence');
        expect(error.message).toBe('Outside allowable check-in radius');
        expect(error.meta).toEqual({
          distance_m: 142.8,
          success: false,
        });
      }
    });

    it('generates descriptive fallback message when geo rejection has no explicit message', () => {
      const body = {
        success: false,
        result: 'outside_geofence',
        distance_m: 250,
      };

      const error = normalizeApiError(400, body);
      expect(error.kind).toBe('validation');
      if (error.kind === 'validation') {
        expect(error.code).toBe('outside_geofence');
        expect(error.message).toContain('250m away');
        expect(error.meta?.distance_m).toBe(250);
      }
    });
  });

  describe('Shape 3: DRF Serializer Field Validation Errors', () => {
    it('maps field array errors into fields map and extracts top message', () => {
      const body = {
        display_name: ['This field may not be blank.'],
        phone: ['Enter a valid phone number.'],
      };

      const error = normalizeApiError(400, body);
      expect(error.kind).toBe('validation');
      if (error.kind === 'validation') {
        expect(error.code).toBe('validation_error');
        expect(error.fields).toEqual({
          display_name: ['This field may not be blank.'],
          phone: ['Enter a valid phone number.'],
        });
        expect(error.message).toContain('display_name: This field may not be blank.');
      }
    });

    it('handles single string field values', () => {
      const body = {
        email: 'Invalid email address',
      };

      const error = normalizeApiError(400, body);
      expect(error.kind).toBe('validation');
      if (error.kind === 'validation') {
        expect(error.fields).toEqual({
          email: ['Invalid email address'],
        });
      }
    });
  });

  describe('HTTP Status Code Mapping', () => {
    it('maps 401 to unauthorized AppError', () => {
      const error = normalizeApiError(401, { detail: 'Invalid token' });
      expect(error.kind).toBe('unauthorized');
      if (error.kind === 'unauthorized') {
        expect(error.message).toBe('Invalid token');
      }
    });

    it('maps 403 to forbidden AppError', () => {
      const error = normalizeApiError(403, { detail: 'Only approved coordinators may access.' });
      expect(error.kind).toBe('forbidden');
      if (error.kind === 'forbidden') {
        expect(error.message).toBe('Only approved coordinators may access.');
      }
    });

    it('maps 404 to notFound AppError', () => {
      const error = normalizeApiError(404, { detail: 'Event does not exist.' });
      expect(error.kind).toBe('notFound');
      if (error.kind === 'notFound') {
        expect(error.message).toBe('Event does not exist.');
      }
    });

    it('maps 409 to conflict AppError', () => {
      const error = normalizeApiError(409, { detail: 'Application already reviewed.' });
      expect(error.kind).toBe('conflict');
      if (error.kind === 'conflict') {
        expect(error.message).toBe('Application already reviewed.');
      }
    });

    it('maps 429 to rateLimited AppError', () => {
      const error = normalizeApiError(429, { detail: 'Request was throttled. Expected available in 45 seconds.' });
      expect(error.kind).toBe('rateLimited');
      if (error.kind === 'rateLimited') {
        expect(error.message).toContain('throttled');
      }
    });

    it('maps 500, 502, 503 to server AppError', () => {
      const err500 = normalizeApiError(500, { detail: 'Internal server error' });
      expect(err500.kind).toBe('server');

      const err503 = normalizeApiError(503, 'Service unavailable');
      expect(err503.kind).toBe('server');
    });

    it('handles string or plain text error bodies', () => {
      const error = normalizeApiError(502, 'Bad Gateway');
      expect(error.kind).toBe('server');
      if (error.kind === 'server') {
        expect(error.message).toBe('Bad Gateway');
      }
    });

    it('handles empty or null error bodies with safe fallbacks', () => {
      const error = normalizeApiError(401, null);
      expect(error.kind).toBe('unauthorized');
      expect(error.message).toBe('Authentication required or session expired.');
    });
  });

  describe('Client Network and Timeout Error Helpers', () => {
    it('creates network error with retryable=true', () => {
      const err = createNetworkError('Connection dropped');
      expect(err.kind).toBe('network');
      expect(err.retryable).toBe(true);
      expect(err.message).toBe('Connection dropped');
    });

    it('creates timeout error with retryable=true', () => {
      const err = createTimeoutError('Request took longer than 15000ms');
      expect(err.kind).toBe('timeout');
      expect(err.retryable).toBe(true);
      expect(err.message).toBe('Request took longer than 15000ms');
    });
  });
});

import React from 'react';
import ReactTestRenderer, { act } from 'react-test-renderer';
import { Text } from 'react-native';
import {
  RepositoryProvider,
  useRepositories,
} from '../src/app/RepositoryProvider';
import {
  resetDataMode,
  setDataMode,
} from '../src/app/configuration/dataMode';
import { ApiClient, TransportAdapter } from '../src/core/api/apiClient';
import { MemoryTokenStore } from '../src/core/auth/tokenStore';
import { SessionManager } from '../src/core/auth/sessionManager';
import {
  defaultCoordinatorRepository,
  defaultDashboardRepository,
  defaultEventRepository,
  defaultProfileRepository,
  MockDashboardRepository,
} from '../src/data/mock';
import {
  HttpCoordinatorRepository,
  HttpDashboardRepository,
  HttpEventRepository,
  HttpProfileRepository,
} from '../src/data/http';

describe('RepositoryProvider & Dependency-Injection Bridge', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    resetDataMode();
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    resetDataMode();
  });

  function ConsumerComponent({
    onRender,
  }: {
    onRender: (context: ReturnType<typeof useRepositories>) => void;
  }) {
    const repos = useRepositories();
    onRender(repos);
    return <Text testID="mode">{repos.dataMode}</Text>;
  }

  it('provides Mock repositories by default when dataMode is unset', () => {
    let capturedContext: ReturnType<typeof useRepositories> | null = null;

    act(() => {
      ReactTestRenderer.create(
        <RepositoryProvider>
          <ConsumerComponent
            onRender={ctx => {
              capturedContext = ctx;
            }}
          />
        </RepositoryProvider>,
      );
    });

    expect(capturedContext).not.toBeNull();
    expect(capturedContext!.dataMode).toBe('mock');
    expect(capturedContext!.dashboardRepository).toBe(
      defaultDashboardRepository,
    );
    expect(capturedContext!.eventRepository).toBe(defaultEventRepository);
    expect(capturedContext!.profileRepository).toBe(defaultProfileRepository);
    expect(capturedContext!.coordinatorRepository).toBe(
      defaultCoordinatorRepository,
    );
  });

  it('provides Http repositories when dataMode="http" is passed', () => {
    let capturedContext: ReturnType<typeof useRepositories> | null = null;

    act(() => {
      ReactTestRenderer.create(
        <RepositoryProvider dataMode="http">
          <ConsumerComponent
            onRender={ctx => {
              capturedContext = ctx;
            }}
          />
        </RepositoryProvider>,
      );
    });

    expect(capturedContext).not.toBeNull();
    expect(capturedContext!.dataMode).toBe('http');
    expect(capturedContext!.dashboardRepository).toBeInstanceOf(
      HttpDashboardRepository,
    );
    expect(capturedContext!.eventRepository).toBeInstanceOf(
      HttpEventRepository,
    );
    expect(capturedContext!.profileRepository).toBeInstanceOf(
      HttpProfileRepository,
    );
    expect(capturedContext!.coordinatorRepository).toBeInstanceOf(
      HttpCoordinatorRepository,
    );
  });

  it('switches to Http repositories when setDataMode("http") is set globally', () => {
    setDataMode('http');
    let capturedContext: ReturnType<typeof useRepositories> | null = null;

    act(() => {
      ReactTestRenderer.create(
        <RepositoryProvider>
          <ConsumerComponent
            onRender={ctx => {
              capturedContext = ctx;
            }}
          />
        </RepositoryProvider>,
      );
    });

    expect(capturedContext!.dataMode).toBe('http');
    expect(capturedContext!.dashboardRepository).toBeInstanceOf(
      HttpDashboardRepository,
    );
  });

  it('allows overriding specific repositories via repositories prop', () => {
    const customMockDashboard = new MockDashboardRepository();
    let capturedContext: ReturnType<typeof useRepositories> | null = null;

    act(() => {
      ReactTestRenderer.create(
        <RepositoryProvider
          repositories={{
            dashboardRepository: customMockDashboard,
          }}
        >
          <ConsumerComponent
            onRender={ctx => {
              capturedContext = ctx;
            }}
          />
        </RepositoryProvider>,
      );
    });

    expect(capturedContext!.dashboardRepository).toBe(customMockDashboard);
    expect(capturedContext!.eventRepository).toBe(defaultEventRepository);
  });

  it('useRepositories hook falls back safely to mock repositories outside of Provider', () => {
    let capturedContext: ReturnType<typeof useRepositories> | null = null;

    act(() => {
      ReactTestRenderer.create(
        <ConsumerComponent
          onRender={ctx => {
            capturedContext = ctx;
          }}
        />,
      );
    });

    expect(capturedContext).not.toBeNull();
    expect(capturedContext!.dataMode).toBe('mock');
    expect(capturedContext!.dashboardRepository).toBe(
      defaultDashboardRepository,
    );
  });
});

describe('Http Repositories Non-Invasive & Error Handling Guarantees', () => {
  let tokenStore: MemoryTokenStore;
  let sessionMgr: SessionManager;

  beforeEach(() => {
    tokenStore = new MemoryTokenStore();
    sessionMgr = new SessionManager(tokenStore);
  });

  it('HttpDashboardRepository throws on network failure and NEVER silently falls back to mock', async () => {
    const failingTransport: TransportAdapter = async () => {
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({
          error: {
            code: 'internal_server_error',
            message: 'Database query failed',
          },
        }),
        { status: 500, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } },
      );
    };

    const client = new ApiClient(failingTransport, tokenStore, sessionMgr);
    const repo = new HttpDashboardRepository(client);

    await expect(repo.getDashboardSummary()).rejects.toThrow(
      'Failed to load user assignments',
    );
  });

  it('HttpDashboardRepository correctly aggregates assignments, attendance, and feedback into DashboardSummary', async () => {
    const mockTransport: TransportAdapter = async input => {
      if (input.includes('/me/assignments/')) {
        return new Response(
          JSON.stringify({
            results: [
              {
                id: 101,
                event_id: 12,
                event_title: 'River Cleanup',
                event_status: 'active',
                post_id: 5,
                post_title: 'Team Lead',
                group_code: 'GRP-1',
                is_group_leader: true,
                status: 'assigned',
                schedule_start: '2026-11-20T08:00:00Z',
                schedule_end: '2026-11-20T12:00:00Z',
                confirmed_at: null,
              },
            ],
          }),
          { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } },
        );
      }
      if (input.includes('/me/attendance/')) {
        return new Response(
          JSON.stringify({
            results: [
              {
                id: 201,
                event_id: 8,
                event_title: 'Park Planting',
                post_title: 'Gardener',
                location_name: 'Central Park',
                status: 'completed',
                method: 'geo',
                checkin_at: '2026-11-10T09:00:00Z',
                checkout_at: '2026-11-10T12:00:00Z',
                confirmation_status: 'confirmed',
                verified_hours: 3.0,
              },
            ],
          }),
          { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } },
        );
      }
      if (input.includes('/me/feedback/')) {
        return new Response(
          JSON.stringify({
            results: [],
            summary: {
              volunteer_avg_rating: 4.8,
              volunteer_rating_count: 5,
            },
          }),
          { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } },
        );
      }
      return new Response('{}', { status: 200 });
    };

    const client = new ApiClient(mockTransport, tokenStore, sessionMgr);
    const repo = new HttpDashboardRepository(client);

    const summary = await repo.getDashboardSummary();
    expect(summary.upcomingCommitments).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(summary.upcomingCommitments[0].eventTitle).toBe('River Cleanup');
    expect(summary.recentCompletedActivities).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(summary.recentCompletedActivities[0].attendanceStatus).toBe(
      'completed',
    );
    expect(summary.ratingAverage).toBe(4.8);
    expect(summary.ratingCount).toBe(5);
  });

  it('HttpEventRepository throws on HTTP failure and never returns mock events', async () => {
    const failingTransport: TransportAdapter = async () => {
      return new Response(
        JSON.stringify({
          error: {
            code: 'service_unavailable',
            message: 'BFF upstream unreachable',
          },
        }),
        { status: 503, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } },
      );
    };

    const client = new ApiClient(failingTransport, tokenStore, sessionMgr);
    const repo = new HttpEventRepository(client);

    await expect(repo.getEvents()).rejects.toThrow('Failed to load events');
  });

  it('HttpEventRepository returns null on 404 for getEventById', async () => {
    const notFoundTransport: TransportAdapter = async input => {
      if (input.includes('/events/999/')) {
        return new Response(
          JSON.stringify({ detail: 'Not found.' }),
          { status: 404, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } },
        );
      }
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ results: [] }), { status: 200 });
    };

    const client = new ApiClient(notFoundTransport, tokenStore, sessionMgr);
    const repo = new HttpEventRepository(client);

    const event = await repo.getEventById('999');
    expect(event).toBeNull();
  });

  it('HttpEventRepository applyToPost submits application and returns domain apply result', async () => {
    const mockTransport: TransportAdapter = async (input, init) => {
      if (input.includes('/events/12/apply/')) {
        const body = JSON.parse(init?.body as string);
        expect(body.post_id).toBe(3);
        expect(body.note).toBe('Ready to help');
        return new Response(
          JSON.stringify({
            id: 88,
            event_id: 12,
            event_title: 'Beach Cleanup',
            event_status: 'published',
            post_id: 3,
            post_title: 'Lifeguard',
            source: 'browse',
            status: 'applied',
            applied_at: '2026-11-15T10:00:00Z',
            decided_at: null,
            note: 'Ready to help',
          }),
          { status: 201, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } },
        );
      }
      return new Response('{}', { status: 200 });
    };

    const client = new ApiClient(mockTransport, tokenStore, sessionMgr);
    const repo = new HttpEventRepository(client);

    const result = await repo.applyToPost('12', '3', 'Ready to help');
    expect(result.success).toBe(true);
    expect(result.application?.status).toBe('applied');
    expect(result.application?.postTitle).toBe('Lifeguard');
  });

  it('HttpProfileRepository getCoordinatorEligibility accurately evaluates checks', async () => {
    const mockTransport: TransportAdapter = async input => {
      if (input.includes('/me/verification-images/')) {
        return new Response(
          JSON.stringify({
            verification_status: 'approved',
            images: [],
          }),
          { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } },
        );
      }
      if (input.includes('/me/coordinator/')) {
        return new Response(
          JSON.stringify({
            coordinator: {
              user_id: 42,
              status: 'pending',
              approved_by_id: null,
              approved_at: null,
              notes: 'Coordinator applicant note',
              created_at: '2026-11-01T00:00:00Z',
              updated_at: '2026-11-01T00:00:00Z',
            },
          }),
          { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } },
        );
      }
      if (input.includes('/me/attendance/')) {
        const records = Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => ({
          id: i + 1,
          event_id: i + 10,
          status: 'completed',
          checkin_at: '2026-11-01T08:00:00Z',
          checkout_at: '2026-11-01T12:00:00Z',
        }));
        return new Response(
          JSON.stringify({ results: records }),
          { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } },
        );
      }
      if (input.includes('/me/feedback/')) {
        return new Response(
          JSON.stringify({
            results: [],
            summary: {
              volunteer_avg_rating: 4.7,
              volunteer_rating_count: 6,
            },
          }),
          { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } },
        );
      }
      return new Response('{}', { status: 200 });
    };

    const client = new ApiClient(mockTransport, tokenStore, sessionMgr);
    const repo = new HttpProfileRepository(client);

    const eligibility = await repo.getCoordinatorEligibility();
    expect(eligibility.isVerified).toBe(true);
    expect(eligibility.completedAttendanceEventsCount).toBe(6);
    expect(eligibility.meetsCompletedEventsRequirement).toBe(true);
    expect(eligibility.meetsGoodRatingRequirement).toBe(true);
    expect(eligibility.coordinatorStatus).toBe('pending');
  });

  it('HttpCoordinatorRepository transitionEventStatus routes to exact transition endpoints', async () => {
    const calledUrls: string[] = [];
    const mockTransport: TransportAdapter = async (input, init) => {
      calledUrls.push(input);
      if (input.includes('/events/5/publish/')) {
        return new Response(
          JSON.stringify({
            id: 5,
            status: 'published',
            published_at: '2026-11-15T12:00:00Z',
          }),
          { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } },
        );
      }
      if (input.includes('/coordinator/events/5/summary/')) {
        return new Response(
          JSON.stringify({
            id: 5,
            title: 'Sample Event',
            status: 'published',
            start_at: '2026-11-20T08:00:00Z',
            end_at: '2026-11-20T12:00:00Z',
            acceptance_mode: 'manual',
            auto_assign_enabled: false,
            max_participants: 20,
            geo_checkin_enabled: true,
            map_provider: 'openstreetmap',
            pending_count: 0,
            accepted_count: 0,
            waitlisted_count: 0,
            assignment_count: 0,
            checked_in_count: 0,
            pending_confirmations: 0,
          }),
          { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } },
        );
      }
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ results: [] }), { status: 200 });
    };

    const client = new ApiClient(mockTransport, tokenStore, sessionMgr);
    const repo = new HttpCoordinatorRepository(client);

    const result = await repo.transitionEventStatus('5', 'published');
    expect(result.success).toBe(true);
    expect(calledUrls.some(u => u.includes('/events/5/publish/'))).toBe(true);
  });
});

/**

import React from 'react';
import ReactTestRenderer from 'react-test-renderer';
import { SafeAreaProvider } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { SettingsScreen } from '../src/features/settings/screens/SettingsScreen';
import { MainDrawerContent } from '../src/navigation/MainDrawerContent';
import { UserContext, UserContextValue } from '../src/app/UserContext';
import { ordinaryVolunteerUser } from '../src/data/mock/fixtures/demoUsers';
import { ThemeProvider } from '../src/theme/ThemeProvider';
import {
  getDataMode,
  resetDataMode,
  setDataMode,
} from '../src/app/configuration/dataMode';
import { eventApi } from '../src/data/endpoints/eventApi';

const initialMetrics = {
  frame: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 390, height: 844 },
  insets: { top: 47, left: 0, right: 0, bottom: 34 },
};

function createMockNavigation() {
  return {
    navigate: jest.fn(),
    openDrawer: jest.fn(),
    closeDrawer: jest.fn(),
    toggleDrawer: jest.fn(),
    dispatch: jest.fn(),
    reset: jest.fn(),
    goBack: jest.fn(),
    isFocused: jest.fn(() => true),
    canGoBack: jest.fn(() => true),
    getParent: jest.fn(),
    getState: jest.fn(),
    addListener: jest.fn(() => () => {}),
    removeListener: jest.fn(),
    setParams: jest.fn(),
    setOptions: jest.fn(),
  } as any;
}

function createMockDrawerProps() {
  return {
    state: {
      index: 0,
      routes: [
        { key: 'Settings-key', name: 'Settings' },
      ],
      routeNames: ['Settings'],
      type: 'drawer',
      stale: false,
      key: 'drawer-key',
    },
    navigation: createMockNavigation(),
    descriptors: {},
  } as any;
}

function renderWithTheme(ui: React.ReactElement) {
  return (
    <SafeAreaProvider initialMetrics={initialMetrics}>
      <ThemeProvider>{ui}</ThemeProvider>
    </SafeAreaProvider>
  );
}

function safeStringify(obj: unknown): string {
  const seen = new WeakSet();
  return JSON.stringify(obj, (key, value) => {
    if (key === '_owner') {
      return undefined;
    }
    if (typeof value === 'object' && value !== null) {
      if (seen.has(value)) {
        return undefined;
      }
      seen.add(value);
    }
    return value;
  });
}

describe('SettingsScreen', () => {
  it('renders appearance options (System, Light, Dark) and changes theme mode', async () => {
    const nav = createMockNavigation();

    let renderer!: ReactTestRenderer.ReactTestRenderer;
    await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
      renderer = ReactTestRenderer.create(
        renderWithTheme(<SettingsScreen navigation={nav} />),
      );
    });

    let json = safeStringify(renderer.toJSON());
    expect(json).toContain('Appearance & Theme');
    expect(json).toContain('System');
    expect(json).toContain('Light');
    expect(json).toContain('Dark');

    // Switch to dark
    const darkButton = renderer.root
      .findAllByProps({ accessibilityRole: 'button' })
      .find(b => b.props.accessibilityLabel === 'Dark');

    expect(darkButton).toBeDefined();

    await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
      darkButton!.props.onPress();
    });

    json = safeStringify(renderer.toJSON());
    expect(json).toContain('Active: DARK (Dark Theme)');
  });

  it('does NOT render the Volunteer/Coordinator role switch in Settings', async () => {
    const nav = createMockNavigation();

    let renderer!: ReactTestRenderer.ReactTestRenderer;
    await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
      renderer = ReactTestRenderer.create(
        renderWithTheme(<SettingsScreen navigation={nav} />),
      );
    });

    const json = safeStringify(renderer.toJSON());

    // Role switcher should NOT exist in Settings
    expect(json).not.toContain('QA DEMO PROFILE SWITCHER');
    expect(json).not.toContain('Switch to Ordinary Volunteer');
    expect(json).not.toContain('Switch to Approved Coordinator');
    expect(json).not.toContain('DEMO FIXTURE SWITCHER');
    expect(json).not.toContain('Current Mock Account');
  });

  it('renders application information and demo logout action with feedback', async () => {
    const nav = createMockNavigation();

    let renderer!: ReactTestRenderer.ReactTestRenderer;
    await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
      renderer = ReactTestRenderer.create(
        renderWithTheme(<SettingsScreen navigation={nav} />),
      );
    });

    let json = safeStringify(renderer.toJSON());
    expect(json).toContain('About Application');
    expect(json).toContain('Volunteer Coordination System');
    expect(json).toContain('Version: 0.8.1');

    // Logout button
    const logoutButton = renderer.root
      .findAllByProps({ accessibilityRole: 'button' })
      .find(b => b.props.accessibilityLabel === 'Log Out (Demo)');

    expect(logoutButton).toBeDefined();

    await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
      logoutButton!.props.onPress();
    });

    json = safeStringify(renderer.toJSON());
    expect(json).toContain(
      'Demo only — session logout will be handled by authentication provider in production.',
    );
  });

  describe('Connection & DataMode Diagnostics (Phase 9)', () => {
    beforeEach(async () => {
      await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
        resetDataMode();
      });
      jest.restoreAllMocks();
    });

    afterEach(async () => {
      await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
        resetDataMode();
      });
      jest.restoreAllMocks();
    });

    it('renders API diagnostics panel in development mode without exposing secrets', async () => {
      const originalDev = (globalThis as any).__DEV__;
      (globalThis as any).__DEV__ = true;

      try {
        const nav = createMockNavigation();
        let renderer!: ReactTestRenderer.ReactTestRenderer;
        await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
          renderer = ReactTestRenderer.create(
            renderWithTheme(<SettingsScreen navigation={nav} />),
          );
        });

        const json = safeStringify(renderer.toJSON());
        expect(json).toContain('API & Data Mode Diagnostics');
        expect(json).toContain('Active Data Mode: MOCK');
        expect(json).toContain('API Base URL:');
        expect(json).toContain('Test BFF Connection');

        // STRICT CONSTRAINT 1: NEVER display tokens, phone numbers, or secrets
        expect(json).not.toMatch(/token/i);
        expect(json).not.toMatch(/\+?[0-9]{10,}/);
        expect(json).not.toContain('password');
        expect(json).not.toContain('secret');

        await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
          renderer.unmount();
        });
      } finally {
        (globalThis as any).__DEV__ = originalDev;
      }
    });

    it('hides API diagnostics panel in production mode (__DEV__ = false)', async () => {
      const originalDev = (globalThis as any).__DEV__;
      (globalThis as any).__DEV__ = false;

      try {
        const nav = createMockNavigation();
        let renderer!: ReactTestRenderer.ReactTestRenderer;
        await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
          renderer = ReactTestRenderer.create(
            renderWithTheme(<SettingsScreen navigation={nav} />),
          );
        });

        const json = safeStringify(renderer.toJSON());
        expect(json).not.toContain('API & Data Mode Diagnostics');
        expect(json).not.toContain('Test BFF Connection');

        await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
          renderer.unmount();
        });
      } finally {
        (globalThis as any).__DEV__ = originalDev;
      }
    });

    it('switches data mode between mock and http safely via diagnostic buttons', async () => {
      const originalDev = (globalThis as any).__DEV__;
      (globalThis as any).__DEV__ = true;

      try {
        const nav = createMockNavigation();
        let renderer!: ReactTestRenderer.ReactTestRenderer;
        await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
          renderer = ReactTestRenderer.create(
            renderWithTheme(<SettingsScreen navigation={nav} />),
          );
        });

        expect(getDataMode()).toBe('mock');

        // Switch to HTTP
        const httpButton = renderer.root
          .findAllByProps({ accessibilityRole: 'button' })
          .find(b => b.props.accessibilityLabel === 'Switch to HTTP');
        expect(httpButton).toBeDefined();

        await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
          httpButton!.props.onPress();
        });

        expect(getDataMode()).toBe('http');
        let json = safeStringify(renderer.toJSON());
        expect(json).toContain('Active Data Mode: HTTP');

        // Switch back to Mock
        const mockButton = renderer.root
          .findAllByProps({ accessibilityRole: 'button' })
          .find(b => b.props.accessibilityLabel === 'Switch to Mock');
        expect(mockButton).toBeDefined();

        await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
          mockButton!.props.onPress();
        });

        expect(getDataMode()).toBe('mock');
        json = safeStringify(renderer.toJSON());
        expect(json).toContain('Active Data Mode: MOCK');

        await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
          renderer.unmount();
        });
      } finally {
        (globalThis as any).__DEV__ = originalDev;
      }
    });

    it('pings BFF and displays reachability and latency', async () => {
      const originalDev = (globalThis as any).__DEV__;
      (globalThis as any).__DEV__ = true;

      jest.spyOn(eventApi, 'getCharityFields').mockResolvedValueOnce({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: [],
      });

      try {
        const nav = createMockNavigation();
        let renderer!: ReactTestRenderer.ReactTestRenderer;
        await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
          renderer = ReactTestRenderer.create(
            renderWithTheme(<SettingsScreen navigation={nav} />),
          );
        });

        const pingButton = renderer.root
          .findAllByProps({ accessibilityRole: 'button' })
          .find(b => b.props.accessibilityLabel === 'Test BFF Connection');
        expect(pingButton).toBeDefined();

        await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
          pingButton!.props.onPress();
        });

        const json = safeStringify(renderer.toJSON());
        expect(json).toContain('BFF Reachable: HTTP 200');

        await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
          renderer.unmount();
        });
      } finally {
        (globalThis as any).__DEV__ = originalDev;
      }
    });
  });
});

describe('Production Safety: Side-Drawer QA Demo Switcher', () => {
  it('excludes QA profile switcher from drawer when __DEV__ is false (production)', async () => {
    const originalDev = (globalThis as any).__DEV__;
    (globalThis as any).__DEV__ = false;

    try {
      const mockValue: UserContextValue = {
        currentUser: ordinaryVolunteerUser,
        setCurrentUser: jest.fn(),
        isCoordinator: false,
        switchUserPreset: jest.fn(),
      };
      const drawerProps = createMockDrawerProps();

      let renderer!: ReactTestRenderer.ReactTestRenderer;
      await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
        renderer = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          <SafeAreaProvider initialMetrics={initialMetrics}>
            <ThemeProvider>
              <UserContext.Provider value={mockValue}>
                <MainDrawerContent {...drawerProps} />
              </UserContext.Provider>
            </ThemeProvider>
          </SafeAreaProvider>,
        );
      });

      const json = safeStringify(renderer.toJSON());
      expect(json).not.toContain('QA DEMO PROFILE SWITCHER');
      expect(json).not.toContain('Switch to ordinary volunteer');
      expect(json).not.toContain('Switch to coordinator');
    } finally {
      (globalThis as any).__DEV__ = originalDev;
    }
  });

  it('includes QA profile switcher in drawer when __DEV__ is true (development)', async () => {
    const originalDev = (globalThis as any).__DEV__;
    (globalThis as any).__DEV__ = true;

    try {
      const mockValue: UserContextValue = {
        currentUser: ordinaryVolunteerUser,
        setCurrentUser: jest.fn(),
        isCoordinator: false,
        switchUserPreset: jest.fn(),
      };
      const drawerProps = createMockDrawerProps();

      let renderer!: ReactTestRenderer.ReactTestRenderer;
      await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
        renderer = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          <SafeAreaProvider initialMetrics={initialMetrics}>
            <ThemeProvider>
              <UserContext.Provider value={mockValue}>
                <MainDrawerContent {...drawerProps} />
              </UserContext.Provider>
            </ThemeProvider>
          </SafeAreaProvider>,
        );
      });

      const json = safeStringify(renderer.toJSON());
      expect(json).toContain('QA DEMO PROFILE SWITCHER');
      expect(json).toContain('Volunteer');
      expect(json).toContain('Coordinator');
    } finally {
      (globalThis as any).__DEV__ = originalDev;
    }
  });
});

/**
 * @format

import React from 'react';
import { Text } from 'react-native';
import ReactTestRenderer from 'react-test-renderer';
import { AppIcon, IconName } from '../src/shared/components/AppIcon';
import { darkColors, darkTheme } from '../src/theme/darkTheme';
import { lightColors, lightTheme } from '../src/theme/lightTheme';
import {
  resolveThemeMode,
  ThemeContext,
  ThemeContextValue,
  ThemeProvider,
} from '../src/theme/ThemeProvider';
import {
  MemoryThemeStorage,
  PluggableThemeStorage,
  THEME_STORAGE_KEY,
  ThemeStorage,
} from '../src/theme/themeStorage';
import { radius, spacing, ThemeMode, typography } from '../src/theme/tokens';

describe('Theme Mode Resolution', () => {
  it('resolves explicit light mode regardless of system appearance', () => {
    expect(resolveThemeMode('light', 'dark')).toBe('light');
    expect(resolveThemeMode('light', 'light')).toBe('light');
    expect(resolveThemeMode('light', null)).toBe('light');
  });

  it('resolves explicit dark mode regardless of system appearance', () => {
    expect(resolveThemeMode('dark', 'light')).toBe('dark');
    expect(resolveThemeMode('dark', 'dark')).toBe('dark');
    expect(resolveThemeMode('dark', null)).toBe('dark');
  });

  it('resolves system mode based on system appearance', () => {
    expect(resolveThemeMode('system', 'dark')).toBe('dark');
    expect(resolveThemeMode('system', 'light')).toBe('light');
  });

  it('defaults system mode to light when system appearance is undefined or null', () => {
    expect(resolveThemeMode('system', undefined)).toBe('light');
    expect(resolveThemeMode('system', null)).toBe('light');
  });
});

describe('Theme Definitions & Tokens', () => {
  const requiredColorKeys = [
    'background',
    'surface',
    'surfaceRaised',
    'textPrimary',
    'textSecondary',
    'border',
    'primary',
    'onPrimary',
    'accent',
    'success',
    'warning',
    'danger',
    'muted',
  ] as const;

  it('lightTheme contains all required color keys and correct mode', () => {
    expect(lightTheme.mode).toBe('light');
    for (const key of requiredColorKeys) {
      expect(lightColors[key]).toBeDefined();
      expect(typeof lightColors[key]).toBe('string');
    }
  });

  it('darkTheme contains all required color keys and correct mode', () => {
    expect(darkTheme.mode).toBe('dark');
    for (const key of requiredColorKeys) {
      expect(darkColors[key]).toBeDefined();
      expect(typeof darkColors[key]).toBe('string');
    }
  });

  it('centralizes tokens across themes', () => {
    expect(lightTheme.spacing).toBe(spacing);
    expect(darkTheme.spacing).toBe(spacing);
    expect(lightTheme.radius).toBe(radius);
    expect(darkTheme.radius).toBe(radius);
    expect(lightTheme.typography).toBe(typography);
    expect(darkTheme.typography).toBe(typography);
  });
});

describe('Theme Storage & Preference Persistence', () => {
  it('strictly isolates storage key to display appearance preference', () => {
    expect(THEME_STORAGE_KEY).toBe('@volunteer_mgmt:theme_preference');
  });

  it('MemoryThemeStorage gets, sets, and clears theme preference', async () => {
    const storage = new MemoryThemeStorage();
    expect(await storage.getThemeMode()).toBeNull();

    await storage.setThemeMode('dark');
    expect(await storage.getThemeMode()).toBe('dark');

    await storage.setThemeMode('light');
    expect(await storage.getThemeMode()).toBe('light');

    await storage.setThemeMode('system');
    expect(await storage.getThemeMode()).toBe('system');

    await storage.clearThemeMode();
    expect(await storage.getThemeMode()).toBeNull();
  });

  it('MemoryThemeStorage accepts valid initial preference and ignores invalid values', async () => {
    const storage = new MemoryThemeStorage('dark');
    expect(await storage.getThemeMode()).toBe('dark');

    // cast invalid mode to verify runtime defensive guard
    await storage.setThemeMode('invalid_mode' as unknown as ThemeMode);
    expect(await storage.getThemeMode()).toBe('dark');
  });

  it('PluggableThemeStorage delegates to fallback memory storage when native package is absent', async () => {
    const pluggable = new PluggableThemeStorage();
    expect(await pluggable.getThemeMode()).toBeNull();

    await pluggable.setThemeMode('dark');
    expect(await pluggable.getThemeMode()).toBe('dark');

    await pluggable.clearThemeMode();
    expect(await pluggable.getThemeMode()).toBeNull();
  });

  it('PluggableThemeStorage routes to custom injected storage', async () => {
    const customMemory: ThemeStorage = {
      getThemeMode: jest.fn(async () => 'light' as ThemeMode),
      setThemeMode: jest.fn(async () => {}),
      clearThemeMode: jest.fn(async () => {}),
    };

    const pluggable = new PluggableThemeStorage();
    pluggable.setCustomStorage(customMemory);

    const mode = await pluggable.getThemeMode();
    expect(mode).toBe('light');
    expect(customMemory.getThemeMode).toHaveBeenCalled();

    await pluggable.setThemeMode('dark');
    expect(customMemory.setThemeMode).toHaveBeenCalledWith('dark');

    await pluggable.clearThemeMode();
    expect(customMemory.clearThemeMode).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

describe('ThemeProvider & Flash Prevention', () => {
  it('renders children immediately when initialMode is provided', async () => {
    let renderer!: ReactTestRenderer.ReactTestRenderer;
    await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
      renderer = ReactTestRenderer.create(
        <ThemeProvider initialMode="dark">
          <Text testID="child-content">Main Screen Content</Text>
        </ThemeProvider>,
      );
    });

    const root = renderer.root;
    const textNode = root.findByProps({ testID: 'child-content' });
    expect(textNode.props.children).toBe('Main Screen Content');
  });

  it('prevents theme flash by delaying child rendering until storage resolves', async () => {
    let resolveStorage!: (value: ThemeMode | null) => void;
    const pendingStorage: ThemeStorage = {
      getThemeMode: () =>
        new Promise<ThemeMode | null>(resolve => {
          resolveStorage = resolve;
        }),
      setThemeMode: jest.fn(async () => {}),
      clearThemeMode: jest.fn(async () => {}),
    };

    let renderer!: ReactTestRenderer.ReactTestRenderer;
    await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
      renderer = ReactTestRenderer.create(
        <ThemeProvider storage={pendingStorage}>
          <Text testID="child-content">Main Screen Content</Text>
        </ThemeProvider>,
      );
    });

    // Before storage resolution, children must not be rendered (null)
    expect(renderer.toJSON()).toBeNull();

    // Now resolve storage with dark theme preference
    await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
      resolveStorage('dark');
    });

    // Children are rendered now with dark theme
    const textNode = renderer.root.findByProps({ testID: 'child-content' });
    expect(textNode.props.children).toBe('Main Screen Content');
  });

  it('updates active theme and persists preference to storage when setThemeMode is invoked', async () => {
    const memoryStorage = new MemoryThemeStorage();
    let capturedContext!: ThemeContextValue;

    function TestConsumer(): React.JSX.Element {
      return (
        <ThemeContext.Consumer>
          {val => {
            if (val) {
              capturedContext = val;
            }
            return <Text testID="consumer">{capturedContext?.themeMode}</Text>;
          }}
        </ThemeContext.Consumer>
      );
    }

    let renderer!: ReactTestRenderer.ReactTestRenderer;
    await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
      renderer = ReactTestRenderer.create(
        <ThemeProvider storage={memoryStorage} initialMode="light">
          <TestConsumer />
        </ThemeProvider>,
      );
    });

    expect(capturedContext.themeMode).toBe('light');
    expect(capturedContext.theme.mode).toBe('light');

    // Switch to dark mode
    await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
      capturedContext.setThemeMode('dark');
    });

    expect(capturedContext.themeMode).toBe('dark');
    expect(capturedContext.theme.mode).toBe('dark');
    expect(await memoryStorage.getThemeMode()).toBe('dark');
    expect(renderer.root.findByProps({ testID: 'consumer' }).props.children).toBe('dark');
  });

  it('falls back gracefully without crashing when storage.getThemeMode rejects', async () => {
    const failingStorage: ThemeStorage = {
      getThemeMode: jest.fn().mockRejectedValue(new Error('Storage failure')),
      setThemeMode: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined),
      clearThemeMode: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined),
    };

    let renderer!: ReactTestRenderer.ReactTestRenderer;
    await ReactTestRenderer.act(async () => {
      renderer = ReactTestRenderer.create(
        <ThemeProvider storage={failingStorage}>
          <Text testID="fallback-content">Content Loaded</Text>
        </ThemeProvider>,
      );
    });

    const textNode = renderer.root.findByProps({ testID: 'fallback-content' });
    expect(textNode.props.children).toBe('Content Loaded');
  });
});

        <ThemeProvider initialMode="light">
          <AppIcon name="search" />
        </ThemeProvider>,
      );

    expect(textNode.props['aria-hidden']).toBe(true);

    expect(textNode.props.accessibilityRole).toBeUndefined();
    expect(textNode.props.accessibilityLabel).toBeUndefined();

        <ThemeProvider initialMode="light">
          <AppIcon name="search" accessibilityLabel="Search shifts and events" />
        </ThemeProvider>,
      );
    });

/**
 * @format
 */

import {
  AUTH_TOKEN_STORAGE_KEY,
  MemoryTokenStore,
  PluggableTokenStore,
} from '../src/core/auth/tokenStore';
import { SessionManager } from '../src/core/auth/sessionManager';

describe('TokenStore & SessionManager', () => {
  describe('MemoryTokenStore', () => {
    it('sets, retrieves, and clears token in memory', async () => {
      const store = new MemoryTokenStore();
      expect(await store.getToken()).toBeNull();

      await store.setToken('test-drf-token-12345');
      expect(await store.getToken()).toBe('test-drf-token-12345');

      await store.clearToken();
      expect(await store.getToken()).toBeNull();
    });

    it('trims whitespace when storing token', async () => {
      const store = new MemoryTokenStore();
      await store.setToken('   abc123token   ');
      expect(await store.getToken()).toBe('abc123token');
    });
  });

  describe('PluggableTokenStore', () => {
    it('uses custom storage backend when provided', async () => {
      const memory = new MemoryTokenStore();
      const pluggable = new PluggableTokenStore();
      pluggable.setCustomStorage(memory);

      await pluggable.setToken('custom-token-xyz');
      expect(await pluggable.getToken()).toBe('custom-token-xyz');

      await pluggable.clearToken();
      expect(await pluggable.getToken()).toBeNull();
    });

    it('isolates authentication token storage key from theme preference key', () => {
      expect(AUTH_TOKEN_STORAGE_KEY).toBe('@volunteer_mgmt:auth_token');
      expect(AUTH_TOKEN_STORAGE_KEY).not.toBe('@volunteer_mgmt:theme_preference');
    });

    it('falls back to internal memory storage when AsyncStorage is absent', async () => {
      const pluggable = new PluggableTokenStore();
      await pluggable.setToken('fallback-token');
      expect(await pluggable.getToken()).toBe('fallback-token');
      await pluggable.clearToken();
      expect(await pluggable.getToken()).toBeNull();
    });
  });

  describe('SessionManager Lifecycle and Invariants', () => {
    let memoryStore: MemoryTokenStore;
    let sessionMgr: SessionManager;

    beforeEach(() => {
      memoryStore = new MemoryTokenStore();
      sessionMgr = new SessionManager(memoryStore);
    });

    it('correctly tracks authentication status with hasValidToken', async () => {
      expect(await sessionMgr.hasValidToken()).toBe(false);

      await sessionMgr.setToken('valid-token-777');
      expect(await sessionMgr.hasValidToken()).toBe(true);
      expect(await sessionMgr.getToken()).toBe('valid-token-777');

      await sessionMgr.logout();
      expect(await sessionMgr.hasValidToken()).toBe(false);
      expect(await sessionMgr.getToken()).toBeNull();
    });

    it('invokes onUnauthorized listener ONLY when handleUnauthorized is called (HTTP 401)', async () => {
      await sessionMgr.setToken('session-token-999');

      let unauthorizedTriggered = false;
      const unsubscribe = sessionMgr.onUnauthorized(() => {
        unauthorizedTriggered = true;
      });

      await sessionMgr.handleUnauthorized();

      expect(unauthorizedTriggered).toBe(true);
      expect(await sessionMgr.getToken()).toBeNull();
      expect(await sessionMgr.hasValidToken()).toBe(false);

      unsubscribe();
    });

    it('allows unsubscribing from onUnauthorized callback', async () => {
      let callCount = 0;
      const unsubscribe = sessionMgr.onUnauthorized(() => {
        callCount++;
      });

      await sessionMgr.handleUnauthorized();
      expect(callCount).toBe(1);

      unsubscribe();

      await sessionMgr.handleUnauthorized();
      expect(callCount).toBe(1);
    });

    it('CRITICAL INVARIANT: token is preserved on non-401 scenarios', async () => {
      await sessionMgr.setToken('secure-user-token');

      // Simulating what happens during 403, 404, 409, 429, 500, network/timeout errors:
      // handleUnauthorized is NOT called
      expect(await sessionMgr.getToken()).toBe('secure-user-token');
      expect(await sessionMgr.hasValidToken()).toBe(true);
    });
  });
});

/**
 * @format
