import React from 'react';
import { StatusBar, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';
import { NavigationContainer } from '@react-navigation/native';
import { MainDrawerNavigator } from '../navigation/MainDrawerNavigator';
import { useAppTheme } from '../theme';
import { AppProviders } from './AppProviders';
import { useCurrentUser } from './UserContext';
import { isHttpMode } from './configuration/dataMode';
import { OtpLoginScreen } from '../features/auth';
import { LoadingState } from '../shared';

export function AppNavigationRoot(): React.JSX.Element {
  const { theme, isDark } = useAppTheme();
  const { isAuthenticated, isBootstrapping } = useCurrentUser();
  const httpMode = isHttpMode();

  if (httpMode && isBootstrapping) {
    return (
      <View
        style={[
          styles.centeredContainer,
          { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background },
        ]}
      >
        <StatusBar barStyle={isDark ? 'light-content' : 'dark-content'} />
        <LoadingState label="Connecting and restoring session..." />
      </View>
    );
  }

  if (httpMode && !isAuthenticated) {
    return (
      <>
        <StatusBar barStyle={isDark ? 'light-content' : 'dark-content'} />
        <OtpLoginScreen />
      </>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <StatusBar barStyle={isDark ? 'light-content' : 'dark-content'} />
      <NavigationContainer>
        <MainDrawerNavigator />
      </NavigationContainer>
    </>
  );
}

export function AppRoot(): React.JSX.Element {
  return (
    <AppProviders>
      <AppNavigationRoot />
    </AppProviders>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  centeredContainer: {
    flex: 1,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    padding: 24,
  },
});

================================================================================
/**
 * @format
 */

export type DataMode = 'mock' | 'http';

let currentDataMode: DataMode = 'mock';
const dataModeListeners = new Set<(mode: DataMode) => void>();

/**
 * Returns the active data mode ('mock' or 'http').
 * Defaults to 'mock' to preserve offline/mock baseline until explicitly enabled.
 */
export function getDataMode(): DataMode {
  return currentDataMode;
}

/**
 * Subscribes to runtime data mode changes.
 * Returns an unsubscribe callback.
 */
export function addDataModeListener(
  listener: (mode: DataMode) => void,
): () => void {
  dataModeListeners.add(listener);
  return () => {
    dataModeListeners.delete(listener);
  };
}

/**
 * Updates the active data mode and notifies registered listeners.
 */
export function setDataMode(mode: DataMode): void {
  currentDataMode = mode;
  dataModeListeners.forEach(listener => {
    try {
      listener(mode);
    } catch {
      // Listener error must not crash
    }
  });
}

/**
 * Convenience check for HTTP mode.
 */
export function isHttpMode(): boolean {
  return currentDataMode === 'http';
}

/**
 * Convenience check for Mock mode.
 */
export function isMockMode(): boolean {
  return currentDataMode === 'mock';
}

/**
 * Resets the data mode back to the default 'mock' and notifies listeners.
 */
export function resetDataMode(): void {
  currentDataMode = 'mock';
  dataModeListeners.forEach(listener => {
    try {
      listener('mock');
    } catch {
      // Listener error must not crash
    }
  });
}

/**
 * @format
 */

import React, { createContext, useContext, useMemo } from 'react';
import { DataMode, getDataMode } from './configuration/dataMode';
import type {
  CoordinatorRepository,
  EventRepository,
  IDashboardRepository,
  ProfileRepository,
} from '../data/contracts';
import {
  defaultCoordinatorRepository,
  defaultDashboardRepository,
  defaultEventRepository,
  defaultProfileRepository,
} from '../data/mock';
import {
  defaultHttpCoordinatorRepository,
  defaultHttpDashboardRepository,
  defaultHttpEventRepository,
  defaultHttpProfileRepository,
} from '../data/http';

export interface RepositoryContextType {
  dashboardRepository: IDashboardRepository;
  eventRepository: EventRepository;
  profileRepository: ProfileRepository;
  coordinatorRepository: CoordinatorRepository;
  dataMode: DataMode;
}

const fallbackContext: RepositoryContextType = {
  dashboardRepository: defaultDashboardRepository,
  eventRepository: defaultEventRepository,
  profileRepository: defaultProfileRepository,
  coordinatorRepository: defaultCoordinatorRepository,
  dataMode: 'mock',
};

const RepositoryContext = createContext<RepositoryContextType | null>(null);

export interface RepositoryProviderProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  dataMode?: DataMode;
  repositories?: Partial<RepositoryContextType>;
}

export function RepositoryProvider({
  children,
  dataMode: explicitDataMode,
  repositories: overrideRepositories,
}: RepositoryProviderProps): React.JSX.Element {
  const activeMode = explicitDataMode ?? getDataMode();

  const contextValue = useMemo<RepositoryContextType>(() => {
    const baseRepositories: Omit<RepositoryContextType, 'dataMode'> =
      activeMode === 'http'
        ? {
            dashboardRepository: defaultHttpDashboardRepository,
            eventRepository: defaultHttpEventRepository,
            profileRepository: defaultHttpProfileRepository,
            coordinatorRepository: defaultHttpCoordinatorRepository,
          }
        : {
            dashboardRepository: defaultDashboardRepository,
            eventRepository: defaultEventRepository,
            profileRepository: defaultProfileRepository,
            coordinatorRepository: defaultCoordinatorRepository,
          };

    return {
      dashboardRepository:
        overrideRepositories?.dashboardRepository ??
        baseRepositories.dashboardRepository,
      eventRepository:
        overrideRepositories?.eventRepository ??
        baseRepositories.eventRepository,
      profileRepository:
        overrideRepositories?.profileRepository ??
        baseRepositories.profileRepository,
      coordinatorRepository:
        overrideRepositories?.coordinatorRepository ??
        baseRepositories.coordinatorRepository,
      dataMode: overrideRepositories?.dataMode ?? activeMode,
    };
  }, [activeMode, overrideRepositories]);

  return (
    <RepositoryContext.Provider value={contextValue}>
      {children}
    </RepositoryContext.Provider>
  );
}

/**
 * Hook to access repositories and current data mode from the DI bridge.
 * If used outside a RepositoryProvider, falls back gracefully to the mock repositories
 * ensuring full backward compatibility with legacy screen unit tests.
 */
export function useRepositories(): RepositoryContextType {
  const context = useContext(RepositoryContext);
  return context ?? fallbackContext;
}

import React, {

import { defaultCoordinatorRepository } from '../data/mock/MockCoordinatorRepository';

} from './configuration/dataMode';
import { sessionManager } from '../core/auth/sessionManager';
import { authApi } from '../data/endpoints/authApi';
import { profileApi } from '../data/endpoints/profileApi';
import { resetDiscoveryCache } from '../features/events/screens/EventDiscoveryScreen';
import { resetApplicationsCache } from '../features/events/screens/MyApplicationsScreen';
import { resetAssignmentsCache } from '../features/events/screens/MyAssignmentsScreen';

export type UserPreset = 'volunteer' | 'coordinator' | 'pending';

export interface RequestOtpResult {
  success: boolean;
  devCode?: string;
  error?: string;
}

export interface VerifyOtpResult {
  success: boolean;
  error?: string;
}

export interface UserContextValue {
  currentUser: UserProfileSnapshot;
  setCurrentUser: (user: UserProfileSnapshot) => void;
  isCoordinator: boolean;
  switchUserPreset: (preset: UserPreset) => void;
  isAuthenticated?: boolean;
  isBootstrapping?: boolean;
  requestOtp?: (
    phone: string,
    purpose?: 'login' | 'qr_registration',
  ) => Promise<RequestOtpResult>;
  verifyOtp?: (
    phone: string,
    code: string,
    displayName?: string,
  ) => Promise<VerifyOtpResult>;
  logout?: () => Promise<void>;
  refreshSessionUser?: () => Promise<void>;
}

export interface FullUserContextValue extends UserContextValue {
  isAuthenticated: boolean;
  isBootstrapping: boolean;
  requestOtp: (
    phone: string,
    purpose?: 'login' | 'qr_registration',
  ) => Promise<RequestOtpResult>;
  verifyOtp: (
    phone: string,
    code: string,
    displayName?: string,
  ) => Promise<VerifyOtpResult>;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
  refreshSessionUser: () => Promise<void>;
}

export const UserContext = createContext<UserContextValue | undefined>(
  undefined,
);

export interface UserProviderProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
  initialUser?: UserProfileSnapshot;
  initialAuthenticated?: boolean;
}

export function UserProvider({
  children,
  initialUser = ordinaryVolunteerUser,
  initialAuthenticated,
}: UserProviderProps): React.JSX.Element {
  const [dataMode, setDataModeState] = useState<DataMode>(() => getDataMode());
  const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] =
    useState<UserProfileSnapshot>(initialUser);
  const [isAuthenticated, setIsAuthenticated] = useState<boolean>(() => {
    if (initialAuthenticated !== undefined) {
      return initialAuthenticated;
    }
    return getDataMode() === 'mock';
  });
  const [isBootstrapping, setIsBootstrapping] = useState<boolean>(
    () => getDataMode() === 'http',
  );

  // Sync with global dataMode changes
  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubscribe = addDataModeListener(newMode => {
      setDataModeState(newMode);
      if (newMode === 'mock') {
        setIsAuthenticated(true);
        setIsBootstrapping(false);
      }
    });
    return unsubscribe;
  }, []);

  // Update mock coordinator repository whenever identity changes
  useEffect(() => {
    if (defaultCoordinatorRepository.setCoordinatorIdentity) {
      defaultCoordinatorRepository.setCoordinatorIdentity({
        id: currentUser.id,
        displayName: currentUser.displayName,
        email: currentUser.email,
      });
    }
  }, [currentUser]);

  // Subscribe to session manager 401 unauthorized notifications
  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubscribe = sessionManager.onUnauthorized(() => {
      resetDiscoveryCache();
      resetApplicationsCache();
      resetAssignmentsCache();
      if (getDataMode() === 'http') {
        setIsAuthenticated(false);
      }
    });
    return unsubscribe;
  }, []);

  const refreshSessionUser = useCallback(async () => {
    const [meRes, coordRes] = await Promise.all([
      profileApi.getProfile(),
      profileApi.getCoordinatorProfile(),
    ]);

    if (!meRes.ok) {
      if (meRes.status === 401) {
        setIsAuthenticated(false);
      }
      return;
    }

    const coordStatus: CoordinatorStatus =
      (coordRes.ok &&
        (coordRes.data?.coordinator?.status as CoordinatorStatus)) ||
      'none';
    const acctStatus =
      (meRes.data.account_status as UserAccountStatus) || 'active';

    const snapshot: UserProfileSnapshot = {
      id: String(meRes.data.id),
      displayName: meRes.data.display_name,
      email: meRes.data.phone || 'volunteer@example.com',
      coordinatorStatus: coordStatus,
      accountStatus: acctStatus,
    };

    setCurrentUser(snapshot);
    setIsAuthenticated(true);
  }, []);

  // Session bootstrap on cold start in HTTP mode
  useEffect(() => {
    if (dataMode !== 'http') {
      setIsBootstrapping(false);
      setIsAuthenticated(true);
      return;
    }

    let isMounted = true;
    setIsBootstrapping(true);

    async function bootstrap() {
      const token = await sessionManager.getToken();
      if (!token || token.trim().length === 0) {
        if (isMounted) {
          setIsAuthenticated(false);
          setIsBootstrapping(false);
        }
        return;
      }

      try {
        await refreshSessionUser();
      } catch {
        // Network or non-auth error: preserve token, do not invalidate
      } finally {
        if (isMounted) {
          setIsBootstrapping(false);
        }
      }
    }

    bootstrap();

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [dataMode, refreshSessionUser]);

  const requestOtp = useCallback(
    async (
      phone: string,
      purpose: 'login' | 'qr_registration' = 'login',
    ): Promise<RequestOtpResult> => {
      const res = await authApi.requestOtp({ phone, purpose });
      if (!res.ok) {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: res.error.message,
        };
      }
      return {
        success: true,
        devCode: res.data.dev_code,
      };
    },
    [],
  );

  const verifyOtp = useCallback(
    async (
      phone: string,
      code: string,
      displayName?: string,
    ): Promise<VerifyOtpResult> => {
      const res = await authApi.verifyOtp({
        phone,
        code,
        purpose: 'login',
        display_name: displayName,
      });

      if (!res.ok) {
        return {
          success: false,
          error: res.error.message,
        };
      }

      await sessionManager.setToken(res.data.token);

      try {
        await refreshSessionUser();
      } catch {
        // Fallback to minimal snapshot from verify response if profile call fails
        const snapshot: UserProfileSnapshot = {
          id: String(res.data.user.id),
          displayName: res.data.user.display_name,
          email: phone,
          coordinatorStatus: 'none',
          accountStatus: 'active',
        };
        setCurrentUser(snapshot);
        setIsAuthenticated(true);
      }

      return {
        success: true,
      };
    },
    [refreshSessionUser],
  );

  const logout = useCallback(async (): Promise<void> => {
    resetDiscoveryCache();
    resetApplicationsCache();
    resetAssignmentsCache();
    if (getDataMode() === 'http') {
      try {
        await authApi.logout();
      } catch {
        // Ignore server error during logout
      }
      await sessionManager.logout();
      setIsAuthenticated(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const isCoordinator = useMemo(
    () => hasCoordinatorAccess(currentUser),
    [currentUser],
  );

  const switchUserPreset = useCallback((preset: UserPreset) => {
    switch (preset) {
      case 'coordinator':
        setCurrentUser(approvedCoordinatorUser);
        break;
      case 'pending':
        setCurrentUser(pendingCoordinatorUser);
        break;
      case 'volunteer':
      default:
        setCurrentUser(ordinaryVolunteerUser);
        break;
    }
  }, []);

  const contextValue = useMemo<FullUserContextValue>(
    () => ({
      currentUser,
      setCurrentUser,
      isCoordinator,
      switchUserPreset,
      isAuthenticated,
      isBootstrapping,
      requestOtp,
      verifyOtp,
      logout,
      refreshSessionUser,
    }),
    [
      currentUser,
      isCoordinator,
      switchUserPreset,
      isAuthenticated,
      isBootstrapping,
      requestOtp,
      verifyOtp,
      logout,
      refreshSessionUser,
    ],
  );

  return (
    <UserContext.Provider value={contextValue}>{children}</UserContext.Provider>
  );
}

const fallbackUserContext: FullUserContextValue = {
  currentUser: ordinaryVolunteerUser,
  setCurrentUser: () => {},
  isCoordinator: false,
  switchUserPreset: () => {},
  isAuthenticated: true,
  isBootstrapping: false,
  requestOtp: async () => ({ success: true }),
  verifyOtp: async () => ({ success: true }),
  logout: async () => {},
  refreshSessionUser: async () => {},
};

export function useCurrentUser(): FullUserContextValue {
  const context = useContext(UserContext);
  if (!context) {
    return fallbackUserContext;
  }

  return {
    ...fallbackUserContext,
    ...context,
  };
}

/**
 * @format
 */

import { buildApiUrl, getApiConfig } from './apiConfig';
import {
  AppError,
  createNetworkError,
  createTimeoutError,
  normalizeApiError,
} from './apiError';
import { apiFailure, apiSuccess, ApiResult } from './apiResult';
import { defaultTokenStore, TokenStore } from '../auth/tokenStore';
import { sessionManager, SessionManager } from '../auth/sessionManager';

export type TransportAdapter = (
  input: string,
  init?: RequestInit,
) => Promise<Response>;

export interface RequestOptions {
  headers?: Record<string, string>;
  authRequired?: boolean;
  timeoutMs?: number;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
  params?: Record<
    string,
    string | number | boolean | undefined | null | Array<string | number>
  >;
}

/**
 * Standard fetch transport implementation.
 */
const defaultTransport: TransportAdapter = (input, init) => fetch(input, init);

/**
 * Serializes query parameters into URL search string.
 */
function serializeQueryParams(
  params?: Record<
    string,
    string | number | boolean | undefined | null | Array<string | number>
  >,
): string {
  if (!params) {
    return '';
  }
  const searchParams = new URLSearchParams();
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(params)) {
    if (value === undefined || value === null) {
      continue;
    }
    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
      for (const item of value) {
        searchParams.append(key, String(item));
      }
    } else {
      searchParams.append(key, String(value));
    }
  }
  const queryString = searchParams.toString();
  return queryString ? `?${queryString}` : '';
}

export class ApiClient {
  private transport: TransportAdapter;
  private tokenStore: TokenStore;
  private sessionMgr: SessionManager;

  constructor(
    transport: TransportAdapter = defaultTransport,
    tokenStore: TokenStore = defaultTokenStore,
    sessionMgr: SessionManager = sessionManager,
  ) {
    this.transport = transport;
    this.tokenStore = tokenStore;
    this.sessionMgr = sessionMgr;
  }

  /**
   * Allows injecting a custom TransportAdapter (e.g. mock transport in unit tests).
   */
  public setTransport(transport: TransportAdapter): void {
    this.transport = transport;
  }

  /**
   * Checks whether a non-empty session token is stored.
   */
  public async hasValidToken(): Promise<boolean> {
    return this.sessionMgr.hasValidToken();
  }

  /**
   * Internal request executor handling URL construction, auth headers, timeouts,
   * cancellation, 204 handling, and error normalization.
   */
  public async request<T>(
    method: 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PATCH' | 'PUT' | 'DELETE',
    endpointPath: string,
    body?: unknown,
    options: RequestOptions = {},
  ): Promise<ApiResult<T>> {
    const config = getApiConfig();
    const query = serializeQueryParams(options.params);
    const fullUrl = `${buildApiUrl(endpointPath, config)}${query}`;

    const headers: Record<string, string> = {
      Accept: 'application/json',
      ...(options.headers || {}),
    };

    // Inject DRF Token header when required
    const authRequired = options.authRequired ?? true;
    if (authRequired && !headers.Authorization && !headers.authorization) {
      const token = await this.tokenStore.getToken();
      if (token && token.trim().length > 0) {
        headers.Authorization = `Token ${token.trim()}`;
      }
    }

    let requestBody: RequestInit['body'];
    if (body !== undefined && body !== null) {
      if (typeof FormData !== 'undefined' && body instanceof FormData) {
        requestBody = body as unknown as RequestInit['body'];
      } else if (typeof body === 'string') {
        requestBody = body;
        if (!headers['Content-Type']) {
          headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
        }
      } else {
        requestBody = JSON.stringify(body);
        if (!headers['Content-Type']) {
          headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
        }
      }
    }

    // Timeout & Abort management
    const timeoutMs = options.timeoutMs ?? config.defaultTimeoutMs;
    const controller = new AbortController();
    let isTimedOut = false;

    const timeoutTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      isTimedOut = true;
      controller.abort();
    }, timeoutMs);

    // If caller provided their own signal, listen to it as well
    if (options.signal) {
      options.signal.addEventListener('abort', () => {
        controller.abort();
      });
    }

    try {
      const response = await this.transport(fullUrl, {
        method,
        headers,
        body: requestBody,
        signal: controller.signal,
      });

      clearTimeout(timeoutTimer);

      // Handle 204 No Content
      if (response.status === 204) {
        return apiSuccess(undefined as unknown as T, 204);
      }

      // Read response body safely
      let responseBody: unknown;
      const text = await response.text();
      if (text && text.trim().length > 0) {
        try {
          responseBody = JSON.parse(text);
        } catch {
          responseBody = text;
        }
      }

      // Check success
      if (response.ok) {
        return apiSuccess(responseBody as T, response.status);
      }

      // On 401 Unauthorized, invalidate session immediately
      if (response.status === 401) {
        await this.sessionMgr.handleUnauthorized();
      }

      const error = normalizeApiError(response.status, responseBody);
      return apiFailure(error, response.status);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      clearTimeout(timeoutTimer);

      if (isTimedOut) {
        return apiFailure(createTimeoutError(`Request timed out after ${timeoutMs}ms.`));
      }

      const message =
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Network request failed.';
      return apiFailure(createNetworkError(message));
    }
  }

  public async get<T>(
    endpointPath: string,
    options?: RequestOptions,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<T>> {
    return this.request<T>('GET', endpointPath, undefined, options);
  }

  public async post<T>(
    endpointPath: string,
    body?: unknown,
    options?: RequestOptions,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<T>> {
    return this.request<T>('POST', endpointPath, body, options);
  }

  public async patch<T>(
    endpointPath: string,
    body?: unknown,
    options?: RequestOptions,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<T>> {
    return this.request<T>('PATCH', endpointPath, body, options);
  }

  public async put<T>(
    endpointPath: string,
    body?: unknown,
    options?: RequestOptions,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<T>> {
    return this.request<T>('PUT', endpointPath, body, options);
  }

  public async delete<T>(
    endpointPath: string,
    options?: RequestOptions,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<T>> {
    return this.request<T>('DELETE', endpointPath, undefined, options);
  }

  public async postMultipart<T>(
    endpointPath: string,
    formData: FormData,
    options?: RequestOptions,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<T>> {
    return this.request<T>('POST', endpointPath, formData, options);
  }
}

export const defaultApiClient = new ApiClient();

/**
 * @format
 */

import { Platform } from 'react-native';

export interface ApiConfig {
  baseUrl: string;
  apiPrefix: string;
  defaultTimeoutMs: number;
}

/**
 * Resolves the default base host depending on platform.
 * Android emulator routes host machine loopback to 10.0.2.2.
 * iOS simulators and web default to localhost.
 */
function getDefaultBaseHost(): string {
  if (Platform.OS === 'android') {
    return 'http://10.0.2.2:8000';
  }
  return 'http://localhost:8000';
}

const DEFAULT_CONFIG: ApiConfig = {
  baseUrl: getDefaultBaseHost(),
  apiPrefix: '/api/v1',
  defaultTimeoutMs: 15000,
};

let currentConfig: ApiConfig = { ...DEFAULT_CONFIG };

/**
 * Returns current API configuration.
 */
export function getApiConfig(): ApiConfig {
  return { ...currentConfig };
}

/**
 * Updates partial or full API configuration.
 */
export function setApiConfig(overrides: Partial<ApiConfig>): void {
  currentConfig = {
    ...currentConfig,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

/**
 * Resets API configuration to platform defaults.
 */
export function resetApiConfig(): void {
  currentConfig = { ...DEFAULT_CONFIG };
}

/**
 * Builds a normalized, absolute URL for a given BFF endpoint path.
 * Guarantees exactly one leading slash on prefix and endpoint without duplicate slashes.
 *
 * Example:
 * buildApiUrl('/events/') -> "http://10.0.2.2:8000/api/v1/events/"
 * buildApiUrl('auth/otp/request/') -> "http://10.0.2.2:8000/api/v1/auth/otp/request/"
 */
export function buildApiUrl(endpointPath: string, config: ApiConfig = currentConfig): string {
  const normalizedBase = config.baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, '');
  const prefix = config.apiPrefix ? `/${config.apiPrefix.replace(/^\/+|\/+$/g, '')}` : '';
  const cleanPath = endpointPath.startsWith('/') ? endpointPath : `/${endpointPath}`;

  // If path already starts with the prefix, don't duplicate it
  if (prefix && cleanPath.startsWith(prefix)) {
    return `${normalizedBase}${cleanPath}`;
  }

  return `${normalizedBase}${prefix}${cleanPath}`;
}

/**
 * @format
 */

export type AppError =
  | { kind: 'network'; retryable: boolean; message: string }
  | { kind: 'timeout'; retryable: boolean; message: string }
  | { kind: 'unauthorized'; code?: string; message: string }
  | { kind: 'forbidden'; code: string; message: string }
  | { kind: 'notFound'; code: string; message: string }
  | {
      kind: 'validation';
      code: string;
      message: string;
      fields?: Record<string, string[]>;
      meta?: Record<string, unknown>;
    }
  | { kind: 'conflict'; code: string; message: string }
  | { kind: 'rateLimited'; code: string; message: string }
  | { kind: 'server'; code?: string; message: string }
  | { kind: 'unknown'; message: string };

interface StandardDomainErrorEnvelope {
  error: {
    code?: string;
    message?: string;
    detail?: string;
    [key: string]: unknown;
  };
}

interface GeoRejectionEnvelope {
  result?: string;
  distance_m?: number;
  message?: string;
  detail?: string;
  success?: boolean;
  [key: string]: unknown;
}

/**
 * Type guard for standard domain error envelope: {"error": {"code": "...", "message": "..."}}
 */
function isStandardDomainEnvelope(
  body: unknown,
): body is StandardDomainErrorEnvelope {
  return (
    typeof body === 'object' &&
    body !== null &&
    'error' in body &&
    typeof (body as Record<string, unknown>).error === 'object' &&
    (body as Record<string, unknown>).error !== null
  );
}

/**
 * Type guard for Geo check-in rejection envelope: {"result": "...", "distance_m": ..., "success": false}
 */
function isGeoRejectionEnvelope(body: unknown): body is GeoRejectionEnvelope {
  if (typeof body !== 'object' || body === null) {
    return false;
  }
  const obj = body as Record<string, unknown>;
  return 'result' in obj && ('distance_m' in obj || obj.success === false);
}

/**
 * Parses DRF field validation errors into Record<string, string[]>:
 * e.g., { "phone": ["This field is required."], "notes": "Invalid text" }
 */
function extractDrfFieldErrors(
  bodyObj: Record<string, unknown>,
): Record<string, string[]> | undefined {
  const fields: Record<string, string[]> = {};
  const ignoredKeys = new Set([
    'error',
    'code',
    'detail',
    'message',
    'result',
    'success',
    'distance_m',
  ]);

  let foundFieldErrors = false;

  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(bodyObj)) {
    if (ignoredKeys.has(key)) {
      continue;
    }
    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
      fields[key] = value.map(v => String(v));
      foundFieldErrors = true;
    } else if (typeof value === 'string') {
      fields[key] = [value];
      foundFieldErrors = true;
    }
  }

  return foundFieldErrors ? fields : undefined;
}

/**
 * Normalizes HTTP status codes and responses into strongly-typed AppError union.
 * Supports all 3 Django BFF error representations:
 * 1. Standard domain error: {"error": {"code": "...", "message": "..."}}
 * 2. Geo check-in rejection: {"result": "outside_geofence", "distance_m": 120, "success": false}
 * 3. DRF serializer field validation: {"field_name": ["error message"]}
 */
export function normalizeApiError(status: number, body: unknown): AppError {
  let code: string | undefined;
  let message: string | undefined;
  let fields: Record<string, string[]> | undefined;
  let meta: Record<string, unknown> | undefined;

  if (typeof body === 'object' && body !== null) {
    const obj = body as Record<string, unknown>;

    // Case 1: Standard Domain Error
    if (isStandardDomainEnvelope(body)) {
      code = body.error.code;
      message = body.error.message || body.error.detail;
    }
    // Case 2: Geo check-in rejection
    else if (isGeoRejectionEnvelope(body)) {
      code = body.result;
      message =
        body.message ||
        body.detail ||
        (body.result === 'outside_geofence'
          ? `Outside allowable check-in radius (${Math.round(body.distance_m ?? 0)}m away)`
          : body.result);
      meta = {
        distance_m: body.distance_m,
        success: body.success,
      };
    }
    // Case 3: DRF field errors or general object
    else {
      if (typeof obj.detail === 'string') {
        message = obj.detail;
      } else if (typeof obj.message === 'string') {
        message = obj.message;
      }
      if (typeof obj.code === 'string') {
        code = obj.code;
      }
      fields = extractDrfFieldErrors(obj);
      if (!message && fields) {
        const firstField = Object.keys(fields)[0];
        const firstError = fields[firstField]?.[0];
        message = firstError ? `${firstField}: ${firstError}` : 'Validation error';
      }
    }
  } else if (typeof body === 'string' && body.trim().length > 0) {
    message = body;
  }

  // Status-based mapping
  switch (status) {
    case 400:
    case 422:
      return {
        kind: 'validation',
        code: code || 'validation_error',
        message: message || 'Validation failed. Please check your input.',
        fields,
        meta,
      };

    case 401:
      return {
        kind: 'unauthorized',
        code: code || 'unauthorized',
        message: message || 'Authentication required or session expired.',
      };

    case 403:
      return {
        kind: 'forbidden',
        code: code || 'permission_denied',
        message: message || 'You do not have permission to perform this action.',
      };

    case 404:
      return {
        kind: 'notFound',
        code: code || 'not_found',
        message: message || 'The requested resource was not found.',
      };

    case 409:
      return {
        kind: 'conflict',
        code: code || 'conflict',
        message: message || 'The request conflicts with current server state.',
      };

    case 429:
      return {
        kind: 'rateLimited',
        code: code || 'throttled',
        message: message || 'Too many requests. Please slow down.',
      };

    case 500:
    case 502:
    case 503:
    case 504:
      return {
        kind: 'server',
        code: code || 'server_error',
        message: message || 'A server error occurred. Please try again later.',
      };

    default:
      if (status >= 400 && status < 500) {
        return {
          kind: 'validation',
          code: code || 'client_error',
          message: message || `Client error (${status})`,
          fields,
          meta,
        };
      }
      if (status >= 500) {
        return {
          kind: 'server',
          code: code || 'server_error',
          message: message || `Server error (${status})`,
        };
      }
      return {
        kind: 'unknown',
        message: message || `Unexpected response (${status})`,
      };
  }
}

/**
 * Creates a client-side network error.
 */
export function createNetworkError(
  message = 'Network connection failed.',
): Extract<AppError, { kind: 'network' }> {
  return {
    kind: 'network',
    retryable: true,
    message,
  };
}

/**
 * Creates a client-side timeout error.
 */
export function createTimeoutError(
  message = 'Request timed out.',
): Extract<AppError, { kind: 'timeout' }> {
  return {
    kind: 'timeout',
    retryable: true,
    message,
  };
}

/**
 * @format
 */

import type { AppError } from './apiError';

export type ApiResult<T> =
  | { ok: true; data: T; status: number }
  | { ok: false; error: AppError; status?: number };

/**
 * Constructs a successful ApiResult.
 */
export function apiSuccess<T>(data: T, status = 200): ApiResult<T> {
  return {
    ok: true,
    data,
    status,
  };
}

/**
 * Constructs a failed ApiResult.
 */
export function apiFailure<T = never>(error: AppError, status?: number): ApiResult<T> {
  return {
    ok: false,
    error,
    status,
  };
}

/**
 * @format
 */

import { defaultTokenStore, TokenStore } from './tokenStore';

export type UnauthorizedListener = () => void;

export class SessionManager {
  private tokenStore: TokenStore;
  private unauthorizedListeners: Set<UnauthorizedListener> = new Set();

  constructor(tokenStore: TokenStore = defaultTokenStore) {
    this.tokenStore = tokenStore;
  }

  /**
   * Registers a listener callback invoked ONLY on HTTP 401 Unauthorized responses.
   * Returns an unsubscribe function.
   */
  public onUnauthorized(listener: UnauthorizedListener): () => void {
    this.unauthorizedListeners.add(listener);
    return () => {
      this.unauthorizedListeners.delete(listener);
    };
  }

  /**
   * Invoked ONLY when an HTTP 401 Unauthorized response is received.
   * Clears the active auth token and notifies listeners.
   *
   * STRICT ARCHITECTURAL INVARIANT:
   * NEVER called on HTTP 403 (Forbidden), 404 (Not Found), 409 (Conflict),
   * 429 (Rate Limited), 5xx (Server Error), or network/timeout errors.
   */
  public async handleUnauthorized(): Promise<void> {
    await this.tokenStore.clearToken();
    for (const listener of this.unauthorizedListeners) {
      try {
        listener();
      } catch {
        // Listener error must not prevent other listeners from being called
      }
    }
  }

  /**
   * Stores a new session token.
   */
  public async setToken(token: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.tokenStore.setToken(token);
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the current session token.
   */
  public async getToken(): Promise<string | null> {
    return this.tokenStore.getToken();
  }

  /**
   * Checks whether a non-empty token is stored.
   */
  public async hasValidToken(): Promise<boolean> {
    const token = await this.tokenStore.getToken();
    return Boolean(token && token.trim().length > 0);
  }

  /**
   * Explicitly logs out the user and clears the persisted session token.
   */
  public async logout(): Promise<void> {
    await this.tokenStore.clearToken();
  }

  /**
   * Allows swapping the underlying TokenStore (useful for tests or custom store injection).
   */
  public setTokenStore(store: TokenStore): void {
    this.tokenStore = store;
  }
}

export const sessionManager = new SessionManager();

/**
 * @format
 */

/**
 * Storage key strictly isolated to authentication token storage.
 * Isolated from theme preference (@volunteer_mgmt:theme_preference).
 */
export const AUTH_TOKEN_STORAGE_KEY = '@volunteer_mgmt:auth_token';

export interface TokenStore {
  getToken(): Promise<string | null>;
  setToken(token: string): Promise<void>;
  clearToken(): Promise<void>;
}

/**
 * In-memory fallback token store for tests and platforms without async storage.
 */
export class MemoryTokenStore implements TokenStore {
  private token: string | null = null;

  constructor(initialToken: string | null = null) {
    this.token = initialToken;
  }

  async getToken(): Promise<string | null> {
    return this.token;
  }

  async setToken(token: string): Promise<void> {
    this.token = token.trim();
  }

  async clearToken(): Promise<void> {
    this.token = null;
  }
}

/**
 * Pluggable token store.
 * Attempts to use `@react-native-async-storage/async-storage` when available;
 * falls back to in-memory store seamlessly.
 */
export class PluggableTokenStore implements TokenStore {
  private fallback: TokenStore;
  private customStorage: TokenStore | null = null;

  constructor(fallback: TokenStore = new MemoryTokenStore()) {
    this.fallback = fallback;
  }

  /**
   * Allows injecting a custom storage backend (e.g., in unit tests).
   */
  public setCustomStorage(storage: TokenStore | null): void {
    this.customStorage = storage;
  }

  async getToken(): Promise<string | null> {
    if (this.customStorage) {
      return this.customStorage.getToken();
    }
    try {
      // Dynamic try-require pattern matching themeStorage.ts
      const asyncStorage = require('@react-native-async-storage/async-storage');
      const storageModule = asyncStorage.default || asyncStorage;
      if (storageModule && typeof storageModule.getItem === 'function') {
        const val = await storageModule.getItem(AUTH_TOKEN_STORAGE_KEY);
        return typeof val === 'string' && val.length > 0 ? val : null;
      }
    } catch {
      // Package not installed or unavailable; use memory fallback
    }
    return this.fallback.getToken();
  }

  async setToken(token: string): Promise<void> {
    const trimmed = token.trim();
    if (this.customStorage) {
      return this.customStorage.setToken(trimmed);
    }
    try {
      const asyncStorage = require('@react-native-async-storage/async-storage');
      const storageModule = asyncStorage.default || asyncStorage;
      if (storageModule && typeof storageModule.setItem === 'function') {
        await storageModule.setItem(AUTH_TOKEN_STORAGE_KEY, trimmed);
        return;
      }
    } catch {
      // Fallback to memory
    }
    return this.fallback.setToken(trimmed);
  }

  async clearToken(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.customStorage) {
      return this.customStorage.clearToken();
    }
    try {
      const asyncStorage = require('@react-native-async-storage/async-storage');
      const storageModule = asyncStorage.default || asyncStorage;
      if (storageModule && typeof storageModule.removeItem === 'function') {
        await storageModule.removeItem(AUTH_TOKEN_STORAGE_KEY);
        return;
      }
    } catch {
      // Fallback to memory
    }
    return this.fallback.clearToken();
  }
}

export const defaultTokenStore = new PluggableTokenStore();

    mockClient = new ApiClient(deterministicFetch, tokenStore, sessionMgr);
    eventRepo = new HttpEventRepository(mockClient);
    coordinatorRepo = new HttpCoordinatorRepository(mockClient);
    coordinatorRepo.setCoordinatorIdentity({

  it('executes full mobile lifecycle seamlessly across Http repositories with parity', async () => {
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Step 1: OTP Request & Verify
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const otpReq = await authApi.requestOtp({ phone: '+15551234567', purpose: 'login' }, mockClient);
    expect(otpReq.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!otpReq.ok) return;
    expect(otpReq.data.dev_code).toBe('654321');

    const otpVerify = await authApi.verifyOtp(
      { phone: '+15551234567', code: '654321', purpose: 'login' },
      mockClient,
    );
    expect(otpVerify.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!otpVerify.ok) return;
    expect(otpVerify.data.token).toBe('e2e-valid-jwt-token-xyz');
    expect(otpVerify.data.user.id).toBe(42);

    // Save token to session store
    await sessionMgr.setToken(otpVerify.data.token);
    expect(await tokenStore.getToken()).toBe('e2e-valid-jwt-token-xyz');

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Step 2: Session Bootstrap (Profile & Coordinator status)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const meRes = await profileApi.getProfile(mockClient);
    expect(meRes.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!meRes.ok) return;
    expect(meRes.data.display_name).toBe('Alex Johnson');
    expect(meRes.data.account_status).toBe('active');

    const coordRes = await profileApi.getCoordinatorProfile(mockClient);
    expect(coordRes.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!coordRes.ok) return;
    expect(coordRes.data.coordinator).toBeNull();

    const profileSnapshot = await profileRepo.getProfile();
    expect(profileSnapshot.id).toBe('42');
    expect(profileSnapshot.displayName).toBe('Alex Johnson');

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Step 3: Browse Events & Filters
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const events = await eventRepo.getEvents({ query: 'Coastal' });
    expect(events.length).toBe(1);
    expect(events[0].id).toBe('101');
    expect(events[0].title).toBe('Coastal Cleanup & Dune Restoration');
    expect(events[0].spotsRemaining).toBe(10);

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Step 4: Event Detail & Posts
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const eventDetail = await eventRepo.getEventById('101');
    expect(eventDetail).not.toBeNull();
    expect(eventDetail!.id).toBe('101');
    expect(eventDetail!.posts.length).toBe(2);
    expect(eventDetail!.posts[0].id).toBe('501');
    expect(eventDetail!.posts[0].title).toBe('Team Leader - Dune Team');
    expect(eventDetail!.geoCheckinEnabled).toBe(true);

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Step 5: Volunteer Applies to Post
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const applyResult = await eventRepo.applyToPost('101', '501', 'Passionate about dune ecology');
    expect(applyResult.success).toBe(true);
    expect(serverApplications.length).toBe(1);
    expect(serverApplications[0].status).toBe('submitted');

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Step 6: Coordinator Accepts Application & Auto-Assigns
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const coordApps = await coordinatorRepo.getApplications('101');
    expect(coordApps.length).toBe(1);
    expect(coordApps[0].id).toBe('901');
    expect(coordApps[0].applicantName).toBe('Alex Johnson');

    const acceptResult = await coordinatorRepo.updateApplicationStatus('901', 'accepted');
    expect(acceptResult.success).toBe(true);

    const autoAssignResult = await coordinatorRepo.runAutoAssignment('101');
    expect(autoAssignResult.success).toBe(true);
    expect(serverAssignments.length).toBe(1);
    expect(serverAssignments[0].status).toBe('assigned');

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Step 7: Volunteer Confirms Assignment
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const myAssignments = await eventRepo.getUserAssignments();
    expect(myAssignments.length).toBe(1);
    expect(myAssignments[0].id).toBe('801');
    expect(myAssignments[0].status).toBe('assigned');

    const confirmResult = await eventRepo.confirmAssignment!('801');
    expect(confirmResult.success).toBe(true);
    expect(serverAssignments[0].status).toBe('confirmed');

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Step 8: Volunteer Acknowledges Onboarding Instructions
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const onboarding = await eventRepo.getEventOnboarding!('101');
    expect(onboarding.requireOnboardingAck).toBe(true);
    expect(onboarding.acknowledged).toBe(false);

    const ackResult = await eventRepo.acknowledgeOnboarding!('101', 'Boots ready');
    expect(ackResult.success).toBe(true);
    expect(ackResult.acknowledged).toBe(true);

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Step 9: Volunteer Geo Check-In (Constraint 3)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const geoResult = await eventRepo.submitGeoCheckIn!('101', 37.7749, -122.4194, 5);
    expect(geoResult.success).toBe(true);
    expect(geoResult.result).toBe('accepted');
    expect(geoResult.distanceM).toBe(14.2);
    expect(serverAttendance.length).toBe(1);

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Step 10: Coordinator Confirms Geo Attendance
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const attendanceRoster = await coordinatorRepo.getAttendanceRoster('101');
    expect(attendanceRoster.length).toBe(1);
    expect(attendanceRoster[0].id).toBe('701');
    expect(attendanceRoster[0].volunteerName).toBe('Alex Johnson');

    const confirmAttResult = await coordinatorRepo.confirmGeoAttendance('701');
    expect(confirmAttResult.success).toBe(true);

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Step 11: Volunteer Completes Shift Checkout (Constraint 1)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const checkoutResult = await eventRepo.checkOutAttendance!('701');
    expect(checkoutResult.success).toBe(true);
    expect(serverAttendance[0].attendance_status).toBe('completed');

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Step 12: Volunteer Submits Event Feedback
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const feedbackResult = await eventRepo.submitEventFeedback!(
      '101',
      5,
      'Excellent coordination, loved restoring the dunes!',
      ['organized', 'supportive_team'],
      'volunteer_to_event',
    );
    expect(feedbackResult.success).toBe(true);
    expect(feedbackReceived.score).toBe(5);
    expect(feedbackReceived.comment).toBe('Excellent coordination, loved restoring the dunes!');

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Step 13: Logout & Screen Cache Clearing (Constraint 2)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const logoutRes = await authApi.logout(mockClient);
    expect(logoutRes.ok).toBe(true);
    await sessionMgr.logout();
    expect(await tokenStore.getToken()).toBeNull();

    // Verify cache resets
    resetDiscoveryCache();
    resetApplicationsCache();
    resetAssignmentsCache();

    const discoveryCache = getDiscoveryCache();
    expect(discoveryCache.searchQuery).toBe('');
    expect(discoveryCache.scrollOffset).toBe(0);

    const appsCache = getApplicationsCache();
    expect(appsCache.statusFilter).toBe('all');
    expect(appsCache.scrollOffset).toBe(0);

    const assignCache = getAssignmentsCache();
    expect(assignCache.statusFilter).toBe('all');
    expect(assignCache.scrollOffset).toBe(0);
  });
});

/**
 * @format
 */

import type {
  CoordinatorApplicationItem,
  CoordinatorApplicationStatus,
  CoordinatorAssignmentItem,
  CoordinatorAttendanceItem,
  CoordinatorEventDetail,
  CoordinatorEventStatus,
  CoordinatorEventSummary,
  CoordinatorOverviewMetrics,
  CreateEventWizardDraft,
} from '../../domain/models/coordinator';

export interface CoordinatorOperationResult<T = void> {
  success: boolean;
  message: string;
  data?: T;
}

export interface CoordinatorRepository {
  /**
   * Fetches high-level metrics for the coordinator dashboard.
   */
  getOverviewMetrics(): Promise<CoordinatorOverviewMetrics>;

  /**
   * Fetches the list of events owned or coordinated by the user.
   */
  getMyEvents(): Promise<CoordinatorEventSummary[]>;

  /**
   * Fetches full coordinator details for an event by ID.
   */
  getEventDetail(eventId: string): Promise<CoordinatorEventDetail | null>;

  /**
   * Creates a new draft event using the validated wizard data.
   * Enforces that status is 'draft', version is 1, and owner is the coordinator.
   */
  createDraftEvent(
    draft: CreateEventWizardDraft,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorEventDetail>>;

  /**
   * Transitions an event to a valid target status.
   * Rejects writes if the event is in owner-as-monitor mode.
   */
  transitionEventStatus(
    eventId: string,
    targetStatus: CoordinatorEventStatus,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorEventDetail>>;

  /**
   * Fetches applications for coordinator events, optionally filtered by eventId.
   */
  getApplications(eventId?: string): Promise<CoordinatorApplicationItem[]>;

  /**
   * Updates an application status (accept, reject, waitlist).
   * Rejects writes if the event is in owner-as-monitor mode.
   */
  updateApplicationStatus(
    applicationId: string,
    status: CoordinatorApplicationStatus,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorApplicationItem>>;

  /**
   * Fetches active and confirmed assignments for coordinator events.
   */
  getAssignments(eventId?: string): Promise<CoordinatorAssignmentItem[]>;

  /**
   * Fetches accepted applications that do not currently have an active assignment.
   */
  getUnassignedApplications(
    eventId?: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorApplicationItem[]>;

  /**
   * Runs auto-assignment demo preview.
   * Rejects writes if the event is in owner-as-monitor mode.
   */
  runAutoAssignment(
    eventId: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorAssignmentItem[]>>;

  /**
   * Manually assigns an accepted application to a post.
   * Rejects writes if the event is in owner-as-monitor mode.
   */
  assignVolunteerManually(
    applicationId: string,
    postId: string,
    groupCode?: string,
    isGroupLeader?: boolean,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorAssignmentItem>>;

  /**
   * Cancels an existing assignment.
   * Rejects writes if the event is in owner-as-monitor mode.
   */
  cancelAssignment(
    assignmentId: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorAssignmentItem>>;

  /**
   * Fetches the full attendance roster for an event.
   */
  getAttendanceRoster(eventId?: string): Promise<CoordinatorAttendanceItem[]>;

  /**
   * Fetches pending geo check-in attempts requiring coordinator confirmation.
   */
  getPendingGeoConfirmations(
    eventId?: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorAttendanceItem[]>;

  /**
   * Confirms a pending geo check-in attempt.
   * Rejects writes if the event is in owner-as-monitor mode.
   */
  confirmGeoAttendance(
    attendanceId: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorAttendanceItem>>;

  /**
   * Rejects a pending geo check-in attempt.
   * Rejects writes if the event is in owner-as-monitor mode.
   */
  rejectGeoAttendance(
    attendanceId: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorAttendanceItem>>;

  /**
   * Manually checks in a volunteer on-site.
   * Rejects writes if the event is in owner-as-monitor mode.
   */
  manualCheckIn(
    attendanceId: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorAttendanceItem>>;

  /**
   * Hands over event coordination to another approved coordinator.
   */
  handoverEvent?(
    eventId: string,
    newCoordinatorUserId: string,
    reason?: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult>;

  /**
   * Retakes event coordination as the original event owner.
   */
  retakeEvent?(
    eventId: string,
    reason?: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult>;

  /**
   * Generates a new coordinator QR token for an event.
   */
  createQrToken?(
    eventId: string,
    purpose: 'walkin_registration' | 'checkin' | 'join_event',
    maxUses?: number | null,
    ttlMinutes?: number,
  ): Promise<
    CoordinatorOperationResult<{
      id: number;
      rawToken: string;
      expiresAt: string;
    }>
  >;

  /**
   * Revokes an active coordinator QR token.
   */
  revokeQrToken?(qrId: string): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult>;
}

/**
 * @format

import type {
  EventApplicationState,
  EventDetail,
  EventFilters,
  EventSummary,
  VolunteerApplicationItem,
  VolunteerAssignmentItem,
} from '../../domain/models/event';

export interface EventApplyResult {
  success: boolean;
  message: string;
  application?: EventApplicationState;
}

export interface EventRepository {
  /**
   * Fetches the list of published/active events matching optional filter criteria.
   */
  getEvents(filters?: EventFilters): Promise<EventSummary[]>;

  /**
   * Fetches full event details by ID, including posts and current demo user application state.
   */
  getEventById(id: string): Promise<EventDetail | null>;

  /**
   * Fetches active charity fields taxonomy.
   */
  getCharityFields?(): Promise<Array<{ code: string; label: string; sort_order?: number }>>;

  /**
   * Submits a demo application to an open post on an event.
   * Note: UI-only phase returns a mock success result clearly marked demo-only.
   */
  applyToPost(
    eventId: string,
    postId: string,
    note?: string,
  ): Promise<EventApplyResult>;

  /**
   * Fetches the volunteer's submitted applications.
   */
  getUserApplications(): Promise<VolunteerApplicationItem[]>;

  /**
   * Withdraws a pending or waitlisted volunteer application.
   */
  withdrawApplication(
    applicationId: string,
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string }>;

  /**
   * Fetches the volunteer's assigned, confirmed, and completed shifts.
   */
  getUserAssignments(): Promise<VolunteerAssignmentItem[]>;

  /**
   * Confirms the volunteer's assigned shift participation.
   */
  confirmAssignment?(
    assignmentId: string,
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string }>;

  /**
   * Cancels the volunteer's own scheduled shift participation (not the event).
   * Completed participation cannot be cancelled.
   */
  cancelAssignment(
    assignmentId: string,
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string }>;

  /**
   * Fetches the onboarding briefing status for an event.
   */
  getEventOnboarding?(eventId: string): Promise<{
    eventId: string;
    onboardingText: string | null;
    onboardingVersion: number;
    requireOnboardingAck: boolean;
    acknowledged: boolean;
  }>;

  /**
   * Acknowledges the onboarding briefing for an event.
   */
  acknowledgeOnboarding?(
    eventId: string,
    notes?: string,
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string; acknowledged: boolean }>;

  /**
   * Submits volunteer geo check-in telemetry for an event.
   */
  submitGeoCheckIn?(
    eventId: string,
    latitude: number,
    longitude: number,
    accuracyM?: number,
  ): Promise<{
    success: boolean;
    message: string;
    result?: string;
    distanceM?: number;
    confirmationStatus?: string;
    attendanceId?: string;
  }>;

  /**
   * Redeems a QR token (check-in, walk-in, join event).
   */
  redeemQrToken?(token: string): Promise<{
    success: boolean;
    message: string;
    result?: string;
    eventId?: string;
    attendanceId?: string;
    applicationId?: string;
  }>;

  /**
   * Checks out of an active attendance shift.
   */
  checkOutAttendance?(
    attendanceId: string,
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string; checkedOutAt?: string }>;

  /**
   * Submits feedback for an event.
   */
  submitEventFeedback?(
    eventId: string,
    score: number,
    comment?: string,
    tags?: string[],
    direction?:
      | 'coordinator_to_volunteer'
      | 'volunteer_to_event'
      | 'volunteer_to_coordinator',
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string; feedbackId?: string }>;
}

  purpose?: 'login' | 'qr_registration';

 */

  dev_code?: string;
}

export interface OtpVerifyDto {
  phone: string;
  code: string;
  purpose?: 'login' | 'qr_registration';
  display_name?: string;
}

 */
export interface AuthUserDto {
  id: number;
  uuid: string | null;
  display_name: string;
  verification_status: string;
}

/**
 * Success response for `POST /api/v1/auth/otp/verify/`.
 */
export interface OtpVerifyResponseDto {
  token: string;
  user: AuthUserDto;

/**

 * Returned by `GET /api/v1/events/`.
 */
export interface PublicEventDto {
  id: number;

  description: string | null;
  purpose_type: 'charity_event' | 'help_wanted_event';
  location_name: string | null;
  landmark_text: string | null;

  longitude: string | number | null;
  geo_radius_m: number | null;
  geo_checkin_enabled: boolean;
  map_provider: string;
  start_at: string;
  end_at: string;
  application_deadline: string | null;
  status: string;
  acceptance_mode: 'auto' | 'manual';
  max_participants: number | null;
  meals_provided: boolean;
  lodging_provided: boolean;
  travel_support_provided: boolean;
  compensation_kind: string;
  coordinator_name: string | null;
  charity_fields: string[];
  spots_remaining: number;
}

/**
 * Event role / post matching `PublicEventPostSerializer`.

export interface PublicEventPostDto {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  description: string | null;
  status: 'open' | 'full' | 'closed';
  physical_level: 'none' | 'light' | 'medium' | 'heavy';
  required_skills: string[];
  leader_needed: boolean;
  new_volunteers_allowed: boolean;
  required_headcount: number;
  assigned_count: number;
  remaining: number;
  priority?: number;
}

  onboarding_version?: number;
  compensation_note?: string | null;

  post_id?: number | null;
  source?: 'browse' | 'qr_walkin' | 'invite';
  qr_token_id?: number | null;
  note?: string | null;
}

  event_status: string | null;
  post_id: number | null;
  post_title: string | null;

/**
 * @format
 */

import { ApiClient, defaultApiClient } from '../../core/api/apiClient';
import type { ApiResult } from '../../core/api/apiResult';
import type {
  OtpRequestDto,
  OtpRequestResponseDto,
  OtpVerifyDto,
  OtpVerifyResponseDto,
} from '../dto/authDto';

export interface LogoutResponseDto {
  status: string;
  message?: string;
}

export const authApi = {
  /**
   * Request OTP code via SMS/delivery service.
   * Public endpoint (no auth required).
   */
  requestOtp(
    data: OtpRequestDto,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<OtpRequestResponseDto>> {
    return client.post<OtpRequestResponseDto>('auth/otp/request/', data, {
      authRequired: false,
    });
  },

  /**
   * Verify OTP code and obtain authentication token.
   * Public endpoint (no auth required).
   */
  verifyOtp(
    data: OtpVerifyDto,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<OtpVerifyResponseDto>> {
    return client.post<OtpVerifyResponseDto>('auth/otp/verify/', data, {
      authRequired: false,
    });
  },

  /**
   * Invalidate active authentication session on the server.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  logout(
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<LogoutResponseDto>> {
    return client.post<LogoutResponseDto>('auth/logout/', {});
  },
};

/**

import { ApiClient, defaultApiClient } from '../../core/api/apiClient';
import type { ApiResult } from '../../core/api/apiResult';
import type {
  CheckoutResponseDto,
  FeedbackSubmitRequestDto,
  FeedbackSubmitResponseDto,
  GeoCheckinRequestDto,
  GeoCheckinResponseDto,
  ManualCheckinRequestDto,
  ManualCheckinResponseDto,
  OnboardingAcknowledgeRequestDto,
  OnboardingAcknowledgeResponseDto,
  OnboardingStatusDto,
  QrRedeemResponseDto,
  QrResolveResponseDto,
  QrTokenCreateRequestDto,
  QrTokenDto,
} from '../dto/checkinDto';

export const checkinApi = {
  /**
   * Fetches the onboarding briefing status for an event.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  getOnboarding(
    eventId: number | string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<OnboardingStatusDto>> {
    return client.get<OnboardingStatusDto>(`events/${eventId}/onboarding/`);
  },

  /**
   * Acknowledges the onboarding briefing for an event.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  acknowledgeOnboarding(
    eventId: number | string,
    data: OnboardingAcknowledgeRequestDto = {},
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<OnboardingAcknowledgeResponseDto>> {
    return client.post<OnboardingAcknowledgeResponseDto>(
      `events/${eventId}/onboarding/acknowledge/`,
      data,
    );
  },

  /**
   * Previews / resolves a QR token without consuming it.
   * Public endpoint.
   */
  resolveQrToken(
    token: string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<QrResolveResponseDto>> {
    return client.post<QrResolveResponseDto>(
      'qr/resolve/',
      { token },
      { authRequired: false },
    );
  },

  /**
   * Redeems a QR token (walkin, checkin, join_event).
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  redeemQrToken(
    token: string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<QrRedeemResponseDto>> {
    return client.post<QrRedeemResponseDto>('qr/redeem/', { token });
  },

  /**
   * Performs check-in-only QR token redemption.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  qrCheckIn(
    token: string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<QrRedeemResponseDto>> {
    return client.post<QrRedeemResponseDto>('attendance/qr-checkin/', {
      token,
    });
  },

  /**
   * Creates a new QR token for an event (coordinator action).
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  createCoordinatorQrToken(
    eventId: number | string,
    data: QrTokenCreateRequestDto,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<QrTokenDto>> {
    return client.post<QrTokenDto>(
      `coordinator/events/${eventId}/qr-tokens/`,
      data,
    );
  },

  /**
   * Revokes an existing coordinator QR token.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  revokeCoordinatorQrToken(
    qrId: number | string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<{ id: number; revoked_at: string }>> {
    return client.post<{ id: number; revoked_at: string }>(
      `coordinator/qr-tokens/${qrId}/revoke/`,
      {},
    );
  },

  /**
   * Submits volunteer geo check-in telemetry for an event.
   * Note: Server returns HTTP 200 on success and HTTP 400 with response payload on rejection.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  submitGeoCheckIn(
    data: GeoCheckinRequestDto,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<GeoCheckinResponseDto>> {
    return client.post<GeoCheckinResponseDto>('attendance/geo-checkin/', data);
  },

  /**
   * Checks out of an active attendance shift.
   * Note: Always POST attendance/{attendance_id}/checkout/.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  checkoutAttendance(
    attendanceId: number | string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<CheckoutResponseDto>> {
    return client.post<CheckoutResponseDto>(
      `attendance/${attendanceId}/checkout/`,
      {},
    );
  },

  /**
   * Performs manual on-site check-in (coordinator action).
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  manualCheckIn(
    data: ManualCheckinRequestDto,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<ManualCheckinResponseDto>> {
    return client.post<ManualCheckinResponseDto>(
      'attendance/manual-checkin/',
      data,
    );
  },

  /**
   * Submits event feedback.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  submitEventFeedback(
    eventId: number | string,
    data: FeedbackSubmitRequestDto,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<FeedbackSubmitResponseDto>> {
    return client.post<FeedbackSubmitResponseDto>(
      `events/${eventId}/feedback/`,
      data,
    );
  },
};

/**
 * @format
 */

import { ApiClient, defaultApiClient } from '../../core/api/apiClient';
import type { ApiResult } from '../../core/api/apiResult';
import type { ResultsListDto } from '../dto/commonDto';
import type {
  AttendanceRosterDto,
  CoordinatorApplicationDto,
  CoordinatorAssignmentDto,
  CoordinatorEventListItemDto,
  EventCreateRequestDto,
  EventSummaryViewDto,
} from '../dto/coordinatorDto';
import type { PublicEventPostDto } from '../dto/eventDto';

export const coordinatorApi = {
  /**
   * Fetches events owned or coordinated by the user.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  getMyEvents(
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<ResultsListDto<CoordinatorEventListItemDto>>> {
    return client.get<ResultsListDto<CoordinatorEventListItemDto>>(
      'coordinator/events/',
    );
  },

  /**
   * Fetches coordinator-level summary metrics for an event.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  getEventSummary(
    eventId: number | string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<EventSummaryViewDto>> {
    return client.get<EventSummaryViewDto>(
      `coordinator/events/${eventId}/summary/`,
    );
  },

  /**
   * Creates a new event in draft status.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  createEvent(
    data: EventCreateRequestDto,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<{ id: number; status: string }>> {
    return client.post<{ id: number; status: string }>(
      'coordinator/events/',
      data,
    );
  },

  /**
   * Updates an existing event.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  updateEvent(
    eventId: number | string,
    data: Partial<EventCreateRequestDto>,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<
    ApiResult<{ id: number; status: string; onboarding_version?: number }>
  > {
    return client.patch<{
      id: number;
      status: string;
      onboarding_version?: number;
    }>(`coordinator/events/${eventId}/`, data);
  },

  /**
   * Publishes a draft event.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  publishEvent(
    eventId: number | string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<
    ApiResult<{ id: number; status: string; published_at?: string }>
  > {
    return client.post<{
      id: number;
      status: string;
      published_at?: string;
    }>(`events/${eventId}/publish/`, {});
  },

  /**
   * Activates a published event.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  activateEvent(
    eventId: number | string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<
    ApiResult<{ id: number; status: string; activated_at?: string }>
  > {
    return client.post<{
      id: number;
      status: string;
      activated_at?: string;
    }>(`events/${eventId}/activate/`, {});
  },

  /**
   * Completes an active event.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  completeEvent(
    eventId: number | string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<
    ApiResult<{ id: number; status: string; completed_at?: string }>
  > {
    return client.post<{
      id: number;
      status: string;
      completed_at?: string;
    }>(`events/${eventId}/complete/`, {});
  },

  /**
   * Cancels an event with a reason.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  cancelEvent(
    eventId: number | string,
    reason: string = 'Cancelled by coordinator',
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<
    ApiResult<{ id: number; status: string; cancelled_at?: string }>
  > {
    return client.post<{
      id: number;
      status: string;
      cancelled_at?: string;
    }>(`events/${eventId}/cancel/`, { reason });
  },

  /**
   * Hands over event coordination to another approved coordinator.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  handoverEvent(
    eventId: number | string,
    newCoordinatorUserId: number,
    reason: string = '',
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<{ id: number; current_coordinator_id: number }>> {
    return client.post<{ id: number; current_coordinator_id: number }>(
      `events/${eventId}/handover/`,
      {
        new_coordinator_user_id: newCoordinatorUserId,
        reason,
      },
    );
  },

  /**
   * Retakes event coordination as the original event owner.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  retakeEvent(
    eventId: number | string,
    reason: string = '',
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<{ id: number; current_coordinator_id: number }>> {
    return client.post<{ id: number; current_coordinator_id: number }>(
      `events/${eventId}/retake/`,
      { reason },
    );
  },

  /**
   * Fetches posts for a coordinator event.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  getEventPosts(
    eventId: number | string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<
    ApiResult<ResultsListDto<PublicEventPostDto> | PublicEventPostDto[]>
  > {
    return client.get<
      ResultsListDto<PublicEventPostDto> | PublicEventPostDto[]
    >(`coordinator/events/${eventId}/posts/`);
  },

  /**
   * Fetches applications for a coordinator event.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  getApplications(
    eventId: number | string,
    status?: string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<ResultsListDto<CoordinatorApplicationDto>>> {
    return client.get<ResultsListDto<CoordinatorApplicationDto>>(
      `coordinator/events/${eventId}/applications/`,
      { params: status ? { status } : undefined },
    );
  },

  /**
   * Updates application status (accept, reject, waitlist).
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  decideApplication(
    applicationId: number | string,
    action: 'accept' | 'reject' | 'waitlist',
    data: { post_id?: number | null; note?: string } = {},
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<{ id: number; status: string }>> {
    return client.post<{ id: number; status: string }>(
      `coordinator/applications/${applicationId}/${action}/`,
      data,
    );
  },

  /**
   * Runs auto-assignment on an event.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  runAutoAssignment(
    eventId: number | string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<{ assigned?: number; message?: string }>> {
    return client.post<{ assigned?: number; message?: string }>(
      `coordinator/events/${eventId}/auto-assign/`,
      {},
    );
  },

  /**
   * Manually assigns a volunteer application to an event post.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  manualAssign(
    eventId: number | string,
    data: {
      application_id: number;
      post_id?: number | null;
      group_code?: string | null;
      is_group_leader?: boolean;
    },
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<CoordinatorAssignmentDto>> {
    return client.post<CoordinatorAssignmentDto>(
      `coordinator/events/${eventId}/manual-assign/`,
      data,
    );
  },

  /**
   * Fetches assignments for an event.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  getAssignments(
    eventId: number | string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<ResultsListDto<CoordinatorAssignmentDto>>> {
    return client.get<ResultsListDto<CoordinatorAssignmentDto>>(
      `coordinator/events/${eventId}/assignments/`,
    );
  },

  /**
   * Fetches unassigned accepted applications for an event.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  getUnassignedApplications(
    eventId: number | string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<ResultsListDto<CoordinatorApplicationDto>>> {
    return client.get<ResultsListDto<CoordinatorApplicationDto>>(
      `coordinator/events/${eventId}/unassigned/`,
    );
  },

  /**
   * Fetches full attendance roster for an event.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  getAttendanceRoster(
    eventId: number | string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<ResultsListDto<AttendanceRosterDto>>> {
    return client.get<ResultsListDto<AttendanceRosterDto>>(
      `coordinator/events/${eventId}/attendance/`,
    );
  },

  /**
   * Fetches pending geo check-in attempts for an event.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  getPendingGeoConfirmations(
    eventId: number | string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<ResultsListDto<AttendanceRosterDto>>> {
    return client.get<ResultsListDto<AttendanceRosterDto>>(
      `coordinator/events/${eventId}/attendance/pending/`,
    );
  },

  /**
   * Confirms a pending geo check-in attempt.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  confirmAttendance(
    attendanceId: number | string,
    note: string = '',
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<{ id: number; confirmation_status: string }>> {
    return client.post<{ id: number; confirmation_status: string }>(
      `coordinator/attendance/${attendanceId}/confirm/`,
      { note },
    );
  },

  /**
   * Rejects a pending geo check-in attempt.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  rejectAttendance(
    attendanceId: number | string,
    reason: string = 'Rejected by coordinator',
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<{ id: number; confirmation_status: string }>> {
    return client.post<{ id: number; confirmation_status: string }>(
      `coordinator/attendance/${attendanceId}/reject/`,
      { reason },
    );
  },

  /**
   * Checks in a volunteer manually on-site.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  manualCheckIn(
    data: {
      event_id: number;
      user_id: number;
      note?: string;
    },
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<{ id: number; status: string; method: string }>> {
    return client.post<{ id: number; status: string; method: string }>(
      'attendance/manual-checkin/',
      data,
    );
  },
};

/**

import { ApiClient, defaultApiClient } from '../../core/api/apiClient';
import type { ApiResult } from '../../core/api/apiResult';
import type { PaginatedResponseDto, ResultsListDto } from '../dto/commonDto';
import type {
  ApplicationSubmitRequestDto,
  CharityFieldDto,
  PublicEventDetailDto,
  PublicEventDto,
  PublicEventPostDto,
  VolunteerApplicationDto,
  VolunteerAssignmentDto,
} from '../dto/eventDto';

export interface EventQueryParams {
  search?: string;
  charity_field?: string;
  starts_after?: string;
  starts_before?: string;
  upcoming?: boolean | string;
  status?: string;
  page?: number;
  [key: string]: string | number | boolean | undefined | null | Array<string | number>;
}

export const eventApi = {
  /**
   * Fetches active charity fields taxonomy.
   * Public endpoint.
   */
  getCharityFields(
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<CharityFieldDto[] | ResultsListDto<CharityFieldDto>>> {
    return client.get<CharityFieldDto[] | ResultsListDto<CharityFieldDto>>(
      'charity-fields/',
      { authRequired: false },
    );
  },

  /**
   * Fetches public events matching search and filter criteria.
   * Public endpoint.
   */
  getEvents(
    params?: EventQueryParams,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<PaginatedResponseDto<PublicEventDto> | PublicEventDto[]>> {
    return client.get<PaginatedResponseDto<PublicEventDto> | PublicEventDto[]>(
      'events/',
      { params, authRequired: false },
    );
  },

  /**
   * Fetches full public event detail with posts and conditional fields.
   * Public/mixed endpoint.
   */
  getEventDetail(
    id: number | string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<PublicEventDetailDto>> {
    return client.get<PublicEventDetailDto>(`events/${id}/`, {
      authRequired: false,
    });
  },

  /**
   * Fetches open posts and capacity for an event.
   * Public endpoint.
   */
  getEventPosts(
    id: number | string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<PublicEventPostDto[] | ResultsListDto<PublicEventPostDto>>> {
    return client.get<PublicEventPostDto[] | ResultsListDto<PublicEventPostDto>>(
      `events/${id}/posts/`,
      { authRequired: false },
    );
  },

  /**
   * Submits a volunteer application for an event post.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  applyToEvent(
    id: number | string,
    data: ApplicationSubmitRequestDto,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<VolunteerApplicationDto>> {
    return client.post<VolunteerApplicationDto>(`events/${id}/apply/`, data);
  },

  /**
   * Fetches the current user's submitted applications.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  getMyApplications(
    params?: { event_id?: number | string },
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<
    ApiResult<
      | PaginatedResponseDto<VolunteerApplicationDto>
      | ResultsListDto<VolunteerApplicationDto>
      | VolunteerApplicationDto[]
    >
  > {
    return client.get<
      | PaginatedResponseDto<VolunteerApplicationDto>
      | ResultsListDto<VolunteerApplicationDto>
      | VolunteerApplicationDto[]
    >('me/applications/', { params });
  },

  /**
   * Withdraws a pending or waitlisted volunteer application.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  withdrawApplication(
    id: number | string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<{ id: number; status: string }>> {
    return client.post<{ id: number; status: string }>(
      `applications/${id}/withdraw/`,
      {},
    );
  },

  /**
   * Fetches the current user's assignments.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  getMyAssignments(
    params?: { event_id?: number | string },
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<
    ApiResult<
      | PaginatedResponseDto<VolunteerAssignmentDto>
      | ResultsListDto<VolunteerAssignmentDto>
      | VolunteerAssignmentDto[]
    >
  > {
    return client.get<
      | PaginatedResponseDto<VolunteerAssignmentDto>
      | ResultsListDto<VolunteerAssignmentDto>
      | VolunteerAssignmentDto[]
    >('me/assignments/', { params });
  },

  /**
   * Confirms a scheduled shift assignment.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  confirmAssignment(
    id: number | string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<{ id: number; status: string }>> {
    return client.post<{ id: number; status: string }>(
      `assignments/${id}/confirm/`,
      {},
    );
  },

  /**
   * Cancels a scheduled shift assignment.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  cancelAssignment(
    id: number | string,
    reason: string = '',
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<{ id: number; status: string }>> {
    return client.post<{ id: number; status: string }>(
      `assignments/${id}/cancel/`,
      { reason },
    );
  },
};

/**
