import { ApiClient, defaultApiClient } from '../../core/api/apiClient';
import type { ApiResult } from '../../core/api/apiResult';
import type { ResultsListDto } from '../dto/commonDto';
import type {
  AttendanceHistoryItemDto,
  AvailabilityExceptionDto,
  AvailabilityResponseDto,
  AvailabilityRuleDto,
  AvailabilityRulesReplaceDto,
  CharityInterestsDto,
  CoordinatorProfileEnvelopeDto,
  FeedbackSummaryResponseDto,
  ProfileUpdateRequestDto,
  UserProfileDto,
  VerificationStatusResponseDto,
} from '../dto/profileDto';

export const profileApi = {
  /**
   * Fetches current user's profile.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  getProfile(
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<UserProfileDto>> {
    return client.get<UserProfileDto>('me/');
  },

  /**
   * Updates allow-listed profile fields. Unknown keys trigger HTTP 400.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  updateProfile(
    data: ProfileUpdateRequestDto,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<UserProfileDto>> {
    return client.patch<UserProfileDto>('me/', data);
  },

  /**
   * Fetches user's selected charity interests.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  getCharityInterests(
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<CharityInterestsDto>> {
    return client.get<CharityInterestsDto>('me/charity-interests/');
  },

  /**
   * Replaces user's charity interests.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  setCharityInterests(
    fieldCodes: string[],
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<CharityInterestsDto>> {
    return client.put<CharityInterestsDto>('me/charity-interests/', {
      field_codes: fieldCodes,
    });
  },

  /**
   * Fetches user's weekly availability rules and exceptions.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  getAvailability(
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<AvailabilityResponseDto>> {
    return client.get<AvailabilityResponseDto>('me/availability/');
  },

  /**
   * Replaces all weekly availability rules.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  replaceAvailabilityRules(
    data: AvailabilityRulesReplaceDto,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<{ rules: AvailabilityRuleDto[] }>> {
    return client.put<{ rules: AvailabilityRuleDto[] }>('me/availability/', data);
  },

  /**
   * Upserts a date-specific availability exception.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  upsertAvailabilityException(
    data: AvailabilityExceptionDto,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<AvailabilityExceptionDto>> {
    return client.post<AvailabilityExceptionDto>(
      'me/availability/exceptions/',
      data,
    );
  },

  /**
   * Deletes an availability exception by ID.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  deleteAvailabilityException(
    exceptionId: number | string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<{ status?: string }>> {
    return client.delete<{ status?: string }>(
      `me/availability/exceptions/${exceptionId}/`,
    );
  },

  /**
   * Fetches identity verification status and uploaded image records.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  getVerificationStatus(
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<VerificationStatusResponseDto>> {
    return client.get<VerificationStatusResponseDto>('me/verification-images/');
  },

  /**
   * Fetches coordinator application status and record.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  getCoordinatorProfile(
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<CoordinatorProfileEnvelopeDto>> {
    return client.get<CoordinatorProfileEnvelopeDto>('me/coordinator/');
  },

  /**
   * Submits an application for coordinator role.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  applyForCoordinator(
    notes: string,
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<CoordinatorProfileEnvelopeDto>> {
    return client.post<CoordinatorProfileEnvelopeDto>('me/coordinator/', {
      notes,
    });
  },

  /**
   * Fetches user's attendance history records.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  getAttendanceHistory(
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<ResultsListDto<AttendanceHistoryItemDto>>> {
    return client.get<ResultsListDto<AttendanceHistoryItemDto>>('me/attendance/');
  },

  /**
   * Fetches feedback summary and ratings.
   * Authenticated endpoint.
   */
  getFeedbackSummary(
    client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient,
  ): Promise<ApiResult<FeedbackSummaryResponseDto>> {
    return client.get<FeedbackSummaryResponseDto>('me/feedback/');
  },
};

/**

import React from 'react';
import ReactTestRenderer, { act } from 'react-test-renderer';
import { SafeAreaProvider } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { ApiClient } from '../../../core/api/apiClient';
import { MemoryTokenStore } from '../../../core/auth/tokenStore';
import { SessionManager } from '../../../core/auth/sessionManager';
import { checkinApi } from '../../endpoints/checkinApi';
import { coordinatorApi } from '../../endpoints/coordinatorApi';
import { HttpEventRepository } from '../HttpEventRepository';
import { HttpCoordinatorRepository } from '../HttpCoordinatorRepository';
import { MockEventRepository } from '../../mock/MockEventRepository';
import { ThemeProvider } from '../../../theme/ThemeProvider';
import { MyAssignmentsScreen } from '../../../features/events/screens/MyAssignmentsScreen';
import type {
  AttendanceRosterDto,
  CoordinatorEventListItemDto,
} from '../../dto/coordinatorDto';
import type {
  CheckoutResponseDto,
  FeedbackSubmitResponseDto,
  GeoCheckinResponseDto,
  OnboardingAcknowledgeResponseDto,
  OnboardingStatusDto,
  QrRedeemResponseDto,
  QrResolveResponseDto,
  QrTokenDto,
} from '../../dto/checkinDto';

const initialMetrics = {
  frame: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 390, height: 844 },
  insets: { top: 47, left: 0, right: 0, bottom: 34 },
};

function createMockNavigation() {
  return {
    navigate: jest.fn(),
    openDrawer: jest.fn(),
    closeDrawer: jest.fn(),
    toggleDrawer: jest.fn(),
    dispatch: jest.fn(),
    reset: jest.fn(),
    goBack: jest.fn(),
    isFocused: jest.fn(() => true),
    canGoBack: jest.fn(() => true),
    getParent: jest.fn(),
    getState: jest.fn(),
    addListener: jest.fn(() => () => {}),
    removeListener: jest.fn(),
    setParams: jest.fn(),
  };

describe('Phase 8: Onboarding, QR, Attendance & Feedback Integration', () => {
  let mockClient: ApiClient;
  let eventRepo: HttpEventRepository;
  let coordinatorRepo: HttpCoordinatorRepository;

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
    const tokenStore = new MemoryTokenStore();
    tokenStore.setToken('valid-phase8-token');
    const sessionManager = new SessionManager(tokenStore);
    mockClient = new ApiClient(
      async () => new Response(JSON.stringify({})),
      tokenStore,
      sessionManager,
    );
    eventRepo = new HttpEventRepository(mockClient);
    coordinatorRepo = new HttpCoordinatorRepository(mockClient);
    coordinatorRepo.setCoordinatorIdentity({
      id: 'usr-coord-99',
      displayName: 'Alice Coordinator',
      email: 'alice@example.com',
    });
  });

  describe('1. Volunteer Onboarding Briefing & Acknowledgment', () => {
    it('fetches event onboarding briefing status', async () => {
      const mockStatus: OnboardingStatusDto = {
        event_id: 101,
        onboarding_text: 'Please review safety protocols before arrival.',
        onboarding_version: 2,
        require_onboarding_ack: true,
        acknowledged: false,
      };

      jest
        .spyOn(checkinApi, 'getOnboarding')
        .mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, status: 200, data: mockStatus });

      const result = await eventRepo.getEventOnboarding!('101');

      expect(result.eventId).toBe('101');
      expect(result.onboardingText).toBe('Please review safety protocols before arrival.');
      expect(result.onboardingVersion).toBe(2);
      expect(result.requireOnboardingAck).toBe(true);
      expect(result.acknowledged).toBe(false);
    });

    it('acknowledges event onboarding briefing', async () => {
      const mockAck: OnboardingAcknowledgeResponseDto = {
        event_id: 101,
        onboarding_version: 2,
        acknowledged: true,
      };

      const ackSpy = jest
        .spyOn(checkinApi, 'acknowledgeOnboarding')
        .mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, status: 200, data: mockAck });

      const result = await eventRepo.acknowledgeOnboarding!('101', 'Reviewed gear checklist');

      expect(result.success).toBe(true);
      expect(result.acknowledged).toBe(true);
      expect(ackSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        '101',
        { notes: 'Reviewed gear checklist' },
        mockClient,
      );
    });
  });

  describe('2. Volunteer Geo Check-In Telemetry & Error Normalization', () => {
    it('handles successful geo check-in within geofence', async () => {
      const mockResponse: GeoCheckinResponseDto = {
        result: 'accepted',
        attendance_id: 801,
        distance_m: 25.5,
        confirmation_status: 'confirmed',
        message: 'Checked in successfully within event geofence.',
        success: true,
      };

      const checkInSpy = jest
        .spyOn(checkinApi, 'submitGeoCheckIn')
        .mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, status: 200, data: mockResponse });

      const result = await eventRepo.submitGeoCheckIn!('101', 16.805, 96.155, 10);

      expect(result.success).toBe(true);
      expect(result.result).toBe('accepted');
      expect(result.distanceM).toBeCloseTo(25.5);
      expect(result.attendanceId).toBe('801');
      expect(checkInSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        {
          event_id: 101,
          latitude: 16.805,
          longitude: 96.155,
          accuracy_m: 10,
        },
        mockClient,
      );
    });

    it('preserves distance_m and error message on outside_geofence HTTP 400 without crashing', async () => {
      jest.spyOn(checkinApi, 'submitGeoCheckIn').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: false,
        status: 400,
        error: {
          kind: 'validation',
          code: 'outside_geofence',
          message: 'Outside allowable check-in radius (350m away)',
          meta: {
            distance_m: 350.2,
            success: false,
          },
        },
      });

      const result = await eventRepo.submitGeoCheckIn!('101', 16.9, 96.2, 15);

      expect(result.success).toBe(false);
      expect(result.result).toBe('outside_geofence');
      expect(result.distanceM).toBeCloseTo(350.2);
      expect(result.message).toContain('Outside allowable check-in radius');
    });
  });

  describe('3. QR Resolve, Redeem & Check-In', () => {
    it('resolves QR token validity without consuming', async () => {
      const mockResolve: QrResolveResponseDto = {
        event_id: 101,
        purpose: 'checkin',
        is_valid: true,
        reason: null,
      };

      const resolveSpy = jest
        .spyOn(checkinApi, 'resolveQrToken')
        .mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, status: 200, data: mockResolve });

      const res = await checkinApi.resolveQrToken('qr-token-abc', mockClient);

      expect(res.ok).toBe(true);
      if (res.ok) {
        expect(res.data.is_valid).toBe(true);
        expect(res.data.purpose).toBe('checkin');
      }
      expect(resolveSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('qr-token-abc', mockClient);
    });

    it('redeems QR token for volunteer shift check-in', async () => {
      const mockRedeem: QrRedeemResponseDto = {
        result: 'checked_in',
        purpose: 'checkin',
        event_id: 101,
        attendance_id: 802,
      };

      const redeemSpy = jest
        .spyOn(checkinApi, 'redeemQrToken')
        .mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, status: 200, data: mockRedeem });

      const result = await eventRepo.redeemQrToken!('qr-token-checkin-123');

      expect(result.success).toBe(true);
      expect(result.result).toBe('checked_in');
      expect(result.attendanceId).toBe('802');
      expect(redeemSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('qr-token-checkin-123', mockClient);
    });

    it('handles QR check-in endpoint attendance/qr-checkin/', async () => {
      const qrCheckInSpy = jest
        .spyOn(checkinApi, 'qrCheckIn')
        .mockResolvedValue({
          ok: true,
          status: 200,
          data: { result: 'checked_in', event_id: 101, attendance_id: 803 },
        });

      const res = await checkinApi.qrCheckIn('qr-token-only', mockClient);
      expect(res.ok).toBe(true);
      expect(qrCheckInSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('qr-token-only', mockClient);
    });
  });

  describe('4. Attendance Shift Checkout (Constraint 1: attendance/{id}/checkout/)', () => {
    it('calls checkout endpoint on attendance ID and returns checkout_at', async () => {
      const mockCheckout: CheckoutResponseDto = {
        id: 801,
        status: 'checked_out',
        checkout_at: '2026-12-01T13:00:00Z',
      };

      const checkoutSpy = jest
        .spyOn(checkinApi, 'checkoutAttendance')
        .mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, status: 200, data: mockCheckout });

      const result = await eventRepo.checkOutAttendance!('801');

      expect(result.success).toBe(true);
      expect(result.checkedOutAt).toBe('2026-12-01T13:00:00Z');
      expect(checkoutSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('801', mockClient);
    });
  });

  describe('5. Coordinator QR Token Lifecycle', () => {
    it('creates coordinator QR token for walk-in registration', async () => {
      const mockToken: QrTokenDto = {
        id: 55,
        raw_token: 'qr-raw-walkin-token-xyz',
        purpose: 'walkin_registration',
        max_uses: 50,
        expires_at: '2026-12-01T18:00:00Z',
      };

      const createSpy = jest
        .spyOn(checkinApi, 'createCoordinatorQrToken')
        .mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, status: 201, data: mockToken });

      const result = await coordinatorRepo.createQrToken!(
        '101',
        'walkin_registration',
        50,
        240,
      );

      expect(result.success).toBe(true);
      expect(result.data?.id).toBe(55);
      expect(result.data?.rawToken).toBe('qr-raw-walkin-token-xyz');
      expect(createSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        '101',
        {
          purpose: 'walkin_registration',
          max_uses: 50,
          ttl_minutes: 240,
        },
        mockClient,
      );
    });

    it('revokes coordinator QR token', async () => {
      const revokeSpy = jest
        .spyOn(checkinApi, 'revokeCoordinatorQrToken')
        .mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, status: 200, data: { id: 55, revoked_at: '2026-12-01T12:00:00Z' } });

      const result = await coordinatorRepo.revokeQrToken!('55');

      expect(result.success).toBe(true);
      expect(revokeSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('55', mockClient);
    });
  });

  describe('6. Coordinator Attendance Roster, Geo Approval & Manual Check-In (Constraint 2)', () => {
    it('loads roster and pending geo confirmations into cache', async () => {
      const mockRoster: AttendanceRosterDto[] = [
        {
          assignment_id: 301,
          event_id: 101,
          user_id: 201,
          display_name: 'Volunteer Sarah',
          post_id: 401,
          post_title: 'Shoreline Cleaner',
          group_code: 'GRP-A',
          is_group_leader: false,
          assignment_status: 'confirmed',
          attendance_id: 801,
          attendance_status: 'scheduled',
          method: null,
          walk_in_flag: false,
          checkin_at: null,
          checkin_distance_m: 45,
          confirmation_status: 'pending',
        },
      ];

      jest
        .spyOn(coordinatorApi, 'getAttendanceRoster')
        .mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, status: 200, data: { results: mockRoster } });

      const roster = await coordinatorRepo.getAttendanceRoster('101');
      expect(roster).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(roster[0].isPendingGeoConfirmation).toBe(true);
    });

    it('confirms and rejects geo attendance', async () => {
      const confSpy = jest
        .spyOn(coordinatorApi, 'confirmAttendance')
        .mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, status: 200, data: { id: 801, confirmation_status: 'confirmed' } });
      const rejSpy = jest
        .spyOn(coordinatorApi, 'rejectAttendance')
        .mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, status: 200, data: { id: 801, confirmation_status: 'rejected' } });

      const confRes = await coordinatorRepo.confirmGeoAttendance('801');
      expect(confRes.success).toBe(true);
      expect(confSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('801', 'Confirmed by coordinator', mockClient);

      const rejRes = await coordinatorRepo.rejectGeoAttendance('801');
      expect(rejRes.success).toBe(true);
      expect(rejSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('801', 'Rejected by coordinator', mockClient);
    });

    it('manualCheckIn extracts event_id and user_id from cached roster item (Constraint 2)', async () => {
      const mockRoster: AttendanceRosterDto[] = [
        {
          assignment_id: 302,
          event_id: 101,
          user_id: 202,
          display_name: 'Volunteer Dave',
          post_id: 402,
          post_title: 'Logistics Sorter',
          group_code: 'GRP-B',
          is_group_leader: false,
          assignment_status: 'confirmed',
          attendance_id: null, // Scheduled, no attendance ID yet
          attendance_status: null,
          method: null,
          walk_in_flag: false,
          checkin_at: null,
          checkin_distance_m: null,
          confirmation_status: null,
        },
      ];

      jest
        .spyOn(coordinatorApi, 'getAttendanceRoster')
        .mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, status: 200, data: { results: mockRoster } });

      const manualSpy = jest
        .spyOn(coordinatorApi, 'manualCheckIn')
        .mockResolvedValue({
          ok: true,
          status: 200,
          data: { id: 805, status: 'checked_in', method: 'manual' },
        });

      // Load roster to populate attendanceCache
      await coordinatorRepo.getAttendanceRoster('101');

      // Check-in by assignment ID
      const result = await coordinatorRepo.manualCheckIn('302');

      expect(result.success).toBe(true);
      expect(manualSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        {
          event_id: 101,
          user_id: 202,
          note: 'Manual on-site check-in',
        },
        mockClient,
      );
    });
  });

  describe('7. Event Feedback Submission', () => {
    it('submits volunteer event feedback with rating score and tags', async () => {
      const mockFeedback: FeedbackSubmitResponseDto = {
        id: 991,
        status: 'published',
      };

      const feedbackSpy = jest
        .spyOn(checkinApi, 'submitEventFeedback')
        .mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, status: 201, data: mockFeedback });

      const result = await eventRepo.submitEventFeedback!(
        '101',
        5,
        'Excellent coordination and great teamwork.',
        ['teamwork', 'safety'],
        'volunteer_to_event',
      );

      expect(result.success).toBe(true);
      expect(result.feedbackId).toBe('991');
      expect(feedbackSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        '101',
        {
          direction: 'volunteer_to_event',
          score: 5,
          comment: 'Excellent coordination and great teamwork.',
          tags: ['teamwork', 'safety'],
        },
        mockClient,
      );
    });
  });

  describe('8. UI Screen Component Integration (MyAssignmentsScreen)', () => {
    it('renders assignments, allows viewing onboarding, check-in, checkout, and feedback', async () => {
      const mockRepo = new MockEventRepository();
      const onboardingSpy = jest.spyOn(mockRepo, 'getEventOnboarding');
      const checkinSpy = jest.spyOn(mockRepo, 'submitGeoCheckIn');
      const checkoutSpy = jest.spyOn(mockRepo, 'checkOutAttendance');
      const feedbackSpy = jest.spyOn(mockRepo, 'submitEventFeedback');

      const mockNav = createMockNavigation();

      let renderer: ReactTestRenderer.ReactTestRenderer;
      await act(async () => {
        renderer = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          <SafeAreaProvider initialMetrics={initialMetrics}>
            <ThemeProvider>
              <MyAssignmentsScreen
                navigation={mockNav as any}
                repository={mockRepo}
              />
            </ThemeProvider>
          </SafeAreaProvider>,
        );
      });

      // Verify onboarding trigger
      const assignments = await mockRepo.getUserAssignments();
      expect(assignments.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);

      const firstAssignment = assignments[0];
      await act(async () => {
        await mockRepo.getEventOnboarding(firstAssignment.eventId);
      });
      expect(onboardingSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(firstAssignment.eventId);

      // Verify geo check-in trigger
      await act(async () => {
        await mockRepo.submitGeoCheckIn(firstAssignment.eventId, 16.805, 96.155, 15);
      });
      expect(checkinSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(firstAssignment.eventId, 16.805, 96.155, 15);

      // Verify checkout trigger
      await act(async () => {
        await mockRepo.checkOutAttendance(firstAssignment.id);
      });
      expect(checkoutSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(firstAssignment.id);

      // Verify feedback trigger
      await act(async () => {
        await mockRepo.submitEventFeedback(firstAssignment.eventId, 5, 'Great mission');
      });
      expect(feedbackSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(firstAssignment.eventId, 5, 'Great mission');

      await act(async () => {
        renderer!.unmount();
      });
    });
  });
});

  describe('3. Event Lifecycle Transitions & Owner-as-Monitor (Handover/Retake)', () => {
    it('transitions event lifecycle through published, active, completed, and cancelled', async () => {
      const publishSpy = jest.spyOn(coordinatorApi, 'publishEvent').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,

        ok: true,

      });
      const completeSpy = jest.spyOn(coordinatorApi, 'completeEvent').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: { id: 1, status: 'completed', completed_at: '2026-12-01T12:00:00Z' },
      });
      const cancelSpy = jest.spyOn(coordinatorApi, 'cancelEvent').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: { id: 1, status: 'cancelled', cancelled_at: '2026-12-01T12:00:00Z' },
      });

      jest.spyOn(coordinatorRepo, 'getEventDetail').mockResolvedValue({
        id: '1',
        title: 'Event',
        description: '',
        status: 'published',
        purposeType: 'charity_event',
        startAt: '2026-12-01T08:00:00Z',
        endAt: '2026-12-01T12:00:00Z',
        locationName: 'Location',
        charityFields: [],
        ownerId: '99',
        currentCoordinatorId: '99',
        coordinatorName: 'Alice',
        isOwnerAsMonitor: false,
        pendingApplicationCount: 0,
        acceptedApplicantCount: 0,
        assignedCount: 0,
        checkedInCount: 0,
        latitude: 0,
        longitude: 0,
        geoRadiusM: 200,
        geoCheckinEnabled: true,
        geoCheckinOpensBeforeMin: 30,
        requireCheckinConfirmation: false,
        mapProvider: 'openstreetmap',
        acceptanceMode: 'manual',
        autoAssignEnabled: false,
        leaderStrategy: 'none',
        mealsProvided: false,
        lodgingProvided: false,
        travelSupportProvided: false,
        compensationKind: 'unpaid',
        onboardingVersion: 1,
        requireOnboardingAck: false,
        qrRegistrationEnabled: false,
        posts: [],
      });

      const resPub = await coordinatorRepo.transitionEventStatus('1', 'published');
      expect(resPub.success).toBe(true);
      expect(publishSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('1', mockClient);

      const resAct = await coordinatorRepo.transitionEventStatus('1', 'active');
      expect(resAct.success).toBe(true);
      expect(activateSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('1', mockClient);

      const resComp = await coordinatorRepo.transitionEventStatus('1', 'completed');
      expect(resComp.success).toBe(true);
      expect(completeSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('1', mockClient);

      const resCanc = await coordinatorRepo.transitionEventStatus('1', 'cancelled');
      expect(resCanc.success).toBe(true);
      expect(cancelSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('1', 'Cancelled by coordinator', mockClient);
    });

    it('hands over and retakes coordinator ownership', async () => {
      const handoverSpy = jest
        .spyOn(coordinatorApi, 'handoverEvent')
        .mockResolvedValue({
          ok: true,
          status: 200,
          data: { id: 101, current_coordinator_id: 888 },
        });

      const retakeSpy = jest.spyOn(coordinatorApi, 'retakeEvent').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: { id: 101, current_coordinator_id: 99 },
      });

      const handoverRes = await coordinatorRepo.handoverEvent!(
        '101',
        '888',
        'Passing to Bob',
      );
      expect(handoverRes.success).toBe(true);
      expect(handoverSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('101', 888, 'Passing to Bob', mockClient);

      const retakeRes = await coordinatorRepo.retakeEvent!('101', 'Bob unavailable');
      expect(retakeRes.success).toBe(true);
      expect(retakeSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('101', 'Bob unavailable', mockClient);
    });

    it('enforces owner-as-monitor guard in MockCoordinatorRepository', async () => {
      const mockRepo = new MockCoordinatorRepository();
      // Hand over event 'evt-coord-001' to new coordinator 'usr-bob'
      const hRes = await mockRepo.handoverEvent!('evt-coord-001', 'usr-bob', 'Handing over');
      expect(hRes.success).toBe(true);

      const detail = await mockRepo.getEventDetail('evt-coord-001');
      expect(detail?.isOwnerAsMonitor).toBe(true);
      expect(detail?.currentCoordinatorId).toBe('usr-bob');

      // Attempting to transition status should fail in monitor mode
      const transRes = await mockRepo.transitionEventStatus('evt-coord-001', 'active');
      expect(transRes.success).toBe(false);
      expect(transRes.message).toContain('Action disabled in read-only monitor mode');

      // Retake coordination
      const retRes = await mockRepo.retakeEvent!('evt-coord-001', 'Retaking');
      expect(retRes.success).toBe(true);

      const retDetail = await mockRepo.getEventDetail('evt-coord-001');
      expect(retDetail?.isOwnerAsMonitor).toBe(false);
    });
  });

  describe('3. Coordinator Applications Review & T2 Capabilities', () => {
    it('fetches applications and preserves volunteer T2 capabilities', async () => {
      const mockApps: CoordinatorApplicationDto[] = [
        {
          id: 501,
          post_id: 201,
          post_title: 'Shoreline Cleaner',
          user_id: 301,
          display_name: 'Volunteer Sarah',
          source: 'browse',
          status: 'accepted',
          applied_at: '2026-11-20T10:00:00Z',
          decided_at: '2026-11-21T11:00:00Z',
          decided_by_id: 99,
          skills: ['first_aid', 'heavy_lifting'],
          physical_ok: true,
          can_lift_heavy: true,
          has_leader_experience: true,
          new_to_volunteer: false,
        },
      ];

      jest
        .spyOn(coordinatorApi, 'getApplications')
        .mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, status: 200, data: { results: mockApps } });

      const apps = await coordinatorRepo.getApplications('101');

      expect(apps).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(apps[0].id).toBe('501');
      expect(apps[0].applicantName).toBe('Volunteer Sarah');
      expect(apps[0].t2Capabilities).toBeDefined();
      expect(apps[0].t2Capabilities?.skills).toEqual(['first_aid', 'heavy_lifting']);
      expect(apps[0].t2Capabilities?.canLiftHeavy).toBe(true);
      expect(apps[0].t2Capabilities?.hasLeaderExperience).toBe(true);
    });

    it('decides applications via accept, reject, and waitlist', async () => {
      const decideSpy = jest
        .spyOn(coordinatorApi, 'decideApplication')
        .mockResolvedValue({
          ok: true,
          status: 200,
          data: { id: 501, status: 'accepted' },
        });

      const acceptRes = await coordinatorRepo.updateApplicationStatus('501', 'accepted');
      expect(acceptRes.success).toBe(true);
      expect(decideSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('501', 'accept', {}, mockClient);

      const rejectRes = await coordinatorRepo.updateApplicationStatus('501', 'rejected');
      expect(rejectRes.success).toBe(true);
      expect(decideSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('501', 'reject', {}, mockClient);

      const waitlistRes = await coordinatorRepo.updateApplicationStatus('501', 'waitlisted');
      expect(waitlistRes.success).toBe(true);
      expect(decideSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('501', 'waitlist', {}, mockClient);
    });
  });

  describe('4. Coordinator Assignments (Auto & Manual Assign)', () => {
    it('executes auto-assignment and returns assignments list', async () => {
      const mockAsgDto: CoordinatorAssignmentDto = {
        id: 701,
        post_id: 201,
        post_title: 'Shoreline Cleaner',
        user_id: 301,
        display_name: 'Volunteer Sarah',
        group_code: 'GRP-A',
        is_group_leader: true,
        status: 'confirmed',
        assigned_by: 'coordinator',
        schedule_start: '2026-12-01T08:00:00Z',
        schedule_end: '2026-12-01T12:00:00Z',
        confirmed_at: '2026-11-22T09:00:00Z',
      };

      const autoSpy = jest
        .spyOn(coordinatorApi, 'runAutoAssignment')
        .mockResolvedValue({
          ok: true,
          status: 200,
          data: { assigned: 1, message: 'Auto assignment complete' },
        });

      jest.spyOn(coordinatorApi, 'getAssignments').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: { results: [mockAsgDto] },
      });

      const result = await coordinatorRepo.runAutoAssignment('101');

      expect(result.success).toBe(true);
      expect(autoSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('101', mockClient);
      expect(result.data).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(result.data![0].groupCode).toBe('GRP-A');
      expect(result.data![0].isGroupLeader).toBe(true);
    });

    it('assigns volunteer manually passing numeric post_id or null fallback', async () => {
      const mockAsgDto: CoordinatorAssignmentDto = {
        id: 702,
        post_id: 201,
        post_title: 'Shoreline Cleaner',
        user_id: 302,
        display_name: 'Volunteer Dave',
        group_code: 'GRP-B',
        is_group_leader: false,
        status: 'assigned',
        assigned_by: 'coordinator',
        schedule_start: '2026-12-01T08:00:00Z',
        schedule_end: '2026-12-01T12:00:00Z',
        confirmed_at: null,
      };

      const manualSpy = jest
        .spyOn(coordinatorApi, 'manualAssign')
        .mockResolvedValue({
          ok: true,
          status: 201,
          data: mockAsgDto,
        });

      jest.spyOn(coordinatorRepo, 'getMyEvents').mockResolvedValue([
        {
          id: '101',
          title: 'River Sweep',
          description: '',
          status: 'active',
          purposeType: 'charity_event',
          startAt: '2026-12-01T08:00:00Z',
          endAt: '2026-12-01T12:00:00Z',
          locationName: 'Location',
          charityFields: [],
          ownerId: '99',
          currentCoordinatorId: '99',
          coordinatorName: 'Alice',
          isOwnerAsMonitor: false,
          pendingApplicationCount: 0,
          acceptedApplicantCount: 0,
          assignedCount: 0,
          checkedInCount: 0,
        },
      ]);

      // 1. With numeric postId
      const resWithPost = await coordinatorRepo.assignVolunteerManually(
        '502',
        '201',
        'GRP-B',
        false,
      );
      expect(resWithPost.success).toBe(true);
      expect(manualSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        '101',
        {
          application_id: 502,
          post_id: 201,
          group_code: 'GRP-B',
          is_group_leader: false,
        },
        mockClient,
      );

      // 2. With empty / non-numeric postId -> should send post_id: null
      const resWithoutPost = await coordinatorRepo.assignVolunteerManually(
        '502',
        '',
        'GRP-B',
        false,
      );
      expect(resWithoutPost.success).toBe(true);
      expect(manualSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        '101',
        {
          application_id: 502,
          post_id: null,
          group_code: 'GRP-B',
          is_group_leader: false,
        },
        mockClient,
      );
    });

    it('cancels assignment via eventApi', async () => {
      const cancelSpy = jest
        .spyOn(eventApi, 'cancelAssignment')
        .mockResolvedValue({
          ok: true,
          status: 200,
          data: {
            id: 701,
            status: 'cancelled',
          },
        });

      const res = await coordinatorRepo.cancelAssignment('701');
      expect(res.success).toBe(true);
      expect(cancelSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('701', 'Cancelled by coordinator', mockClient);
    });
  });

  describe('5. Attendance & Geo-Confirmation Queue', () => {
    it('manages attendance roster, geo confirmation, and manual check-in', async () => {
      const mockRosterDto: AttendanceRosterDto = {
        assignment_id: 701,
        event_id: 101,
        user_id: 301,
        display_name: 'Volunteer Sarah',
        post_id: 201,
        post_title: 'Shoreline Cleaner',
        group_code: 'GRP-A',
        is_group_leader: false,
        assignment_status: 'confirmed',
        attendance_id: 801,
        attendance_status: 'scheduled',
        method: null,
        walk_in_flag: false,
        checkin_at: null,
        checkin_distance_m: 55,
        confirmation_status: 'pending',
      };

      jest
        .spyOn(coordinatorApi, 'getAttendanceRoster')
        .mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, status: 200, data: { results: [mockRosterDto] } });
      const confirmSpy = jest
        .spyOn(coordinatorApi, 'confirmAttendance')
        .mockResolvedValue({
          ok: true,
          status: 200,
          data: { id: 801, confirmation_status: 'confirmed' },
        });
      const checkinSpy = jest
        .spyOn(coordinatorApi, 'manualCheckIn')
        .mockResolvedValue({
          ok: true,
          status: 200,
          data: {
            id: 801,
            status: 'checked_in',
            method: 'manual',
          },
        });

      // 1. Load roster
      const roster = await coordinatorRepo.getAttendanceRoster('101');
      expect(roster).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(roster[0].isPendingGeoConfirmation).toBe(true);

      // 2. Confirm geo check-in
      const confRes = await coordinatorRepo.confirmGeoAttendance('801');
      expect(confRes.success).toBe(true);
      expect(confirmSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('801', 'Confirmed by coordinator', mockClient);

      // 3. Manual check-in
      const manualRes = await coordinatorRepo.manualCheckIn('801');
      expect(manualRes.success).toBe(true);
      expect(checkinSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        { event_id: 101, user_id: 301, note: 'Manual on-site check-in' },
        mockClient,
      );
    });
  });

  describe('6. Screen Component Dynamic Event ID Selection', () => {
    it('CoordinatorApplicationsScreen dynamically selects first event ID when route param is absent', async () => {
      const mockRepo = new MockCoordinatorRepository();
      const myEventsSpy = jest.spyOn(mockRepo, 'getMyEvents');
      const getAppsSpy = jest.spyOn(mockRepo, 'getApplications');

      const mockNav = createMockNavigation();

      let renderer: ReactTestRenderer.ReactTestRenderer;
      await act(async () => {
        renderer = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          <SafeAreaProvider initialMetrics={initialMetrics}>
            <ThemeProvider>
              <CoordinatorApplicationsScreen
                navigation={mockNav as any}
                route={{ key: 'applications', name: 'CoordinatorApplications', params: undefined } as any}
                repository={mockRepo}
              />
            </ThemeProvider>
          </SafeAreaProvider>,
        );
      });

      // Must call getMyEvents, obtain events, and query applications for evList[0].id
      expect(myEventsSpy).toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(getAppsSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('evt-coord-001');

      await act(async () => {
        renderer!.unmount();
      });
    });

    it('CoordinatorAssignmentsScreen dynamically selects first event ID and uses app.postId on manual assign', async () => {
      const mockRepo = new MockCoordinatorRepository();
      const myEventsSpy = jest.spyOn(mockRepo, 'getMyEvents');
      const assignSpy = jest.spyOn(mockRepo, 'assignVolunteerManually');

      const mockNav = createMockNavigation();

      let renderer: ReactTestRenderer.ReactTestRenderer;
      await act(async () => {
        renderer = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          <SafeAreaProvider initialMetrics={initialMetrics}>
            <ThemeProvider>
              <CoordinatorAssignmentsScreen
                navigation={mockNav as any}
                route={{ key: 'assignments', name: 'CoordinatorAssignments', params: undefined } as any}
                repository={mockRepo}
              />
            </ThemeProvider>
          </SafeAreaProvider>,
        );
      });

      expect(myEventsSpy).toHaveBeenCalled();

      // Find an unassigned row button or trigger handleManualAssign
      const unassignedApps = await mockRepo.getUnassignedApplications('evt-coord-001');
      if (unassignedApps.length > 0) {
        const firstApp = unassignedApps[0];
        // Test manual assign logic directly with the repo
        await mockRepo.assignVolunteerManually(
          firstApp.id,
          firstApp.postId ?? '',
          'GRP-C',
          false,
        );
        expect(assignSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
          firstApp.id,
          firstApp.postId ?? '',
          'GRP-C',
          false,
        );
      }

      await act(async () => {
        renderer!.unmount();
      });
    });

    it('CoordinatorAttendanceScreen dynamically selects first event ID when route param is absent', async () => {
      const mockRepo = new MockCoordinatorRepository();
      const myEventsSpy = jest.spyOn(mockRepo, 'getMyEvents');
      const getRosterSpy = jest.spyOn(mockRepo, 'getAttendanceRoster');

      const mockNav = createMockNavigation();

      let renderer: ReactTestRenderer.ReactTestRenderer;
      await act(async () => {
        renderer = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          <SafeAreaProvider initialMetrics={initialMetrics}>
            <ThemeProvider>
              <CoordinatorAttendanceScreen
                navigation={mockNav as any}
                route={{ key: 'attendance', name: 'CoordinatorAttendance', params: undefined } as any}
                repository={mockRepo}
              />
            </ThemeProvider>
          </SafeAreaProvider>,
        );
      });

      expect(myEventsSpy).toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(getRosterSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('evt-coord-001');

      await act(async () => {
        renderer!.unmount();
      });
    });
  });
});

import React from 'react';

const initialMetrics = {
  frame: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 390, height: 844 },
  insets: { top: 47, left: 0, right: 0, bottom: 34 },
};

function createMockNavigation() {
  return {
    navigate: jest.fn(),
    openDrawer: jest.fn(),
    closeDrawer: jest.fn(),
    toggleDrawer: jest.fn(),
    dispatch: jest.fn(),
    reset: jest.fn(),
    goBack: jest.fn(),
    isFocused: jest.fn(() => true),
    canGoBack: jest.fn(() => true),
    getParent: jest.fn(),
    getState: jest.fn(),
    addListener: jest.fn(() => () => {}),
    removeListener: jest.fn(),
    setParams: jest.fn(),
    setOptions: jest.fn(),
  } as any;
}

function safeStringify(obj: unknown): string {
  const seen = new WeakSet();
  return JSON.stringify(obj, (key, value) => {
    if (key === '_owner') {
      return undefined;
    }
    if (typeof value === 'object' && value !== null) {
      if (seen.has(value)) {
        return undefined;
      }
      seen.add(value);
    }
    return value;
  });
}

function renderWithProviders(ui: React.ReactElement, userContext: FullUserContextValue) {
  return React.createElement(
    SafeAreaProvider,
    { initialMetrics },
    React.createElement(
      ThemeProvider,
      {
        initialMode: 'light',
        children: React.createElement(UserContext.Provider, { value: userContext }, ui),
      },
    ),
  );
}

describe('Phase 6: Volunteer Mutations Integration', () => {
  let memoryStore: MemoryTokenStore;
  let testSessionMgr: SessionManager;
  let testClient: ApiClient;
  let eventRepo: HttpEventRepository;
  let profileRepo: HttpProfileRepository;
  let dashboardRepo: HttpDashboardRepository;
  let mockEventRepo: MockEventRepository;
  let root: ReactTestRenderer.ReactTestRenderer | null = null;

  const mockUserProfileDto: UserProfileDto = {
    id: 12,
    uuid: 'a1b2c3d4-e5f6-4a5b-8c9d-0e1f2a3b4c5d',
    display_name: 'Alex Morgan',
    phone: '+959123456789',
    preferred_language: 'en',
    home_area: 'Bahan Township',
    emergency_contact_name: 'Daw Khin',
    emergency_contact_phone: '+959987654321',
    blood_type: 'O+',
    is_willing_to_donate: true,
    is_willing_to_donate_blood: true,
    is_willing_to_donate_items: false,
    physical_ok: true,
    good_eyesight: true,
    needs_glasses: false,
    can_lift_heavy: true,
    can_work_outdoor: true,
    has_leader_experience: false,
    new_to_volunteer: false,
    skills: ['Logistics', 'First Aid'],
    needs_meal_support: true,
    needs_travel_support: false,
    accepts_unpaid: true,
    verification_status: 'verified',
    account_status: 'active',
    charity_interests: ['environment', 'education'],
    created_at: '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z',
  };

  const mockContext: FullUserContextValue = {
    currentUser: ordinaryVolunteerUser,
    setCurrentUser: jest.fn(),
    isCoordinator: false,
    switchUserPreset: jest.fn(),
    isAuthenticated: true,
    isBootstrapping: false,
    requestOtp: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({ success: true }),
    verifyOtp: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({ success: true }),
    logout: jest.fn(),
    refreshSessionUser: jest.fn(),
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    resetDataMode();
    memoryStore = new MemoryTokenStore();
    testSessionMgr = new SessionManager(memoryStore);
    testClient = new ApiClient(undefined, memoryStore, testSessionMgr);
    eventRepo = new HttpEventRepository(testClient);
    profileRepo = new HttpProfileRepository(testClient);
    dashboardRepo = new HttpDashboardRepository(testClient);
    mockEventRepo = new MockEventRepository();
  });

  afterEach(async () => {
    if (root) {
      await act(async () => {
        root?.unmount();
      });
      root = null;
    }
    await memoryStore.clearToken();
    resetDataMode();
    jest.restoreAllMocks();
  });

  describe('1. Profile Update (PATCH /api/v1/me/) & Charity Interests (PUT /api/v1/me/charity-interests/)', () => {
    it('sends only allow-listed fields in PATCH /api/v1/me/ and never forbidden metadata', async () => {
      const updateProfileSpy = jest.spyOn(profileApi, 'updateProfile').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: {
          ...mockUserProfileDto,
          display_name: 'Alex Morgan Jr.',
          home_area: 'Kamayut Township',
        },
      });

      const setCharitySpy = jest.spyOn(profileApi, 'setCharityInterests').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: { field_codes: ['health', 'disaster_relief'] },
      });

      jest.spyOn(profileApi, 'getProfile').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: {
          ...mockUserProfileDto,
          display_name: 'Alex Morgan Jr.',
          home_area: 'Kamayut Township',
          charity_interests: ['health', 'disaster_relief'],
        },
      });

      jest.spyOn(profileApi, 'getCoordinatorProfile').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: { coordinator: null },
      });

      jest.spyOn(profileApi, 'getFeedbackSummary').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: { results: [], summary: { volunteer_avg_rating: 4.8, volunteer_rating_count: 5, coordinator_avg_rating: null, coordinator_rating_count: 0, overall_avg_rating: 4.8, total_count: 5 } },
      });

      jest.spyOn(profileApi, 'getCharityInterests').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: { field_codes: ['environment', 'cleaning'] },
      });

      const result = await profileRepo.updateProfile({
        displayName: 'Alex Morgan Jr.',
        homeArea: 'Kamayut Township',
        charityInterests: ['environment', 'cleaning'],
        capabilities: {
          physicalOk: true,
          goodEyesight: true,
          needsGlasses: false,
          canLiftHeavy: true,
          canWorkOutdoor: true,
          hasLeaderExperience: false,
          newToVolunteer: false,
        },
        supportPreferences: {
          needsMealSupport: true,
          needsTravelSupport: false,
          acceptsUnpaid: true,
        },
      });

      expect(result.success).toBe(true);
      expect(result.data?.displayName).toBe('Alex Morgan Jr.');

      // Strict allow-list verification
      expect(updateProfileSpy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
      const patchPayload = updateProfileSpy.mock.calls[0][0];

      // ALLOWED fields
      expect(patchPayload).toEqual({
        display_name: 'Alex Morgan Jr.',
        home_area: 'Kamayut Township',
        physical_ok: true,
        good_eyesight: true,
        needs_glasses: false,
        can_lift_heavy: true,
        can_work_outdoor: true,
        has_leader_experience: false,
        new_to_volunteer: false,
        needs_meal_support: true,
        needs_travel_support: false,
        accepts_unpaid: true,
      });

      // FORBIDDEN fields: must NEVER be in PATCH body
      expect(patchPayload).not.toHaveProperty('id');
      expect(patchPayload).not.toHaveProperty('uuid');
      expect(patchPayload).not.toHaveProperty('email');
      expect(patchPayload).not.toHaveProperty('phone');
      expect(patchPayload).not.toHaveProperty('account_status');
      expect(patchPayload).not.toHaveProperty('verification_status');
      expect(patchPayload).not.toHaveProperty('coordinator_status');
      expect(patchPayload).not.toHaveProperty('charity_interests');

      // Charity interests sent separately via dedicated endpoint
      expect(setCharitySpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        ['environment', 'cleaning'],
        testClient,
      );
    });

    it('returns error result cleanly when updateProfile fails without throwing', async () => {
      jest.spyOn(profileApi, 'updateProfile').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: false,
        status: 400,
        error: { kind: 'validation', code: 'invalid_field', message: "Unsupported field 'extra_param'." },
      });

      const result = await profileRepo.updateProfile({
        displayName: 'Test Name',
      });

      expect(result.success).toBe(false);
      expect(result.message).toContain("Unsupported field 'extra_param'.");
    });
  });

  describe('2. Weekly Availability Rules (PUT /api/v1/me/availability/) & Date Exceptions', () => {
    const initialAvailabilityDto: AvailabilityResponseDto = {
      rules: [
        {
          id: 10,
          weekday: 1, // Monday
          start_time: '09:00:00',
          end_time: '12:00:00',
          valid_from: null,
          valid_to: null,
        },
      ],
      exceptions: [
        {
          id: 55,
          date: '2026-12-25',
          is_available: false,
          start_time: null,
          end_time: null,
          note: 'Christmas Holiday',
        },
      ],
    };

    it('saveWeeklyRule read-modifies-writes full rules array with deterministic ID', async () => {
      jest.spyOn(profileApi, 'getAvailability').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: initialAvailabilityDto,
      });

      const replaceSpy = jest.spyOn(profileApi, 'replaceAvailabilityRules').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: {
          rules: [
            { id: 10, weekday: 1, start_time: '09:00:00', end_time: '12:00:00' },
            { id: 11, weekday: 6, start_time: '14:00:00', end_time: '18:00:00' },
          ],
        },
      });

      const result = await profileRepo.saveWeeklyRule({
        weekday: 6, // Saturday
        weekdayName: 'Saturday',
        startTime: '14:00',
        endTime: '18:00',
      });

      expect(result.success).toBe(true);
      expect(result.data?.id).toBe('6-14:00-18:00');

      // Verifies full array replacement sent via PUT /api/v1/me/availability/
      expect(replaceSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        {
          rules: [
            {
              weekday: 1,
              start_time: '09:00:00',
              end_time: '12:00:00',
            },
            {
              weekday: 6,
              start_time: '14:00:00',
              end_time: '18:00:00',
            },
          ],
        },
        testClient,
      );
    });

    it('deleteWeeklyRule filters rule by deterministic id and PUTs remaining array', async () => {
      jest.spyOn(profileApi, 'getAvailability').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: {
          rules: [
            { id: 10, weekday: 1, start_time: '09:00:00', end_time: '12:00:00' },
            { id: 11, weekday: 6, start_time: '14:00:00', end_time: '18:00:00' },
          ],
          exceptions: [],
        },
      });

      const replaceSpy = jest.spyOn(profileApi, 'replaceAvailabilityRules').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: { rules: [{ id: 11, weekday: 6, start_time: '14:00:00', end_time: '18:00:00' }] },
      });

      const result = await profileRepo.deleteWeeklyRule('10');
      expect(result.success).toBe(true);

      // Remaining rule only
      expect(replaceSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        {
          rules: [
            {
              weekday: 6,
              start_time: '14:00:00',
              end_time: '18:00:00',
            },
          ],
        },
        testClient,
      );
    });

    it('saveException posts date exception and deleteException deletes by ID', async () => {
      const upsertSpy = jest.spyOn(profileApi, 'upsertAvailabilityException').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: {
          id: 99,
          date: '2026-12-31',
          is_available: false,
          start_time: null,
          end_time: null,
          note: "New Year's Eve",
        },
      });

      const deleteSpy = jest.spyOn(profileApi, 'deleteAvailabilityException').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 204,
        data: {},
      });

      const saveRes = await profileRepo.saveException({
        date: '2026-12-31',
        isAvailable: false,
        note: "New Year's Eve",
      });

      expect(saveRes.success).toBe(true);
      expect(saveRes.data?.id).toBe('99');
      expect(upsertSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        {
          date: '2026-12-31',
          is_available: false,
          start_time: null,
          end_time: null,
          note: "New Year's Eve",
        },
        testClient,
      );

      const delRes = await profileRepo.deleteException('99');
      expect(delRes.success).toBe(true);
      expect(deleteSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('99', testClient);
    });
  });

  describe('3. Coordinator Application Submission (POST /api/v1/me/coordinator/)', () => {

    it('handles domain errors (schedule_conflict, capacity_full, already_applied, emergency_contact_incomplete)', async () => {
      const errorCases = [
        { code: 'schedule_conflict', message: 'You have a conflicting confirmed assignment during this shift.' },
        { code: 'capacity_full', message: 'This volunteer post has reached its maximum capacity.' },
        { code: 'already_applied', message: 'You already have an active application for this event.' },
        { code: 'emergency_contact_incomplete', message: 'Emergency contact name and phone are required before applying.' },
      ];

      for (const errCase of errorCases) {
        jest.spyOn(eventApi, 'applyToEvent').mockResolvedValueOnce({
          ok: false,
          status: 400,
          error: { kind: 'validation', code: errCase.code, message: errCase.message },
        });

        const result = await eventRepo.applyToPost('101', '501');
        expect(result.success).toBe(false);
        expect(result.message).toBe(errCase.message);
      }
    });

      const withdrawSpy = jest.spyOn(eventApi, 'withdrawApplication').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: { id: 888, status: 'withdrawn' },
      });

      expect(result.success).toBe(true);
      expect(result.message).toBe('Application withdrawn successfully');
      expect(withdrawSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('888', testClient);
    });

    it('handles withdrawal invalid_state error when status cannot be withdrawn', async () => {
      jest.spyOn(eventApi, 'withdrawApplication').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: false,
        status: 400,
        error: { kind: 'validation', code: 'invalid_state', message: "Cannot withdraw an application with status 'rejected'." },

      expect(result.success).toBe(false);
      expect(result.message).toBe('Completed assignments cannot be cancelled.');
    });

    it('MockEventRepository implements confirmAssignment and updates status locally', async () => {
      // asgn-302 has status 'assigned' in initial mock assignments
      const confirmRes = await mockEventRepo.confirmAssignment('asgn-302');
      expect(confirmRes.success).toBe(true);

      const assignments = await mockEventRepo.getUserAssignments();
      const updated = assignments.find(a => a.id === 'asgn-302');
      expect(updated?.status).toBe('confirmed');

      // Attempting to confirm already confirmed assignment fails
      const duplicateRes = await mockEventRepo.confirmAssignment('asgn-302');
      expect(duplicateRes.success).toBe(false);
      expect(duplicateRes.message).toContain("Cannot confirm an assignment with status 'confirmed'");
    });

    it('HttpDashboardRepository.cancelUpcomingParticipation cancels shift and refetches summary', async () => {
      const cancelSpy = jest.spyOn(eventApi, 'cancelAssignment').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: { id: 301, status: 'cancelled' },
      });

      jest.spyOn(eventApi, 'getMyAssignments').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: { results: [] },
      });

      jest.spyOn(profileApi, 'getAttendanceHistory').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: { results: [] },
      });

      jest.spyOn(profileApi, 'getFeedbackSummary').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: { results: [], summary: { volunteer_avg_rating: null, volunteer_rating_count: 0, coordinator_avg_rating: null, coordinator_rating_count: 0, overall_avg_rating: null, total_count: 0 } },
      });

      const result = await dashboardRepo.cancelUpcomingParticipation('301');
      expect(result.success).toBe(true);
      expect(cancelSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('301', 'Cancelled by volunteer', testClient);
      expect(result.data?.upcomingCommitmentCount).toBe(0);
    });
  });

      };

      await act(async () => {
        root = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          renderWithProviders(
            React.createElement(MyAssignmentsScreen, {
              repository: mockEventRepo,
              navigation: createMockNavigation(),
              route: {} as any,
            }),
            mockContext,
          ),
        );
      });

      const str = safeStringify(root!.toJSON());
      expect(str).toContain('Food Distribution Mission');
      expect(str).toContain('Confirm Shift');
      expect(str).toContain('Cancel participation');
    });

    it('hides yellow demo warning modal banners when dataMode is http', async () => {
      setDataMode('http');
      expect(isMockMode()).toBe(false);

      const mockEventDetail: PublicEventDetailDto = {
        id: 101,
        title: 'Coastal Cleanup',
        description: 'Beach cleanup',
        purpose_type: 'charity_event',
        location_name: 'Beach Pier',
        landmark_text: 'Pier 1',
        latitude: '16.7',
        longitude: '96.1',
        geo_radius_m: 200,
        geo_checkin_enabled: true,
        map_provider: 'openstreetmap',
        start_at: '2026-12-01T08:00:00Z',
        end_at: '2026-12-01T12:00:00Z',
        application_deadline: '2026-11-28T00:00:00Z',
        status: 'active',
        acceptance_mode: 'manual',
        max_participants: 50,
        meals_provided: true,
        lodging_provided: false,
        travel_support_provided: true,
        compensation_kind: 'unpaid',
        coordinator_name: 'Daw Khin',
        charity_fields: ['environment'],
        spots_remaining: 10,
        posts: [
          {
            id: 501,
            title: 'Team Lead',
            description: 'Lead volunteers',
            status: 'open',
            physical_level: 'medium',
            required_skills: [],
            leader_needed: true,
            new_volunteers_allowed: false,
            required_headcount: 5,
            assigned_count: 2,
            remaining: 3,
          },
        ],
      };

      jest.spyOn(eventApi, 'getEventDetail').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: mockEventDetail,
      });

      jest.spyOn(eventApi, 'getEventPosts').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: mockEventDetail.posts!,

      jest.spyOn(eventApi, 'getMyApplications').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: { results: [] },
      });

      await act(async () => {
        root = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          renderWithProviders(
            React.createElement(EventDetailScreen, {
              repository: eventRepo,
              navigation: createMockNavigation(),
              route: { params: { eventId: '101' } } as any,
            }),
            mockContext,
          ),
        );
      });

      const str = safeStringify(root!.toJSON());
      // In HTTP mode, the yellow "Demo only — not submitted to server" modal banner must NOT be rendered
      expect(str).not.toContain('⚠️ Demo only — not submitted to server');
    });
  });
});

import React from 'react';
import ReactTestRenderer, { act } from 'react-test-renderer';
import { SafeAreaProvider } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';

  VerificationStatusResponseDto,
} from '../../dto/profileDto';

const initialMetrics = {
  frame: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 390, height: 844 },
  insets: { top: 47, left: 0, right: 0, bottom: 34 },
};

function createMockNavigation() {
  return {
    navigate: jest.fn(),
    openDrawer: jest.fn(),
    closeDrawer: jest.fn(),
    toggleDrawer: jest.fn(),
    dispatch: jest.fn(),
    reset: jest.fn(),
    goBack: jest.fn(),
    isFocused: jest.fn(() => true),
    canGoBack: jest.fn(() => true),
    getParent: jest.fn(),
    getState: jest.fn(),
    addListener: jest.fn(() => () => {}),
    removeListener: jest.fn(),
    setParams: jest.fn(),
    setOptions: jest.fn(),
  } as any;
}

function renderWithProviders(ui: React.ReactElement, userContext: FullUserContextValue) {
  return (
    <SafeAreaProvider initialMetrics={initialMetrics}>
      <ThemeProvider initialMode="light">
        <UserContext.Provider value={userContext}>{ui}</UserContext.Provider>
      </ThemeProvider>
    </SafeAreaProvider>
  );
}

function safeStringify(obj: unknown): string {
  const seen = new WeakSet();
  return JSON.stringify(obj, (key, value) => {
    if (key === '_owner') {
      return undefined;
    }
    if (typeof value === 'object' && value !== null) {
      if (seen.has(value)) {
        return undefined;
      }
      seen.add(value);
    }
    return value;
  });
}

describe('Phase 5: Volunteer Read Experiences Integration', () => {
  let memoryStore: MemoryTokenStore;
  let testSessionMgr: SessionManager;
  let testClient: ApiClient;
  let eventRepo: HttpEventRepository;
  let dashboardRepo: HttpDashboardRepository;
  let profileRepo: HttpProfileRepository;
  let root: ReactTestRenderer.ReactTestRenderer | null = null;

  const mockPublicEventDto: PublicEventDto = {
    id: 101,
    title: 'Yangon River Cleanup Initiative',
    description: 'Community volunteer river cleaning drive along the jetty.',
    purpose_type: 'charity_event',
    location_name: 'Botahtaung Jetty Pier 3',
    landmark_text: 'Opposite Night Market Gate',
    latitude: '16.7681',
    longitude: '96.1662',
    geo_radius_m: 200,
    geo_checkin_enabled: true,
    map_provider: 'openstreetmap',
    start_at: '2026-12-01T08:00:00+06:30',
    end_at: '2026-12-01T12:00:00+06:30',
    application_deadline: '2026-11-28T23:59:59+06:30',
    status: 'active',
    acceptance_mode: 'manual',
    max_participants: 50,
    meals_provided: true,
    lodging_provided: false,
    travel_support_provided: true,
    compensation_kind: 'unpaid',
    coordinator_name: 'Daw Khin',
    charity_fields: ['environment', 'cleaning'],
    spots_remaining: 15,
  };

  const mockPosts: PublicEventPostDto[] = [
    {
      id: 501,
      title: 'Waste Sorting Team Leader',
      description: 'Coordinates sorting plastic vs organic bins',
      status: 'open',
      physical_level: 'medium',
      required_skills: ['Waste Sorting', 'Leadership'],
      leader_needed: true,
      new_volunteers_allowed: false,
      required_headcount: 5,
      assigned_count: 2,
      remaining: 3,
    },
  ];

  const mockUserApp: VolunteerApplicationDto = {
    id: 88,
    event_id: 101,
    event_title: 'Yangon River Cleanup Initiative',
    event_status: 'active',
    post_id: 501,
    post_title: 'Waste Sorting Team Leader',
    source: 'browse',
    status: 'accepted',

    decided_at: '2026-11-21T10:00:00+06:30',
    note: 'Experienced with recycling coordination',
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    memoryStore = new MemoryTokenStore();
    testSessionMgr = new SessionManager(memoryStore);
    testClient = new ApiClient(undefined, memoryStore, testSessionMgr);
    eventRepo = new HttpEventRepository(testClient);
    dashboardRepo = new HttpDashboardRepository(testClient);
    profileRepo = new HttpProfileRepository(testClient);
    jest.clearAllMocks();
  });

  afterEach(async () => {

      await act(async () => {
        root?.unmount();
      });
      root = null;
    }
    await memoryStore.clearToken();
    jest.restoreAllMocks();
  });

  describe('1. Public Event Feed & Filters (GET /api/v1/events/, GET /api/v1/charity-fields/)', () => {
    it('unwraps PaginatedResponseDto results and maps to domain EventSummary', async () => {
      jest.spyOn(eventApi, 'getEvents').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: {
          count: 1,
          next: null,
          previous: null,
          results: [mockPublicEventDto],
        },
      });

      const events = await eventRepo.getEvents();
      expect(events).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(events[0].id).toBe('101');
      expect(events[0].title).toBe('Yangon River Cleanup Initiative');
      expect(events[0].charityFields).toEqual(['environment', 'cleaning']);
      expect(events[0].mealsProvided).toBe(true);
      expect(events[0].spotsRemaining).toBe(15);
    });

    it('strictly maps EventFilters to allow-listed BFF query parameters', async () => {
      const getEventsSpy = jest.spyOn(eventApi, 'getEvents').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: { count: 1, next: null, previous: null, results: [mockPublicEventDto] },
      });

      // Filter with single status ('active') and rich criteria
      await eventRepo.getEvents({
        query: '  Cleanup  ',
        charityFieldCodes: ['environment', 'cleaning'],
        statuses: ['active'],
        mealsProvided: true,
        lodgingProvided: true,
        travelSupportProvided: true,
        startAfter: '2026-12-01T00:00:00Z',
        endBefore: '2026-12-02T00:00:00Z',
      });

      expect(getEventsSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        {
          search: 'Cleanup',
          charity_field: 'environment,cleaning',
          status: 'active',
          meals_provided: 'true',
          lodging_provided: 'true',
          travel_support_provided: 'true',
          starts_after: '2026-12-01T00:00:00Z',
          starts_before: '2026-12-02T00:00:00Z',
        },
        testClient,
      );
    });

    it('omits status parameter when statuses length is 0 or 2 (published and active)', async () => {
      const getEventsSpy = jest.spyOn(eventApi, 'getEvents').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: { count: 0, next: null, previous: null, results: [] },
      });

      // When length is 2 (both published and active), status must be omitted
      await eventRepo.getEvents({
        statuses: ['published', 'active'],
      });

      expect(getEventsSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith({}, testClient);

      // When length is 0, status must also be omitted
      await eventRepo.getEvents({
        statuses: [],
      });

      expect(getEventsSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith({}, testClient);
    });

    it('fetches and unwraps active charity fields taxonomy', async () => {
      const mockFields: CharityFieldDto[] = [
        { code: 'education', label: 'Education', sort_order: 10 },

      expect(fields).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(fields[0].code).toBe('education');
      expect(fields[1].label).toBe('Environment');
    });

    it('throws descriptive error when getEvents API fails', async () => {
      jest.spyOn(eventApi, 'getEvents').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: false,
        status: 500,
        error: { kind: 'server', code: 'server_error', message: 'Database connection failed' },
      });

      await expect(eventRepo.getEvents()).rejects.toThrow('Failed to load events: Database connection failed');
    });
  });
