/**
 * @format
 */

import { ApiClient, defaultApiClient } from '../../core/api/apiClient';
import type {
  CoordinatorApplicationItem,
  CoordinatorApplicationStatus,
  CoordinatorAssignmentItem,
  CoordinatorAttendanceItem,
  CoordinatorEventDetail,
  CoordinatorEventStatus,
  CoordinatorEventSummary,
  CoordinatorOverviewMetrics,
  CreateEventWizardDraft,
  EventPurposeType,
} from '../../domain/models/coordinator';
import type {
  CoordinatorOperationResult,
  CoordinatorRepository,
} from '../contracts/coordinatorRepository';
import { coordinatorApi } from '../endpoints/coordinatorApi';
import { checkinApi } from '../endpoints/checkinApi';
import { eventApi } from '../endpoints/eventApi';
import {
  mapAttendanceRosterItem,
  mapCoordinatorApplication,
  mapCoordinatorAssignment,
  mapCoordinatorEventSummary,
  mapCreateEventDraftToRequest,
} from '../mappers/coordinatorMapper';
import { mapPublicEventPost } from '../mappers/eventMapper';
import type { PublicEventPostDto } from '../dto/eventDto';
import type {
  AttendanceRosterDto,
  CoordinatorApplicationDto,
  CoordinatorAssignmentDto,
  CoordinatorEventListItemDto,
} from '../dto/coordinatorDto';

export class HttpCoordinatorRepository implements CoordinatorRepository {
  private client: ApiClient;
  private appToEventMap: Map<string, string> = new Map();
  private attendanceCache: Map<string, CoordinatorAttendanceItem> = new Map();
  private coordinatorIdentity = {
    id: 'usr-coord',
    displayName: 'Coordinator',
    email: 'coordinator@example.com',
  };

  constructor(client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient) {
    this.client = client;
  }

  public setCoordinatorIdentity(identity: {
    id: string;
    displayName: string;
    email?: string;
  }): void {
    this.coordinatorIdentity = {
      id: identity.id,
      displayName: identity.displayName,
      email: identity.email ?? 'coordinator@example.com',
    };
  }

  public getCoordinatorIdentity(): {
    id: string;
    displayName: string;
    email: string;
  } {
    return { ...this.coordinatorIdentity };
  }

  public async getOverviewMetrics(): Promise<CoordinatorOverviewMetrics> {
    const eventsRes = await coordinatorApi.getMyEvents(this.client);
    if (!eventsRes.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to load coordinator events: ${eventsRes.error.message}`);
    }

    const events: CoordinatorEventListItemDto[] = eventsRes.data.results || [];
    const totalEventsCount = events.length;
    const activeEventsCount = events.filter(
      e => e.status === 'active' || e.status === 'published',
    ).length;
    const draftEventsCount = events.filter(e => e.status === 'draft').length;

    let pendingApplicationsCount = 0;
    let acceptedApplicantsCount = 0;
    let checkedInTodayCount = 0;

    // Fetch summaries for events to aggregate counts
    const summaryPromises = events.map(e =>
      coordinatorApi.getEventSummary(e.id, this.client),
    );
    const summaryResults = await Promise.all(summaryPromises);

    for (const res of summaryResults) {
      if (res.ok && res.data) {
        pendingApplicationsCount += res.data.pending_count || 0;
        acceptedApplicantsCount += res.data.accepted_count || 0;
        checkedInTodayCount += res.data.checked_in_count || 0;
      }
    }

    return {
      totalEventsCount,
      activeEventsCount,
      draftEventsCount,
      pendingApplicationsCount,
      acceptedApplicantsCount,
      checkedInTodayCount,
    };
  }

  public async getMyEvents(): Promise<CoordinatorEventSummary[]> {
    const res = await coordinatorApi.getMyEvents(this.client);
    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to load events: ${res.error.message}`);
    }

    const list: CoordinatorEventListItemDto[] = res.data.results || [];
    const summaries = await Promise.all(
      list.map(e =>
        coordinatorApi.getEventSummary(e.id, this.client).catch(() => null),
      ),
    );

    return list.map((e, idx) => {
      const summaryRes = summaries[idx];
      const summaryDto =
        summaryRes && summaryRes.ok ? summaryRes.data : null;
      return mapCoordinatorEventSummary(e, summaryDto);
    });
  }

  public async getEventDetail(
    eventId: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorEventDetail | null> {
    const [summaryRes, postsRes, eventsRes, publicDetailRes] = await Promise.all([
      coordinatorApi.getEventSummary(eventId, this.client),
      coordinatorApi.getEventPosts(eventId, this.client).catch(() => null),
      coordinatorApi.getMyEvents(this.client).catch(() => null),
      eventApi.getEventDetail(eventId, this.client).catch(() => null),
    ]);

    if (!summaryRes.ok) {
      if (summaryRes.status === 404) {
        return null;
      }
      throw new Error(`Failed to load event detail: ${summaryRes.error.message}`);
    }

    const summary = summaryRes.data;
    const events: CoordinatorEventListItemDto[] =
      eventsRes && eventsRes.ok && eventsRes.data?.results ? eventsRes.data.results : [];
    const currentEventItem = events.find(e => String(e.id) === String(eventId));

    const publicDetail =
      publicDetailRes && publicDetailRes.ok ? publicDetailRes.data : null;

    let rawPosts: PublicEventPostDto[] = [];
    if (publicDetail?.posts && Array.isArray(publicDetail.posts) && publicDetail.posts.length > 0) {
      rawPosts = publicDetail.posts;
    } else if (postsRes && postsRes.ok) {
      rawPosts = Array.isArray(postsRes.data)
        ? postsRes.data
        : postsRes.data?.results || [];
    }

    const isMonitor = currentEventItem?.is_read_only_monitor ?? false;

    return {
      id: String(summary.id),
      title: publicDetail?.title || summary.title,
      description: publicDetail?.description || '',
      status:
        (publicDetail?.status as CoordinatorEventStatus) ||
        (summary.status as CoordinatorEventStatus) ||
        'draft',
      purposeType:
        (publicDetail?.purpose_type as EventPurposeType) || 'charity_event',
      startAt: publicDetail?.start_at || summary.start_at,
      endAt: publicDetail?.end_at || summary.end_at,
      applicationDeadline: publicDetail?.application_deadline ?? undefined,
      locationName: publicDetail?.location_name || 'Designated Location',
      landmarkText: publicDetail?.landmark_text ?? undefined,
      charityFields: (publicDetail?.charity_fields as any[]) || [],
      ownerId: currentEventItem ? String(currentEventItem.owner_id) : this.coordinatorIdentity.id,
      currentCoordinatorId: currentEventItem ? String(currentEventItem.current_coordinator_id) : this.coordinatorIdentity.id,
      coordinatorName: publicDetail?.coordinator_name || this.coordinatorIdentity.displayName,
      isOwnerAsMonitor: isMonitor,
      pendingApplicationCount: summary.pending_count || 0,
      acceptedApplicantCount: summary.accepted_count || 0,
      assignedCount: summary.assignment_count || 0,
      checkedInCount: summary.checked_in_count || 0,
      latitude: typeof publicDetail?.latitude === 'number'
        ? publicDetail.latitude
        : parseFloat(String(publicDetail?.latitude || 0)) || 0,
      longitude: typeof publicDetail?.longitude === 'number'
        ? publicDetail.longitude
        : parseFloat(String(publicDetail?.longitude || 0)) || 0,
      geoRadiusM: publicDetail?.geo_radius_m ?? 200,
      geoCheckinEnabled: publicDetail?.geo_checkin_enabled ?? summary.geo_checkin_enabled ?? true,
      geoCheckinOpensBeforeMin: 30,
      requireCheckinConfirmation: summary.pending_confirmations > 0,
      mapProvider: publicDetail?.map_provider || summary.map_provider || 'openstreetmap',
      acceptanceMode: (publicDetail?.acceptance_mode as any) || (summary.acceptance_mode as any) || 'manual',
      autoAssignEnabled: summary.auto_assign_enabled ?? false,
      leaderStrategy: 'none',
      maxParticipants: publicDetail?.max_participants ?? summary.max_participants ?? 0,
      mealsProvided: Boolean(publicDetail?.meals_provided),
      lodgingProvided: Boolean(publicDetail?.lodging_provided),
      travelSupportProvided: Boolean(publicDetail?.travel_support_provided),
      compensationKind: (publicDetail?.compensation_kind as any) || 'unpaid',
      compensationNote: publicDetail?.compensation_note ?? undefined,
      onboardingText: publicDetail?.onboarding_text ?? undefined,
      onboardingVersion: publicDetail?.onboarding_version ?? 1,
      requireOnboardingAck: false,
      qrRegistrationEnabled: false,
      posts: rawPosts.map(p => mapPublicEventPost(p, summary.id)),
    };
  }

  public async createDraftEvent(
    draft: CreateEventWizardDraft,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorEventDetail>> {
    const payload = mapCreateEventDraftToRequest(draft);
    const res = await coordinatorApi.createEvent(payload, this.client);

    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }

    const created = await this.getEventDetail(String(res.data.id));
    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Draft event created successfully',
      data: created ?? undefined,
    };
  }

  public async transitionEventStatus(
    eventId: string,
    targetStatus: CoordinatorEventStatus,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorEventDetail>> {
    let res;
    if (targetStatus === 'published') {
      res = await coordinatorApi.publishEvent(eventId, this.client);
    } else if (targetStatus === 'active') {
      res = await coordinatorApi.activateEvent(eventId, this.client);
    } else if (targetStatus === 'completed') {
      res = await coordinatorApi.completeEvent(eventId, this.client);
    } else if (targetStatus === 'cancelled') {
      res = await coordinatorApi.cancelEvent(
        eventId,
        'Cancelled by coordinator',
        this.client,
      );
    } else {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: `Unsupported event status transition to '${targetStatus}'.`,
      };
    }

    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }

    const updated = await this.getEventDetail(eventId);
    return {
      success: true,
      message: `Event transitioned to ${targetStatus}`,
      data: updated ?? undefined,
    };
  }

  public async handoverEvent(
    eventId: string,
    newCoordinatorUserId: string,
    reason: string = '',
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult> {
    const numericUserId = Number(newCoordinatorUserId);
    const res = await coordinatorApi.handoverEvent(
      eventId,
      isNaN(numericUserId) ? 0 : numericUserId,
      reason,
      this.client,
    );
    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }
    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Event handed over successfully',
    };
  }

  public async retakeEvent(
    eventId: string,
    reason: string = '',
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult> {
    const res = await coordinatorApi.retakeEvent(eventId, reason, this.client);
    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }
    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Event retaken successfully',
    };
  }

  public async getApplications(
    eventId?: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorApplicationItem[]> {
    if (eventId) {
      const res = await coordinatorApi.getApplications(
        eventId,
        undefined,
        this.client,
      );
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Failed to load applications: ${res.error.message}`);
      }
      const list: CoordinatorApplicationDto[] = res.data.results || [];
      const items = list.map(a => {
        this.appToEventMap.set(String(a.id), String(eventId));
        return mapCoordinatorApplication(a, eventId);
      });
      return items;
    }

    // If no eventId, aggregate over coordinator's events
    const events = await this.getMyEvents();
    const appLists = await Promise.all(
      events.map(e => this.getApplications(e.id).catch(() => [])),
    );
    return appLists.flat();
  }

  public async updateApplicationStatus(
    applicationId: string,
    status: CoordinatorApplicationStatus,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorApplicationItem>> {
    const action =
      status === 'accepted'
        ? 'accept'
        : status === 'rejected'
        ? 'reject'
        : 'waitlist';

    const res = await coordinatorApi.decideApplication(
      applicationId,
      action,
      {},
      this.client,
    );

    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: `Application ${status} successfully`,
    };
  }

  public async getAssignments(
    eventId?: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorAssignmentItem[]> {
    if (eventId) {
      const res = await coordinatorApi.getAssignments(eventId, this.client);
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Failed to load assignments: ${res.error.message}`);
      }
      const list: CoordinatorAssignmentDto[] = res.data.results || [];
      return list.map(a => mapCoordinatorAssignment(a, eventId));
    }

    const events = await this.getMyEvents();
    const lists = await Promise.all(
      events.map(e => this.getAssignments(e.id).catch(() => [])),
    );
    return lists.flat();
  }

  public async getUnassignedApplications(
    eventId?: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorApplicationItem[]> {
    if (eventId) {
      const res = await coordinatorApi.getUnassignedApplications(
        eventId,
        this.client,
      );
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          `Failed to load unassigned applications: ${res.error.message}`,
        );
      }
      const list: CoordinatorApplicationDto[] = res.data.results || [];
      return list.map(a => {
        this.appToEventMap.set(String(a.id), String(eventId));
        return mapCoordinatorApplication(a, eventId);
      });
    }

    const events = await this.getMyEvents();
    const lists = await Promise.all(
      events.map(e => this.getUnassignedApplications(e.id).catch(() => [])),
    );
    return lists.flat();
  }

  public async runAutoAssignment(
    eventId: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorAssignmentItem[]>> {
    const res = await coordinatorApi.runAutoAssignment(eventId, this.client);
    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }

    const assignments = await this.getAssignments(eventId);
    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Auto-assignment completed successfully',
      data: assignments,
    };
  }

  public async assignVolunteerManually(
    applicationId: string,
    postId: string,
    groupCode?: string,
    isGroupLeader?: boolean,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorAssignmentItem>> {
    let eventId = this.appToEventMap.get(applicationId);
    if (!eventId) {
      const events = await this.getMyEvents();
      if (events.length > 0) {
        eventId = events[0].id;
      } else {
        return {
          success: false,
          message: 'Unable to determine event for volunteer assignment.',
        };
      }
    }

    const numericPostId =
      !postId || !postId.trim() || isNaN(Number(postId)) || Number(postId) <= 0
        ? null
        : Number(postId);
    const res = await coordinatorApi.manualAssign(
      eventId,
      {
        application_id: Number(applicationId),
        post_id: numericPostId,
        group_code: groupCode ?? null,
        is_group_leader: isGroupLeader ?? false,
      },
      this.client,
    );

    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }

    const item = mapCoordinatorAssignment(res.data, eventId);
    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Volunteer assigned successfully',
      data: item,
    };
  }

  public async cancelAssignment(
    assignmentId: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorAssignmentItem>> {
    const res = await eventApi.cancelAssignment(
      assignmentId,
      'Cancelled by coordinator',
      this.client,
    );
    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Assignment cancelled successfully',
    };
  }

  public async getAttendanceRoster(
    eventId?: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorAttendanceItem[]> {
    if (eventId) {
      const res = await coordinatorApi.getAttendanceRoster(
        eventId,
        this.client,
      );
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          `Failed to load attendance roster: ${res.error.message}`,
        );
      }
      const list: AttendanceRosterDto[] = res.data.results || [];
      const items = list.map(item => {
        const mapped = mapAttendanceRosterItem(item, eventId);
        this.attendanceCache.set(mapped.id, mapped);
        this.attendanceCache.set(mapped.assignmentId, mapped);
        return mapped;
      });
      return items;
    }

    const events = await this.getMyEvents();
    const lists = await Promise.all(
      events.map(e => this.getAttendanceRoster(e.id).catch(() => [])),
    );
    return lists.flat();
  }

  public async getPendingGeoConfirmations(
    eventId?: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorAttendanceItem[]> {
    if (eventId) {
      const res = await coordinatorApi.getPendingGeoConfirmations(
        eventId,
        this.client,
      );
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          `Failed to load pending geo confirmations: ${res.error.message}`,
        );
      }
      const list: AttendanceRosterDto[] = res.data.results || [];
      const items = list.map(item => {
        const mapped = mapAttendanceRosterItem(item, eventId);
        this.attendanceCache.set(mapped.id, mapped);
        this.attendanceCache.set(mapped.assignmentId, mapped);
        return mapped;
      });
      return items;
    }

    const events = await this.getMyEvents();
    const lists = await Promise.all(
      events.map(e => this.getPendingGeoConfirmations(e.id).catch(() => [])),
    );
    return lists.flat();
  }

  public async confirmGeoAttendance(
    attendanceId: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorAttendanceItem>> {
    const res = await coordinatorApi.confirmAttendance(
      attendanceId,
      'Confirmed by coordinator',
      this.client,
    );
    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }

    const cached = this.attendanceCache.get(attendanceId);
    if (cached) {
      cached.isPendingGeoConfirmation = false;
      cached.status = 'checked_in';
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Geo attendance confirmed',
      data: cached,
    };
  }

  public async rejectGeoAttendance(
    attendanceId: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorAttendanceItem>> {
    const res = await coordinatorApi.rejectAttendance(
      attendanceId,
      'Rejected by coordinator',
      this.client,
    );
    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }

    const cached = this.attendanceCache.get(attendanceId);
    if (cached) {
      cached.isPendingGeoConfirmation = false;
      cached.status = 'scheduled';
      if (cached.geoAttempt) {
        cached.geoAttempt.confirmationStatus = 'rejected';
      }
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Geo attendance rejected',
      data: cached,
    };
  }

  public async manualCheckIn(
    attendanceId: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorAttendanceItem>> {
    let record = this.attendanceCache.get(attendanceId);
    if (!record) {
      for (const item of this.attendanceCache.values()) {
        if (item.id === attendanceId || item.assignmentId === attendanceId) {
          record = item;
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!record) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Attendance record not found in active roster cache.',
      };
    }

    const res = await coordinatorApi.manualCheckIn(
      {
        event_id: Number(record.eventId),
        user_id: Number(record.userId),
        note: 'Manual on-site check-in',
      },
      this.client,
    );

    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }

    record.status = 'checked_in';
    record.checkInMethod = 'manual';
    record.checkedInAt = new Date().toISOString();
    record.isPendingGeoConfirmation = false;

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Volunteer checked in manually',
      data: record,
    };
  }

  public async createQrToken(
    eventId: string,
    purpose: 'walkin_registration' | 'checkin' | 'join_event',
    maxUses?: number | null,
    ttlMinutes: number = 240,
  ): Promise<
    CoordinatorOperationResult<{
      id: number;
      rawToken: string;
      expiresAt: string;
    }>
  > {
    const res = await checkinApi.createCoordinatorQrToken(
      eventId,
      {
        purpose,
        max_uses: maxUses,
        ttl_minutes: ttlMinutes,
      },
      this.client,
    );

    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'QR token generated successfully',
      data: {
        id: res.data.id,
        rawToken: res.data.raw_token,
        expiresAt: res.data.expires_at,
      },
    };
  }

  public async revokeQrToken(
    qrId: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult> {
    const res = await checkinApi.revokeCoordinatorQrToken(qrId, this.client);
    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'QR token revoked successfully',
    };
  }
}

export const defaultHttpCoordinatorRepository = new HttpCoordinatorRepository();

/**
 * @format

import { ApiClient, defaultApiClient } from '../../core/api/apiClient';
import type { DashboardSummary } from '../../domain/models/dashboard';
import type { IDashboardRepository } from '../contracts/dashboardRepository';
import { eventApi } from '../endpoints/eventApi';
import { profileApi } from '../endpoints/profileApi';
import { mapDashboardSummary } from '../mappers/dashboardMapper';
import type { VolunteerAssignmentDto } from '../dto/eventDto';
import type { AttendanceHistoryItemDto } from '../dto/profileDto';

export class HttpDashboardRepository implements IDashboardRepository {
  private client: ApiClient;

  constructor(client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient) {
    this.client = client;
  }

  public async getDashboardSummary(_userId?: string): Promise<DashboardSummary> {
    const [assignmentsRes, attendanceRes, feedbackRes] = await Promise.all([
      eventApi.getMyAssignments(undefined, this.client),
      profileApi.getAttendanceHistory(this.client),
      profileApi.getFeedbackSummary(this.client),
    ]);

    if (!assignmentsRes.ok) {
      throw new Error(
        `Failed to load user assignments: ${assignmentsRes.error.message}`,
      );
    }

    if (!attendanceRes.ok) {
      throw new Error(
        `Failed to load attendance history: ${attendanceRes.error.message}`,
      );
    }

    const assignments: VolunteerAssignmentDto[] = Array.isArray(assignmentsRes.data)
      ? assignmentsRes.data
      : 'results' in assignmentsRes.data
      ? assignmentsRes.data.results
      : [];

    const attendance: AttendanceHistoryItemDto[] = Array.isArray(attendanceRes.data)
      ? attendanceRes.data
      : 'results' in attendanceRes.data
      ? attendanceRes.data.results
      : [];

    const feedback = feedbackRes.ok ? feedbackRes.data : null;

    return mapDashboardSummary(assignments, attendance, feedback);
  }

  public async cancelUpcomingParticipation(
    commitmentId: string,
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string; data?: DashboardSummary }> {
    const cancelRes = await eventApi.cancelAssignment(
      commitmentId,
      'Cancelled by volunteer',
      this.client,
    );

    if (!cancelRes.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: cancelRes.error.message,
      };
    }

    const updatedSummary = await this.getDashboardSummary();
    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Participation cancelled successfully',
      data: updatedSummary,
    };
  }
}

export const defaultHttpDashboardRepository = new HttpDashboardRepository();

/**
 * @format
 */

import { ApiClient, defaultApiClient } from '../../core/api/apiClient';
import type {
  EventApplicationState,
  EventDetail,
  EventFilters,
  EventSummary,
  VolunteerApplicationItem,
  VolunteerAssignmentItem,
} from '../../domain/models/event';
import type {
  EventApplyResult,
  EventRepository,
} from '../contracts/eventRepository';
import { eventApi, type EventQueryParams } from '../endpoints/eventApi';
import { checkinApi } from '../endpoints/checkinApi';
import {
  mapPublicEventDetail,
  mapPublicEventSummary,
  mapVolunteerApplication,
  mapVolunteerAssignment,
} from '../mappers/eventMapper';
import type {
  CharityFieldDto,
  PublicEventDto,
  PublicEventPostDto,
  VolunteerApplicationDto,
  VolunteerAssignmentDto,
} from '../dto/eventDto';

export class HttpEventRepository implements EventRepository {
  private client: ApiClient;

  constructor(client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient) {
    this.client = client;
  }

  public async getEvents(filters?: EventFilters): Promise<EventSummary[]> {
    const params: EventQueryParams = {};

    if (filters) {
      if (filters.query && filters.query.trim().length > 0) {
        params.search = filters.query.trim();
      }
      if (filters.charityFieldCodes && filters.charityFieldCodes.length > 0) {
        params.charity_field = filters.charityFieldCodes.join(',');
      }
      if (filters.statuses && filters.statuses.length === 1) {
        params.status = filters.statuses[0];
      }
      if (filters.mealsProvided === true) {
        params.meals_provided = 'true';
      }
      if (filters.lodgingProvided === true) {
        params.lodging_provided = 'true';
      }
      if (filters.travelSupportProvided === true) {
        params.travel_support_provided = 'true';
      }
      if (filters.startAfter) {
        params.starts_after = filters.startAfter;
      }
      if (filters.endBefore) {
        params.starts_before = filters.endBefore;
      }
    }

    const res = await eventApi.getEvents(params, this.client);
    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to load events: ${res.error.message}`);
    }

    const events: PublicEventDto[] = Array.isArray(res.data)
      ? res.data
      : 'results' in res.data
      ? res.data.results
      : [];

    return events.map(mapPublicEventSummary);
  }

  public async getEventById(id: string): Promise<EventDetail | null> {
    const hasToken = await this.client.hasValidToken();

    const [eventRes, postsRes, appsRes] = await Promise.all([
      eventApi.getEventDetail(id, this.client),
      eventApi.getEventPosts(id, this.client),
      hasToken
        ? eventApi.getMyApplications({ event_id: id }, this.client)
        : Promise.resolve({
            ok: true as const,
            status: 200,
            data: { results: [] as VolunteerApplicationDto[] },
          }),
    ]);

    if (!eventRes.ok) {
      if (eventRes.status === 404) {
        return null;
      }
      throw new Error(`Failed to load event: ${eventRes.error.message}`);
    }

    let posts: PublicEventPostDto[] = [];
    if (postsRes.ok) {
      posts = Array.isArray(postsRes.data)
        ? postsRes.data
        : 'results' in postsRes.data
        ? postsRes.data.results
        : [];
    } else if (eventRes.data.posts && eventRes.data.posts.length > 0) {
      posts = eventRes.data.posts;
    }

    if (posts.length > 0) {
      eventRes.data.posts = posts;
    }

    let userAppDto: VolunteerApplicationDto | null = null;
    if (appsRes.ok) {
      const apps: VolunteerApplicationDto[] = Array.isArray(appsRes.data)
        ? appsRes.data
        : 'results' in appsRes.data
        ? appsRes.data.results
        : [];

      userAppDto = apps.find(a => String(a.event_id) === String(id)) ?? null;
    }

    return mapPublicEventDetail(eventRes.data, userAppDto);
  }

  public async applyToPost(
    eventId: string,
    postId: string,
    note?: string,
  ): Promise<EventApplyResult> {
    const numericPostId = isNaN(Number(postId)) ? null : Number(postId);
    const res = await eventApi.applyToEvent(
      eventId,
      {
        event_id: Number(eventId),
        post_id: numericPostId,
        note: note ?? '',
        source: 'browse',
      },
      this.client,
    );

    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }

    const app = res.data;
    const applicationState: EventApplicationState = {
      id: String(app.id),
      eventId: String(app.event_id),
      postId: app.post_id ? String(app.post_id) : postId,
      postTitle: app.post_title || 'Applied Role',
      status: (app.status as any) || 'applied',
      appliedAt: app.applied_at || new Date().toISOString(),
      volunteerNote: app.note ?? note,
    };

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Application submitted successfully',
      application: applicationState,
    };
  }

  public async getUserApplications(): Promise<VolunteerApplicationItem[]> {
    const res = await eventApi.getMyApplications(undefined, this.client);
    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to load applications: ${res.error.message}`);
    }

    const apps: VolunteerApplicationDto[] = Array.isArray(res.data)
      ? res.data
      : 'results' in res.data
      ? res.data.results
      : [];

    return apps.map(mapVolunteerApplication);
  }

  public async withdrawApplication(
    applicationId: string,
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string }> {
    const res = await eventApi.withdrawApplication(applicationId, this.client);
    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Application withdrawn successfully',
    };
  }

  public async getUserAssignments(): Promise<VolunteerAssignmentItem[]> {
    const res = await eventApi.getMyAssignments(undefined, this.client);
    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to load assignments: ${res.error.message}`);
    }

    const assignments: VolunteerAssignmentDto[] = Array.isArray(res.data)
      ? res.data
      : 'results' in res.data
      ? res.data.results
      : [];

    return assignments.map(mapVolunteerAssignment);
  }

  public async confirmAssignment(
    assignmentId: string,
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string }> {
    const res = await eventApi.confirmAssignment(assignmentId, this.client);
    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Assignment confirmed successfully',
    };
  }

  public async cancelAssignment(
    assignmentId: string,
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string }> {
    const res = await eventApi.cancelAssignment(
      assignmentId,
      'Cancelled by volunteer',
      this.client,
    );
    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Assignment cancelled successfully',
    };
  }

  public async getCharityFields(): Promise<CharityFieldDto[]> {
    const res = await eventApi.getCharityFields(this.client);
    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to load charity fields: ${res.error.message}`);
    }

    return Array.isArray(res.data)
      ? res.data
      : 'results' in res.data
      ? res.data.results
      : [];
  }

  public async getEventOnboarding(eventId: string): Promise<{
    eventId: string;
    onboardingText: string | null;
    onboardingVersion: number;
    requireOnboardingAck: boolean;
    acknowledged: boolean;
  }> {
    const res = await checkinApi.getOnboarding(eventId, this.client);
    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to load onboarding briefing: ${res.error.message}`);
    }

    return {
      eventId: String(res.data.event_id),
      onboardingText: res.data.onboarding_text,
      onboardingVersion: res.data.onboarding_version,
      requireOnboardingAck: res.data.require_onboarding_ack,
      acknowledged: res.data.acknowledged,
    };
  }

  public async acknowledgeOnboarding(
    eventId: string,
    notes?: string,
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string; acknowledged: boolean }> {
    const res = await checkinApi.acknowledgeOnboarding(
      eventId,
      { notes },
      this.client,
    );
    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
        acknowledged: false,
      };
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Onboarding acknowledged successfully',
      acknowledged: res.data.acknowledged,
    };
  }

  public async submitGeoCheckIn(
    eventId: string,
    latitude: number,
    longitude: number,
    accuracyM?: number,
  ): Promise<{
    success: boolean;
    message: string;
    result?: string;
    distanceM?: number;
    confirmationStatus?: string;
    attendanceId?: string;
  }> {
    const res = await checkinApi.submitGeoCheckIn(
      {
        event_id: Number(eventId),
        latitude,
        longitude,
        accuracy_m: accuracyM ?? null,
      },
      this.client,
    );

    if (res.ok) {
      return {
        success: res.data.success ?? true,
        message: res.data.message || 'Geo check-in verified successfully.',
        result: res.data.result,
        distanceM: res.data.distance_m ?? undefined,
        confirmationStatus: res.data.confirmation_status ?? undefined,
        attendanceId: res.data.attendance_id
          ? String(res.data.attendance_id)
          : undefined,
      };
    }

    const error = res.error;
    const distanceM =
      error.kind === 'validation' &&
      error.meta &&
      typeof error.meta.distance_m === 'number'
        ? error.meta.distance_m
        : undefined;

    return {
      success: false,
      message: error.message,
      result: 'code' in error ? error.code : undefined,
      distanceM,
    };
  }

  public async redeemQrToken(token: string): Promise<{
    success: boolean;
    message: string;
    result?: string;
    eventId?: string;
    attendanceId?: string;
    applicationId?: string;
  }> {
    const res = await checkinApi.redeemQrToken(token, this.client);
    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
        result: 'code' in res.error ? res.error.code : undefined,
      };
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'QR token redeemed successfully.',
      result: res.data.result,
      eventId: res.data.event_id ? String(res.data.event_id) : undefined,
      attendanceId: res.data.attendance_id
        ? String(res.data.attendance_id)
        : undefined,
      applicationId: res.data.application_id
        ? String(res.data.application_id)
        : undefined,
    };
  }

  public async checkOutAttendance(
    attendanceId: string,
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string; checkedOutAt?: string }> {
    const res = await checkinApi.checkoutAttendance(attendanceId, this.client);
    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Shift checkout completed successfully.',
      checkedOutAt: res.data.checkout_at,
    };
  }

  public async submitEventFeedback(
    eventId: string,
    score: number,
    comment?: string,
    tags?: string[],
    direction:
      | 'coordinator_to_volunteer'
      | 'volunteer_to_event'
      | 'volunteer_to_coordinator' = 'volunteer_to_event',
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string; feedbackId?: string }> {
    const res = await checkinApi.submitEventFeedback(
      eventId,
      {
        direction,
        score,
        comment: comment || '',
        tags: tags || [],
      },
      this.client,
    );

    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Feedback submitted successfully.',
      feedbackId: String(res.data.id),
    };
  }
}

export const defaultHttpEventRepository = new HttpEventRepository();

/**
 * @format
 */

import { ApiClient, defaultApiClient } from '../../core/api/apiClient';
import type {
  AvailabilityException,
  VolunteerAvailability,
  WeeklyAvailabilityRule,
} from '../../domain/models/availability';
import type { CoordinatorStatus } from '../../domain/models';
import type { VolunteerProfile } from '../../domain/models/profile';
import type { VerificationCoordinatorData } from '../../domain/models/verification';
import type {
  CoordinatorEligibilityData,
  ProfileOperationResult,
  ProfileRepository,
} from '../contracts/profileRepository';
import { profileApi } from '../endpoints/profileApi';
import {
  formatToTimeWithSeconds,
  mapAvailability,
  mapAvailabilityRulesReplaceRequest,
  mapProfileUpdateRequest,
  mapUserProfile,
  mapVerificationCoordinatorData,
  normalizeTimeString,
} from '../mappers/profileMapper';
import type { AvailabilityExceptionDto } from '../dto/profileDto';

const WEEKDAY_NAMES = [
  '',
  'Monday',
  'Tuesday',
  'Wednesday',
  'Thursday',
  'Friday',
  'Saturday',
  'Sunday',
];

export class HttpProfileRepository implements ProfileRepository {
  private client: ApiClient;

  constructor(client: ApiClient = defaultApiClient) {
    this.client = client;
  }

  public async getProfile(): Promise<VolunteerProfile> {
    const [profRes, coordRes, feedbackRes, interestsRes] = await Promise.all([
      profileApi.getProfile(this.client),
      profileApi.getCoordinatorProfile(this.client),
      profileApi.getFeedbackSummary(this.client),
      profileApi.getCharityInterests(this.client),
    ]);

    if (!profRes.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to load profile: ${profRes.error.message}`);
    }

    if (interestsRes.ok && interestsRes.data.field_codes) {
      profRes.data.charity_interests = interestsRes.data.field_codes;
    }

    return mapUserProfile(
      profRes.data,
      coordRes.ok ? coordRes.data : null,
      feedbackRes.ok ? feedbackRes.data : null,
    );
  }

  public async updateProfile(
    updates: Partial<VolunteerProfile>,
  ): Promise<ProfileOperationResult<VolunteerProfile>> {
    const dto = mapProfileUpdateRequest(updates);

    if (updates.charityInterests) {
      const interestsRes = await profileApi.setCharityInterests(
        updates.charityInterests,
        this.client,
      );
      if (!interestsRes.ok) {
        return {
          success: false,
          message: `Failed to update charity interests: ${interestsRes.error.message}`,
        };
      }
    }

    if (Object.keys(dto).length > 0) {
      const updateRes = await profileApi.updateProfile(dto, this.client);
      if (!updateRes.ok) {
        return {
          success: false,
          message: updateRes.error.message,
        };
      }
    }

    const updatedProfile = await this.getProfile();
    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Profile updated successfully',
      data: updatedProfile,
    };
  }

  public async getAvailability(): Promise<VolunteerAvailability> {
    const res = await profileApi.getAvailability(this.client);
    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to load availability: ${res.error.message}`);
    }

    return mapAvailability(res.data);
  }

  public async saveWeeklyRule(
    rule: Omit<WeeklyAvailabilityRule, 'id'>,
  ): Promise<ProfileOperationResult<WeeklyAvailabilityRule>> {
    const curr = await this.getAvailability();
    const weekdayName =
      rule.weekdayName || WEEKDAY_NAMES[rule.weekday] || `Day ${rule.weekday}`;

    const normStart = normalizeTimeString(rule.startTime);
    const normEnd = normalizeTimeString(rule.endTime);
    const deterministicId = `${rule.weekday}-${normStart}-${normEnd}`;

    const newRule: WeeklyAvailabilityRule = {
      ...rule,
      weekdayName,
      startTime: normStart,
      endTime: normEnd,
      id: deterministicId,
    };

    const updatedRules: WeeklyAvailabilityRule[] = [
      ...curr.weeklyRules.filter(
        r => !(r.weekday === rule.weekday && r.startTime === normStart),
      ),
      newRule,
    ];

    const payload = mapAvailabilityRulesReplaceRequest(updatedRules);
    const res = await profileApi.replaceAvailabilityRules(payload, this.client);
    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Rule saved successfully',
      data: newRule,
    };
  }

  public async deleteWeeklyRule(
    ruleId: string,
  ): Promise<ProfileOperationResult> {
    const curr = await this.getAvailability();
    const remaining = curr.weeklyRules.filter(r => r.id !== ruleId);
    const payload = mapAvailabilityRulesReplaceRequest(remaining);
    const res = await profileApi.replaceAvailabilityRules(payload, this.client);

    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Rule deleted successfully',
    };
  }

  public async saveException(
    exception: Omit<AvailabilityException, 'id'>,
  ): Promise<ProfileOperationResult<AvailabilityException>> {
    const dto: AvailabilityExceptionDto = {
      date: exception.date,
      is_available: exception.isAvailable,
      start_time:
        exception.isAvailable && exception.startTime
          ? formatToTimeWithSeconds(exception.startTime)
          : null,
      end_time:
        exception.isAvailable && exception.endTime
          ? formatToTimeWithSeconds(exception.endTime)
          : null,
      note: exception.note ?? null,
    };

    const res = await profileApi.upsertAvailabilityException(dto, this.client);
    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Exception saved successfully',
      data: {
        ...exception,
        id: String(res.data.id || exception.date),
      },
    };
  }

  public async deleteException(
    exceptionId: string,
  ): Promise<ProfileOperationResult> {
    const res = await profileApi.deleteAvailabilityException(
      exceptionId,
      this.client,
    );
    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Exception deleted successfully',
    };
  }

  public async getVerificationStatus(): Promise<VerificationCoordinatorData> {
    const [verRes, coordRes] = await Promise.all([
      profileApi.getVerificationStatus(this.client),
      profileApi.getCoordinatorProfile(this.client),
    ]);

    if (!verRes.ok) {
      throw new Error(
        `Failed to load verification status: ${verRes.error.message}`,
      );
    }

    return mapVerificationCoordinatorData(
      verRes.data,
      coordRes.ok ? coordRes.data : null,
    );
  }

  public async getCoordinatorEligibility(): Promise<CoordinatorEligibilityData> {
    const [verRes, coordRes, attendanceRes, feedbackRes] = await Promise.all([
      profileApi.getVerificationStatus(this.client),
      profileApi.getCoordinatorProfile(this.client),
      profileApi.getAttendanceHistory(this.client),
      profileApi.getFeedbackSummary(this.client),
    ]);

    const verificationStatus = verRes.ok
      ? (verRes.data.verification_status as any) || 'unverified'
      : 'unverified';
    const isVerified =
      verificationStatus === 'approved' || verificationStatus === 'verified';

    const attendanceRecords =
      attendanceRes.ok && attendanceRes.data.results
        ? attendanceRes.data.results
        : [];
    const completedAttendance = attendanceRecords.filter(
      att => att.status === 'completed',
    );
    const completedAttendanceEventsCount = completedAttendance.length;
    const meetsCompletedEventsRequirement =
      isVerified || completedAttendanceEventsCount >= 5;

    const summary = feedbackRes.ok ? feedbackRes.data.summary : null;
    const ratingAvg =
      summary?.volunteer_avg_rating ?? summary?.overall_avg_rating ?? null;
    const ratingCount =
      summary?.volunteer_rating_count ?? summary?.total_count ?? 0;
    const meetsGoodRatingRequirement = isVerified
      ? true
      : ratingAvg !== null
      ? ratingAvg >= 4.0
      : null;

    let coordinatorStatus: CoordinatorStatus = 'none';
    if (coordRes.ok && coordRes.data.coordinator) {
      coordinatorStatus = coordRes.data.coordinator
        .status as CoordinatorStatus;
    }

    return {
      verificationStatus,
      isVerified,
      completedAttendanceEventsCount,
      meetsCompletedEventsRequirement,
      meetsGoodRatingRequirement,
      ratingAverage: ratingAvg,
      ratingCount,
      coordinatorStatus,
    };
  }

  public async submitCoordinatorApplication(
    note: string,
  ): Promise<ProfileOperationResult> {
    const res = await profileApi.applyForCoordinator(note, this.client);
    if (!res.ok) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: res.error.message,
      };
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Coordinator application submitted successfully',
    };
  }
}

export const defaultHttpProfileRepository = new HttpProfileRepository();

import {
  mapAttendanceRosterItem,
  mapCoordinatorApplication,
  mapCoordinatorAssignment,
  mapCoordinatorEventSummary,
  mapCreateEventDraftToRequest,
} from '../coordinatorMapper';
import type {
  AttendanceRosterDto,
  CoordinatorApplicationDto,
  CoordinatorAssignmentDto,
  CoordinatorEventListItemDto,
  EventSummaryViewDto,
} from '../../dto/coordinatorDto';
import type { CreateEventWizardDraft } from '../../../domain/models/coordinator';

describe('coordinatorMapper', () => {
  describe('mapCreateEventDraftToRequest', () => {
    it('correctly normalizes leaderStrategy and compensationKind enum choices', () => {
      const draft: CreateEventWizardDraft = {
        title: 'Community Garden Workshop',
        description: 'Hands-on organic gardening training',
        purposeType: 'charity_event',
        charityFieldCodes: ['environment', 'teaching'],
        locationName: 'People Park Greenhouse',
        landmarkText: 'West Entrance',
        latitude: 16.798,
        longitude: 96.145,
        geoRadiusM: 120,
        geoCheckinEnabled: true,
        geoCheckinOpensBeforeMin: 45,
        requireCheckinConfirmation: true,
        mapProvider: 'openstreetmap',
        startAt: '2026-12-15T08:00:00+06:30',
        endAt: '2026-12-15T12:00:00+06:30',
        applicationDeadline: '2026-12-10T18:00:00+06:30',
        acceptanceMode: 'manual',
        autoAssignEnabled: true,
        groupSize: 5,
        leaderStrategy: 'highest_rated', // Mobile enum to be translated
        maxParticipants: 30,
        mealsProvided: true,
        lodgingProvided: false,
        travelSupportProvided: true,
        compensationKind: 'stipend', // Mobile enum to be translated
        compensationNote: '50,000 MMK daily',
        onboardingText: 'Wear garden boots',
        requireOnboardingAck: true,
        qrRegistrationEnabled: true,
      };

      const requestDto = mapCreateEventDraftToRequest(draft);

      expect(requestDto.title).toBe('Community Garden Workshop');
      // Translated enum: 'highest_rated' -> 'first_experienced'
      expect(requestDto.leader_strategy).toBe('first_experienced');
      // Translated enum: 'stipend' -> 'arranged_outside_system'
      expect(requestDto.compensation_kind).toBe('arranged_outside_system');
      expect(requestDto.compensation_note).toContain('Stipend Support: 50,000 MMK daily');
      expect(requestDto.start_at).toBe('2026-12-15T08:00:00+06:30');
      expect(requestDto.end_at).toBe('2026-12-15T12:00:00+06:30');
      expect(requestDto.application_deadline).toBe('2026-12-10T18:00:00+06:30');
      expect(requestDto.charity_field_codes).toEqual(['environment', 'teaching']);
    });

    it('maps unpaid and none strategy without altering note', () => {
      const draft: CreateEventWizardDraft = {
        title: 'Simple Clean-up',
        purposeType: 'charity_event',
        charityFieldCodes: ['cleaning'],
        locationName: 'Downtown',
        latitude: 16.8,
        longitude: 96.1,
        geoRadiusM: 100,
        geoCheckinEnabled: false,
        geoCheckinOpensBeforeMin: 30,
        requireCheckinConfirmation: false,
        mapProvider: 'openstreetmap',
        startAt: '2026-12-01T09:00:00+06:30',
        endAt: '2026-12-01T11:00:00+06:30',
        acceptanceMode: 'auto',
        autoAssignEnabled: true,
        leaderStrategy: 'none',
        mealsProvided: false,
        lodgingProvided: false,
        travelSupportProvided: false,
        compensationKind: 'unpaid',
        requireOnboardingAck: false,
        qrRegistrationEnabled: false,
      };

      const requestDto = mapCreateEventDraftToRequest(draft);
      expect(requestDto.leader_strategy).toBe('none');
      expect(requestDto.compensation_kind).toBe('unpaid');
      expect(requestDto.compensation_note).toBeNull();
    });
  });

  describe('mapCoordinatorEventSummary', () => {
    it('maps CoordinatorEventListItemDto and binds metrics from summary view', () => {
      const itemDto: CoordinatorEventListItemDto = {
        id: 77,
        title: 'Disaster Relief Logistics',
        status: 'published',
        start_at: '2026-12-20T08:00:00Z',
        end_at: '2026-12-20T17:00:00Z',
        owner_id: 5,
        current_coordinator_id: 5,
        is_owner: true,
        is_current_coordinator: true,
        is_read_only_monitor: false,
        created_at: '2026-11-01T00:00:00Z',
      };

      const summaryDto: EventSummaryViewDto = {
        id: 77,
        title: 'Disaster Relief Logistics',
        status: 'published',
        start_at: '2026-12-20T08:00:00Z',
        end_at: '2026-12-20T17:00:00Z',
        acceptance_mode: 'manual',
        auto_assign_enabled: true,
        max_participants: 40,
        geo_checkin_enabled: true,
        map_provider: 'openstreetmap',
        pending_count: 12,
        accepted_count: 20,
        waitlisted_count: 3,
        assignment_count: 18,
        checked_in_count: 0,
        pending_confirmations: 0,
      };

      const summary = mapCoordinatorEventSummary(itemDto, summaryDto);

      expect(summary.id).toBe('77');
      expect(summary.title).toBe('Disaster Relief Logistics');
      expect(summary.isOwnerAsMonitor).toBe(false);
      expect(summary.pendingApplicationCount).toBe(12);
      expect(summary.acceptedApplicantCount).toBe(20);
      expect(summary.assignedCount).toBe(18);
      expect(summary.coordinatorName).toBe('You (Current Coordinator)');
    });
  });

  describe('mapCoordinatorApplication & mapCoordinatorAssignment', () => {
    it('maps CoordinatorApplicationDto and sets t2Capabilities when accepted', () => {
      const appDto: CoordinatorApplicationDto = {
        id: 401,
        user_id: 88,
        display_name: 'Ma Hla Hla',
        post_id: 10,
        post_title: 'Translator',
        source: 'browse',
        status: 'accepted',
        applied_at: '2026-11-12T10:00:00Z',
        decided_at: '2026-11-13T10:00:00Z',
        decided_by_id: 5,
        skills: ['English', 'Burmese'],
        has_leader_experience: true,
        physical_ok: true,
      };

      const item = mapCoordinatorApplication(appDto, '77', 'Relief Event');
      expect(item.id).toBe('401');
      expect(item.eventId).toBe('77');
      expect(item.eventTitle).toBe('Relief Event');
      expect(item.applicantName).toBe('Ma Hla Hla');
      expect(item.postTitle).toBe('Translator');
      expect(item.status).toBe('accepted');
      expect(item.t2Capabilities).toBeDefined();
      expect(item.t2Capabilities?.hasLeaderExperience).toBe(true);
      expect(item.t2Capabilities?.skills).toEqual(['English', 'Burmese']);
    });

    it('maps CoordinatorAssignmentDto to domain model', () => {
      const assignDto: CoordinatorAssignmentDto = {
        id: 601,
        user_id: 88,
        display_name: 'Ma Hla Hla',
        post_id: 10,
        post_title: 'Translator',
        group_code: 'GRP-LANG',
        is_group_leader: true,
        status: 'confirmed',
        assigned_by: 'system_auto',
        schedule_start: '2026-12-20T08:00:00Z',
        schedule_end: '2026-12-20T17:00:00Z',
        confirmed_at: '2026-11-15T10:00:00Z',
      };

      const item = mapCoordinatorAssignment(assignDto, '77', 'Relief Event');
      expect(item.id).toBe('601');
      expect(item.eventId).toBe('77');
      expect(item.eventTitle).toBe('Relief Event');
      expect(item.volunteerName).toBe('Ma Hla Hla');
      expect(item.isGroupLeader).toBe(true);
      expect(item.groupCode).toBe('GRP-LANG');
      expect(item.status).toBe('confirmed');
    });
  });

  describe('mapAttendanceRosterItem', () => {
    it('correctly translates pending confirmation status and geo metadata', () => {
      const rosterDto: AttendanceRosterDto = {
        assignment_id: 501,
        event_id: 77,
        user_id: 88,
        display_name: 'Ma Hla Hla',
        post_id: 10,
        post_title: 'Translator',
        group_code: null,
        is_group_leader: false,
        assignment_status: 'confirmed',
        attendance_id: 901,
        attendance_status: 'checked_in',
        method: 'geo',
        walk_in_flag: false,
        checkin_at: '2026-12-20T07:55:00Z',
        checkin_distance_m: 85.5,
        confirmation_status: 'pending', // Geo checkin awaiting coordinator review
      };

      const item = mapAttendanceRosterItem(rosterDto, 'Relief Event');
      expect(item.id).toBe('901');
      expect(item.status).toBe('pending_confirmation');
      expect(item.isPendingGeoConfirmation).toBe(true);
      expect(item.checkInMethod).toBe('geo');
      expect(item.geoAttempt).toBeDefined();
      expect(item.geoAttempt?.distanceMeters).toBe(85.5);
    });

    it('maps expected attendance status to scheduled', () => {
      const rosterDto: AttendanceRosterDto = {
        assignment_id: 502,
        event_id: 77,
        user_id: 89,
        display_name: 'Ko Min',
        post_id: 11,
        post_title: 'Logistics',
        group_code: null,
        is_group_leader: false,
        assignment_status: 'assigned',
        attendance_id: null,
        attendance_status: 'expected',
        method: null,
        walk_in_flag: false,
        checkin_at: null,
        checkin_distance_m: null,
        confirmation_status: null,
      };

      const item = mapAttendanceRosterItem(rosterDto);
      expect(item.id).toBe('502'); // falls back to assignment_id
      expect(item.status).toBe('scheduled');
      expect(item.isPendingGeoConfirmation).toBe(false);
    });
  });
});

/**

import { mapDashboardSummary } from '../dashboardMapper';
import type { AttendanceHistoryItemDto, FeedbackSummaryResponseDto } from '../../dto/profileDto';
import type { VolunteerAssignmentDto } from '../../dto/eventDto';

describe('dashboardMapper', () => {
  const sampleAssignments: VolunteerAssignmentDto[] = [
    {

    expect(summary.ratingAverage).toBe(4.9);
    expect(summary.ratingCount).toBe(8);
  });

  it('handles empty inputs gracefully with zero counts and null ratings', () => {
    const summary = mapDashboardSummary([], [], null);

    expect(summary.completedShiftCount).toBe(0);
    expect(summary.completedEventCount).toBe(0);
    expect(summary.upcomingCommitmentCount).toBe(0);
    expect(summary.upcomingCommitments).toEqual([]);
    expect(summary.recentCompletedActivities).toEqual([]);
    expect(summary.ratingAverage).toBeNull();
    expect(summary.ratingCount).toBe(0);
  });

import {

describe('eventMapper', () => {

import {
  formatToTimeWithSeconds,
  mapAvailability,
  mapAvailabilityRulesReplaceRequest,
  mapProfileUpdateRequest,

import type {
  AvailabilityResponseDto,

  VerificationStatusResponseDto,
} from '../../dto/profileDto';
import type { VolunteerProfile } from '../../../domain/models/profile';

describe('profileMapper', () => {
  describe('Time normalization helpers', () => {
    it('normalizes HH:mm:ss to HH:mm correctly', () => {
      expect(normalizeTimeString('08:30:00')).toBe('08:30');
      expect(normalizeTimeString('9:5:00')).toBe('09:05');
      expect(normalizeTimeString('18:00')).toBe('18:00');
      expect(normalizeTimeString(null)).toBe('00:00');
    });

    it('formats HH:mm to HH:mm:00 for TimeField backend storage', () => {
      expect(formatToTimeWithSeconds('08:30')).toBe('08:30:00');
      expect(formatToTimeWithSeconds('14:45:00')).toBe('14:45:00');
    });
  });

  describe('mapUserProfile', () => {
    const sampleUserDto: UserProfileDto = {
      id: 201,
      uuid: 'usr-uuid-201',
      display_name: 'Ko Thura',
      phone: '+95912345678',
      preferred_language: 'my',
      home_area: 'Kamayut, Yangon',
      emergency_contact_name: 'Daw Nu Nu',
      emergency_contact_phone: '+95987654321',
      blood_type: 'O+',
      is_willing_to_donate: true,
      is_willing_to_donate_blood: true,
      is_willing_to_donate_items: false,
      physical_ok: true,
      good_eyesight: true,
      needs_glasses: false,
      can_lift_heavy: true,
      can_work_outdoor: true,
      has_leader_experience: true,
      new_to_volunteer: false,
      skills: ['first aid', 'logistics'],
      needs_meal_support: false,
      needs_travel_support: true,
      accepts_unpaid: true,
      verification_status: 'verified',
      account_status: 'active',
      charity_interests: ['disaster_support', 'health'],
      created_at: '2026-01-10T08:00:00Z',
    };

    const sampleCoordEnvelope: CoordinatorProfileEnvelopeDto = {
      coordinator: {
        user_id: 201,
        status: 'approved',
        approved_by_id: 1,
        approved_at: '2026-03-01T10:00:00Z',
        notes: 'Experienced volunteer coordinator',
        created_at: '2026-02-15T09:00:00Z',
        updated_at: '2026-03-01T10:00:00Z',

    };

    const sampleFeedbackSummary: FeedbackSummaryResponseDto = {
      results: [],

        volunteer_avg_rating: 4.85,
        volunteer_rating_count: 14,

    it('maps UserProfileDto composite into domain VolunteerProfile', () => {
      const profile = mapUserProfile(
        sampleUserDto,

        sampleFeedbackSummary,
      );

      expect(profile.id).toBe('201');
      // Preserves phone under email property for UI backward compatibility
      expect(profile.email).toBe('+95912345678');
      expect(profile.displayName).toBe('Ko Thura');
      expect(profile.preferredLanguage).toBe('my');
      expect(profile.homeArea).toBe('Kamayut, Yangon');
      expect(profile.accountStatus).toBe('active');
      expect(profile.verificationStatus).toBe('verified');
      expect(profile.coordinatorStatus).toBe('approved');

      expect(profile.ratingCount).toBe(14);

      expect(profile.supportPreferences.needsTravelSupport).toBe(true);
      expect(profile.donationPreferences.bloodType).toBe('O+');

    it('handles null phone, coordinator envelope, and feedback metrics safely', () => {
      const userWithoutPhone: UserProfileDto = {
        ...sampleUserDto,
        phone: null,
      };

      const profile = mapUserProfile(userWithoutPhone, null, null);
      expect(profile.email).toBe('Verified Phone Account');

        homeArea: 'Bahan, Yangon',
        emergencyContactName: 'U Tin',
        emergencyContactPhone: '+95911112222',

          needsGlasses: true,

        },
        donationPreferences: {

          isWillingToDonateBlood: true,
          isWillingToDonateItems: true,
        },
      };

      const payload = mapProfileUpdateRequest(updates);

      // Verify exact allow-listed keys exist
      expect(payload.display_name).toBe('Thura Updated');
      expect(payload.preferred_language).toBe('en');
      expect(payload.home_area).toBe('Bahan, Yangon');
      expect(payload.emergency_contact_name).toBe('U Tin');
      expect(payload.emergency_contact_phone).toBe('+95911112222');
      expect(payload.skills).toEqual(['teaching', 'cooking']);
      expect(payload.needs_glasses).toBe(true);
      expect(payload.can_lift_heavy).toBe(false);
      expect(payload.needs_meal_support).toBe(true);
      expect(payload.blood_type).toBe('B+');
      expect(payload.is_willing_to_donate_blood).toBe(true);
      expect(payload.is_willing_to_donate).toBe(true);

      // Verify that non-allow-listed keys are NOT in the payload
      const payloadKeys = Object.keys(payload);
      expect(payloadKeys).not.toContain('id');
      expect(payloadKeys).not.toContain('email');
      expect(payloadKeys).not.toContain('account_status');
      expect(payloadKeys).not.toContain('verification_status');
      expect(payloadKeys).not.toContain('coordinator_status');
      expect(payloadKeys).not.toContain('charity_interests'); // handled via separate PUT endpoint
    });
  });

  describe('mapAvailability & mapAvailabilityRulesReplaceRequest', () => {
    it('maps AvailabilityResponseDto and normalizes time strings and weekday names', () => {
      const responseDto: AvailabilityResponseDto = {
        rules: [
          {
            id: 11,

          {
            id: 12,
            weekday: 6, // Saturday
            start_time: '13:30:00',
            end_time: '17:00:00',
          },
        ],
        exceptions: [
          {
            id: 21,
            date: '2026-12-25',
            is_available: false,
            note: 'Christmas blackout',
          },
        ],
      };

      const availability = mapAvailability(responseDto);
      expect(availability.weeklyRules).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(availability.weeklyRules[0].weekdayName).toBe('Monday');
      expect(availability.weeklyRules[0].startTime).toBe('08:00');
      expect(availability.weeklyRules[0].endTime).toBe('12:00');
      expect(availability.weeklyRules[1].weekdayName).toBe('Saturday');
      expect(availability.weeklyRules[1].startTime).toBe('13:30');

      expect(availability.exceptions).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(availability.exceptions[0].date).toBe('2026-12-25');
      expect(availability.exceptions[0].isAvailable).toBe(false);
      expect(availability.exceptions[0].note).toBe('Christmas blackout');
    });

    it('maps weekly rules to replacement DTO with seconds appended', () => {
      const rules = [
        {
          id: '1',
          weekday: 2,
          weekdayName: 'Tuesday',
          startTime: '09:00',
          endTime: '15:00',
        },
      ];

      const replaceDto = mapAvailabilityRulesReplaceRequest(rules);
      expect(replaceDto.rules).toEqual([
        {
          weekday: 2,
          start_time: '09:00:00',
          end_time: '15:00:00',
        },
      ]);
    });
  });

  describe('mapVerificationCoordinatorData', () => {
    it('maps verification response and translates "approved" image status to "verified"', () => {
      const verifDto: VerificationStatusResponseDto = {
        verification_status: 'verified',
        images: [
          {
            id: 301,
            image_type: 'mock_selfie',
            status: 'approved',
            is_current: true,
            reviewed_at: '2026-02-01T12:00:00Z',
            created_at: '2026-01-30T10:00:00Z',
          },
          {
            id: 302,
            image_type: 'id_license',
            status: 'pending',
            is_current: true,
            reviewed_at: null,
            created_at: '2026-02-01T08:00:00Z',
          },
        ],
      };

      const data = mapVerificationCoordinatorData(verifDto, null);
      expect(data.verificationStatus).toBe('verified');
      expect(data.canApplyForCoordinator).toBe(true);
      expect(data.imageSlots).toHaveLength(3);

      const selfieSlot = data.imageSlots.find(s => s.imageType === 'mock_selfie');
      expect(selfieSlot?.status).toBe('verified'); // mapped from 'approved'

      const idSlot = data.imageSlots.find(s => s.imageType === 'id_license');
      expect(idSlot?.status).toBe('pending');

      const driverSlot = data.imageSlots.find(s => s.imageType === 'driver_license');
      expect(driverSlot?.status).toBe('none'); // not uploaded
    });
  });
});

/**
 * @format
 */

import type {
  AttendanceRosterDto,
  CoordinatorApplicationDto,
  CoordinatorAssignmentDto,
  CoordinatorEventListItemDto,
  EventCreateRequestDto,
  EventSummaryViewDto,
} from '../dto/coordinatorDto';
import type {
  AttendanceRecordStatus,
  CheckInMethod,
  CoordinatorApplicationItem,
  CoordinatorApplicationStatus,
  CoordinatorAssignmentItem,
  CoordinatorAssignmentStatus,
  CoordinatorAttendanceItem,
  CoordinatorEventStatus,
  CoordinatorEventSummary,
  CreateEventWizardDraft,
  VolunteerT2Capabilities,
} from '../../domain/models/coordinator';

/**
 * Maps wizard draft to EventCreateRequestDto matching EventCreateSerializer.
 * Translates mobile domain choices into backend allow-listed serializer choices:
 * - leaderStrategy ('first_assigned' | 'highest_rated' -> 'first_experienced')
 * - compensationKind ('stipend' | 'travel_reimbursement' -> 'arranged_outside_system')
 */
export function mapCreateEventDraftToRequest(
  draft: CreateEventWizardDraft,
): EventCreateRequestDto {
  let leaderStrategy: 'none' | 'first_experienced' | 'manual' = 'none';
  if (
    draft.leaderStrategy === 'first_assigned' ||
    draft.leaderStrategy === 'highest_rated'
  ) {
    leaderStrategy = 'first_experienced';
  }

  let compensationKind: 'unpaid' | 'arranged_outside_system' = 'unpaid';
  let compensationNote: string | null = draft.compensationNote ?? null;

  if (
    draft.compensationKind === 'stipend' ||
    draft.compensationKind === 'travel_reimbursement'
  ) {
    compensationKind = 'arranged_outside_system';
    const kindLabel =
      draft.compensationKind === 'stipend'
        ? 'Stipend Support'
        : 'Travel Reimbursement';
    compensationNote = draft.compensationNote
      ? `${kindLabel}: ${draft.compensationNote}`
      : kindLabel;
  }

  return {
    title: draft.title.trim(),
    description: draft.description ? draft.description.trim() : null,
    purpose_type: draft.purposeType,
    location_name: draft.locationName ? draft.locationName.trim() : null,
    landmark_text: draft.landmarkText ? draft.landmarkText.trim() : null,
    latitude: typeof draft.latitude === 'number' ? draft.latitude : null,
    longitude: typeof draft.longitude === 'number' ? draft.longitude : null,
    geo_radius_m: draft.geoRadiusM ?? 100,
    geo_checkin_enabled: Boolean(draft.geoCheckinEnabled),
    geo_checkin_opens_before_min: draft.geoCheckinOpensBeforeMin ?? 30,
    require_checkin_confirmation: Boolean(draft.requireCheckinConfirmation),
    map_provider: draft.mapProvider === 'google' ? 'google' : 'openstreetmap',
    start_at: draft.startAt,
    end_at: draft.endAt,
    application_deadline: draft.applicationDeadline ?? null,
    acceptance_mode: draft.acceptanceMode,
    auto_assign_enabled: Boolean(draft.autoAssignEnabled),
    group_size: draft.groupSize ?? null,
    leader_strategy: leaderStrategy,
    max_participants: draft.maxParticipants ?? null,
    meals_provided: Boolean(draft.mealsProvided),
    lodging_provided: Boolean(draft.lodgingProvided),
    travel_support_provided: Boolean(draft.travelSupportProvided),
    compensation_kind: compensationKind,
    compensation_note: compensationNote,
    onboarding_text: draft.onboardingText ? draft.onboardingText.trim() : null,
    require_onboarding_ack: Boolean(draft.requireOnboardingAck),
    qr_registration_enabled: Boolean(draft.qrRegistrationEnabled),
    charity_field_codes: draft.charityFieldCodes || [],
  };
}

/**
 * Maps CoordinatorApplicationDto to domain CoordinatorApplicationItem.
 */
export function mapCoordinatorApplication(
  dto: CoordinatorApplicationDto,
  eventId?: string | number,
  eventTitle = 'Volunteer Event',
): CoordinatorApplicationItem {
  let source: 'browse' | 'qr_walkin' | 'invite' = 'browse';
  if (dto.source === 'qr_walkin' || dto.source === 'invite') {
    source = dto.source;
  }

  let t2Capabilities: VolunteerT2Capabilities | undefined = undefined;
  if (dto.status === 'accepted') {
    t2Capabilities = {
      skills: dto.skills || [],
      physicalOk: Boolean(dto.physical_ok),
      goodEyesight: true,
      needsGlasses: false,
      canLiftHeavy: Boolean(dto.can_lift_heavy),
      canWorkOutdoor: true,
      hasLeaderExperience: Boolean(dto.has_leader_experience),
      newToVolunteer: Boolean(dto.new_to_volunteer),
      preferredLanguage: 'en',
      homeArea: '',
      needsMealSupport: false,
      needsTravelSupport: false,
      acceptsUnpaid: true,
      verificationStatus: 'verified',
    };
  }

  return {
    id: String(dto.id),
    eventId: String(eventId ?? ''),
    eventTitle,
    userId: String(dto.user_id),
    applicantName: dto.display_name,
    status: dto.status as CoordinatorApplicationStatus,
    appliedAt: dto.applied_at,
    source,
    postId: dto.post_id !== null && dto.post_id !== undefined ? String(dto.post_id) : undefined,
    postTitle: dto.post_title ?? 'General Volunteer Support',
    t2Capabilities,
  };
}

 * Maps CoordinatorAssignmentDto to domain CoordinatorAssignmentItem.

export function mapCoordinatorAssignment(

    assignedAt: dto.confirmed_at ?? dto.schedule_start,

/**
 * Maps AttendanceRosterDto to domain CoordinatorAttendanceItem.
 */
export function mapAttendanceRosterItem(
  dto: AttendanceRosterDto,
  eventTitle = 'Volunteer Event',
): CoordinatorAttendanceItem {
  let status: AttendanceRecordStatus = 'scheduled';
  let isPendingGeoConfirmation = false;

  if (dto.confirmation_status === 'pending') {
    status = 'pending_confirmation';
    isPendingGeoConfirmation = true;
  } else if (dto.attendance_status === 'completed') {
    status = 'completed';
  } else if (dto.attendance_status === 'checked_in') {
    status = 'checked_in';
  } else if (dto.attendance_status === 'absent' || dto.attendance_status === 'excused') {
    status = 'no_show';
  } else {
    status = 'scheduled';
  }

  let checkInMethod: CheckInMethod | undefined = undefined;
  if (dto.method === 'geo' || dto.method === 'qr' || dto.method === 'manual') {
    checkInMethod = dto.method;
  }

  let geoAttempt = undefined;
  if (dto.checkin_distance_m !== null && dto.checkin_distance_m !== undefined) {
    geoAttempt = {
      attemptedAt: dto.checkin_at || new Date().toISOString(),
      withinGeofence: status !== 'pending_confirmation',
      distanceMeters: Number(dto.checkin_distance_m),
      confirmationStatus:
        dto.confirmation_status === 'confirmed' || dto.confirmation_status === 'rejected'
          ? (dto.confirmation_status as 'confirmed' | 'rejected')
          : ('pending' as const),
    };
  }

  return {
    id: String(dto.attendance_id ?? dto.assignment_id),
    eventId: String(dto.event_id),
    eventTitle,
    assignmentId: String(dto.assignment_id),
    userId: String(dto.user_id),
    volunteerName: dto.display_name,
    postTitle: dto.post_title ?? 'General Volunteer',
    groupCode: dto.group_code ?? undefined,
    status,
    checkInMethod,
    checkedInAt: dto.checkin_at ?? undefined,
    isPendingGeoConfirmation,
    geoAttempt,
  };
}

/**
 * @format
 */

import type {
  AttendanceHistoryItemDto,
  FeedbackSummaryResponseDto,
} from '../dto/profileDto';
import type { VolunteerAssignmentDto } from '../dto/eventDto';
import type {
  CompletedActivity,
  DashboardSummary,
  UpcomingCommitment,
} from '../../domain/models/dashboard';

/**
 * Computes verified hours from check-in and check-out timestamps.
 * Falls back to 3.0 hours if exact duration cannot be computed.
 */
function calculateVerifiedHours(
  checkinIso?: string | null,
  checkoutIso?: string | null,
  providedHours?: number | null,
): number {
  if (typeof providedHours === 'number' && providedHours > 0) {
    return Math.round(providedHours * 10) / 10;
  }
  if (checkinIso && checkoutIso) {
    const diffMs = new Date(checkoutIso).getTime() - new Date(checkinIso).getTime();
    if (!isNaN(diffMs) && diffMs > 0) {
      const hours = diffMs / (1000 * 60 * 60);
      return Math.round(hours * 10) / 10;
    }
  }
  return 3.0; // Standard shift default
}

/**
 * Maps assignments, attendance records, and feedback metrics into DashboardSummary.
 * Strictly enforces that completed activities are attendance-backed (`status === 'completed'`).
 */
export function mapDashboardSummary(
  assignments: VolunteerAssignmentDto[] = [],
  attendanceRecords: AttendanceHistoryItemDto[] = [],
  feedbackSummary?: FeedbackSummaryResponseDto | null,
): DashboardSummary {
  // Filter active upcoming shifts: 'assigned' or 'confirmed'
  const upcomingCommitments: UpcomingCommitment[] = assignments
    .filter(a => a.status === 'assigned' || a.status === 'confirmed')
    .map(a => ({
      id: String(a.id),
      eventId: String(a.event_id),
      eventTitle: a.event_title || 'Volunteer Event',
      roleName: a.post_title || 'Assigned Volunteer',
      startAt: a.schedule_start,
      endAt: a.schedule_end,
      location: 'Event Location',
      status: a.status === 'confirmed' ? 'confirmed' : 'assigned',
    }));

  // Filter completed activities: strictly status === 'completed'
  const completedAttendance = attendanceRecords.filter(
    att => att.status === 'completed',
  );

  const recentCompletedActivities: CompletedActivity[] = completedAttendance.map(
    att => {
      const verifiedAt =
        att.checkout_at || att.checkin_at || new Date().toISOString();
      const hours = calculateVerifiedHours(
        att.checkin_at,
        att.checkout_at,
        att.verified_hours,
      );

      return {
        id: String(att.id),
        eventId: String(att.event_id),
        eventTitle: att.event_title || 'Community Service Activity',
        roleName: att.post_title || 'Volunteer Contributor',
        completedAt: verifiedAt,
        attendanceStatus: 'completed',
        verifiedHours: hours,
        location: att.location_name || 'Designated Location',
        attendanceVerifiedAt: verifiedAt,
      };
    },
  );

  const completedShiftCount = completedAttendance.length;
  const uniqueEventIds = new Set(completedAttendance.map(att => att.event_id));
  const completedEventCount = uniqueEventIds.size;

  const summary = feedbackSummary?.summary;
  const ratingAverage =
    summary?.volunteer_avg_rating ??
    summary?.overall_avg_rating ??
    summary?.as_volunteer?.avg_overall ??
    null;

  const ratingCount =
    summary?.volunteer_rating_count ??
    summary?.total_count ??
    summary?.as_volunteer?.count ??
    0;

  return {
    completedShiftCount,
    completedEventCount,
    upcomingCommitmentCount: upcomingCommitments.length,
    ratingAverage,
    ratingCount,
    upcomingCommitments,
    recentCompletedActivities,
  };
}

/**
