import type {
  ApplicationSubmitRequestDto,
  PublicEventDetailDto,
  PublicEventDto,
  PublicEventPostDto,
  VolunteerApplicationDto,
  VolunteerAssignmentDto,
} from '../dto/eventDto';
import type {
  CharityCategoryCode,
  EventApplicationState,
  EventApplicationStatus,
  EventDetail,
  EventPost,
  EventStatus,
  EventSummary,
  PhysicalLevel,
  PostStatus,
  VolunteerApplicationItem,
  VolunteerAssignmentItem,
  VolunteerAssignmentStatus,
} from '../../domain/models/event';

/**
 * Normalizes event status to domain union 'published' | 'active'.
 */
function normalizeEventStatus(statusStr: string): EventStatus {
  if (statusStr === 'active') {
    return 'active';
  }
  return 'published';
}

/**
 * Normalizes post status to domain union 'open' | 'full' | 'closed'.
 */
function normalizePostStatus(statusStr: string): PostStatus {
  if (statusStr === 'full' || statusStr === 'closed') {
    return statusStr;
  }
  return 'open';
}

/**
 * Normalizes post physical level.
 */
function normalizePhysicalLevel(levelStr: string): PhysicalLevel {
  if (levelStr === 'light' || levelStr === 'medium' || levelStr === 'heavy') {
    return levelStr;
  }
  return 'none';
}

/**
 * Maps PublicEventDto to domain EventSummary model.
 */
export function mapPublicEventSummary(dto: PublicEventDto): EventSummary {
  return {
    id: String(dto.id),
    title: dto.title,
    description: dto.description ?? '',
    purposeType: dto.purpose_type,
    status: normalizeEventStatus(dto.status),
    locationName: dto.location_name ?? '',
    landmarkText: dto.landmark_text ?? undefined,
    startAt: dto.start_at,
    endAt: dto.end_at,
    applicationDeadline: dto.application_deadline ?? undefined,
    charityFields: (dto.charity_fields || []) as CharityCategoryCode[],
    coordinatorName: dto.coordinator_name ?? 'Coordinator',
    spotsRemaining: typeof dto.spots_remaining === 'number' ? dto.spots_remaining : 0,
    mealsProvided: Boolean(dto.meals_provided),
    lodgingProvided: Boolean(dto.lodging_provided),
    travelSupportProvided: Boolean(dto.travel_support_provided),
    compensationKind: dto.compensation_kind ?? 'unpaid',
  };
}

/**
 * Maps PublicEventPostDto to domain EventPost model.
 */
export function mapPublicEventPost(
  dto: PublicEventPostDto,
  eventId: string | number,
): EventPost {
  const reqHeadcount = typeof dto.required_headcount === 'number' ? dto.required_headcount : 1;
  const assigned = typeof dto.assigned_count === 'number' ? dto.assigned_count : 0;
  const rem = typeof dto.remaining === 'number' ? dto.remaining : Math.max(0, reqHeadcount - assigned);

  return {
    id: String(dto.id),
    eventId: String(eventId),
    title: dto.title,
    description: dto.description ?? undefined,
    requiredHeadcount: reqHeadcount,
    filledHeadcount: assigned,
    remainingCapacity: rem,
    physicalLevel: normalizePhysicalLevel(dto.physical_level),
    requiredSkills: Array.isArray(dto.required_skills) ? dto.required_skills : [],
    leaderNeeded: Boolean(dto.leader_needed),
    newVolunteersAllowed: Boolean(dto.new_volunteers_allowed),
    status: normalizePostStatus(dto.status),
  };
}

/**
 * Maps PublicEventDetailDto and optional user application to domain EventDetail model.
 */
export function mapPublicEventDetail(
  dto: PublicEventDetailDto,
  userApp?: VolunteerApplicationDto | null,
): EventDetail {
  const summary = mapPublicEventSummary(dto);
  const posts: EventPost[] = Array.isArray(dto.posts)
    ? dto.posts.map(p => mapPublicEventPost(p, dto.id))
    : [];

  let userApplication: EventApplicationState | null = null;
  if (userApp) {
    userApplication = {
      id: String(userApp.id),
      eventId: String(userApp.event_id),
      postId: String(userApp.post_id ?? ''),
      postTitle: userApp.post_title ?? 'General Volunteer Support',
      status: userApp.status as EventApplicationStatus,
      appliedAt: userApp.applied_at,
      volunteerNote: userApp.note ?? undefined,
    };
  }

  const lat =
    dto.latitude !== null && dto.latitude !== undefined && dto.latitude !== ''
      ? Number(dto.latitude)
      : undefined;

  const lng =
    dto.longitude !== null && dto.longitude !== undefined && dto.longitude !== ''
      ? Number(dto.longitude)
      : undefined;

  return {
    ...summary,
    landmarkText: dto.landmark_text ?? undefined,
    compensationNote: dto.compensation_note ?? undefined,
    onboardingText: dto.onboarding_text ?? undefined,
    latitude: !isNaN(lat as number) ? lat : undefined,
    longitude: !isNaN(lng as number) ? lng : undefined,
    geoCheckinEnabled: Boolean(dto.geo_checkin_enabled),
    posts,
    userApplication,
  };
}

/**
 * Maps VolunteerApplicationDto to domain VolunteerApplicationItem.
 */
export function mapVolunteerApplication(
  dto: VolunteerApplicationDto,
): VolunteerApplicationItem {
  return {
    id: String(dto.id),
    eventId: String(dto.event_id),
    eventTitle: dto.event_title ?? 'Volunteer Event',
    postId: String(dto.post_id ?? ''),
    postTitle: dto.post_title ?? 'General Volunteer Support',
    status: dto.status as EventApplicationStatus,
    appliedAt: dto.applied_at,
    volunteerNote: dto.note ?? undefined,
  };
}

/**
 * Maps VolunteerAssignmentDto to domain VolunteerAssignmentItem.
 */
export function mapVolunteerAssignment(
  dto: VolunteerAssignmentDto,
): VolunteerAssignmentItem {
  return {
    id: String(dto.id),
    eventId: String(dto.event_id),
    eventTitle: dto.event_title ?? 'Volunteer Assignment',
    postId: String(dto.post_id ?? ''),
    postTitle: dto.post_title ?? 'Assigned Shift',
    status: dto.status as VolunteerAssignmentStatus,
    startAt: dto.schedule_start,
    endAt: dto.schedule_end,
    location: 'Event Location',
    assignedAt: dto.confirmed_at ?? dto.schedule_start,
  };
}

/**
 * Generates an allow-listed ApplicationSubmitRequestDto payload.
 */
export function mapApplicationSubmitRequest(
  eventId: string | number,
  postId?: string | number | null,
  note?: string,
): ApplicationSubmitRequestDto {
  return {
    event_id: Number(eventId),
    post_id:
      postId !== undefined && postId !== null && postId !== ''
        ? Number(postId)
        : null,
    source: 'browse',
    note: note && note.trim().length > 0 ? note.trim() : null,
  };
}

import type {
  AvailabilityResponseDto,
  AvailabilityRulesReplaceDto,
  CoordinatorProfileEnvelopeDto,
  FeedbackSummaryResponseDto,
  ProfileUpdateRequestDto,
  UserProfileDto,
  VerificationStatusResponseDto,
} from '../dto/profileDto';
import type {
  AvailabilityException,
  VolunteerAvailability,
  WeeklyAvailabilityRule,
} from '../../domain/models/availability';
import type { CoordinatorStatus } from '../../domain/models';
import type {
  BloodType,
  UserLanguage,
  VolunteerProfile,
} from '../../domain/models/profile';
import type {
  VerificationCoordinatorData,
  VerificationImageSlot,
  VerificationImageType,
  VerificationStatus,
} from '../../domain/models/verification';

const WEEKDAY_NAMES: Record<number, string> = {
  1: 'Monday',
  2: 'Tuesday',
  3: 'Wednesday',
  4: 'Thursday',
  5: 'Friday',
  6: 'Saturday',
  7: 'Sunday',
};

/**
 * Trims PostgreSQL TimeField values ("08:00:00" -> "08:00").
 */
export function normalizeTimeString(timeStr?: string | null): string {
  if (!timeStr) {
    return '00:00';
  }
  const parts = timeStr.trim().split(':');
  if (parts.length >= 2) {
    return `${parts[0].padStart(2, '0')}:${parts[1].padStart(2, '0')}`;
  }
  return timeStr;
}

/**
 * Ensures TimeField string has seconds ("08:00" -> "08:00:00").
 */
export function formatToTimeWithSeconds(timeStr: string): string {
  const normalized = normalizeTimeString(timeStr);
  return `${normalized}:00`;
}

/**
 * Maps UserProfileDto composite into domain VolunteerProfile.
 * Note: Backwards compatibility - maps phone into email property so UI displays without change.
 */
export function mapUserProfile(
  userDto: UserProfileDto,
  coordinatorEnvelope?: CoordinatorProfileEnvelopeDto | null,
  feedbackSummary?: FeedbackSummaryResponseDto | null,
): VolunteerProfile {
  const coordStatus: CoordinatorStatus =
    (coordinatorEnvelope?.coordinator?.status as CoordinatorStatus) || 'none';

  const verifStatus =
    (userDto.verification_status as 'none' | 'pending' | 'verified' | 'rejected') || 'none';

  const acctStatus =
    (userDto.account_status as 'active' | 'blocked' | 'closed') || 'active';

  const summary = feedbackSummary?.summary;
  const ratingAverage =
    summary?.volunteer_avg_rating ??
    summary?.overall_avg_rating ??
    summary?.as_volunteer?.avg_overall ??
    null;

  const ratingCount =
    summary?.volunteer_rating_count ??
    summary?.total_count ??
    summary?.as_volunteer?.count ??
    0;

  return {
    id: String(userDto.id),
    email: userDto.phone ? userDto.phone : 'Verified Phone Account',
    accountStatus: acctStatus,
    verificationStatus: verifStatus,
    coordinatorStatus: coordStatus,
    isAdmin: false,
    isStaff: false,
    ratingAverage,
    ratingCount,

    displayName: userDto.display_name || 'Volunteer',
    preferredLanguage: (userDto.preferred_language as UserLanguage) || 'en',
    homeArea: userDto.home_area || '',
    emergencyContactName: userDto.emergency_contact_name || '',
    emergencyContactPhone: userDto.emergency_contact_phone || '',
    skills: Array.isArray(userDto.skills) ? userDto.skills : [],
    charityInterests: (userDto.charity_interests || []) as CharityCategoryCode[],

    capabilities: {
      physicalOk: Boolean(userDto.physical_ok),
      goodEyesight: Boolean(userDto.good_eyesight),
      needsGlasses: Boolean(userDto.needs_glasses),
      canLiftHeavy: Boolean(userDto.can_lift_heavy),
      canWorkOutdoor: Boolean(userDto.can_work_outdoor),
      hasLeaderExperience: Boolean(userDto.has_leader_experience),
      newToVolunteer: Boolean(userDto.new_to_volunteer),
    },

    supportPreferences: {
      needsMealSupport: Boolean(userDto.needs_meal_support),
      needsTravelSupport: Boolean(userDto.needs_travel_support),
      acceptsUnpaid: Boolean(userDto.accepts_unpaid),
    },

    donationPreferences: {
      bloodType: (userDto.blood_type as BloodType) || 'unknown',
      isWillingToDonateBlood: Boolean(userDto.is_willing_to_donate_blood),
      isWillingToDonateItems: Boolean(userDto.is_willing_to_donate_items),
    },
  };
}

/**
 * Maps partial VolunteerProfile updates to strict ProfileUpdateSerializer allow-list.
 * CRITICAL: Emits ONLY the 20 approved keys; any extraneous property causes HTTP 400.
 */
export function mapProfileUpdateRequest(
  updates: Partial<VolunteerProfile>,
): ProfileUpdateRequestDto {
  const payload: ProfileUpdateRequestDto = {};

  if (updates.displayName !== undefined) {
    payload.display_name = updates.displayName.trim();
  }
  if (updates.preferredLanguage !== undefined) {
    payload.preferred_language = updates.preferredLanguage;
  }
  if (updates.homeArea !== undefined) {
    payload.home_area = updates.homeArea.trim();
  }
  if (updates.emergencyContactName !== undefined) {
    payload.emergency_contact_name = updates.emergencyContactName.trim();
  }
  if (updates.emergencyContactPhone !== undefined) {
    payload.emergency_contact_phone = updates.emergencyContactPhone.trim();
  }
  if (updates.skills !== undefined) {
    payload.skills = updates.skills;
  }

  if (updates.capabilities) {
    if (updates.capabilities.physicalOk !== undefined) {
      payload.physical_ok = updates.capabilities.physicalOk;
    }
    if (updates.capabilities.goodEyesight !== undefined) {
      payload.good_eyesight = updates.capabilities.goodEyesight;
    }
    if (updates.capabilities.needsGlasses !== undefined) {
      payload.needs_glasses = updates.capabilities.needsGlasses;
    }
    if (updates.capabilities.canLiftHeavy !== undefined) {
      payload.can_lift_heavy = updates.capabilities.canLiftHeavy;
    }
    if (updates.capabilities.canWorkOutdoor !== undefined) {
      payload.can_work_outdoor = updates.capabilities.canWorkOutdoor;
    }
    if (updates.capabilities.hasLeaderExperience !== undefined) {
      payload.has_leader_experience = updates.capabilities.hasLeaderExperience;
    }
    if (updates.capabilities.newToVolunteer !== undefined) {
      payload.new_to_volunteer = updates.capabilities.newToVolunteer;
    }
  }

  if (updates.supportPreferences) {
    if (updates.supportPreferences.needsMealSupport !== undefined) {
      payload.needs_meal_support = updates.supportPreferences.needsMealSupport;
    }
    if (updates.supportPreferences.needsTravelSupport !== undefined) {
      payload.needs_travel_support = updates.supportPreferences.needsTravelSupport;
    }
    if (updates.supportPreferences.acceptsUnpaid !== undefined) {
      payload.accepts_unpaid = updates.supportPreferences.acceptsUnpaid;
    }
  }

  if (updates.donationPreferences) {
    if (updates.donationPreferences.bloodType !== undefined) {
      payload.blood_type = updates.donationPreferences.bloodType;
    }
    if (updates.donationPreferences.isWillingToDonateBlood !== undefined) {
      payload.is_willing_to_donate_blood =
        updates.donationPreferences.isWillingToDonateBlood;
    }
    if (updates.donationPreferences.isWillingToDonateItems !== undefined) {
      payload.is_willing_to_donate_items =
        updates.donationPreferences.isWillingToDonateItems;
    }
    // Set aggregate is_willing_to_donate if either is true
    payload.is_willing_to_donate = Boolean(
      payload.is_willing_to_donate_blood || payload.is_willing_to_donate_items,
    );
  }

  return payload;
}

/**
 * Maps AvailabilityResponseDto to domain VolunteerAvailability model.
 */
export function mapAvailability(
  dto: AvailabilityResponseDto,
): VolunteerAvailability {
  const weeklyRules: WeeklyAvailabilityRule[] = (dto.rules || []).map(r => {
    const normStart = normalizeTimeString(r.start_time);
    const normEnd = normalizeTimeString(r.end_time);
    const deterministicId =
      r.id != null ? String(r.id) : `${r.weekday}-${normStart}-${normEnd}`;

    return {
      id: deterministicId,
      weekday: r.weekday,
      weekdayName: WEEKDAY_NAMES[r.weekday] || `Day ${r.weekday}`,
      startTime: normStart,
      endTime: normEnd,
      validFrom: r.valid_from ?? undefined,
      validTo: r.valid_to ?? undefined,
    };
  });

  const exceptions: AvailabilityException[] = (dto.exceptions || []).map(e => ({
    id: String(e.id ?? `exc-${e.date}`),
    date: e.date,
    isAvailable: Boolean(e.is_available),
    startTime: e.start_time ? normalizeTimeString(e.start_time) : undefined,
    endTime: e.end_time ? normalizeTimeString(e.end_time) : undefined,
    note: e.note ?? undefined,
  }));

  return {
    weeklyRules,
    exceptions,
  };
}

/**
 * Maps domain weekly rules into AvailabilityRulesReplaceDto payload.
 */
export function mapAvailabilityRulesReplaceRequest(
  rules: WeeklyAvailabilityRule[],
): AvailabilityRulesReplaceDto {
  return {
    rules: rules.map(r => {
      const item: AvailabilityRulesReplaceDto['rules'][number] = {
        weekday: r.weekday,
        start_time: formatToTimeWithSeconds(r.startTime),
        end_time: formatToTimeWithSeconds(r.endTime),
      };
      if (r.validFrom !== undefined && r.validFrom !== null) {
        item.valid_from = r.validFrom;
      }
      if (r.validTo !== undefined && r.validTo !== null) {
        item.valid_to = r.validTo;
      }
      return item;
    }),
  };
}

/**
 * Normalizes backend verification image status to domain status.
 */
function normalizeVerificationStatus(statusStr: string): VerificationStatus {
  if (statusStr === 'approved' || statusStr === 'verified') {
    return 'verified';
  }
  if (statusStr === 'pending') {
    return 'pending';
  }
  if (statusStr === 'rejected') {
    return 'rejected';
  }
  return 'none';
}

/**
 * Maps VerificationStatusResponseDto and coordinator envelope to VerificationCoordinatorData.
 */
export function mapVerificationCoordinatorData(
  verificationDto: VerificationStatusResponseDto,
  coordinatorEnvelope?: CoordinatorProfileEnvelopeDto | null,
): VerificationCoordinatorData {
  const verifStatus = normalizeVerificationStatus(
    verificationDto.verification_status,
  );
  const coordStatus: CoordinatorStatus =
    (coordinatorEnvelope?.coordinator?.status as CoordinatorStatus) || 'none';

  const imageMap = new Map(
    (verificationDto.images || []).map(img => [img.image_type, img]),
  );

  const slotConfigs: Array<{
    imageType: VerificationImageType;
    label: string;
    description: string;
  }> = [
    {
      imageType: 'mock_selfie',
      label: 'Photo Selfie',
      description: 'Clear frontal photo of your face for on-site visual identity match.',
    },
    {
      imageType: 'id_license',
      label: 'NRC / Citizen ID',
      description: 'Official national identity card (front and back clearly legible).',
    },
    {
      imageType: 'driver_license',
      label: "Driver's License (Optional)",
      description: 'Required if volunteering for logistics or transport roles.',
    },
  ];

  const imageSlots: VerificationImageSlot[] = slotConfigs.map(cfg => {
    const record = imageMap.get(cfg.imageType);
    return {
      imageType: cfg.imageType,
      label: cfg.label,
      description: cfg.description,
      status: record ? normalizeVerificationStatus(record.status) : 'none',
      fileFormat: 'jpeg',
      uploadedAt: record ? record.created_at : undefined,
    };
  });

  return {
    verificationStatus: verifStatus,
    coordinatorStatus: coordStatus,
    canApplyForCoordinator: verifStatus === 'verified',
    imageSlots,
    coordinatorApplicationNote: coordinatorEnvelope?.coordinator?.notes ?? undefined,
  };
}

  public async createDraftEvent(
    draft: CreateEventWizardDraft,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorEventDetail>> {
    // 1. Validate charity categories count (must be between 1 and 3)
    if (
      !draft.charityFieldCodes ||
      draft.charityFieldCodes.length < 1 ||
      draft.charityFieldCodes.length > 3
    ) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Ensure between 1 and 3 charity fields are provided.',
      };
    }

    // 2. Validate schedule: end must be later than start
    const startTime = new Date(draft.startAt).getTime();
    const endTime = new Date(draft.endAt).getTime();
    if (isNaN(startTime) || isNaN(endTime) || endTime <= startTime) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Event end time must be later than start time.',
      };
    }

    if (draft.applicationDeadline) {
      const deadlineTime = new Date(draft.applicationDeadline).getTime();
      if (!isNaN(deadlineTime) && deadlineTime > startTime) {
        return {
          success: false,
          message:
            'Application deadline must be on or before the event start time.',
        };
      }
    }

    // 3. Validate geoRadius contract: 20 to 5000 meters
    if (
      draft.geoRadiusM !== undefined &&
      (draft.geoRadiusM < 20 || draft.geoRadiusM > 5000)
    ) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Geofence radius must be between 20 and 5000 meters.',
      };
    }

    // 4. Create draft event using dynamic coordinator fixture identity
    const newEvent: CoordinatorEventDetail = {
      id: this.idGenerator('evt-draft'),
      title: draft.title.trim(),
      description: draft.description?.trim() ?? '',
      status: 'draft', // Forced to draft
      purposeType: draft.purposeType,
      startAt: draft.startAt,
      endAt: draft.endAt,
      applicationDeadline: draft.applicationDeadline,
      locationName: draft.locationName.trim(),
      landmarkText: draft.landmarkText?.trim(),
      charityFields: [...draft.charityFieldCodes],
      ownerId: this.coordinatorIdentity.id,
      currentCoordinatorId: this.coordinatorIdentity.id,
      coordinatorName: this.coordinatorIdentity.displayName,
      isOwnerAsMonitor: false,
      pendingApplicationCount: 0,
      acceptedApplicantCount: 0,
      assignedCount: 0,
      checkedInCount: 0,
      latitude: draft.latitude,
      longitude: draft.longitude,
      geoRadiusM: draft.geoRadiusM,
      geoCheckinEnabled: draft.geoCheckinEnabled,
      geoCheckinOpensBeforeMin: draft.geoCheckinOpensBeforeMin,
      requireCheckinConfirmation: draft.requireCheckinConfirmation,
      mapProvider: draft.mapProvider,
      acceptanceMode: draft.acceptanceMode,
      autoAssignEnabled: draft.autoAssignEnabled,
      groupSize: draft.groupSize,
      leaderStrategy: draft.leaderStrategy,
      maxParticipants: draft.maxParticipants,
      mealsProvided: draft.mealsProvided,
      lodgingProvided: draft.lodgingProvided,
      travelSupportProvided: draft.travelSupportProvided,
      compensationKind: draft.compensationKind,
      compensationNote: draft.compensationNote?.trim(),
      onboardingText: draft.onboardingText?.trim(),
      onboardingVersion: 1, // Forced to 1
      requireOnboardingAck: draft.requireOnboardingAck,
      qrRegistrationEnabled: draft.qrRegistrationEnabled,
      posts: [],
    };

    this.events.unshift(newEvent);
    this.metrics.totalEventsCount += 1;
    this.metrics.draftEventsCount += 1;

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Demo only — not submitted to server',
      data: deepClone(newEvent),
    };
  }

  public async transitionEventStatus(
    eventId: string,
    targetStatus: CoordinatorEventStatus,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorEventDetail>> {
    const event = this.events.find(e => e.id === eventId);
    if (!event) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Event not found',
      };
    }

    // Owner-as-monitor guard: writes and transitions are disabled
    if (event.isOwnerAsMonitor) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          'Action disabled in read-only monitor mode. Coordination is held by another coordinator.',
      };
    }

    // Lifecycle state machine validation
    const current = event.status;
    const allowed = getAllowedTransitions(current);
    if (!allowed.includes(targetStatus)) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: `Invalid transition from ${current} to ${targetStatus}`,
      };
    }

    event.status = targetStatus;

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Demo only — not submitted to server',
      data: deepClone(event),
    };
  }

  public async getApplications(
    eventId?: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorApplicationItem[]> {
    const list = eventId
      ? this.applications.filter(a => a.eventId === eventId)
      : this.applications;
    return deepClone(list);
  }

  public async updateApplicationStatus(
    applicationId: string,
    status: CoordinatorApplicationStatus,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorApplicationItem>> {
    const app = this.applications.find(a => a.id === applicationId);
    if (!app) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Application not found',
      };
    }

    const event = this.events.find(e => e.id === app.eventId);
    if (event?.isOwnerAsMonitor) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          'Action disabled in read-only monitor mode. Coordination is held by another coordinator.',
      };
    }

    app.status = status;

    // Enforce privacy rule: T2 capabilities appear only for accepted applicants
    if (status === 'accepted') {
      if (!app.t2Capabilities) {
        app.t2Capabilities = {
          skills: ['general_assistance', 'community_outreach'],
          physicalOk: true,
          goodEyesight: true,
          needsGlasses: false,
          canLiftHeavy: false,
          canWorkOutdoor: true,
          hasLeaderExperience: false,
          newToVolunteer: false,
          preferredLanguage: 'my',
          homeArea: 'Yangon',
          needsMealSupport: true,
          needsTravelSupport: false,
          acceptsUnpaid: true,
          verificationStatus: 'verified',
        };
      }
    } else {
      // Remove T2 semi-private capabilities for pending/waitlisted/rejected applicants
      delete app.t2Capabilities;
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Demo only — not submitted to server',
      data: deepClone(app),
    };
  }

  public async getAssignments(
    eventId?: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorAssignmentItem[]> {
    const list = eventId
      ? this.assignments.filter(a => a.eventId === eventId)
      : this.assignments;
    return deepClone(list);
  }

  public async getUnassignedApplications(
    eventId?: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorApplicationItem[]> {
    const acceptedApps = this.applications.filter(
      a =>
        a.status === 'accepted' &&
        (!eventId || a.eventId === eventId) &&
        !this.assignments.some(
          asg => asg.applicationId === a.id && asg.status !== 'cancelled',
        ),
    );
    return deepClone(acceptedApps);
  }

  public async runAutoAssignment(
    eventId: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorAssignmentItem[]>> {
    const event = this.events.find(e => e.id === eventId);
    if (!event) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Event not found',
      };
    }

    if (event.isOwnerAsMonitor) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          'Action disabled in read-only monitor mode. Coordination is held by another coordinator.',
      };
    }

    const unassigned = await this.getUnassignedApplications(eventId);
    if (unassigned.length === 0) {
      return {
        success: true,
        message: 'All accepted volunteers are already assigned.',
        data: [],
      };
    }

    const created: CoordinatorAssignmentItem[] = [];
    unassigned.forEach((app, index) => {
      const newAsg: CoordinatorAssignmentItem = {
        id: this.idGenerator('asg-auto'),
        eventId: app.eventId,
        eventTitle: app.eventTitle,
        applicationId: app.id,
        userId: app.userId,
        volunteerName: app.applicantName,
        postId: app.postId ?? 'post-default',
        postTitle: app.postTitle ?? 'General Volunteer Support',
        status: 'assigned',
        groupCode: `GRP-${String.fromCharCode(65 + (index % 3))}`,
        isGroupLeader: index === 0,
        assignedAt: this.clock().toISOString(),
      };
      this.assignments.push(newAsg);
      created.push(newAsg);
    });

    event.assignedCount += created.length;

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Demo only — not submitted to server',
      data: deepClone(created),
    };
  }

  public async assignVolunteerManually(
    applicationId: string,
    postId: string,
    groupCode = 'GRP-MANUAL',
    isGroupLeader = false,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorAssignmentItem>> {
    const app = this.applications.find(a => a.id === applicationId);
    if (!app) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Application not found',
      };
    }

    const event = this.events.find(e => e.id === app.eventId);
    if (event?.isOwnerAsMonitor) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          'Action disabled in read-only monitor mode. Coordination is held by another coordinator.',
      };
    }

    if (app.status !== 'accepted') {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Only accepted applicants may be assigned to posts.',
      };
    }

    const newAsg: CoordinatorAssignmentItem = {
      id: this.idGenerator('asg-manual'),
      eventId: app.eventId,
      eventTitle: app.eventTitle,
      applicationId: app.id,
      userId: app.userId,
      volunteerName: app.applicantName,
      postId: postId,
      postTitle: app.postTitle ?? 'Volunteer Post',
      status: 'assigned',
      groupCode,
      isGroupLeader,
      assignedAt: this.clock().toISOString(),
    };

    this.assignments.push(newAsg);
    if (event) {
      event.assignedCount += 1;
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Demo only — not submitted to server',
      data: deepClone(newAsg),
    };
  }

  public async cancelAssignment(
    assignmentId: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorAssignmentItem>> {
    const asg = this.assignments.find(a => a.id === assignmentId);
    if (!asg) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Assignment not found',
      };
    }

    const event = this.events.find(e => e.id === asg.eventId);
    if (event?.isOwnerAsMonitor) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          'Action disabled in read-only monitor mode. Coordination is held by another coordinator.',
      };
    }

    if (asg.status === 'completed') {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Completed assignments cannot be cancelled.',
      };
    }

    asg.status = 'cancelled';
    if (event && event.assignedCount > 0) {
      event.assignedCount -= 1;
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Demo only — not submitted to server',
      data: deepClone(asg),
    };
  }

  public async getAttendanceRoster(
    eventId?: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorAttendanceItem[]> {
    const list = eventId
      ? this.attendance.filter(a => a.eventId === eventId)
      : this.attendance;
    return deepClone(list);
  }

  public async getPendingGeoConfirmations(
    eventId?: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorAttendanceItem[]> {
    const list = this.attendance.filter(
      a =>
        a.isPendingGeoConfirmation &&
        (!eventId || a.eventId === eventId),
    );
    return deepClone(list);
  }

  public async confirmGeoAttendance(
    attendanceId: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorAttendanceItem>> {
    const record = this.attendance.find(a => a.id === attendanceId);
    if (!record) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Attendance record not found',
      };
    }

    const event = this.events.find(e => e.id === record.eventId);
    if (event?.isOwnerAsMonitor) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          'Action disabled in read-only monitor mode. Coordination is held by another coordinator.',
      };
    }

    record.status = 'checked_in';
    record.isPendingGeoConfirmation = false;
    record.checkedInAt = this.clock().toISOString();
    if (record.geoAttempt) {
      record.geoAttempt.confirmationStatus = 'confirmed';
    }

    if (event) {
      event.checkedInCount += 1;
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Demo only — not submitted to server',
      data: deepClone(record),
    };
  }

  public async rejectGeoAttendance(
    attendanceId: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorAttendanceItem>> {
    const record = this.attendance.find(a => a.id === attendanceId);
    if (!record) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Attendance record not found',
      };
    }

    const event = this.events.find(e => e.id === record.eventId);
    if (event?.isOwnerAsMonitor) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          'Action disabled in read-only monitor mode. Coordination is held by another coordinator.',
      };
    }

    record.status = 'scheduled';
    record.isPendingGeoConfirmation = false;
    if (record.geoAttempt) {
      record.geoAttempt.confirmationStatus = 'rejected';
      record.geoAttempt.note = 'Rejected by coordinator in on-site verification.';
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Demo only — not submitted to server',
      data: deepClone(record),
    };
  }

  public async manualCheckIn(
    attendanceId: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult<CoordinatorAttendanceItem>> {
    const record = this.attendance.find(a => a.id === attendanceId);
    if (!record) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Attendance record not found',
      };
    }

    const event = this.events.find(e => e.id === record.eventId);
    if (event?.isOwnerAsMonitor) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message:
          'Action disabled in read-only monitor mode. Coordination is held by another coordinator.',
      };
    }

    record.status = 'checked_in';
    record.checkInMethod = 'manual';
    record.checkedInAt = this.clock().toISOString();
    record.isPendingGeoConfirmation = false;

    if (event) {
      event.checkedInCount += 1;
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Demo only — not submitted to server',
      data: deepClone(record),
    };
  }

  public async handoverEvent(
    eventId: string,
    newCoordinatorUserId: string,
    _reason?: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult> {
    const event = this.events.find(e => e.id === eventId);
    if (!event) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Event not found',
      };
    }

    event.currentCoordinatorId = newCoordinatorUserId;
    event.isOwnerAsMonitor = true;

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Event handed over successfully',
    };
  }

  public async retakeEvent(
    eventId: string,
    _reason?: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult> {
    const event = this.events.find(e => e.id === eventId);
    if (!event) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Event not found',
      };
    }

    event.currentCoordinatorId = event.ownerId;
    event.isOwnerAsMonitor = false;

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Event retaken successfully',
    };
  }

  public async createQrToken(
    eventId: string,
    purpose: 'walkin_registration' | 'checkin' | 'join_event',
    maxUses?: number | null,
    ttlMinutes: number = 240,
  ): Promise<
    CoordinatorOperationResult<{
      id: number;
      rawToken: string;
      expiresAt: string;
    }>
  > {
    const expiresAt = new Date(
      this.clock().getTime() + ttlMinutes * 60 * 1000,
    ).toISOString();
    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'QR token created successfully (Demo only)',
      data: {
        id: 1001,
        rawToken: `mock-qr-${eventId}-${purpose}-${Date.now()}`,
        expiresAt,
      },
    };
  }

  public async revokeQrToken(
    _qrId: string,
  ): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult> {
    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'QR token revoked successfully (Demo only)',
    };
  }
}

function getAllowedTransitions(
  status: CoordinatorEventStatus,
): CoordinatorEventStatus[] {
  switch (status) {
    case 'draft':
      return ['published', 'cancelled'];
    case 'published':
      return ['active', 'cancelled'];
    case 'active':
      return ['completed', 'cancelled'];
    case 'completed':
    case 'cancelled':
    default:
      return [];
  }
}

export const defaultCoordinatorRepository = new MockCoordinatorRepository();

import type {

export interface MockEventRepositoryOptions {
  idGenerator?: IdGenerator;
  clock?: Clock;
}

export class MockEventRepository implements EventRepository {
  private events: EventDetail[];
  private applications: VolunteerApplicationItem[];
  private assignments: VolunteerAssignmentItem[];
  private idGenerator: IdGenerator;
  private clock: Clock;
  private acknowledgedOnboarding: Set<string> = new Set();

  constructor(
    initialData: EventDetail[] = createEventsFixture(),
    initialApps: VolunteerApplicationItem[] = initialVolunteerApplications,
    initialAssignments: VolunteerAssignmentItem[] = initialVolunteerAssignments,
    options?: MockEventRepositoryOptions,
  ) {
    this.events = deepClone(initialData);
    this.applications = deepClone(initialApps);
    this.assignments = deepClone(initialAssignments);
    this.idGenerator = options?.idGenerator ?? createSequentialIdGenerator(2000);
    this.clock = options?.clock ?? (() => new Date());
  }

  public reset(
    data?: EventDetail[],
    apps?: VolunteerApplicationItem[],
    assignments?: VolunteerAssignmentItem[],
    options?: MockEventRepositoryOptions,
  ): void {
    this.events = deepClone(data ?? createEventsFixture());
    this.applications = deepClone(apps ?? initialVolunteerApplications);
    this.assignments = deepClone(assignments ?? initialVolunteerAssignments);
    if (options?.idGenerator) {
      this.idGenerator = options.idGenerator;
    } else {
      this.idGenerator = createSequentialIdGenerator(2000);
    }
    if (options?.clock) {
      this.clock = options.clock;
    }
  }

  public seed(data: {
    events?: EventDetail[];
    applications?: VolunteerApplicationItem[];
    assignments?: VolunteerAssignmentItem[];
    idGenerator?: IdGenerator;
    clock?: Clock;
  }): void {
    this.reset(data.events, data.applications, data.assignments, {
      idGenerator: data.idGenerator,
      clock: data.clock,
    });
  }

  public setIdGenerator(idGenerator: IdGenerator): void {
    this.idGenerator = idGenerator;
  }

  public setClock(clock: Clock): void {
    this.clock = clock;
  }

  public async getEvents(filters?: EventFilters): Promise<EventSummary[]> {
    let result = [...this.events];

    if (!filters) {
      return result.map(toSummary);
    }

    // 1. Search text filter (title, description, locationName)
    if (filters.query && filters.query.trim().length > 0) {
      const q = filters.query.trim().toLowerCase();
      result = result.filter(
        e =>
          e.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
          e.description.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
          e.locationName.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
          (e.landmarkText && e.landmarkText.toLowerCase().includes(q)),
      );
    }

    // 2. Charity category filter (match any of the selected codes)
    if (filters.charityFieldCodes && filters.charityFieldCodes.length > 0) {
      const selectedCodes = new Set(filters.charityFieldCodes);
      result = result.filter(e =>
        e.charityFields.some(code => selectedCodes.has(code)),
      );
    }

    // 3. Status filter (published or active)
    if (filters.statuses && filters.statuses.length > 0) {
      const statusSet = new Set(filters.statuses);
      result = result.filter(e => statusSet.has(e.status));
    }

    // 4. Meals filter
    if (filters.mealsProvided === true) {
      result = result.filter(e => e.mealsProvided);
    }

    // 5. Lodging filter
    if (filters.lodgingProvided === true) {
      result = result.filter(e => e.lodgingProvided);
    }

    // 6. Travel support filter
    if (filters.travelSupportProvided === true) {
      result = result.filter(e => e.travelSupportProvided);
    }

    return result.map(toSummary);
  }

  public async getEventById(id: string): Promise<EventDetail | null> {
    const found = this.events.find(e => e.id === id);
    if (!found) {
      return null;
    }
    return deepClone(found);
  }

  public async applyToPost(
    eventId: string,
    postId: string,
    note?: string,
  ): Promise<EventApplyResult> {
    const event = this.events.find(e => e.id === eventId);
    if (!event) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Event not found',
      };
    }

    const post = event.posts.find(p => p.id === postId);
    if (!post) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Selected role/post not found',
      };
    }

    if (post.status === 'closed') {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'This post is closed for applications',
      };
    }

    if (event.userApplication && event.userApplication.postId === postId) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'You have already applied to this role',
      };
    }

    const newApplication: EventApplicationState = {
      id: this.idGenerator('app-demo'),
      eventId,
      postId,
      postTitle: post.title,
      status: 'pending',
      appliedAt: this.clock().toISOString(),
      volunteerNote: note,
    };

    // Update local mock state
    event.userApplication = newApplication;
    if (post.remainingCapacity > 0) {
      post.filledHeadcount += 1;
      post.remainingCapacity = Math.max(
        0,
        post.requiredHeadcount - post.filledHeadcount,
      );
      if (post.remainingCapacity === 0) {
        post.status = 'full';
      }
    }
    event.spotsRemaining = Math.max(0, event.spotsRemaining - 1);

    // Also record in the volunteer's submitted applications list
    this.applications.unshift({
      id: newApplication.id,
      eventId: newApplication.eventId,
      eventTitle: event.title,
      postId: newApplication.postId,
      postTitle: newApplication.postTitle,
      status: newApplication.status,
      appliedAt: newApplication.appliedAt,
      volunteerNote: newApplication.volunteerNote,
    });

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Demo only — not submitted to server',
      application: newApplication,
    };
  }

  public async getUserApplications(): Promise<VolunteerApplicationItem[]> {
    return deepClone(this.applications);
  }

  public async withdrawApplication(
    applicationId: string,
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string }> {
    const app = this.applications.find(a => a.id === applicationId);
    if (!app) {
      return { success: false, message: 'Application not found' };
    }
    if (app.status === 'withdrawn') {
      return { success: false, message: 'Application is already withdrawn' };
    }

    app.status = 'withdrawn';

    // Synchronize event.userApplication if matching
    const event = this.events.find(e => e.id === app.eventId);
    if (event?.userApplication && event.userApplication.id === applicationId) {
      event.userApplication.status = 'withdrawn';
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Application withdrawn locally (Demo only — not submitted to server)',
    };
  }

  public async getUserAssignments(): Promise<VolunteerAssignmentItem[]> {
    return deepClone(this.assignments);
  }

  public async confirmAssignment(
    assignmentId: string,
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string }> {
    const asgn = this.assignments.find(a => a.id === assignmentId);
    if (!asgn) {
      return { success: false, message: 'Assignment not found' };
    }

    if (asgn.status !== 'assigned') {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: `Cannot confirm an assignment with status '${asgn.status}'.`,
      };
    }

    asgn.status = 'confirmed';

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Assignment confirmed locally (Demo only — not submitted to server)',
    };
  }

  public async cancelAssignment(
    assignmentId: string,
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string }> {
    const asgn = this.assignments.find(a => a.id === assignmentId);
    if (!asgn) {
      return { success: false, message: 'Assignment not found' };
    }

    // Completed participation cannot be cancelled per business rules
    if (asgn.status === 'completed') {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Completed participation cannot be cancelled.',
      };
    }

    if (asgn.status === 'cancelled') {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Assignment is already cancelled',
      };
    }

    asgn.status = 'cancelled';

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Participation cancelled locally (Demo only — not submitted to server)',
    };
  }

  public async getEventOnboarding(eventId: string): Promise<{
    eventId: string;
    onboardingText: string | null;
    onboardingVersion: number;
    requireOnboardingAck: boolean;
    acknowledged: boolean;
  }> {
    const ev = this.events.find(e => e.id === eventId);
    return {
      eventId,
      onboardingText:
        ev?.onboardingText ??
        'Please review the event mission, safety instructions, and gear requirements before reporting for your shift.',
      onboardingVersion: 1,
      requireOnboardingAck: true,
      acknowledged: this.acknowledgedOnboarding.has(eventId),
    };
  }

  public async acknowledgeOnboarding(
    eventId: string,
    _notes?: string,
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string; acknowledged: boolean }> {
    this.acknowledgedOnboarding.add(eventId);
    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Onboarding acknowledged locally (Demo only)',
      acknowledged: true,
    };
  }

  public async submitGeoCheckIn(
    eventId: string,
    _latitude: number,
    _longitude: number,
    _accuracyM?: number,
  ): Promise<{
    success: boolean;
    message: string;
    result?: string;
    distanceM?: number;
    confirmationStatus?: string;
    attendanceId?: string;
  }> {
    const asgn = this.assignments.find(
      a => a.eventId === eventId && (a.status === 'confirmed' || a.status === 'assigned'),
    );
    if (asgn) {
      asgn.attendanceStatus = 'checked_in';
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Geo check-in verified successfully within event geofence.',
      result: 'accepted',
      distanceM: 32,
      confirmationStatus: 'confirmed',
      attendanceId: 'att-mock-geo-01',
    };
  }

  public async redeemQrToken(token: string): Promise<{
    success: boolean;
    message: string;
    result?: string;
    eventId?: string;
    attendanceId?: string;
    applicationId?: string;
  }> {
    if (!token || !token.trim()) {
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Invalid or empty QR token provided.',
      };
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'QR code verified. Shift check-in completed.',
      result: 'checked_in',
      eventId: 'evt-101',
      attendanceId: 'att-mock-qr-01',
    };
  }

  public async checkOutAttendance(
    attendanceId: string,
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string; checkedOutAt?: string }> {
    const asgn = this.assignments.find(a => a.id === attendanceId || a.attendanceStatus === 'checked_in');
    if (asgn) {
      asgn.status = 'completed';
      asgn.attendanceStatus = 'completed';
      asgn.verifiedHours = 4.0;
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Checked out of volunteer shift successfully.',
      checkedOutAt: this.clock().toISOString(),
    };
  }

  public async submitEventFeedback(
    _eventId: string,
    score: number,
    _comment?: string,
    _tags?: string[],
    _direction?:
      | 'coordinator_to_volunteer'
      | 'volunteer_to_event'
      | 'volunteer_to_coordinator',
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string; feedbackId?: string }> {
    return {
      success: true,
      message: `Thank you for rating your shift (${score}/5 stars)! Feedback recorded.`,
      feedbackId: this.idGenerator('fb'),
    };
  }
}

function toSummary(event: EventDetail): EventSummary {
  return {
    id: event.id,
    title: event.title,
    description: event.description,
    purposeType: event.purposeType,
    status: event.status,
    locationName: event.locationName,
    landmarkText: event.landmarkText,
    startAt: event.startAt,
    endAt: event.endAt,
    applicationDeadline: event.applicationDeadline,
    charityFields: [...event.charityFields],
    coordinatorName: event.coordinatorName,
    spotsRemaining: event.spotsRemaining,
    mealsProvided: event.mealsProvided,
    lodgingProvided: event.lodgingProvided,
    travelSupportProvided: event.travelSupportProvided,
    compensationKind: event.compensationKind,
  };
}

/**

export type CharityCategoryCode =
  | 'teaching'
  | 'food_distribution'
  | 'elderly_care'
  | 'child_care'
  | 'animal_welfare'
  | 'environment'
  | 'disaster_support'
  | 'blood_donation'
  | 'fundraising'
  | 'logistics'
  | 'cleaning'
  | 'cooking'
  | 'other';

export interface CharityCategory {
  code: CharityCategoryCode;
  label: string;
  sortOrder: number;
}

  { code: 'other', label: 'Other', sortOrder: 999 },

export type EventStatus = 'published' | 'active';

export type PhysicalLevel = 'none' | 'light' | 'medium' | 'heavy';

export type PostStatus = 'open' | 'full' | 'closed';

export type EventApplicationStatus =
  | 'pending'
  | 'accepted'
  | 'waitlisted'
  | 'rejected'
  | 'withdrawn'
  | 'expired';

export interface EventPost {
  id: string;
  eventId: string;
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  requiredHeadcount: number;
  filledHeadcount: number;
  remainingCapacity: number;
  physicalLevel: PhysicalLevel;
  requiredSkills: string[];
  leaderNeeded: boolean;
  newVolunteersAllowed: boolean;
  status: PostStatus;
}

export interface EventSummary {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  purposeType: 'charity_event' | 'help_wanted_event';
  status: EventStatus;
  locationName: string;
  landmarkText?: string;
  startAt: string;
  endAt: string;
  applicationDeadline?: string;
  charityFields: CharityCategoryCode[];
  coordinatorName: string;
  spotsRemaining: number;
  mealsProvided: boolean;
  lodgingProvided: boolean;
  travelSupportProvided: boolean;
  compensationKind: string;
}

export interface EventApplicationState {
  id: string;
  eventId: string;
  postId: string;
  postTitle: string;
  status: EventApplicationStatus;
  appliedAt: string;
  volunteerNote?: string;
}

export interface EventDetail extends EventSummary {
  landmarkText?: string;
  compensationNote?: string;
  onboardingText?: string;
  latitude?: number;
  longitude?: number;
  geoCheckinEnabled: boolean;
  posts: EventPost[];
  userApplication?: EventApplicationState | null;
}

export interface EventFilters {
  query?: string;
  charityFieldCodes?: CharityCategoryCode[];
  statuses?: EventStatus[];
  mealsProvided?: boolean;
  lodgingProvided?: boolean;
  travelSupportProvided?: boolean;
  startAfter?: string;
  endBefore?: string;
}

export interface VolunteerApplicationItem {
  id: string;
  eventId: string;
  eventTitle: string;
  postId: string;
  postTitle: string;
  status: EventApplicationStatus;
  appliedAt: string;
  volunteerNote?: string;
}

export type VolunteerAssignmentStatus =
  | 'assigned'
  | 'confirmed'
  | 'completed'
  | 'cancelled';

export interface VolunteerAssignmentItem {
  id: string;
  eventId: string;
  eventTitle: string;
  postId: string;
  postTitle: string;
  status: VolunteerAssignmentStatus;
  startAt: string;
  endAt: string;
  location: string;
  assignedAt: string;
  attendanceStatus?:
    | 'scheduled'
    | 'checked_in'
    | 'pending_confirmation'
    | 'completed'
    | 'absent'
    | 'excused';
  verifiedHours?: number;
}

  } as any;
}

const mockProfileDto: UserProfileDto = {
  id: 42,
  uuid: 'uuid-42',
  display_name: 'Test Live Volunteer',
  phone: '+95912345678',
  preferred_language: 'my',
  home_area: 'Kamaryut, Yangon',
  emergency_contact_name: null,
  emergency_contact_phone: null,
  blood_type: 'O+',
  is_willing_to_donate: true,
  is_willing_to_donate_blood: true,
  is_willing_to_donate_items: false,
  physical_ok: true,
  good_eyesight: true,
  needs_glasses: false,
  can_lift_heavy: true,
  can_work_outdoor: true,
  has_leader_experience: false,
  new_to_volunteer: false,
  skills: ['first_aid'],
  needs_meal_support: false,
  needs_travel_support: false,
  accepts_unpaid: true,
  verification_status: 'approved',
  account_status: 'active',
  charity_interests: [],
  created_at: '2025-01-01T00:00:00Z',
};

describe('Phase 4: Authentication & Session Integration', () => {
  let memoryStorage: MemoryTokenStore;
  let root: ReactTestRenderer.ReactTestRenderer | null = null;

  beforeEach(() => {
    act(() => {
      resetDataMode();
    });
    memoryStorage = new MemoryTokenStore();
    defaultTokenStore.setCustomStorage(memoryStorage);
    sessionManager.setTokenStore(memoryStorage);
    jest.clearAllMocks();

    jest.spyOn(profileApi, 'getProfile').mockResolvedValue({
      ok: true,
      status: 200,
      data: mockProfileDto,
    });
    jest.spyOn(profileApi, 'getCoordinatorProfile').mockResolvedValue({
      ok: true,
      status: 200,
      data: { coordinator: null },
    });
  });

  afterEach(async () => {
    if (root) {
      await act(async () => {
        root?.unmount();
      });
      root = null;
    }
    act(() => {
      resetDataMode();
    });
    await memoryStorage.clearToken();
    jest.restoreAllMocks();
  });

  describe('OtpLoginScreen Component UI & Step Flow', () => {
    it('renders Step 1 with phone input, optional display name, and send code button', async () => {
      await act(async () => {
        root = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          renderWithTheme(<OtpLoginScreen />),
        );
      });

      const rootInstance = root!.root;
      const inputs = rootInstance.findAllByType(AppInput);
      expect(inputs.length).toBe(2);

      const header = rootInstance.findByType(AppHeader);
      expect(header.props.title).toBe('Workshift Sign In');

      const submitButton = rootInstance.findByType(AppButton);
      expect(submitButton.props.label).toBe('Send Verification Code');
    });

    it('shows validation error when attempting to send OTP with empty or default phone', async () => {
      await act(async () => {
        root = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          renderWithTheme(<OtpLoginScreen />),
        );
      });

      const rootInstance = root!.root;
      const phoneInput = rootInstance.findAllByType(AppInput)[0];
      const submitButton = rootInstance.findByType(AppButton);

      // Default value is '+959'
      await act(async () => {
        await submitButton.props.onPress();
      });

      const errorText = rootInstance.findAllByType(AppText).find(t =>
        t.props.children?.toString().includes('Please enter your mobile phone number.'),
      );
      expect(errorText).toBeDefined();

      // Setting empty string
      await act(async () => {
        phoneInput.props.onChangeText('');
      });
      await act(async () => {
        await submitButton.props.onPress();
      });

      const errorTextEmpty = rootInstance.findAllByType(AppText).find(t =>
        t.props.children?.toString().includes('Please enter your mobile phone number.'),
      );
      expect(errorTextEmpty).toBeDefined();
    });

    it('submits phone to requestOtp and transitions to Step 2 with dev code banner', async () => {
      const mockRequestOtp = jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({
        success: true,
        devCode: '123456',
      });

      const mockContext: FullUserContextValue = {
        currentUser: ordinaryVolunteerUser,
        setCurrentUser: jest.fn(),
        isCoordinator: false,
        switchUserPreset: jest.fn(),
        isAuthenticated: false,
        isBootstrapping: false,
        requestOtp: mockRequestOtp,
        verifyOtp: jest.fn(),
        logout: jest.fn(),
        refreshSessionUser: jest.fn(),
      };

      await act(async () => {
        root = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          renderWithTheme(
            <UserContext.Provider value={mockContext}>
              <OtpLoginScreen />
            </UserContext.Provider>,
          ),
        );
      });

      const rootInstance = root!.root;
      const phoneInput = rootInstance.findAllByType(AppInput)[0];
      const submitButton = rootInstance.findByType(AppButton);

      await act(async () => {
        phoneInput.props.onChangeText('+959111222333');
      });

      await act(async () => {
        await submitButton.props.onPress();
      });

      expect(mockRequestOtp).toHaveBeenCalledWith('+959111222333', 'login');

      // Now on Step 2
      const header = rootInstance.findByType(AppHeader);
      expect(header.props.title).toBe('Verify Code');

      // Dev code badge should appear
      const devCodeText = rootInstance.findAllByType(AppText).find(t =>
        t.props.children?.toString().includes('123456'),
      );
      expect(devCodeText).toBeDefined();
    });

    it('allows navigating back from Step 2 to Step 1 via AppHeader back button', async () => {
      const mockRequestOtp = jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({
        success: true,
      });

      const mockContext: FullUserContextValue = {
        currentUser: ordinaryVolunteerUser,
        setCurrentUser: jest.fn(),
        isCoordinator: false,
        switchUserPreset: jest.fn(),
        isAuthenticated: false,
        isBootstrapping: false,
        requestOtp: mockRequestOtp,
        verifyOtp: jest.fn(),
        logout: jest.fn(),
        refreshSessionUser: jest.fn(),
      };

      await act(async () => {
        root = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          renderWithTheme(
            <UserContext.Provider value={mockContext}>
              <OtpLoginScreen />
            </UserContext.Provider>,
          ),
        );
      });

      const rootInstance = root!.root;
      const phoneInput = rootInstance.findAllByType(AppInput)[0];
      await act(async () => {
        phoneInput.props.onChangeText('+959999888777');
      });
      await act(async () => {
        await rootInstance.findByType(AppButton).props.onPress();
      });

      expect(rootInstance.findByType(AppHeader).props.title).toBe('Verify Code');

      // Tap header back
      await act(async () => {
        rootInstance.findByType(AppHeader).props.onBackPress();
      });

      expect(rootInstance.findByType(AppHeader).props.title).toBe('Workshift Sign In');
    });

    it('validates OTP code and submits verifyOtp successfully', async () => {
      const mockVerifyOtp = jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({
        success: true,
      });
      const onLoginSuccess = jest.fn();

      const mockContext: FullUserContextValue = {
        currentUser: ordinaryVolunteerUser,
        setCurrentUser: jest.fn(),
        isCoordinator: false,
        switchUserPreset: jest.fn(),
        isAuthenticated: false,
        isBootstrapping: false,
        requestOtp: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({ success: true }),
        verifyOtp: mockVerifyOtp,
        logout: jest.fn(),
        refreshSessionUser: jest.fn(),
      };

      await act(async () => {
        root = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          renderWithTheme(
            <UserContext.Provider value={mockContext}>
              <OtpLoginScreen onLoginSuccess={onLoginSuccess} />
            </UserContext.Provider>,
          ),
        );
      });

      const rootInstance = root!.root;
      const phoneInput = rootInstance.findAllByType(AppInput)[0];
      const displayNameInput = rootInstance.findAllByType(AppInput)[1];

      await act(async () => {
        phoneInput.props.onChangeText('+95912345678');
        displayNameInput.props.onChangeText('Aung Ko');
      });

      await act(async () => {
        await rootInstance.findByType(AppButton).props.onPress();
      });

      // Step 2: enter code
      const codeInput = rootInstance.findByType(AppInput);
      await act(async () => {
        codeInput.props.onChangeText('654321');
      });

      const verifyButton = rootInstance
        .findAllByType(AppButton)
        .find(b => b.props.label === 'Verify & Continue')!;

      await act(async () => {
        await verifyButton.props.onPress();
      });

      expect(mockVerifyOtp).toHaveBeenCalledWith('+95912345678', '654321', 'Aung Ko');
      expect(onLoginSuccess).toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('shows error banner when verifyOtp returns failure', async () => {
      const mockVerifyOtp = jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({
        success: false,
        error: 'Invalid or expired OTP code',
      });

      const mockContext: FullUserContextValue = {
        currentUser: ordinaryVolunteerUser,
        setCurrentUser: jest.fn(),
        isCoordinator: false,
        switchUserPreset: jest.fn(),
        isAuthenticated: false,
        isBootstrapping: false,
        requestOtp: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({ success: true }),
        verifyOtp: mockVerifyOtp,
        logout: jest.fn(),
        refreshSessionUser: jest.fn(),
      };

      await act(async () => {
        root = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          renderWithTheme(
            <UserContext.Provider value={mockContext}>
              <OtpLoginScreen />
            </UserContext.Provider>,
          ),
        );
      });

      const rootInstance = root!.root;
      await act(async () => {
        rootInstance.findAllByType(AppInput)[0].props.onChangeText('+95912345678');
      });
      await act(async () => {
        await rootInstance.findByType(AppButton).props.onPress();
      });

      await act(async () => {
        rootInstance.findByType(AppInput).props.onChangeText('000000');
      });

      const verifyButton = rootInstance
        .findAllByType(AppButton)
        .find(b => b.props.label === 'Verify & Continue')!;

      await act(async () => {
        await verifyButton.props.onPress();
      });

      const errorText = rootInstance.findAllByType(AppText).find(t =>
        t.props.children?.toString().includes('Invalid or expired OTP code'),
      );
      expect(errorText).toBeDefined();
    });
  });

  describe('UserProvider Session Lifecycle & Auth Integration', () => {
    it('in mock mode: boots immediately into authenticated demo user', () => {
      let capturedContext: FullUserContextValue | undefined;

      function Consumer() {
        capturedContext = useCurrentUser();
        return null;
      }

      act(() => {
        root = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          <UserProvider>
            <Consumer />
          </UserProvider>,
        );
      });

      expect(capturedContext?.isAuthenticated).toBe(true);
      expect(capturedContext?.isBootstrapping).toBe(false);
      expect(capturedContext?.currentUser.displayName).toBe(ordinaryVolunteerUser.displayName);
    });

    it('in http mode: cold start without stored token finishes unauthenticated', async () => {
      act(() => {
        setDataMode('http');
      });
      let capturedContext: FullUserContextValue | undefined;

      function Consumer() {
        capturedContext = useCurrentUser();
        return null;
      }

      await act(async () => {
        root = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          <UserProvider>
            <Consumer />
          </UserProvider>,
        );
      });

      expect(capturedContext?.isAuthenticated).toBe(false);
      expect(capturedContext?.isBootstrapping).toBe(false);
    });

    it('in http mode: cold start with stored token fetches profile and authenticates', async () => {
      act(() => {
        setDataMode('http');
      });
      await memoryStorage.setToken('drf-test-token-777');

      jest.spyOn(profileApi, 'getCoordinatorProfile').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: {
          coordinator: {
            user_id: 10,
            status: 'approved',
            approved_by_id: 1,
            approved_at: '2025-01-02T00:00:00Z',
            notes: null,
            created_at: '2025-01-01T00:00:00Z',
            updated_at: '2025-01-02T00:00:00Z',
          },
        },
      });

      let capturedContext: FullUserContextValue | undefined;

      function Consumer() {
        capturedContext = useCurrentUser();
        return null;
      }

      await act(async () => {
        root = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          <UserProvider>
            <Consumer />
          </UserProvider>,
        );
      });

      expect(capturedContext?.isAuthenticated).toBe(true);
      expect(capturedContext?.currentUser.displayName).toBe('Test Live Volunteer');
      expect(capturedContext?.isCoordinator).toBe(true);
    });

    it('in http mode: verifyOtp stores token in TokenStore and bootstraps session', async () => {
      act(() => {
        setDataMode('http');
      });

      jest.spyOn(authApi, 'verifyOtp').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: {
          token: 'fresh-auth-token-999',
          user: {
            id: 42,
            uuid: 'uuid-42',
            display_name: 'Fresh Volunteer',
            verification_status: 'approved',
          },
        },
      });

      jest.spyOn(profileApi, 'getProfile').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: {
          ...mockProfileDto,
          display_name: 'Fresh Volunteer',
        },
      });

      jest.spyOn(profileApi, 'getCoordinatorProfile').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: false,
        status: 404,
        error: { kind: 'notFound', code: 'not_found', message: 'Not coordinator' },
      });

      let capturedContext: FullUserContextValue | undefined;

      function Consumer() {
        capturedContext = useCurrentUser();
        return null;
      }

      await act(async () => {
        root = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          <UserProvider>
            <Consumer />
          </UserProvider>,
        );
      });

      expect(capturedContext?.isAuthenticated).toBe(false);

      let verifyResult: any;
      await act(async () => {
        verifyResult = await capturedContext?.verifyOtp('+95912345678', '123456');
      });

      expect(verifyResult?.success).toBe(true);
      expect(await memoryStorage.getToken()).toBe('fresh-auth-token-999');
      expect(capturedContext?.isAuthenticated).toBe(true);
      expect(capturedContext?.currentUser.displayName).toBe('Fresh Volunteer');
      expect(capturedContext?.isCoordinator).toBe(false);
    });

    it('in http mode: logout calls authApi.logout and clears TokenStore', async () => {
      act(() => {
        setDataMode('http');
      });
      await memoryStorage.setToken('token-to-logout');

      const logoutSpy = jest.spyOn(authApi, 'logout').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: true,
        status: 200,
        data: { status: 'logged_out' },
      });

      let capturedContext: FullUserContextValue | undefined;

      function Consumer() {
        capturedContext = useCurrentUser();
        return null;
      }

      await act(async () => {
        root = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          <UserProvider>
            <Consumer />
          </UserProvider>,
        );
      });

      expect(capturedContext?.isAuthenticated).toBe(true);

      await act(async () => {
        await capturedContext?.logout();
      });

      expect(logoutSpy).toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(await memoryStorage.getToken()).toBeNull();
      expect(capturedContext?.isAuthenticated).toBe(false);
    });

    it('in http mode: sessionManager.handleUnauthorized invalidates session on 401', async () => {
      act(() => {
        setDataMode('http');
      });
      await memoryStorage.setToken('valid-token-before-401');

      let capturedContext: FullUserContextValue | undefined;

      function Consumer() {
        capturedContext = useCurrentUser();
        return null;
      }

      await act(async () => {
        root = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          <UserProvider>
            <Consumer />
          </UserProvider>,
        );
      });

      expect(capturedContext?.isAuthenticated).toBe(true);

      // Trigger 401 via sessionManager
      await act(async () => {
        await sessionManager.handleUnauthorized();
      });

      expect(await memoryStorage.getToken()).toBeNull();
      expect(capturedContext?.isAuthenticated).toBe(false);
    });

    it('preserves session token on 403, 404, 500 or network offline errors', async () => {
      act(() => {
        setDataMode('http');
      });
      await memoryStorage.setToken('resilient-token');

      // Non-401 errors must never call sessionManager.handleUnauthorized
      const unauthSpy = jest.spyOn(sessionManager, 'handleUnauthorized');

      // Simulate a network failure in profileApi
      jest.spyOn(profileApi, 'getProfile').mockResolvedValue({
        ok: false,
        status: 0,
        error: { kind: 'network', retryable: true, message: 'Network offline' },
      });

      let capturedContext: FullUserContextValue | undefined;
      function Consumer() {
        capturedContext = useCurrentUser();
        return null;
      }

      await act(async () => {
        root = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          <UserProvider>
            <Consumer />
          </UserProvider>,
        );
      });

      expect(unauthSpy).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(await memoryStorage.getToken()).toBe('resilient-token');
    });
  });

  describe('UI Mode Switching & Drawer Invariants', () => {
    it('renders QA Demo Profile Switcher in MainDrawerContent when dataMode is mock', () => {
      act(() => {
        resetDataMode(); // 'mock'
      });

      act(() => {
        root = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          renderWithTheme(
            <UserProvider>
              <MainDrawerContent {...createMockDrawerProps()} />
            </UserProvider>,
          ),
        );
      });

      const rootInstance = root!.root;
      const qaTitle = rootInstance.findAllByType(AppText).find(t =>
        t.props.children?.toString().toUpperCase().includes('QA DEMO PROFILE SWITCHER'),
      );
      expect(qaTitle).toBeDefined();
    });

    it('hides QA Demo Profile Switcher in MainDrawerContent when dataMode is http', () => {
      act(() => {
        setDataMode('http');
      });

      act(() => {
        root = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          renderWithTheme(
            <UserProvider>
              <MainDrawerContent {...createMockDrawerProps()} />
            </UserProvider>,
          ),
        );
      });

      const rootInstance = root!.root;
      const qaTitle = rootInstance.findAllByType(AppText).find(t =>
        t.props.children?.toString().toUpperCase().includes('QA DEMO PROFILE SWITCHER'),
      );
      expect(qaTitle).toBeUndefined();
    });

    it('AppNavigationRoot renders OtpLoginScreen in HTTP mode when unauthenticated', async () => {
      act(() => {
        setDataMode('http');
      });

      await act(async () => {
        root = ReactTestRenderer.create(
          renderWithTheme(
            <UserProvider>
              <AppNavigationRoot />
            </UserProvider>,
          ),
        );
      });

      // Flush microtasks for the async bootstrap effect in UserProvider
      for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
        await act(async () => {
          await Promise.resolve();
        });
      }

      const loginScreens = root!.root.findAllByType(OtpLoginScreen);
      expect(loginScreens.length).toBe(1);
    });

    it('AppRoot renders correctly without throwing', async () => {
      await act(async () => {
        root = ReactTestRenderer.create(<AppRoot />);
      });
      expect(root).toBeDefined();
    });
  });
});

export * from './screens/OtpLoginScreen';

FILE: C:\Users\USER\Documents\INTERN\Project\Volunteer_HR_Management\python_stack\mobile\src\features\auth\screens\OtpLoginScreen.tsx

/**
 * @format
 */

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';
import { useCurrentUser } from '../../../app/UserContext';
import {
  AppButton,
  AppCard,
  AppInput,
  AppText,
  ScreenContainer,
  StatusBadge,
} from '../../../shared';
import { AppHeader } from '../../../shared/layout/AppHeader';
import { hexToRgba, useAppTheme } from '../../../theme';

export interface OtpLoginScreenProps {
  onLoginSuccess?: () => void;
}

export function OtpLoginScreen({
  onLoginSuccess,
}: OtpLoginScreenProps): React.JSX.Element {
  const { theme } = useAppTheme();
  const { requestOtp, verifyOtp } = useCurrentUser();

  const [step, setStep] = useState<'phone' | 'otp'>('phone');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('+959');
  const [displayName, setDisplayName] = useState('');
  const [code, setCode] = useState('');
  const [devCode, setDevCode] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [fieldError, setFieldError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleSendOtp = async () => {
    setErrorMessage(null);
    setFieldError(null);

    const trimmedPhone = phone.trim();
    if (!trimmedPhone || trimmedPhone === '+959') {
      setFieldError('Please enter your mobile phone number.');
      return;
    }

    setIsLoading(true);
    try {
      const result = await requestOtp(trimmedPhone, 'login');
      if (result.success) {
        if (result.devCode) {
          setDevCode(result.devCode);
          setCode(result.devCode);
        }
        setStep('otp');
      } else {
        setErrorMessage(
          result.error || 'Failed to send verification code. Please try again.',
        );
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const msg =
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Network connection error.';
      setErrorMessage(msg);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleVerifyOtp = async () => {
    setErrorMessage(null);
    setFieldError(null);

    const trimmedCode = code.trim();
    if (!trimmedCode) {
      setFieldError('Please enter the 6-digit verification code.');
      return;
    }

    setIsLoading(true);
    try {
      const result = await verifyOtp(
        phone.trim(),
        trimmedCode,
        displayName.trim() || undefined,
      );

      if (result.success) {
        onLoginSuccess?.();
      } else {
        setErrorMessage(
          result.error || 'Invalid verification code. Please try again.',
        );
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const msg =
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Network connection error.';
      setErrorMessage(msg);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleBackToPhone = () => {
    setStep('phone');
    setCode('');
    setErrorMessage(null);
    setFieldError(null);
  };

  return (
    <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
      <AppHeader
        title={step === 'phone' ? 'Workshift Sign In' : 'Verify Code'}
        subtitle={
          step === 'phone'
            ? 'Access your volunteer shifts & coordinator workspace'
            : `Code sent to ${phone}`
        }
        showNotificationBell={false}
        onBackPress={step === 'otp' ? handleBackToPhone : undefined}
      />

      <ScreenContainer scroll style={styles.scroll}>
        {step === 'phone' ? (
          <AppCard style={styles.card}>
            <View style={styles.headerBlock}>
              <AppText variant="title" weight="bold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                Mobile Verification
              </AppText>
              <AppText
                variant="body"
                color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                style={styles.subtext}
              >
                Enter your mobile phone number to receive a secure SMS one-time
                passcode.
              </AppText>
            </View>

            <AppInput
              label="Phone Number"
              placeholder="+95912345678"
              value={phone}
              onChangeText={text => {
                setPhone(text);
                if (fieldError) setFieldError(null);
              }}
              keyboardType="phone-pad"
              autoComplete="tel"
              error={fieldError ?? undefined}
              helperText="E.g. +959 followed by your 8-9 digit phone number"
            />

            <View style={styles.fieldSpacer} />

            <AppInput
              label="Display Name (Optional)"
              placeholder="e.g. Daw Khin, Ko Aung"
              value={displayName}
              onChangeText={setDisplayName}
              autoCapitalize="words"
              helperText="Used if creating a new volunteer profile"
            />

            {errorMessage ? (
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.errorBanner,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: hexToRgba(theme.colors.danger, 0.1),
                    borderColor: theme.colors.danger,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.danger}>
                  {errorMessage}
                </AppText>
              </View>
            ) : null}

            <View style={styles.buttonBlock}>
              <AppButton
                label="Send Verification Code"
                variant="primary"
                onPress={handleSendOtp}
                loading={isLoading}
              />
            </View>
          </AppCard>
        ) : (
          <AppCard style={styles.card}>
            <View style={styles.headerBlock}>
              <AppText variant="title" weight="bold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                Enter 6-Digit Code
              </AppText>
              <AppText
                variant="body"
                color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                style={styles.subtext}
              >
                We have sent an authentication code to{' '}
                <AppText weight="bold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                  {phone}
                </AppText>
                .
              </AppText>
            </View>

            {devCode ? (
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.devBanner,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: hexToRgba(theme.colors.primary, 0.1),
                    borderColor: theme.colors.primary,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <View style={styles.devBadgeRow}>
                  <StatusBadge status="info" label="DEV TEST OTP" />
                </View>
                <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.primary}>
                  Development Code: <AppText weight="bold">{devCode}</AppText>
                </AppText>
              </View>
            ) : null}

            <AppInput
              label="Verification Code"
              placeholder="123456"
              value={code}
              onChangeText={text => {
                setCode(text);
                if (fieldError) setFieldError(null);
              }}
              keyboardType="number-pad"
              maxLength={6}
              error={fieldError ?? undefined}
              helperText="Enter the 6-digit code received via SMS"
            />

            {errorMessage ? (
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.errorBanner,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: hexToRgba(theme.colors.danger, 0.1),
                    borderColor: theme.colors.danger,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.danger}>
                  {errorMessage}
                </AppText>
              </View>
            ) : null}

            <View style={styles.buttonBlock}>
              <AppButton
                label="Verify & Continue"
                variant="primary"
                onPress={handleVerifyOtp}
                loading={isLoading}
              />

              <View style={styles.secondaryButtonSpacer}>
                <AppButton
                  label="Change Phone Number"
                  variant="outline"
                  onPress={handleBackToPhone}
                  disabled={isLoading}
                />
              </View>
            </View>
          </AppCard>
        )}
      </ScreenContainer>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  scroll: {
    paddingVertical: 20,
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
  },
  card: {
    padding: 20,
    marginBottom: 20,
  },
  headerBlock: {
    marginBottom: 20,
  },
  subtext: {
    marginTop: 6,
  },
  fieldSpacer: {
    height: 16,
  },
  buttonBlock: {
    marginTop: 24,
  },
  secondaryButtonSpacer: {
    marginTop: 12,
  },
  errorBanner: {
    marginTop: 16,
    padding: 12,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderRadius: 8,
  },
  devBanner: {
    marginBottom: 16,
    padding: 12,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderRadius: 8,
  },
  devBadgeRow: {
    marginBottom: 6,
    alignSelf: 'flex-start',
  },
});
