/**
 * @format
 */

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import {
  FlatList,
  Pressable,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { CoordinatorRepository } from '../../../data/contracts/coordinatorRepository';
import { defaultCoordinatorRepository } from '../../../data/mock/MockCoordinatorRepository';
import { useRepositories } from '../../../app/RepositoryProvider';
import type {
  CoordinatorApplicationItem,
  CoordinatorApplicationStatus,
  CoordinatorEventSummary,
} from '../../../domain/models/coordinator';
import type { MainDrawerScreenProps } from '../../../navigation/navigationTypes';
import {
  AppHeader,
  AppIcon,
  AppText,
  EmptyState,
  FilterChip,
  LoadingState,
  SearchInput,
} from '../../../shared';
import { useAppTheme } from '../../../theme';
import {
  ApplicationReviewCard,
  CoordinatorEventHeader,
  CoordinatorGuard,
} from '../components';

export interface CoordinatorApplicationsScreenProps
  extends Partial<MainDrawerScreenProps<'CoordinatorApplications'>> {
  repository?: CoordinatorRepository;
}

type FilterOption = 'all' | CoordinatorApplicationStatus;

export function CoordinatorApplicationsScreen({
  route,
  navigation,
  repository: propRepository,
}: CoordinatorApplicationsScreenProps): React.JSX.Element {
  const { theme } = useAppTheme();
  const repositories = useRepositories();
  const repo = propRepository ?? repositories.coordinatorRepository ?? defaultCoordinatorRepository;

  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [events, setEvents] = useState<CoordinatorEventSummary[]>([]);
  const [selectedEventId, setSelectedEventId] = useState<string>(
    route?.params?.eventId ?? '',
  );
  const selectedEventIdRef = React.useRef(selectedEventId);
  selectedEventIdRef.current = selectedEventId;

  const [applications, setApplications] = useState<CoordinatorApplicationItem[]>([]);
  const [activeFilter, setActiveFilter] = useState<FilterOption>('all');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [feedbackBanner, setFeedbackBanner] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const loadData = useCallback(
    async (targetId?: string) => {
      setIsLoading(true);
      try {
        const evList = await repo.getMyEvents();
        setEvents(evList);

        const eventIdToUse =
          targetId ||
          selectedEventIdRef.current ||
          route?.params?.eventId ||
          evList[0]?.id ||
          'evt-coord-001';

        if (selectedEventIdRef.current !== eventIdToUse) {
          selectedEventIdRef.current = eventIdToUse;
          setSelectedEventId(eventIdToUse);
        }

        const appList = await repo.getApplications(eventIdToUse);
        setApplications(appList);
      } catch {
        setFeedbackBanner('Unable to load applications list.');
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    },
    [repo, route?.params?.eventId],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, [loadData]);

  const selectedEvent = useMemo(
    () => events.find(e => e.id === selectedEventId) ?? events[0],
    [events, selectedEventId],
  );

  const isMonitorMode = selectedEvent?.isOwnerAsMonitor ?? false;

  const counts = useMemo(() => {
    return {
      all: applications.length,
      pending: applications.filter(a => a.status === 'pending').length,
      accepted: applications.filter(a => a.status === 'accepted').length,
      waitlisted: applications.filter(a => a.status === 'waitlisted').length,
      rejected: applications.filter(a => a.status === 'rejected').length,
    };
  }, [applications]);

  const filteredApplications = useMemo(() => {
    return applications.filter(app => {
      if (activeFilter !== 'all' && app.status !== activeFilter) {
        return false;
      }
      if (searchQuery.trim().length > 0) {
        const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
        const matchesName = app.applicantName.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        const matchesPost = app.postTitle?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ?? false;
        if (!matchesName && !matchesPost) {
          return false;
        }
      }
      return true;
    });
  }, [applications, activeFilter, searchQuery]);

  const handleUpdateStatus = async (
    applicationId: string,
    targetStatus: CoordinatorApplicationStatus,
  ) => {
    const result = await repo.updateApplicationStatus(
      applicationId,
      targetStatus,
    );
    if (result.success && result.data) {
      setFeedbackBanner(result.message);
      setApplications(prev =>
        prev.map(a => (a.id === applicationId ? result.data! : a)),
      );
    } else {
      setFeedbackBanner(result.message);
    }
  };

  const handleBack = () => {
    if (navigation && navigation.canGoBack()) {
      navigation.goBack();
    } else if (navigation) {
      navigation.navigate('CoordinatorWorkspace');
    }
  };

  const renderHeader = () => (
    <View style={styles.listHeaderContainer}>
      {/* Event Context Header */}
      <CoordinatorEventHeader
        events={events}
        selectedEventId={selectedEventId}
        onSelectEvent={id => {
          setSelectedEventId(id);
          selectedEventIdRef.current = id;
          loadData(id);
        }}
      />

      {/* Feedback Banner */}
      {feedbackBanner ? (
        <View
          style={[
            styles.feedbackBanner,
            {
              backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
              borderColor: theme.colors.primary,
              borderRadius: theme.radius.sm,
            },
          ]}
        >
          <AppText
            variant="caption"
            weight="semibold"
            color={theme.colors.primary}
            style={styles.feedbackText}
          >
            {feedbackBanner}
          </AppText>
          <Pressable
            accessibilityRole="button"
            accessibilityLabel="Dismiss feedback"
            onPress={() => setFeedbackBanner(null)}
          >
            <AppIcon name="close" size={14} color={theme.colors.primary} />
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      ) : null}

      {/* Search Input */}
      <View style={styles.searchWrapper}>
        <SearchInput
          value={searchQuery}
          onChangeText={setSearchQuery}
          placeholder="Search applicants by name or post..."
        />
      </View>

      {/* Status Filter Chips */}
      <ScrollView
        horizontal
        showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
        contentContainerStyle={styles.filterScroll}
      >
        <FilterChip
          label={`All (${counts.all})`}
          selected={activeFilter === 'all'}
          onPress={() => setActiveFilter('all')}
        />
        <FilterChip
          label={`Pending (${counts.pending})`}
          selected={activeFilter === 'pending'}
          onPress={() => setActiveFilter('pending')}
        />
        <FilterChip
          label={`Accepted (${counts.accepted})`}
          selected={activeFilter === 'accepted'}
          onPress={() => setActiveFilter('accepted')}
        />
        <FilterChip
          label={`Waitlisted (${counts.waitlisted})`}
          selected={activeFilter === 'waitlisted'}
          onPress={() => setActiveFilter('waitlisted')}
        />
        <FilterChip
          label={`Rejected (${counts.rejected})`}
          selected={activeFilter === 'rejected'}
          onPress={() => setActiveFilter('rejected')}
        />
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );

  return (
    <CoordinatorGuard navigation={navigation}>
      <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
        <AppHeader
          title="Volunteer Applications"
          subtitle={selectedEvent?.title}
          onBackPress={handleBack}
        />

        {isLoading ? (
          <LoadingState label="Loading applications..." />
        ) : (
          <FlatList
            data={filteredApplications}
            keyExtractor={item => item.id}
            ListHeaderComponent={renderHeader}
            renderItem={({ item }) => (
              <ApplicationReviewCard
                application={item}
                isMonitorMode={isMonitorMode}
                onAccept={id => handleUpdateStatus(id, 'accepted')}
                onReject={id => handleUpdateStatus(id, 'rejected')}
                onWaitlist={id => handleUpdateStatus(id, 'waitlisted')}
              />
            )}
            contentContainerStyle={styles.listContent}
            ListEmptyComponent={
              <EmptyState
                title="No Applications"
                description={`No applications match the "${activeFilter}" filter.`}
              />
            }
          />
        )}
      </View>
    </CoordinatorGuard>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  listContent: {
    padding: 16,
    paddingBottom: 40,
  },
  listHeaderContainer: {
    marginBottom: 8,
  },
  feedbackBanner: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    alignItems: 'center',
    padding: 10,
    borderWidth: 1,
    marginBottom: 12,
  },
  feedbackText: {
    flex: 1,
    marginRight: 8,
  },
  searchWrapper: {
    marginBottom: 10,
  },
  filterScroll: {
    gap: 8,
    paddingBottom: 10,
  },
});

FILE: C:\Users\USER\Documents\INTERN\Project\Volunteer_HR_Management\python_stack\mobile\src\features\coordinator\screens\CoordinatorAssignmentsScreen.tsx

/**
 * @format
 */

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import {
  FlatList,
  Pressable,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { CoordinatorRepository } from '../../../data/contracts/coordinatorRepository';
import { defaultCoordinatorRepository } from '../../../data/mock/MockCoordinatorRepository';
import { useRepositories } from '../../../app/RepositoryProvider';
import type {
  CoordinatorApplicationItem,
  CoordinatorAssignmentItem,
  CoordinatorEventSummary,
} from '../../../domain/models/coordinator';
import type { MainDrawerScreenProps } from '../../../navigation/navigationTypes';
import {
  AppButton,
  AppCard,
  AppHeader,
  AppIcon,
  AppText,
  EmptyState,
  FilterChip,
  LoadingState,
  SectionHeader,
} from '../../../shared';
import { useAppTheme } from '../../../theme';
import {
  AssignmentRow,
  CoordinatorEventHeader,
  CoordinatorGuard,
} from '../components';

export interface CoordinatorAssignmentsScreenProps
  extends Partial<MainDrawerScreenProps<'CoordinatorAssignments'>> {
  repository?: CoordinatorRepository;
}

type TabFilter = 'all' | 'unassigned' | 'assigned';

export function CoordinatorAssignmentsScreen({
  route,
  navigation,
  repository: propRepository,
}: CoordinatorAssignmentsScreenProps): React.JSX.Element {
  const { theme } = useAppTheme();
  const repositories = useRepositories();
  const repo = propRepository ?? repositories.coordinatorRepository ?? defaultCoordinatorRepository;

  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [events, setEvents] = useState<CoordinatorEventSummary[]>([]);
  const [selectedEventId, setSelectedEventId] = useState<string>(
    route?.params?.eventId ?? '',
  );
  const selectedEventIdRef = React.useRef(selectedEventId);
  selectedEventIdRef.current = selectedEventId;

  const [assignments, setAssignments] = useState<CoordinatorAssignmentItem[]>([]);
  const [unassignedApps, setUnassignedApps] = useState<
    CoordinatorApplicationItem[]
  >([]);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabFilter>('all');
  const [feedbackBanner, setFeedbackBanner] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isProcessing, setIsProcessing] = useState(false);

  const loadData = useCallback(
    async (targetId?: string) => {
      setIsLoading(true);
      try {
        const evList = await repo.getMyEvents();
        setEvents(evList);

        const eventIdToUse =
          targetId ||
          selectedEventIdRef.current ||
          route?.params?.eventId ||
          evList[0]?.id ||
          'evt-coord-001';

        if (selectedEventIdRef.current !== eventIdToUse) {
          selectedEventIdRef.current = eventIdToUse;
          setSelectedEventId(eventIdToUse);
        }

        const [asgList, unassignedList] = await Promise.all([
          repo.getAssignments(eventIdToUse),
          repo.getUnassignedApplications(eventIdToUse),
        ]);
        setAssignments(asgList);
        setUnassignedApps(unassignedList);
      } catch {
        setFeedbackBanner('Unable to load assignments data.');
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    },
    [repo, route?.params?.eventId],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, [loadData]);

  const selectedEvent = useMemo(
    () => events.find(e => e.id === selectedEventId) ?? events[0],
    [events, selectedEventId],
  );

  const isMonitorMode = selectedEvent?.isOwnerAsMonitor ?? false;

  const handleRunAutoAssignment = async () => {
    if (!selectedEvent) {
      return;
    }
    setIsProcessing(true);
    try {
      const result = await repo.runAutoAssignment(
        selectedEvent.id,
      );
      setFeedbackBanner(result.message);
      if (result.success) {
        await loadData();
      }
    } catch {
      setFeedbackBanner('Failed to execute auto-assignment.');
    } finally {
      setIsProcessing(false);
    }
  };

  const handleManualAssign = async (applicationId: string) => {
    setIsProcessing(true);
    try {
      const app = unassignedApps.find(a => a.id === applicationId);
      const postIdToAssign = app?.postId ?? '';
      const result =
        await repo.assignVolunteerManually(
          applicationId,
          postIdToAssign,
          'GRP-C',
          false,
        );
      setFeedbackBanner(result.message);
      if (result.success) {
        await loadData();
      }
    } catch {
      setFeedbackBanner('Failed to assign volunteer.');
    } finally {
      setIsProcessing(false);
    }
  };

  const handleCancelAssignment = async (assignmentId: string) => {
    setIsProcessing(true);
    try {
      const result = await repo.cancelAssignment(
        assignmentId,
      );
      setFeedbackBanner(result.message);
      if (result.success) {
        await loadData();
      }
    } catch {
      setFeedbackBanner('Failed to cancel assignment.');
    } finally {
      setIsProcessing(false);
    }
  };

  const handleBack = () => {
    if (navigation && navigation.canGoBack()) {
      navigation.goBack();
    } else if (navigation) {
      navigation.navigate('CoordinatorWorkspace');
    }
  };

  const renderHeader = () => (
    <View style={styles.headerContainer}>
      <CoordinatorEventHeader
        events={events}
        selectedEventId={selectedEventId}
        onSelectEvent={id => {
          setSelectedEventId(id);
          selectedEventIdRef.current = id;
          loadData(id);
        }}
      />

      {feedbackBanner ? (
        <View
          style={[
            styles.feedbackBanner,
            {
              backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
              borderColor: theme.colors.primary,
              borderRadius: theme.radius.sm,
            },
          ]}
        >
          <AppText
            variant="caption"
            weight="semibold"
            color={theme.colors.primary}
            style={styles.feedbackText}
          >
            {feedbackBanner}
          </AppText>
          <Pressable
            accessibilityRole="button"
            accessibilityLabel="Dismiss feedback"
            onPress={() => setFeedbackBanner(null)}
          >
            <AppIcon name="close" size={14} color={theme.colors.primary} />
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      ) : null}

      {/* Auto-Assignment CTA Card */}
      <AppCard style={styles.autoAssignCard}>
        <View style={styles.autoAssignHeader}>
          <AppIcon name="refresh" size={18} color={theme.colors.primary} />
          <AppText
            variant="title"
            weight="bold"
            color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
            style={styles.autoAssignTitle}
          >
            Auto-Assignment Engine
          </AppText>
        </View>

        <AppText
          variant="caption"
          color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
          style={styles.autoAssignDesc}
        >
          Post matching, group sizing, and capacity rules are evaluated by
          server-authoritative functions. Preview auto-placement locally.
        </AppText>

        <View style={styles.autoAssignButtonWrapper}>
          <AppButton
            label="Run Auto-Assignment (Demo Preview)"
            variant="primary"
            loading={isProcessing}
            disabled={isMonitorMode || unassignedApps.length === 0}
            onPress={handleRunAutoAssignment}
          />
        </View>
      </AppCard>

      {/* Tab Filter Chips */}
      <ScrollView
        horizontal
        showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
        contentContainerStyle={styles.tabScroll}
      >
        <FilterChip
          label={`All (${unassignedApps.length + assignments.length})`}
          selected={activeTab === 'all'}
          onPress={() => setActiveTab('all')}
        />
        <FilterChip
          label={`Unassigned (${unassignedApps.length})`}
          selected={activeTab === 'unassigned'}
          onPress={() => setActiveTab('unassigned')}
        />
        <FilterChip
          label={`Assigned (${assignments.length})`}
          selected={activeTab === 'assigned'}
          onPress={() => setActiveTab('assigned')}
        />
      </ScrollView>

      {/* Section 1: Unassigned Accepted Volunteers */}
      {activeTab !== 'assigned' ? (
        <View style={styles.sectionWrapper}>
          <SectionHeader
            title={`Unassigned Accepted Volunteers (${unassignedApps.length})`}
          />
          {unassignedApps.length === 0 ? (
            <AppText
              variant="caption"
              color={theme.colors.muted}
              style={styles.emptyInlineText}
            >
              All accepted volunteers for this event have been assigned to shifts.
            </AppText>
          ) : (
            unassignedApps.map(unassigned => (
              <AssignmentRow
                key={unassigned.id}
                unassignedApplication={unassigned}
                isMonitorMode={isMonitorMode}
                onManualAssign={handleManualAssign}
              />
            ))
          )}
        </View>
      ) : null}

      {/* Section 2 Heading if showing all */}
      {activeTab !== 'unassigned' ? (
        <View style={styles.sectionWrapper}>
          <SectionHeader
            title={`Current Shift Assignments (${assignments.length})`}
          />
        </View>
      ) : null}
    </View>
  );

  const displayAssignments =
    activeTab === 'unassigned' ? [] : assignments;

  return (
    <CoordinatorGuard navigation={navigation}>
      <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
        <AppHeader
          title="Shift Assignments"
          subtitle={selectedEvent?.title}
          onBackPress={handleBack}
        />

        {isLoading ? (
          <LoadingState label="Loading shift assignments..." />
        ) : (
          <FlatList
            data={displayAssignments}
            keyExtractor={item => item.id}
            ListHeaderComponent={renderHeader}
            renderItem={({ item }) => (
              <AssignmentRow
                assignment={item}
                isMonitorMode={isMonitorMode}
                onCancelAssignment={handleCancelAssignment}
              />
            )}
            contentContainerStyle={styles.listContent}
            ListEmptyComponent={
              activeTab === 'assigned' ? (
                <EmptyState
                  title="No Assignments Found"
                  description="No volunteers have been assigned to shifts yet."
                />
              ) : undefined
            }
          />
        )}
      </View>
    </CoordinatorGuard>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  listContent: {
    padding: 16,
    paddingBottom: 40,
  },
  headerContainer: {
    marginBottom: 8,
  },
  feedbackBanner: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    alignItems: 'center',
    padding: 10,
    borderWidth: 1,
    marginBottom: 12,
  },
  feedbackText: {
    flex: 1,
    marginRight: 8,
  },
  autoAssignCard: {
    padding: 14,
    marginBottom: 14,
  },
  autoAssignHeader: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    marginBottom: 6,
  },
  autoAssignTitle: {
    marginLeft: 8,
  },
  autoAssignDesc: {
    marginBottom: 10,
    lineHeight: 18,
  },
  autoAssignButtonWrapper: {
    alignItems: 'flex-start',
  },
  tabScroll: {
    gap: 8,
    marginBottom: 12,
  },
  sectionWrapper: {
    marginBottom: 8,
  },
  emptyInlineText: {
    fontStyle: 'italic',
    paddingHorizontal: 4,
    paddingVertical: 8,
    marginBottom: 8,
  },
});

/**
 * @format
 */

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import {
  FlatList,
  Pressable,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { CoordinatorRepository } from '../../../data/contracts/coordinatorRepository';
import { defaultCoordinatorRepository } from '../../../data/mock/MockCoordinatorRepository';
import { useRepositories } from '../../../app/RepositoryProvider';
import type {
  AttendanceRecordStatus,
  CoordinatorAttendanceItem,
  CoordinatorEventSummary,
} from '../../../domain/models/coordinator';
import type { MainDrawerScreenProps } from '../../../navigation/navigationTypes';
import {
  AppHeader,
  AppIcon,
  AppText,
  EmptyState,
  FilterChip,
  LoadingState,
  SectionHeader,
} from '../../../shared';
import { useAppTheme } from '../../../theme';
import {
  AttendanceRow,
  CoordinatorEventHeader,
  CoordinatorGuard,
} from '../components';

export interface CoordinatorAttendanceScreenProps
  extends Partial<MainDrawerScreenProps<'CoordinatorAttendance'>> {
  repository?: CoordinatorRepository;
}

type RosterFilter = 'all' | AttendanceRecordStatus;

export function CoordinatorAttendanceScreen({
  route,
  navigation,
  repository: propRepository,
}: CoordinatorAttendanceScreenProps): React.JSX.Element {
  const { theme } = useAppTheme();
  const repositories = useRepositories();
  const repo = propRepository ?? repositories.coordinatorRepository ?? defaultCoordinatorRepository;

  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [events, setEvents] = useState<CoordinatorEventSummary[]>([]);
  const [selectedEventId, setSelectedEventId] = useState<string>(
    route?.params?.eventId ?? '',
  );
  const selectedEventIdRef = React.useRef(selectedEventId);
  selectedEventIdRef.current = selectedEventId;

  const [attendanceList, setAttendanceList] = useState<
    CoordinatorAttendanceItem[]
  >([]);
  const [pendingGeoList, setPendingGeoList] = useState<
    CoordinatorAttendanceItem[]
  >([]);
  const [activeFilter, setActiveFilter] = useState<RosterFilter>('all');
  const [feedbackBanner, setFeedbackBanner] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const loadData = useCallback(
    async (targetId?: string) => {
      setIsLoading(true);
      try {
        const evList = await repo.getMyEvents();
        setEvents(evList);

        const eventIdToUse =
          targetId ||
          selectedEventIdRef.current ||
          route?.params?.eventId ||
          evList[0]?.id ||
          'evt-coord-001';

        if (selectedEventIdRef.current !== eventIdToUse) {
          selectedEventIdRef.current = eventIdToUse;
          setSelectedEventId(eventIdToUse);
        }

        const [roster, geoPending] = await Promise.all([
          repo.getAttendanceRoster(eventIdToUse),
          repo.getPendingGeoConfirmations(eventIdToUse),
        ]);
        setAttendanceList(roster);
        setPendingGeoList(geoPending);
      } catch {
        setFeedbackBanner('Unable to load attendance roster.');
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    },
    [repo, route?.params?.eventId],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, [loadData]);

  const selectedEvent = useMemo(
    () => events.find(e => e.id === selectedEventId) ?? events[0],
    [events, selectedEventId],
  );

  const isMonitorMode = selectedEvent?.isOwnerAsMonitor ?? false;

  const counts = useMemo(() => {
    return {
      all: attendanceList.length,
      checkedIn: attendanceList.filter(a => a.status === 'checked_in').length,
      scheduled: attendanceList.filter(a => a.status === 'scheduled').length,
      completed: attendanceList.filter(a => a.status === 'completed').length,
      pendingGeo: pendingGeoList.length,
    };
  }, [attendanceList, pendingGeoList]);

  const filteredRoster = useMemo(() => {
    if (activeFilter === 'all') {
      return attendanceList;
    }
    return attendanceList.filter(a => a.status === activeFilter);
  }, [attendanceList, activeFilter]);

  const handleConfirmGeo = async (attendanceId: string) => {
    const result = await repo.confirmGeoAttendance(
      attendanceId,
    );
    setFeedbackBanner(result.message);
    if (result.success) {
      await loadData();
    }
  };

  const handleRejectGeo = async (attendanceId: string) => {
    const result = await repo.rejectGeoAttendance(
      attendanceId,
    );
    setFeedbackBanner(result.message);
    if (result.success) {
      await loadData();
    }
  };

  const handleManualCheckIn = async (attendanceId: string) => {
    const result = await repo.manualCheckIn(
      attendanceId,
    );
    setFeedbackBanner(result.message);
    if (result.success) {
      await loadData();
    }
  };

  const handleBack = () => {
    if (navigation && navigation.canGoBack()) {
      navigation.goBack();
    } else if (navigation) {
      navigation.navigate('CoordinatorWorkspace');
    }
  };

  const renderHeader = () => (
    <View style={styles.headerContainer}>
      <CoordinatorEventHeader
        events={events}
        selectedEventId={selectedEventId}
        onSelectEvent={id => {
          setSelectedEventId(id);
          selectedEventIdRef.current = id;
          loadData(id);
        }}
      />

      {feedbackBanner ? (
        <View
          style={[
            styles.feedbackBanner,
            {
              backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
              borderColor: theme.colors.primary,
              borderRadius: theme.radius.sm,
            },
          ]}
        >
          <AppText
            variant="caption"
            weight="semibold"
            color={theme.colors.primary}
            style={styles.feedbackText}
          >
            {feedbackBanner}
          </AppText>
          <Pressable
            accessibilityRole="button"
            accessibilityLabel="Dismiss feedback"
            onPress={() => setFeedbackBanner(null)}
          >
            <AppIcon name="close" size={14} color={theme.colors.primary} />
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      ) : null}

      {/* Section 1: Pending Geo Confirmation Queue */}
      <View style={styles.sectionSpacing}>
        <SectionHeader
          title={`Pending Geo Confirmation Queue (${pendingGeoList.length})`}
        />
        {pendingGeoList.length === 0 ? (
          <View
            style={[
              styles.emptyQueueCard,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface,
                borderColor: theme.colors.border,
                borderRadius: theme.radius.sm,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <AppIcon name="check" size={18} color={theme.colors.success} />
            <AppText
              variant="caption"
              color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
              style={styles.emptyQueueText}
            >
              No geo check-in attempts are currently awaiting review.
            </AppText>
          </View>
        ) : (
          pendingGeoList.map(item => (
            <AttendanceRow
              key={item.id}
              attendance={item}
              isMonitorMode={isMonitorMode}
              onConfirmGeo={handleConfirmGeo}
              onRejectGeo={handleRejectGeo}
            />
          ))
        )}
      </View>

      {/* Section 2: Attendance Roster Filters */}
      <View style={styles.rosterHeaderRow}>
        <SectionHeader title={`Attendance Roster (${counts.all})`} />
      </View>

      <ScrollView
        horizontal
        showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
        contentContainerStyle={styles.filterScroll}
      >
        <FilterChip
          label={`All (${counts.all})`}
          selected={activeFilter === 'all'}
          onPress={() => setActiveFilter('all')}
        />
        <FilterChip
          label={`Checked In (${counts.checkedIn})`}
          selected={activeFilter === 'checked_in'}
          onPress={() => setActiveFilter('checked_in')}
        />
        <FilterChip
          label={`Scheduled (${counts.scheduled})`}
          selected={activeFilter === 'scheduled'}
          onPress={() => setActiveFilter('scheduled')}
        />
        <FilterChip
          label={`Completed (${counts.completed})`}
          selected={activeFilter === 'completed'}
          onPress={() => setActiveFilter('completed')}
        />
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );

  const renderFooter = () => (
    <View style={styles.footerContainer}>
      <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.muted} style={styles.privacyNote}>
        Operational Notice: Authoritative distance calculated on server. Raw GPS
        coordinates and device telemetry are hidden per Privacy Rule BR-PRIV-T4. Real
        location permission and GPS capture are not active in this UI preview.
      </AppText>
    </View>
  );

  return (
    <CoordinatorGuard navigation={navigation}>
      <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
        <AppHeader
          title="Attendance & Check-In"
          subtitle={selectedEvent?.title}
          onBackPress={handleBack}
        />

        {isLoading ? (
          <LoadingState label="Loading attendance records..." />
        ) : (
          <FlatList
            data={filteredRoster}
            keyExtractor={item => item.id}
            ListHeaderComponent={renderHeader}
            renderItem={({ item }) => (
              <AttendanceRow
                attendance={item}
                isMonitorMode={isMonitorMode}
                onConfirmGeo={handleConfirmGeo}
                onRejectGeo={handleRejectGeo}
                onManualCheckIn={handleManualCheckIn}
              />
            )}
            ListFooterComponent={renderFooter}
            contentContainerStyle={styles.listContent}
            ListEmptyComponent={
              <EmptyState
                title="No Records Found"
                description={`No attendance records found matching the "${activeFilter}" filter.`}
              />
            }
          />
        )}
      </View>
    </CoordinatorGuard>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  listContent: {
    padding: 16,
    paddingBottom: 40,
  },
  headerContainer: {
    marginBottom: 8,
  },
  feedbackBanner: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    alignItems: 'center',
    padding: 10,
    borderWidth: 1,
    marginBottom: 12,
  },
  feedbackText: {
    flex: 1,
    marginRight: 8,
  },
  sectionSpacing: {
    marginBottom: 14,
  },
  emptyQueueCard: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    padding: 12,
    borderWidth: 1,
    marginBottom: 8,
  },
  emptyQueueText: {
    marginLeft: 8,
    flex: 1,
  },
  rosterHeaderRow: {
    marginBottom: 6,
  },
  filterScroll: {
    gap: 8,
    marginBottom: 12,
  },
  footerContainer: {
    marginTop: 16,
    paddingTop: 12,
    borderTopWidth: StyleSheet.hairlineWidth,
    borderTopColor: 'rgba(128, 128, 128, 0.2)',
  },
  privacyNote: {
    fontStyle: 'italic',
    lineHeight: 18,
    textAlign: 'center',
  },
});

FILE: C:\Users\USER\Documents\INTERN\Project\Volunteer_HR_Management\python_stack\mobile\src\features\coordinator\screens\CoordinatorEventDetailScreen.tsx

/**
 * @format
 */

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Modal,
  Pressable,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import type { CoordinatorRepository } from '../../../data/contracts/coordinatorRepository';
import { defaultCoordinatorRepository } from '../../../data/mock/MockCoordinatorRepository';
import { useRepositories } from '../../../app/RepositoryProvider';
import type {
  CoordinatorEventDetail,
  CoordinatorEventStatus,
} from '../../../domain/models/coordinator';
import type { MainDrawerScreenProps } from '../../../navigation/navigationTypes';
import {
  AppButton,
  AppCard,
  AppHeader,
  AppIcon,
  AppText,
  ErrorState,
  LoadingState,
  SectionHeader,
  StatusBadge,
} from '../../../shared';
import { useAppTheme } from '../../../theme';
import { CoordinatorGuard } from '../components';

export interface CoordinatorEventDetailScreenProps
  extends Partial<MainDrawerScreenProps<'CoordinatorEventDetail'>> {
  eventId?: string;
  repository?: CoordinatorRepository;
}

export function CoordinatorEventDetailScreen({
  route,
  navigation,
  eventId: propEventId,
  repository: propRepository,
}: CoordinatorEventDetailScreenProps): React.JSX.Element {
  const { theme } = useAppTheme();
  const repositories = useRepositories();
  const repository = propRepository ?? repositories.coordinatorRepository ?? defaultCoordinatorRepository;
  const eventId = propEventId ?? route?.params?.eventId;

  const [event, setEvent] = useState<CoordinatorEventDetail | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [feedbackBanner, setFeedbackBanner] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Transition Confirmation Dialog State
  const [pendingTargetStatus, setPendingTargetStatus] =
    useState<CoordinatorEventStatus | null>(null);
  const [isTransitioning, setIsTransitioning] = useState(false);

  const loadDetail = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!eventId) {
      setErrorMessage('No event ID provided');
      setIsLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    try {
      setErrorMessage(null);
      const data = await repository.getEventDetail(eventId);
      if (!data) {
        setErrorMessage('Coordinator event not found');
      } else {
        setEvent(data);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setErrorMessage(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load event details',
      );
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [eventId, repository]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadDetail();
  }, [loadDetail]);

  const handleConfirmTransition = async () => {
    if (!event || !pendingTargetStatus) {
      return;
    }

    setIsTransitioning(true);
    try {
      const result = await repository.transitionEventStatus(
        event.id,
        pendingTargetStatus,
      );

      if (result.success && result.data) {
        setFeedbackBanner(
          `Event status updated to ${pendingTargetStatus.toUpperCase()} (${result.message})`,
        );
        setEvent(result.data);
      } else {
        setFeedbackBanner(result.message);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setFeedbackBanner(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Transition failed',
      );
    } finally {
      setIsTransitioning(false);
      setPendingTargetStatus(null);
    }
  };

  const handleBack = () => {
    if (navigation && navigation.canGoBack()) {
      navigation.goBack();
    } else if (navigation) {
      navigation.navigate('CoordinatorEvents');
    }
  };

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <CoordinatorGuard navigation={navigation}>
        <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
          <AppHeader title="Event Management" onBackPress={handleBack} />
          <LoadingState label="Loading event management details..." />
        </View>
      </CoordinatorGuard>
    );
  }

  if (errorMessage || !event) {
    return (
      <CoordinatorGuard navigation={navigation}>
        <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
          <AppHeader title="Event Management" onBackPress={handleBack} />
          <ErrorState
            title="Event Not Found"
            description={errorMessage ?? 'Could not find coordinator event.'}
            onRetry={loadDetail}
          />
        </View>
      </CoordinatorGuard>
    );
  }

  const isMonitorMode = event.isOwnerAsMonitor;
  const isFinalState =
    event.status === 'completed' || event.status === 'cancelled';

  return (
    <CoordinatorGuard navigation={navigation}>
      <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
        <AppHeader
          title="Event Management"
          subtitle={event.title}
          onBackPress={handleBack}
        />

        <ScrollView
          contentContainerStyle={[
            styles.scrollContent,
            { padding: theme.spacing.md },
          ]}
        >
          {/* Feedback Banner */}
          {feedbackBanner ? (
            <View
              style={[
                styles.feedbackBanner,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                  borderColor: theme.colors.primary,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <AppText
                variant="caption"
                weight="bold"
                color={theme.colors.primary}
              >
                {feedbackBanner}
              </AppText>
            </View>
          ) : null}

          {/* OWNER-AS-MONITOR READ-ONLY BANNER */}
          {isMonitorMode ? (
            <View
              style={[
                styles.monitorBanner,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                  borderColor: theme.colors.accent,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <AppIcon name="alert" size={20} color={theme.colors.accent} />
              <View style={styles.monitorTextCol}>
                <AppText
                  variant="body"
                  weight="bold"
                  color={theme.colors.accent}
                >
                  Read-Only Monitor Mode
                </AppText>
                <AppText
                  variant="caption"
                  color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                  style={styles.monitorSubtext}
                >
                  Coordination of this event has been handed over to{' '}
                  <AppText variant="caption" weight="bold">
                    {event.coordinatorName}
                  </AppText>
                  . As event owner, you can monitor rosters and attendance, but
                  all edit and lifecycle transitions are disabled.
                </AppText>
              </View>
            </View>
          ) : null}

          {/* Event Header Card */}
          <AppCard style={styles.cardSpacing}>
            <View style={styles.headerRow}>
              <View style={styles.statusGroup}>
                <StatusBadge
                  status={
                    event.status === 'active'
                      ? 'info'
                      : event.status === 'published'
                      ? 'success'
                      : event.status === 'draft'
                      ? 'warning'
                      : event.status === 'completed'
                      ? 'success'
                      : 'danger'
                  }
                  label={event.status.toUpperCase()}
                />
                {isMonitorMode ? (
                  <StatusBadge status="warning" label="MONITOR ONLY" />
                ) : null}
              </View>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                Coordinator: {event.coordinatorName}
              </AppText>
            </View>

            <AppText
              variant="heading"
              weight="bold"
              color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
              style={styles.title}
            >
              {event.title}
            </AppText>

            <AppText
              variant="body"
              color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
              style={styles.description}
            >
              {event.description}
            </AppText>

            <View style={styles.categoriesRow}>
              {event.charityFields.map(code => (
                <View
                  key={code}
                  style={[
                    styles.categoryPill,
                    {
                      backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                      borderColor: theme.colors.border,
                      borderRadius: theme.radius.sm,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <AppText
                    variant="caption"
                    weight="semibold"
                    color={theme.colors.primary}
                  >
                    {code}
                  </AppText>
                </View>
              ))}
            </View>
          </AppCard>

          {/* Lifecycle Transitions Control Bar */}
          <SectionHeader title="Event Lifecycle Actions" />
          <AppCard style={styles.cardSpacing}>
            {isFinalState ? (
              <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                Event is in final {event.status.toUpperCase()} state. No further
                lifecycle transitions are permitted.
              </AppText>
            ) : isMonitorMode ? (
              <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.accent}>
                Write and status transition actions are disabled for owner in
                monitor mode.
              </AppText>
            ) : (
              <View style={styles.actionsBar}>
                {/* Draft Actions */}
                {event.status === 'draft' && (
                  <>
                    <AppButton
                      label="Publish Event"
                      variant="primary"
                      disabled={isMonitorMode}
                      onPress={() => setPendingTargetStatus('published')}
                    />
                    <AppButton
                      label="Cancel Event"
                      variant="danger"
                      disabled={isMonitorMode}
                      onPress={() => setPendingTargetStatus('cancelled')}
                    />
                  </>
                )}

                {/* Published Actions */}
                {event.status === 'published' && (
                  <>
                    <AppButton
                      label="Activate Event (Shift Live)"
                      variant="primary"
                      disabled={isMonitorMode}
                      onPress={() => setPendingTargetStatus('active')}
                    />
                    <AppButton
                      label="Cancel Event"
                      variant="danger"
                      disabled={isMonitorMode}
                      onPress={() => setPendingTargetStatus('cancelled')}
                    />
                  </>
                )}

                {/* Active Actions */}
                {event.status === 'active' && (
                  <>
                    <AppButton
                      label="Mark Completed"
                      variant="primary"
                      disabled={isMonitorMode}
                      onPress={() => setPendingTargetStatus('completed')}
                    />
                    <AppButton
                      label="Cancel Event"
                      variant="danger"
                      disabled={isMonitorMode}
                      onPress={() => setPendingTargetStatus('cancelled')}
                    />
                  </>
                )}
              </View>
            )}
          </AppCard>

          {/* Operational Metrics Panel */}
          <SectionHeader title="Operational Roster Summary" />
          <AppCard style={styles.cardSpacing}>
            <View style={styles.metricsGrid}>
              <View style={styles.metricItem}>
                <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                  Pending Apps
                </AppText>
                <AppText
                  variant="heading"
                  weight="bold"
                  color={
                    event.pendingApplicationCount > 0
                      ? theme.colors.warning
                      : theme.colors.textPrimary
                  }
                >
                  {event.pendingApplicationCount}
                </AppText>
              </View>

              <View style={styles.metricItem}>
                <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                  Accepted Roster
                </AppText>
                <AppText
                  variant="heading"
                  weight="bold"
                  color={theme.colors.primary}
                >
                  {event.acceptedApplicantCount}
                </AppText>
              </View>

              <View style={styles.metricItem}>
                <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                  Assigned Shifts
                </AppText>
                <AppText
                  variant="heading"
                  weight="bold"
                  color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
                >
                  {event.assignedCount}
                </AppText>
              </View>

              <View style={styles.metricItem}>
                <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                  Checked In
                </AppText>
                <AppText
                  variant="heading"
                  weight="bold"
                  color={theme.colors.success}
                >
                  {event.checkedInCount}
                </AppText>
              </View>
            </View>

            {/* Operational Management Quick Links */}
            <View style={styles.rosterActionButtons}>
              <View style={styles.rosterBtnCol}>
                <AppButton
                  label="Applications"
                  variant="secondary"
                  onPress={() =>
                    navigation?.navigate?.('CoordinatorApplications', {
                      eventId: event.id,
                    })
                  }
                />
              </View>
              <View style={styles.rosterBtnCol}>
                <AppButton
                  label="Assignments"
                  variant="secondary"
                  onPress={() =>
                    navigation?.navigate?.('CoordinatorAssignments', {
                      eventId: event.id,
                    })
                  }
                />
              </View>
              <View style={styles.rosterBtnCol}>
                <AppButton
                  label="Attendance"
                  variant="secondary"
                  onPress={() =>
                    navigation?.navigate?.('CoordinatorAttendance', {
                      eventId: event.id,
                    })
                  }
                />
              </View>
            </View>
          </AppCard>

          {/* Location & Rules Summary */}
          <SectionHeader title="Location & Access Configuration" />
          <AppCard style={styles.cardSpacing}>
            <View style={styles.detailRow}>
              <AppText variant="caption" weight="bold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                Location:
              </AppText>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                {event.locationName}
              </AppText>
            </View>

            {event.landmarkText ? (
              <View style={styles.detailRow}>
                <AppText variant="caption" weight="bold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                  Landmark:
                </AppText>
                <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                  {event.landmarkText}
                </AppText>
              </View>
            ) : null}

            <View style={styles.detailRow}>
              <AppText variant="caption" weight="bold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                Geo Check-In:
              </AppText>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                {event.geoCheckinEnabled
                  ? `Enabled (${event.geoRadiusM}m radius, ${event.geoCheckinOpensBeforeMin} min buffer)`
                  : 'Disabled'}
              </AppText>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.detailRow}>
              <AppText variant="caption" weight="bold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                Schedule:
              </AppText>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                {event.startAt} to {event.endAt}
              </AppText>
            </View>
          </AppCard>
        </ScrollView>

        {/* Consequential Transition Confirmation Modal */}
        <Modal
          visible={Boolean(pendingTargetStatus)}
          transparent
          animationType="fade"
          onRequestClose={() => setPendingTargetStatus(null)}
        >
          <View style={styles.modalBackdrop}>
            <View
              style={[
                styles.modalCard,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface,
                  borderRadius: theme.radius.lg,
                  borderColor: theme.colors.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
                <AppText variant="title" weight="bold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                  Confirm Status Transition
                </AppText>
                <Pressable
                  accessibilityRole="button"
                  accessibilityLabel="Close confirmation"
                  onPress={() => setPendingTargetStatus(null)}
                  hitSlop={8}
                >
                  <AppIcon name="close" size={20} color={theme.colors.textSecondary} />
                </Pressable>
              </View>

              <View
                style={[
                  styles.demoWarningBox,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                    borderColor: theme.colors.warning,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <AppText
                  variant="caption"
                  weight="bold"
                  color={theme.colors.warning}
                >
                  ⚠️ Demo Preview — not submitted to server
                </AppText>
                <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                  This transition will update local mock state for this preview
                  session.
                </AppText>
              </View>

              <AppText variant="body" color={theme.colors.textPrimary} style={styles.confirmPrompt}>
                Are you sure you want to transition this event from{' '}
                <AppText variant="body" weight="bold">
                  {event.status.toUpperCase()}
                </AppText>{' '}
                to{' '}
                <AppText variant="body" weight="bold">
                  {pendingTargetStatus?.toUpperCase()}
                </AppText>
                ?
              </AppText>

              <View style={styles.modalActionsRow}>
                <View style={styles.modalButtonWrap}>
                  <AppButton
                    label="Cancel"
                    variant="outline"
                    onPress={() => setPendingTargetStatus(null)}
                  />
                </View>
                <View style={styles.modalButtonWrap}>
                  <AppButton
                    label="Confirm (Demo)"
                    variant={
                      pendingTargetStatus === 'cancelled'
                        ? 'danger'
                        : 'primary'
                    }
                    loading={isTransitioning}
                    onPress={handleConfirmTransition}
                  />
                </View>
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>
        </Modal>
      </View>
    </CoordinatorGuard>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  scrollContent: {
    paddingBottom: 40,
  },
  feedbackBanner: {
    padding: 12,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderRadius: 8,
    marginBottom: 16,
  },
  monitorBanner: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'flex-start',
    padding: 14,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderRadius: 8,
    gap: 10,
    marginBottom: 16,

/**
 * @format
 */

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import {
  FlatList,
  RefreshControl,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import type { CoordinatorRepository } from '../../../data/contracts/coordinatorRepository';
import { defaultCoordinatorRepository } from '../../../data/mock/MockCoordinatorRepository';
import { useRepositories } from '../../../app/RepositoryProvider';
import type {
  CoordinatorEventStatus,
  CoordinatorEventSummary,
} from '../../../domain/models/coordinator';
import type { MainDrawerScreenProps } from '../../../navigation/navigationTypes';
import {
  AppButton,
  AppHeader,
  EmptyState,
  ErrorState,
  FilterChip,
  LoadingState,
} from '../../../shared';
import { useAppTheme } from '../../../theme';
import {
  CoordinatorEventCard,
  CoordinatorGuard,
} from '../components';

export interface CoordinatorEventsScreenProps
  extends Partial<MainDrawerScreenProps<'CoordinatorEvents'>> {
  repository?: CoordinatorRepository;
}

type FilterStatus = 'all' | CoordinatorEventStatus | 'monitor';

export function CoordinatorEventsScreen({
  navigation,
  repository: propRepository,
}: CoordinatorEventsScreenProps): React.JSX.Element {
  const { theme } = useAppTheme();
  const repositories = useRepositories();
  const repository = propRepository ?? repositories.coordinatorRepository ?? defaultCoordinatorRepository;

  const [events, setEvents] = useState<CoordinatorEventSummary[]>([]);
  const [selectedFilter, setSelectedFilter] = useState<FilterStatus>('all');
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isRefreshing, setIsRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const fetchEvents = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setErrorMessage(null);
      const data = await repository.getMyEvents();
      setEvents(data);
    } catch (err) {
      setErrorMessage(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load coordinator events',
      );
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
      setIsRefreshing(false);
    }
  }, [repository]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchEvents();
  }, [fetchEvents]);

  const handleRefresh = () => {
    setIsRefreshing(true);
    fetchEvents();
  };

  const filteredEvents = useMemo(() => {
    if (selectedFilter === 'all') {
      return events;
    }
    if (selectedFilter === 'monitor') {
      return events.filter(e => e.isOwnerAsMonitor);
    }
    return events.filter(e => e.status === selectedFilter);
  }, [events, selectedFilter]);

  const handleEventPress = (eventId: string) => {
    navigation?.navigate?.('CoordinatorEventDetail', { eventId });
  };

  const handleBack = () => {
    if (navigation && navigation.canGoBack()) {
      navigation.goBack();
    } else if (navigation) {
      navigation.navigate('CoordinatorWorkspace');
    }
  };

  return (
    <CoordinatorGuard navigation={navigation}>
      <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
        <AppHeader
          title="Coordinator Events"
          subtitle="Managed volunteer projects"
          onBackPress={handleBack}
          rightAction={
            <AppButton
              label="+ New"
              variant="outline"
              onPress={() => navigation?.navigate?.('CreateEventWizard')}
              style={styles.newButton}
            />
          }
        />

        {/* Filter Chips Horizontal Bar */}
        <View
          style={[
            styles.filterBar,
            {
              backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface,
              borderBottomColor: theme.colors.border,
            },
          ]}
        >
          <ScrollView
            horizontal
            showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
            contentContainerStyle={styles.filterScroll}
          >
            <FilterChip
              label="All Events"
              selected={selectedFilter === 'all'}
              onPress={() => setSelectedFilter('all')}
            />
            <FilterChip
              label="Active"
              selected={selectedFilter === 'active'}
              onPress={() => setSelectedFilter('active')}
            />
            <FilterChip
              label="Published"
              selected={selectedFilter === 'published'}
              onPress={() => setSelectedFilter('published')}
            />
            <FilterChip
              label="Draft"
              selected={selectedFilter === 'draft'}
              onPress={() => setSelectedFilter('draft')}
            />
            <FilterChip
              label="Monitor Mode"
              selected={selectedFilter === 'monitor'}
              onPress={() => setSelectedFilter('monitor')}
            />
            <FilterChip
              label="Completed"
              selected={selectedFilter === 'completed'}
              onPress={() => setSelectedFilter('completed')}
            />
          </ScrollView>
        </View>

        {/* Event List */}
        {isLoading ? (
          <LoadingState label="Loading managed events..." />
        ) : errorMessage ? (
          <ErrorState
            title="Events Unavailable"
            description={errorMessage}
            onRetry={fetchEvents}
          />
        ) : (
          <FlatList
            data={filteredEvents}
            keyExtractor={item => item.id}
            contentContainerStyle={[
              styles.listContent,
              { padding: theme.spacing.md },
            ]}
            refreshControl={
              <RefreshControl
                refreshing={isRefreshing}
                onRefresh={handleRefresh}
                tintColor={theme.colors.primary}
              />
            }
            renderItem={({ item }) => (
              <CoordinatorEventCard
                event={item}
                onPress={handleEventPress}
              />
            )}
            ListEmptyComponent={
              <EmptyState
                icon="calendar"
                title="No Events Found"
                description={
                  selectedFilter !== 'all'
                    ? 'No events match the selected status filter.'
                    : 'You have not created or been assigned any coordinator events.'
                }
                actionLabel={
                  selectedFilter !== 'all' ? 'Show All Events' : undefined
                }
                onActionPress={
                  selectedFilter !== 'all'
                    ? () => setSelectedFilter('all')
                    : undefined
                }
              />
            }
          />
        )}
      </View>
    </CoordinatorGuard>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  newButton: {
    minHeight: 32,
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
  },
  filterBar: {
    paddingVertical: 8,
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
  },
  filterScroll: {
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
    gap: 8,
  },
  listContent: {
    flexGrow: 1,
    paddingBottom: 30,
  },
});

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Pressable,
  StyleSheet,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import type { CoordinatorRepository } from '../../../data/contracts/coordinatorRepository';
import { defaultCoordinatorRepository } from '../../../data/mock/MockCoordinatorRepository';
import { useRepositories } from '../../../app/RepositoryProvider';
import type {
  CoordinatorEventSummary,
  CoordinatorOverviewMetrics,
} from '../../../domain/models/coordinator';
import type { MainDrawerScreenProps } from '../../../navigation/navigationTypes';
import {
  AppButton,
  AppCard,
  AppHeader,
  AppIcon,
  AppText,
  ErrorState,
  LoadingState,
  ScreenContainer,
  SectionHeader,
  SegmentedControl,
} from '../../../shared';
import { hexToRgba, useAppTheme } from '../../../theme';
import {
  CoordinatorEventCard,
  CoordinatorGuard,
  CoordinatorMetricCard,
} from '../components';

export interface CoordinatorOverviewScreenProps
  extends Partial<MainDrawerScreenProps<'CoordinatorWorkspace'>> {
  repository?: CoordinatorRepository;
}

export function CoordinatorOverviewScreen({
  navigation,
  repository: propRepository,
}: CoordinatorOverviewScreenProps): React.JSX.Element {
  const { theme, isDark } = useAppTheme();
  const repositories = useRepositories();
  const repository = propRepository ?? repositories.coordinatorRepository ?? defaultCoordinatorRepository;

  const [metrics, setMetrics] = useState<CoordinatorOverviewMetrics | null>(null);
  const [events, setEvents] = useState<CoordinatorEventSummary[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [activeSegment, setActiveSegment] = useState<'applications' | 'assignments' | 'attendance'>('applications');

  const loadDashboard = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setErrorMessage(null);
      const [m, evts] = await Promise.all([
        repository.getOverviewMetrics(),
        repository.getMyEvents(),
      ]);
      setMetrics(m);
      setEvents(evts);
    } catch (err) {
      setErrorMessage(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load workspace data',
      );
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [repository]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadDashboard();
  }, [loadDashboard]);

  const handleEventPress = (eventId: string) => {
    navigation?.navigate?.('CoordinatorEventDetail', { eventId });
  };

  const handleSegmentSelect = (key: 'applications' | 'assignments' | 'attendance') => {
    setActiveSegment(key);
    if (key === 'applications') {
      navigation?.navigate?.('CoordinatorApplications');
    } else if (key === 'assignments') {
      navigation?.navigate?.('CoordinatorAssignments');
    } else if (key === 'attendance') {
      navigation?.navigate?.('CoordinatorAttendance');
    }
  };

  return (
    <CoordinatorGuard navigation={navigation}>
      <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
        <AppHeader
          title="Coordinator Workspace"
          subtitle="Events & roster operations"
          onMenuPress={navigation ? () => navigation.openDrawer() : undefined}
          rightAction={
            <AppButton
              label="+ New"
              variant="outline"
              onPress={() => navigation?.navigate?.('CreateEventWizard')}
              style={styles.newHeaderButton}
            />
          }
        />

        {isLoading ? (
          <LoadingState label="Loading coordinator metrics..." />
        ) : errorMessage || !metrics ? (
          <ErrorState
            title="Workspace Unavailable"
            description={errorMessage ?? 'Could not load coordinator metrics.'}
            onRetry={loadDashboard}
          />
        ) : (
          <ScreenContainer scroll style={styles.scroll}>
            {/* Action Needed Card (.alert) */}
            {metrics.pendingApplicationsCount > 0 ? (
              <AppCard
                variant="alert"
                style={styles.alertCard}
              >
                <View style={styles.alertRow}>
                  <View
                    style={[
                      styles.alertIconBox,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: hexToRgba(theme.colors.warning, isDark ? 0.30 : 0.18),
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <AppIcon name="alert" size={18} color={theme.colors.warning} />
                  </View>

                  <View style={styles.alertTextCol}>
                    <AppText
                      variant="title"
                      weight="extrabold"
                      color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
                      style={styles.alertTitle}
                    >
                      Action Needed: {metrics.pendingApplicationsCount} Pending Applications
                    </AppText>
                    <AppText
                      variant="caption"
                      color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                      style={styles.alertSub}
                    >
                      Waiting for roster placement and team assignments.
                    </AppText>
                  </View>

                  <AppButton
                    label="Review"
                    variant="warning"
                    onPress={() => navigation?.navigate?.('CoordinatorApplications')}
                    style={styles.reviewButton}
                  />
                </View>
              </AppCard>
            ) : null}

            {/* Metrics 2x2 Grid (.grid2) */}
            <SectionHeader title="Operational Metrics" />
            <View style={styles.metricGrid}>
              <View style={styles.metricCol}>
                <CoordinatorMetricCard
                  label="Total Events"
                  value={metrics.totalEventsCount}
                  icon="calendar"
                  color={theme.colors.sky ?? theme.colors.accent}
                  subtext={`${metrics.activeEventsCount} currently live`}
                />
              </View>
              <View style={styles.metricCol}>
                <CoordinatorMetricCard
                  label="Pending Apps"
                  value={metrics.pendingApplicationsCount}
                  icon="user"
                  color={theme.colors.amber ?? theme.colors.warning}
                  subtext="Review required"
                />
              </View>
              <View style={styles.metricCol}>
                <CoordinatorMetricCard
                  label="Accepted Roster"
                  value={metrics.acceptedApplicantsCount}
                  icon="check"
                  color={theme.colors.emerald ?? theme.colors.success}
                  subtext="Volunteers placed"
                />
              </View>
              <View style={styles.metricCol}>
                <CoordinatorMetricCard
                  label="Checked-in Today"
                  value={metrics.checkedInTodayCount}
                  icon="star"
                  color={theme.colors.indigo ?? theme.colors.primary}
                  subtext="Active on-site"
                />
              </View>
            </View>

            {/* Primary Action Button */}
            <View style={styles.createCtaContainer}>
              <AppButton
                label="Launch Create Event Wizard"
                variant="primary"
                leftIcon="plus"
                onPress={() => navigation?.navigate?.('CreateEventWizard')}
              />
            </View>

            {/* Operational Management Segmented Control (.seg) */}
            <SectionHeader title="Operational Roster Management" />
            <SegmentedControl
              options={[
                {
                  key: 'applications',
                  label: 'Applications',
                  count: metrics.pendingApplicationsCount,
                },
                {
                  key: 'assignments',
                  label: 'Assignments',
                  count: metrics.acceptedApplicantsCount,
                },
                {
                  key: 'attendance',
                  label: 'Attendance',
                  count: metrics.checkedInTodayCount,
                },
              ]}
              selectedKey={activeSegment}
              onSelect={handleSegmentSelect}
              style={styles.segmentedControl}
            />

            {/* Managed Events Section */}
            <SectionHeader
              title="My Managed Events"
              actionLabel="View All"
              onActionPress={() => navigation?.navigate?.('CoordinatorEvents')}
            />

            {events.slice(0, 4).map(event => (
              <CoordinatorEventCard
                key={event.id}
                event={event}
                onPress={handleEventPress}
              />
            ))}
          </ScreenContainer>
        )}
      </View>
    </CoordinatorGuard>
  );
}
