/**
 * @format
 */

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Modal,
  Pressable,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import type { EventRepository } from '../../../data/contracts/eventRepository';
import {
  defaultEventRepository,
  MockEventRepository,
} from '../../../data/mock/MockEventRepository';
import { isMockMode } from '../../../app/configuration/dataMode';
import { useRepositories } from '../../../app/RepositoryProvider';
import {
  CHARITY_CATEGORIES,
  CharityCategoryCode,
  EventDetail,
  EventPost,
} from '../../../domain/models/event';
import type { MainDrawerScreenProps } from '../../../navigation/navigationTypes';
import {
  AppButton,
  AppCard,
  AppHeader,
  AppIcon,
  AppInput,
  AppText,
  ErrorState,
  LoadingState,
  SectionHeader,
  StatusBadge,
} from '../../../shared';
import { useAppTheme } from '../../../theme';
import { EventPostCard } from '../components/EventPostCard';

export interface EventDetailScreenProps
  extends Partial<MainDrawerScreenProps<'EventDetail'>> {
  eventId?: string;
  repository?: EventRepository;
}

function formatDateRange(startIso: string, endIso: string): string {
  try {
    const start = new Date(startIso);
    const end = new Date(endIso);
    const dateStr = start.toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
      weekday: 'short',
      month: 'short',
      day: 'numeric',
      year: 'numeric',
    });
    const startTime = start.toLocaleTimeString(undefined, {
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit',
    });
    const endTime = end.toLocaleTimeString(undefined, {
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit',
    });
    return `${dateStr} • ${startTime} - ${endTime}`;
  } catch {
    return `${startIso} - ${endIso}`;
  }
}

function getCategoryLabel(code: CharityCategoryCode): string {
  const match = CHARITY_CATEGORIES.find(c => c.code === code);
  return match ? match.label : code;
}

export function EventDetailScreen({
  route,
  navigation,
  eventId: propEventId,
  repository: propRepository,
}: EventDetailScreenProps): React.JSX.Element {
  const { theme } = useAppTheme();
  const repositories = useRepositories();
  const repository = propRepository ?? repositories.eventRepository ?? defaultEventRepository;
  const eventId = propEventId ?? route?.params?.eventId;

  const [event, setEvent] = useState<EventDetail | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Apply Modal state
  const [selectedPostForApply, setSelectedPostForApply] =
    useState<EventPost | null>(null);
  const [volunteerNote, setVolunteerNote] = useState('');
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [feedbackBanner, setFeedbackBanner] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const fetchDetail = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!eventId) {
      setErrorMessage('No event ID provided');
      setIsLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    try {
      setErrorMessage(null);
      const data = await repository.getEventById(eventId);
      if (!data) {
        setErrorMessage('Event not found or no longer available.');
      } else {
        setEvent(data);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setErrorMessage(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load event details',
      );
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [eventId, repository]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchDetail();
  }, [fetchDetail]);

  const handleApplyPress = (post: EventPost) => {
    setSelectedPostForApply(post);
    setVolunteerNote('');
  };

  const handleConfirmApply = async () => {
    if (!event || !selectedPostForApply) {
      return;
    }

    setIsSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const result = await repository.applyToPost(
        event.id,
        selectedPostForApply.id,
        volunteerNote,
      );

      if (result.success) {
        setFeedbackBanner(
          isMockMode()
            ? `Application recorded locally (${result.message})`
            : result.message,
        );
        setSelectedPostForApply(null);
        // Refresh detail to get updated application and capacity
        await fetchDetail();
      } else {
        setFeedbackBanner(result.message);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setFeedbackBanner(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Application submission failed',
      );
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleBack = () => {
    const returnTo = route?.params?.returnTo;
    if (returnTo && navigation) {
      navigation.navigate(returnTo);
    } else if (navigation && navigation.canGoBack()) {
      navigation.goBack();
    } else if (navigation) {
      navigation.navigate('Events');
    }
  };

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
        <AppHeader title="Event Details" onBackPress={handleBack} />
        <LoadingState label="Loading event information..." />
      </View>
    );
  }

  if (errorMessage || !event) {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
        <AppHeader title="Event Details" onBackPress={handleBack} />
        <ErrorState
          title="Event Not Found"
          description={errorMessage ?? 'Could not find the requested event.'}
          onRetry={fetchDetail}
        />
      </View>
    );
  }

  const isLive = event.status === 'active';
  const statusLabel = isLive ? 'Live / Active Shift' : 'Published';
  const userApplication = event.userApplication;

  return (
    <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
      <AppHeader
        title="Event Details"
        subtitle={event.title}
        onBackPress={handleBack}
      />

      <ScrollView
        contentContainerStyle={[
          styles.scrollContent,
          { padding: theme.spacing.md },
        ]}
      >
        {/* Feedback / Demo confirmation banner */}
        {feedbackBanner ? (
          <View
            style={[
              styles.feedbackBanner,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                borderColor: theme.colors.primary,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <AppText
              variant="caption"
              weight="bold"
              color={theme.colors.primary}
            >
              {feedbackBanner}
            </AppText>
          </View>
        ) : null}

        {/* Title and Header Card */}
        <AppCard style={styles.sectionCard}>
          <View style={styles.titleHeaderRow}>
            <StatusBadge
              status={isLive ? 'info' : 'success'}
              label={statusLabel}
            />
            <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
              Organized by {event.coordinatorName}
            </AppText>
          </View>

          <AppText
            variant="title"
            weight="bold"
            color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
            style={styles.eventTitle}
          >
            {event.title}
          </AppText>

          {/* Charity Categories */}
          <View style={styles.categoriesRow}>
            {event.charityFields.map(code => (
              <View
                key={code}
                style={[
                  styles.categoryPill,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                    borderColor: theme.colors.border,
                    borderRadius: theme.radius.sm,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <AppText
                  variant="caption"
                  weight="semibold"
                  color={theme.colors.primary}
                >
                  {getCategoryLabel(code)}
                </AppText>
              </View>
            ))}
          </View>
        </AppCard>

        {/* User Application Status Panel */}
        <AppCard
          style={[
            styles.sectionCard,
            {
              backgroundColor: userApplication
                ? theme.colors.surfaceRaised
                : theme.colors.surface,
              borderColor: userApplication
                ? theme.colors.primary
                : theme.colors.border,
            },
          ]}
        >
          <View style={styles.appStatusHeader}>
            <AppText
              variant="title"
              weight="bold"
              color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
            >
              Your Application Status
            </AppText>
            {userApplication ? (
              <StatusBadge
                status={
                  userApplication.status === 'accepted'
                    ? 'success'
                    : userApplication.status === 'pending'
                    ? 'warning'
                    : 'info'
                }
                label={userApplication.status.toUpperCase()}
              />
            ) : null}
          </View>

          {userApplication ? (
            <View style={styles.appStatusBody}>
              <AppText variant="body" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                Applied for role:{' '}
                <AppText variant="body" weight="bold">
                  {userApplication.postTitle}
                </AppText>
              </AppText>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                Submitted on{' '}
                {new Date(userApplication.appliedAt).toLocaleDateString()} • Demo
                mode
              </AppText>
              {userApplication.volunteerNote ? (
                <AppText
                  variant="caption"
                  color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                  style={styles.noteText}
                >
                  Your note: "{userApplication.volunteerNote}"
                </AppText>
              ) : null}
            </View>
          ) : (
            <AppText variant="body" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
              You have not applied to any roles in this event yet. Browse the
              positions below to apply.
            </AppText>
          )}
        </AppCard>

        {/* Schedule & Timing */}
        <AppCard style={styles.sectionCard}>
          <SectionHeader title="Schedule & Timing" />
          <View style={styles.infoRow}>
            <AppIcon
              name="calendar"
              size={18}
              color={theme.colors.primary}
              style={styles.infoIcon}
            />
            <View style={styles.infoTextColumn}>
              <AppText variant="caption" weight="bold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                Event Window
              </AppText>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                {formatDateRange(event.startAt, event.endAt)}
              </AppText>
            </View>
          </View>

          {event.applicationDeadline ? (
            <View style={[styles.infoRow, styles.deadlineRow]}>
              <AppIcon
                name="alert"
                size={18}
                color={theme.colors.warning}
                style={styles.infoIcon}
              />
              <View style={styles.infoTextColumn}>
                <AppText variant="caption" weight="bold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                  Application Deadline
                </AppText>
                <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                  {new Date(event.applicationDeadline).toLocaleDateString()} at{' '}
                  {new Date(event.applicationDeadline).toLocaleTimeString()}
                </AppText>
              </View>
            </View>
          ) : null}
        </AppCard>

        {/* Location & Landmark Card */}
        <AppCard style={styles.sectionCard}>
          <SectionHeader title="Location & Access" />
          <View style={styles.infoRow}>
            <AppIcon
              name="location"
              size={18}
              color={theme.colors.primary}
              style={styles.infoIcon}
            />
            <View style={styles.infoTextColumn}>
              <AppText variant="title" weight="bold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                {event.locationName}
              </AppText>
              {event.landmarkText ? (
                <AppText
                  variant="caption"
                  color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                  style={styles.landmarkText}
                >
                  Landmark: {event.landmarkText}
                </AppText>
              ) : null}
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* Map Preview Placeholder Box */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.mapPreviewBox,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                borderColor: theme.colors.border,
                borderRadius: theme.radius.md,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <AppIcon name="map" size={28} color={theme.colors.primary} />
            <AppText
              variant="caption"
              weight="bold"
              color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
              style={styles.mapTitle}
            >
              OpenStreetMap Geographic Preview
            </AppText>
            <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
              Coordinates: {event.latitude ?? 16.8000}°,{' '}
              {event.longitude ?? 96.1500}°
            </AppText>
            <AppText
              variant="caption"
              color={theme.colors.muted}
              style={styles.mapDisclaimer}
            >
              Interactive map SDK not connected in UI preview
            </AppText>
          </View>
        </AppCard>

        {/* Description & Purpose */}
        <AppCard style={styles.sectionCard}>
          <SectionHeader title="About this Event" />
          <AppText
            variant="body"
            color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
            style={styles.bodyDescription}
          >
            {event.description}
          </AppText>
        </AppCard>

        {/* Volunteer Support & Compensation */}
        <AppCard style={styles.sectionCard}>
          <SectionHeader title="Volunteer Support & Amenities" />
          <View style={styles.perksGrid}>
            <View style={styles.perkItem}>
              <AppText variant="caption" weight="bold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                Meals:
              </AppText>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                {event.mealsProvided ? 'Provided on-site' : 'Not provided'}
              </AppText>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.perkItem}>
              <AppText variant="caption" weight="bold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                Lodging:
              </AppText>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                {event.lodgingProvided ? 'Accommodations covered' : 'None'}
              </AppText>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.perkItem}>
              <AppText variant="caption" weight="bold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                Travel Support:
              </AppText>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                {event.travelSupportProvided ? 'Shuttle/stipend provided' : 'Self travel'}
              </AppText>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.perkItem}>
              <AppText variant="caption" weight="bold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                Compensation:
              </AppText>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                {event.compensationKind.toUpperCase()}
              </AppText>
            </View>
          </View>

          {event.compensationNote ? (
            <View
              style={[
                styles.compensationNoteBox,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                  borderRadius: theme.radius.sm,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                Note: {event.compensationNote}
              </AppText>
            </View>
          ) : null}
        </AppCard>

        {/* Event Roles / Posts */}
        <SectionHeader
          title={`Available Roles & Posts (${event.posts.length})`}
        />

        {event.posts.map(post => {
          const isAppliedThisPost =
            userApplication?.postId === post.id;
          return (
            <EventPostCard
              key={post.id}
              post={post}
              isApplied={Boolean(isAppliedThisPost)}
              onApplyPress={handleApplyPress}
            />
          );
        })}
      </ScrollView>

      {/* Demo Apply Confirmation Modal */}
      <Modal
        visible={Boolean(selectedPostForApply)}
        transparent
        animationType="fade"
        onRequestClose={() => setSelectedPostForApply(null)}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalBackdrop}>
          <View
            style={[
              styles.modalCard,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface,
                borderRadius: theme.radius.lg,
                borderColor: theme.colors.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
              <AppText variant="title" weight="bold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                Apply for Volunteer Role
              </AppText>
              <Pressable
                accessibilityRole="button"
                accessibilityLabel="Cancel application"
                onPress={() => setSelectedPostForApply(null)}
                hitSlop={8}
              >
                <AppIcon name="close" size={20} color={theme.colors.textSecondary} />
              </Pressable>
            </View>

            {/* Prominent Demo Warning */}
            {isMockMode() ? (
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.demoAlertBox,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                    borderColor: theme.colors.warning,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <AppText
                  variant="caption"
                  weight="bold"
                  color={theme.colors.warning}
                  style={styles.demoWarningTitle}
                >
                  ⚠️ Demo only — not submitted to server
                </AppText>
                <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                  This is a UI preview. Your application will be stored in local
                  mock state for this session.
                </AppText>
              </View>
            ) : null}

            {/* Selected Post Summary */}
            <View style={styles.modalPostSummary}>
              <AppText variant="body" weight="bold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                {selectedPostForApply?.title}
              </AppText>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                Physical Level: {selectedPostForApply?.physicalLevel.toUpperCase()}
              </AppText>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                Remaining spots: {selectedPostForApply?.remainingCapacity}
              </AppText>
            </View>

            {/* Optional Volunteer Note */}
            <AppInput
              label="Volunteer Note (Optional)"
              placeholder="e.g. Relevant experience or availability details..."
              value={volunteerNote}
              onChangeText={setVolunteerNote}
              numberOfLines={3}
              multiline
              style={styles.modalInput}
            />

            {/* Modal Actions */}
            <View style={styles.modalActionsRow}>
              <View style={styles.modalButtonWrap}>
                <AppButton
                  label="Cancel"
                  variant="outline"
                  onPress={() => setSelectedPostForApply(null)}
                />
              </View>
              <View style={styles.modalButtonWrap}>
                <AppButton
                  label="Confirm Application"
                  variant="primary"
                  loading={isSubmitting}
                  onPress={handleConfirmApply}
                />
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  scrollContent: {
    paddingBottom: 40,
  },
  feedbackBanner: {
    padding: 12,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderRadius: 8,
    marginBottom: 12,
  },
  sectionCard: {
    marginBottom: 12,
  },
  titleHeaderRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    alignItems: 'center',
    marginBottom: 8,
  },
  eventTitle: {
    marginBottom: 10,
  },
  categoriesRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    flexWrap: 'wrap',
    gap: 6,
  },
  categoryPill: {
    paddingHorizontal: 8,
    paddingVertical: 3,
    borderWidth: 1,
  },
  appStatusHeader: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    alignItems: 'center',

/**
 * @format
 */

import React, {
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useRef,
  useState,
} from 'react';
import {
  FlatList,
  NativeScrollEvent,
  NativeSyntheticEvent,
  Pressable,
  RefreshControl,
  StyleSheet,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import type { EventRepository } from '../../../data/contracts/eventRepository';
import {
  defaultEventRepository,
  MockEventRepository,
} from '../../../data/mock/MockEventRepository';
import { useRepositories } from '../../../app/RepositoryProvider';
import {
  EventFilters,
  EventSummary,
} from '../../../domain/models/event';
import type { MainDrawerScreenProps } from '../../../navigation/navigationTypes';
import {
  AppButton,
  AppHeader,
  AppIcon,
  AppText,
  EmptyState,
  ErrorState,
  LoadingState,
  SearchInput,
} from '../../../shared';
import { useAppTheme } from '../../../theme';
import {
  EventCard,
  EventFilterSheet,
} from '../components';

export interface DiscoveryStateCache {
  searchQuery: string;
  activeFilters: EventFilters;
  scrollOffset: number;
}

let discoveryCache: DiscoveryStateCache = {
  searchQuery: '',
  activeFilters: {},
  scrollOffset: 0,
};

export function resetDiscoveryCache(): void {
  discoveryCache = {
    searchQuery: '',
    activeFilters: {},
    scrollOffset: 0,
  };
}

export function getDiscoveryCache(): DiscoveryStateCache {
  return { ...discoveryCache };
}

export interface EventDiscoveryScreenProps
  extends Partial<MainDrawerScreenProps<'Events'>> {
  repository?: EventRepository;
}

export function EventDiscoveryScreen({
  navigation,
  repository: propRepository,
}: EventDiscoveryScreenProps): React.JSX.Element {
  const { theme } = useAppTheme();
  const repositories = useRepositories();
  const repository = propRepository ?? repositories.eventRepository ?? defaultEventRepository;

  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState(() => discoveryCache.searchQuery);
  const [activeFilters, setActiveFilters] = useState<EventFilters>(
    () => discoveryCache.activeFilters,
  );
  const [isFilterSheetVisible, setIsFilterSheetVisible] = useState(false);

  const [events, setEvents] = useState<EventSummary[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isRefreshing, setIsRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const flatListRef = useRef<FlatList<EventSummary>>(null);

  // Compute combined filter query
  const effectiveFilters = useMemo((): EventFilters => {
    return {
      ...activeFilters,
      query: searchQuery.trim().length > 0 ? searchQuery : undefined,
    };
  }, [activeFilters, searchQuery]);

  const activeFilterCount = useMemo(() => {
    let count = 0;
    if (activeFilters.statuses && activeFilters.statuses.length > 0) {
      count += activeFilters.statuses.length;
    }
    if (
      activeFilters.charityFieldCodes &&
      activeFilters.charityFieldCodes.length > 0
    ) {
      count += activeFilters.charityFieldCodes.length;
    }
    if (activeFilters.mealsProvided) {
      count += 1;
    }
    if (activeFilters.lodgingProvided) {
      count += 1;
    }
    if (activeFilters.travelSupportProvided) {
      count += 1;
    }
    return count;
  }, [activeFilters]);

  const fetchEvents = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setErrorMessage(null);
      const data = await repository.getEvents(effectiveFilters);
      setEvents(data);
    } catch (err) {
      setErrorMessage(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load volunteer events',
      );
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
      setIsRefreshing(false);
    }
  }, [effectiveFilters, repository]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchEvents();
  }, [fetchEvents]);

  // Restore scroll offset if returning to a populated list
  useEffect(() => {
    if (events.length > 0 && discoveryCache.scrollOffset > 0) {
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        flatListRef.current?.scrollToOffset({
          offset: discoveryCache.scrollOffset,
          animated: false,
        });
      });
    }
  }, [events.length]);

  const handleRefresh = () => {
    setIsRefreshing(true);
    fetchEvents();
  };

  const handleSearchChange = (text: string) => {
    setSearchQuery(text);
    discoveryCache.searchQuery = text;
  };

  const handleApplyFilters = (newFilters: EventFilters) => {
    setActiveFilters(newFilters);
    discoveryCache.activeFilters = newFilters;
  };

  const handleClearAll = () => {
    setSearchQuery('');
    setActiveFilters({});
    discoveryCache.searchQuery = '';
    discoveryCache.activeFilters = {};
  };

  const handleScroll = (event: NativeSyntheticEvent<NativeScrollEvent>) => {
    discoveryCache.scrollOffset = event.nativeEvent.contentOffset.y;
  };

  const handleEventPress = (eventId: string) => {
    if (navigation) {
      navigation.navigate('EventDetail', {
        eventId,
        returnTo: 'Events',
      });
    }
  };

  const hasAnyFilter = searchQuery.trim().length > 0 || activeFilterCount > 0;

  return (
    <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
      <AppHeader
        title="Discover Events"
        subtitle="Public volunteer opportunities"
        onMenuPress={navigation ? () => navigation.openDrawer() : undefined}
      />

      {/* Single Search & Filter Row */}
      <View
        style={[
          styles.searchRow,
          {
            backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface,
            borderBottomColor: theme.colors.border,
          },
        ]}
      >
        <View style={styles.searchInputWrapper}>
          <SearchInput
            value={searchQuery}
            onChangeText={handleSearchChange}
            onClear={() => handleSearchChange('')}
            placeholder="Search by title, location, or landmark..."
          />
        </View>

        <Pressable
          accessibilityRole="button"
          accessibilityLabel={`Filter events. ${activeFilterCount} active filters.`}
          onPress={() => setIsFilterSheetVisible(true)}
          hitSlop={8}
          style={[
            styles.filterButton,
            {
              backgroundColor:
                activeFilterCount > 0
                  ? theme.colors.primary
                  : theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
              borderColor:
                activeFilterCount > 0
                  ? theme.colors.primary
                  : theme.colors.border,
              borderRadius: theme.radius.sm,
            },
          ]}
        >
          <AppIcon
            name="filter"
            size={18}
            color={
              activeFilterCount > 0
                ? theme.colors.onPrimary
                : theme.colors.textPrimary
            }
          />
          {activeFilterCount > 0 ? (
            <View
              style={[
                styles.filterBadge,
                { backgroundColor: theme.colors.accent },
              ]}
            >
              <AppText
                variant="caption"
                weight="bold"
                color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
                style={styles.filterBadgeText}
              >
                {activeFilterCount}
              </AppText>
            </View>
          ) : null}
        </Pressable>
      </View>

      {/* Active Filters Summary Bar */}
      {hasAnyFilter ? (
        <View
          style={[
            styles.activeFiltersBar,
            {
              backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
              borderBottomColor: theme.colors.border,
            },
          ]}
        >
          <AppText
            variant="caption"
            weight="semibold"
            color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
          >
            Showing filtered results ({events.length} found)
          </AppText>
          <AppButton
            label="Clear all"
            variant="ghost"
            onPress={handleClearAll}
            style={styles.clearAllButton}
          />
        </View>
      ) : null}

      {/* Content Area */}
      {isLoading ? (
        <LoadingState label="Loading published volunteer opportunities..." />
      ) : errorMessage ? (
        <ErrorState
          title="Could not load events"
          description={errorMessage}
          onRetry={fetchEvents}
        />
      ) : (
        <FlatList
          ref={flatListRef}
          data={events}
          keyExtractor={item => item.id}
          contentContainerStyle={[
            styles.listContent,
            { padding: theme.spacing.md },
          ]}
          onScroll={handleScroll}
          scrollEventThrottle={16}
          refreshControl={
            <RefreshControl
              refreshing={isRefreshing}
              onRefresh={handleRefresh}
              tintColor={theme.colors.primary}
            />
          }
          renderItem={({ item }) => (
            <EventCard event={item} onPress={handleEventPress} />
          )}
          ListEmptyComponent={
            <EmptyState
              title="No events found"
              description={
                hasAnyFilter
                  ? 'No published or active events match your current search and filter criteria.'
                  : 'There are currently no published volunteer opportunities available.'
              }
              actionLabel={hasAnyFilter ? 'Reset Filters' : undefined}
              onActionPress={hasAnyFilter ? handleClearAll : undefined}
            />
          }
        />
      )}

      {/* Filter Sheet Modal */}
      <EventFilterSheet
        visible={isFilterSheetVisible}
        filters={activeFilters}
        onClose={() => setIsFilterSheetVisible(false)}
        onApply={handleApplyFilters}
      />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  searchRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
    paddingVertical: 10,
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
    gap: 10,
  },
  searchInputWrapper: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  filterButton: {
    width: 48,
    height: 48,
    borderWidth: 1,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    position: 'relative',
  },
  filterBadge: {
    position: 'absolute',
    top: -4,
    right: -4,
    minWidth: 18,
    height: 18,
    borderRadius: 9,
    paddingHorizontal: 4,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
  },
  filterBadgeText: {
    fontSize: 10,
    lineHeight: 12,
  },
  activeFiltersBar: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    alignItems: 'center',
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
    paddingVertical: 6,
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
  },
  clearAllButton: {
    minHeight: 28,
    paddingHorizontal: 8,
  },
  listContent: {
    flexGrow: 1,
    paddingBottom: 24,
  },
});

/**
 * @format
 */

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  FlatList,
  Modal,
  NativeScrollEvent,
  NativeSyntheticEvent,
  Pressable,
  RefreshControl,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import type { EventRepository } from '../../../data/contracts/eventRepository';
import {
  defaultEventRepository,
  MockEventRepository,
} from '../../../data/mock/MockEventRepository';
import { isMockMode } from '../../../app/configuration/dataMode';
import { useRepositories } from '../../../app/RepositoryProvider';
import {
  EventApplicationStatus,
  VolunteerApplicationItem,
} from '../../../domain/models/event';
import type { MainDrawerScreenProps } from '../../../navigation/navigationTypes';
import {
  AppButton,
  AppCard,
  AppHeader,
  AppText,
  BadgeStatus,
  EmptyState,
  ErrorState,
  FilterChip,
  LoadingState,
  StatusBadge,
} from '../../../shared';
import { useAppTheme } from '../../../theme';

export interface ApplicationsStateCache {
  statusFilter: EventApplicationStatus | 'all';
  scrollOffset: number;
}

let applicationsCache: ApplicationsStateCache = {
  statusFilter: 'all',
  scrollOffset: 0,
};

export function resetApplicationsCache(): void {
  applicationsCache = {
    statusFilter: 'all',
    scrollOffset: 0,
  };
}

export function getApplicationsCache(): ApplicationsStateCache {
  return { ...applicationsCache };
}

export interface MyApplicationsScreenProps
  extends Partial<MainDrawerScreenProps<'MyApplications'>> {
  repository?: EventRepository;
}

function formatDate(isoString?: string): string {
  if (!isoString) {
    return '';
  }
  try {
    const d = new Date(isoString);
    return d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
      month: 'short',
      day: 'numeric',
      year: 'numeric',
    });
  } catch {
    return isoString;
  }
}

function getBadgeConfig(status: EventApplicationStatus): {
  badgeStatus: BadgeStatus;
  label: string;
} {
  switch (status) {
    case 'accepted':
      return { badgeStatus: 'emerald', label: 'Accepted' };
    case 'pending':
      return { badgeStatus: 'amber', label: 'Pending Review' };
    case 'waitlisted':
      return { badgeStatus: 'sky', label: 'Waitlisted' };
    case 'rejected':
      return { badgeStatus: 'rose', label: 'Not Selected' };
    case 'withdrawn':
      return { badgeStatus: 'muted', label: 'Withdrawn' };
    case 'expired':
      return { badgeStatus: 'muted', label: 'Expired' };
    default:
      return { badgeStatus: 'muted', label: status };
  }
}

export function MyApplicationsScreen({
  navigation,
  repository: propRepository,
}: MyApplicationsScreenProps): React.JSX.Element {
  const { theme } = useAppTheme();
  const repositories = useRepositories();
  const repository = propRepository ?? repositories.eventRepository ?? defaultEventRepository;

  const [applications, setApplications] = useState<VolunteerApplicationItem[]>([]);
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<EventApplicationStatus | 'all'>(
    () => applicationsCache.statusFilter,
  );
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isRefreshing, setIsRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Withdraw application confirmation state
  const [appToWithdraw, setAppToWithdraw] =
    useState<VolunteerApplicationItem | null>(null);
  const [isWithdrawing, setIsWithdrawing] = useState(false);
  const [feedbackBanner, setFeedbackBanner] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const flatListRef = useRef<FlatList<VolunteerApplicationItem>>(null);

  const fetchApplications = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setErrorMessage(null);
      const data = await repository.getUserApplications();
      setApplications(data);
    } catch (err) {
      setErrorMessage(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load applications',
      );
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
      setIsRefreshing(false);
    }
  }, [repository]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchApplications();
  }, [fetchApplications]);

  // Restore scroll offset if returning to list
  useEffect(() => {
    if (applications.length > 0 && applicationsCache.scrollOffset > 0) {
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        flatListRef.current?.scrollToOffset({
          offset: applicationsCache.scrollOffset,
          animated: false,
        });
      });
    }
  }, [applications.length]);

  const handleRefresh = () => {
    setIsRefreshing(true);
    fetchApplications();
  };

  const handleFilterChange = (newFilter: EventApplicationStatus | 'all') => {
    setStatusFilter(newFilter);
    applicationsCache.statusFilter = newFilter;
  };

  const handleScroll = (event: NativeSyntheticEvent<NativeScrollEvent>) => {
    applicationsCache.scrollOffset = event.nativeEvent.contentOffset.y;
  };

  const handleOpenDetail = (eventId: string) => {
    if (navigation) {
      navigation.navigate('EventDetail', {
        eventId,
        returnTo: 'MyApplications',
      });
    }
  };

  const handleConfirmWithdraw = async () => {
    if (!appToWithdraw) {
      return;
    }

    setIsWithdrawing(true);
    try {
      const result = await repository.withdrawApplication(appToWithdraw.id);
      if (result.success) {
        setFeedbackBanner(
          isMockMode()
            ? `Application for "${appToWithdraw.eventTitle}" (${appToWithdraw.postTitle}) withdrawn (${result.message})`
            : result.message,
        );
        setAppToWithdraw(null);
        await fetchApplications();
      } else {
        setFeedbackBanner(result.message);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setFeedbackBanner(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to withdraw application',
      );
    } finally {
      setIsWithdrawing(false);
    }
  };

  const filteredApplications = useMemo(() => {
    if (statusFilter === 'all') {
      return applications;
    }
    return applications.filter(a => a.status === statusFilter);
  }, [applications, statusFilter]);

  const filterOptions: Array<{
    key: EventApplicationStatus | 'all';
    label: string;
  }> = [
    { key: 'all', label: 'All' },
    { key: 'pending', label: 'Pending' },
    { key: 'accepted', label: 'Accepted' },
    { key: 'waitlisted', label: 'Waitlisted' },
    { key: 'withdrawn', label: 'Withdrawn' },
  ];

  return (
    <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
      <AppHeader
        title="My Applications"
        subtitle="Submitted volunteer requests"
        onMenuPress={navigation ? () => navigation.openDrawer() : undefined}
      />

      {/* Status Filter Chip Row */}
      <View
        style={[
          styles.filtersContainer,
          {
            backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface,
            borderBottomColor: theme.colors.border,
          },
        ]}
      >
        <ScrollView
          horizontal
          showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
          contentContainerStyle={styles.filterChipsRow}
        >
          {filterOptions.map(opt => (
            <FilterChip
              key={opt.key}
              label={opt.label}
              selected={statusFilter === opt.key}
              onPress={() => handleFilterChange(opt.key)}
            />
          ))}
        </ScrollView>
      </View>

      {/* Feedback banner */}
      {feedbackBanner ? (
        <View
          style={[
            styles.feedbackBanner,
            {
              backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
              borderColor: theme.colors.primary,
            },
          ]}
        >
          <AppText
            variant="caption"
            weight="bold"
            color={theme.colors.primary}
          >
            {feedbackBanner}
          </AppText>
        </View>
      ) : null}

      {/* List content */}
      {isLoading ? (
        <LoadingState label="Loading submitted applications..." />
      ) : errorMessage ? (
        <ErrorState
          title="Could not load applications"
          description={errorMessage}
          onRetry={fetchApplications}
        />
      ) : (
        <FlatList
          ref={flatListRef}
          data={filteredApplications}
          keyExtractor={item => item.id}
          contentContainerStyle={[
            styles.listContent,
            { padding: theme.spacing.md },
          ]}
          onScroll={handleScroll}
          scrollEventThrottle={16}
          refreshControl={
            <RefreshControl
              refreshing={isRefreshing}
              onRefresh={handleRefresh}
              tintColor={theme.colors.primary}
            />
          }
          renderItem={({ item }) => {
            const badge = getBadgeConfig(item.status);
            const canWithdraw =
              item.status === 'pending' ||
              item.status === 'waitlisted' ||
              item.status === 'accepted';

            return (
              <AppCard style={styles.cardSpacing}>
                {/* Header row: Status and Applied date */}
                <View style={styles.cardHeader}>
                  <StatusBadge status={badge.badgeStatus} label={badge.label} />
                  <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                    Applied {formatDate(item.appliedAt)}
                  </AppText>
                </View>

                {/* Main Card Tap Target for Event Detail */}
                <Pressable
                  accessibilityRole="button"
                  accessibilityLabel={`View details for ${item.eventTitle}`}
                  onPress={() => handleOpenDetail(item.eventId)}
                  style={styles.cardContentPressable}
                >
                  <AppText variant="title" weight="bold" style={styles.titleSpacing}>
                    {item.eventTitle}
                  </AppText>

                  <AppText
                    variant="body"
                    color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                    style={styles.roleSpacing}
                  >
                    Applied Role: {item.postTitle}
                  </AppText>

                  {item.volunteerNote ? (
                    <AppText
                      variant="caption"
                      color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                      style={styles.noteSpacing}
                    >
                      Note: "{item.volunteerNote}"
                    </AppText>
                  ) : null}

                  <View style={styles.viewDetailLink}>
                    <AppText
                      variant="caption"
                      weight="semibold"
                      color={theme.colors.primary}
                    >
                      View Event Details →
                    </AppText>
                  </View>
                </Pressable>

                {/* Action footer */}
                {canWithdraw ? (
                  <View style={styles.cardActions}>
                    <AppButton
                      label="Withdraw application"
                      variant="outline"
                      onPress={() => setAppToWithdraw(item)}
                      accessibilityLabel={`Withdraw application for ${item.postTitle}`}
                    />
                  </View>
                ) : item.status === 'accepted' ? (
                  <View style={styles.acceptedNotice}>
                    <AppText
                      variant="caption"
                      weight="semibold"
                      color={theme.colors.success}
                    >
                      ✓ Application accepted — see My Assignments for confirmed shift.
                    </AppText>
                  </View>
                ) : null}
              </AppCard>
            );
          }}
          ListEmptyComponent={
            <EmptyState
              title="No applications found"
              description={
                statusFilter !== 'all'
                  ? `No volunteer applications match the "${statusFilter}" status filter.`
                  : 'You have not submitted any volunteer applications yet.'
              }
              actionLabel={
                statusFilter !== 'all'
                  ? 'Show All Applications'
                  : 'Discover Volunteer Opportunities'
              }
              onActionPress={
                statusFilter !== 'all'
                  ? () => handleFilterChange('all')
                  : () => navigation?.navigate?.('Events')
              }
            />
          }
        />
      )}

      {/* Withdraw confirmation modal */}
      <Modal
        visible={!!appToWithdraw}
        transparent
        animationType="fade"
        onRequestClose={() => setAppToWithdraw(null)}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalBackdrop}>
          <View
            style={[
              styles.modalCard,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface,
                borderRadius: theme.radius.md,
                borderColor: theme.colors.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <AppText variant="title" weight="bold" style={styles.modalTitle}>
              Withdraw Application
            </AppText>

            <AppText
              variant="body"
              color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
              style={styles.modalBody}
            >
              Are you sure you want to withdraw your application for "
              {appToWithdraw?.postTitle}" on "{appToWithdraw?.eventTitle}"?
            </AppText>

            {isMockMode() ? (
              <AppText
                variant="caption"
                color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                style={styles.modalDemoNotice}
              >
                Demo only — your application status will be marked withdrawn in local preview.
              </AppText>
            ) : null}

            <View style={styles.modalActions}>
              <View style={styles.modalButtonWrap}>
                <AppButton
                  label="Keep Application"
                  variant="outline"
                  onPress={() => setAppToWithdraw(null)}
                  disabled={isWithdrawing}
                />
              </View>
              <View style={styles.modalButtonWrap}>
                <AppButton
                  label="Confirm Withdrawal"
                  variant="danger"
                  onPress={handleConfirmWithdraw}
                  loading={isWithdrawing}
                />
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: {
    flex: 1,
  },

    paddingVertical: 10,

  },

  },

/**
 * @format
 */

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import {
  FlatList,
  Modal,
  NativeScrollEvent,
  NativeSyntheticEvent,
  Pressable,
  RefreshControl,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import type { EventRepository } from '../../../data/contracts/eventRepository';
import {
  defaultEventRepository,
  MockEventRepository,
} from '../../../data/mock/MockEventRepository';
import { isMockMode } from '../../../app/configuration/dataMode';
import { useRepositories } from '../../../app/RepositoryProvider';
import {
  VolunteerAssignmentItem,
  VolunteerAssignmentStatus,
} from '../../../domain/models/event';
import type { MainDrawerScreenProps } from '../../../navigation/navigationTypes';
import {
  AppButton,
  AppCard,
  AppHeader,
  AppIcon,
  AppInput,
  AppText,
  BadgeStatus,
  EmptyState,
  ErrorState,
  FilterChip,
  LoadingState,
  StatusBadge,
} from '../../../shared';
import { useAppTheme } from '../../../theme';

export type AssignmentFilterTab = 'all' | 'active' | 'completed' | 'cancelled';

export interface AssignmentsStateCache {
  statusFilter: AssignmentFilterTab;
  scrollOffset: number;
}

let assignmentsCache: AssignmentsStateCache = {
  statusFilter: 'all',
  scrollOffset: 0,
};

export function resetAssignmentsCache(): void {
  assignmentsCache = {
    statusFilter: 'all',
    scrollOffset: 0,
  };
}

export function getAssignmentsCache(): AssignmentsStateCache {
  return { ...assignmentsCache };
}

export interface MyAssignmentsScreenProps
  extends Partial<MainDrawerScreenProps<'MyAssignments'>> {
  repository?: EventRepository;
}

function formatDateTimeRange(startIso?: string, endIso?: string): string {
  if (!startIso) {
    return '';
  }
  try {
    const start = new Date(startIso);
    const dateStr = start.toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
      weekday: 'short',
      month: 'short',
      day: 'numeric',
    });
    const startTime = start.toLocaleTimeString(undefined, {
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit',
    });
    if (endIso) {
      const end = new Date(endIso);
      const endTime = end.toLocaleTimeString(undefined, {
        hour: '2-digit',
        minute: '2-digit',
      });
      return `${dateStr} • ${startTime} - ${endTime}`;
    }
    return `${dateStr} • ${startTime}`;
  } catch {
    return startIso;
  }
}

function getAssignmentBadgeConfig(
  status: VolunteerAssignmentStatus,
  attendanceStatus?: string,
): {
  badgeStatus: BadgeStatus;
  label: string;
} {
  if (status === 'completed' || attendanceStatus === 'completed') {
    return { badgeStatus: 'emerald', label: 'Attendance Verified' };
  }
  switch (status) {
    case 'confirmed':
      return { badgeStatus: 'emerald', label: 'Confirmed Shift' };
    case 'assigned':
      return { badgeStatus: 'sky', label: 'Assigned Shift' };
    case 'cancelled':
      return { badgeStatus: 'rose', label: 'Participation Cancelled' };
    default:
      return { badgeStatus: 'muted', label: status };
  }
}

export function MyAssignmentsScreen({
  navigation,
  repository: propRepository,
}: MyAssignmentsScreenProps): React.JSX.Element {
  const { theme } = useAppTheme();
  const repositories = useRepositories();
  const repository = propRepository ?? repositories.eventRepository ?? defaultEventRepository;

  const [assignments, setAssignments] = useState<VolunteerAssignmentItem[]>([]);
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<AssignmentFilterTab>(
    () => assignmentsCache.statusFilter,
  );
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isRefreshing, setIsRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Cancel participation confirmation state
  const [assignmentToCancel, setAssignmentToCancel] =
    useState<VolunteerAssignmentItem | null>(null);
  const [isCancelling, setIsCancelling] = useState(false);
  const [confirmingId, setConfirmingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [feedbackBanner, setFeedbackBanner] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Onboarding modal state
  const [onboardingItem, setOnboardingItem] =
    useState<VolunteerAssignmentItem | null>(null);
  const [onboardingData, setOnboardingData] = useState<{
    onboardingText: string | null;
    requireOnboardingAck: boolean;
    acknowledged: boolean;
  } | null>(null);
  const [isOnboardingLoading, setIsOnboardingLoading] = useState(false);
  const [isAcknowledging, setIsAcknowledging] = useState(false);

  // Check-In modal state
  const [checkInItem, setCheckInItem] =
    useState<VolunteerAssignmentItem | null>(null);
  const [qrTokenInput, setQrTokenInput] = useState('');
  const [isCheckingIn, setIsCheckingIn] = useState(false);

  // Feedback modal state
  const [feedbackItem, setFeedbackItem] =
    useState<VolunteerAssignmentItem | null>(null);
  const [feedbackScore, setFeedbackScore] = useState(5);
  const [feedbackComment, setFeedbackComment] = useState('');
  const [isSubmittingFeedback, setIsSubmittingFeedback] = useState(false);

  const flatListRef = useRef<FlatList<VolunteerAssignmentItem>>(null);

  const fetchAssignments = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setErrorMessage(null);
      const data = await repository.getUserAssignments();
      setAssignments(data);
    } catch (err) {
      setErrorMessage(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load assignments',
      );
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
      setIsRefreshing(false);
    }
  }, [repository]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchAssignments();
  }, [fetchAssignments]);

  // Restore scroll offset if returning to list
  useEffect(() => {
    if (assignments.length > 0 && assignmentsCache.scrollOffset > 0) {
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        flatListRef.current?.scrollToOffset({
          offset: assignmentsCache.scrollOffset,
          animated: false,
        });
      });
    }
  }, [assignments.length]);

  const handleRefresh = () => {
    setIsRefreshing(true);
    fetchAssignments();
  };

  const handleFilterChange = (newFilter: AssignmentFilterTab) => {
    setStatusFilter(newFilter);
    assignmentsCache.statusFilter = newFilter;
  };

  const handleScroll = (event: NativeSyntheticEvent<NativeScrollEvent>) => {
    assignmentsCache.scrollOffset = event.nativeEvent.contentOffset.y;
  };

  const handleOpenDetail = (eventId: string) => {
    if (navigation) {
      navigation.navigate('EventDetail', {
        eventId,
        returnTo: 'MyAssignments',
      });
    }
  };

  const handleConfirmShift = async (item: VolunteerAssignmentItem) => {
    if (!repository.confirmAssignment) {
      return;
    }
    setConfirmingId(item.id);
    try {
      const result = await repository.confirmAssignment(item.id);
      if (result.success) {
        setFeedbackBanner(
          isMockMode()
            ? `Shift for "${item.eventTitle}" confirmed (${result.message})`
            : result.message,
        );
        await fetchAssignments();
      } else {
        setFeedbackBanner(result.message);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setFeedbackBanner(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to confirm assignment',
      );
    } finally {
      setConfirmingId(null);
    }
  };

  const handleConfirmCancelParticipation = async () => {
    if (!assignmentToCancel) {
      return;
    }

    setIsCancelling(true);
    try {
      const result = await repository.cancelAssignment(assignmentToCancel.id);
      if (result.success) {
        setFeedbackBanner(
          isMockMode()
            ? `Participation in "${assignmentToCancel.eventTitle}" (${assignmentToCancel.postTitle}) cancelled (${result.message})`
            : result.message,
        );
        setAssignmentToCancel(null);
        await fetchAssignments();
      } else {
        setFeedbackBanner(result.message);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setFeedbackBanner(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to cancel participation',
      );
    } finally {
      setIsCancelling(false);
    }
  };

  const handleOpenOnboarding = async (item: VolunteerAssignmentItem) => {
    setOnboardingItem(item);
    setIsOnboardingLoading(true);
    try {
      if (repository.getEventOnboarding) {
        const data = await repository.getEventOnboarding(item.eventId);
        setOnboardingData(data);
      } else {
        setOnboardingData({
          onboardingText:
            'Please review safety guidelines and bring required gear for your shift.',
          requireOnboardingAck: true,
          acknowledged: false,
        });
      }
    } catch {
      setFeedbackBanner('Unable to load onboarding briefing.');
    } finally {
      setIsOnboardingLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleAcknowledgeOnboarding = async () => {
    if (!onboardingItem || !repository.acknowledgeOnboarding) {
      return;
    }
    setIsAcknowledging(true);
    try {
      const res = await repository.acknowledgeOnboarding(onboardingItem.eventId);
      setFeedbackBanner(res.message);
      if (res.success) {
        setOnboardingData(prev =>
          prev ? { ...prev, acknowledged: true } : null,
        );
      }
    } catch {
      setFeedbackBanner('Failed to acknowledge onboarding briefing.');
    } finally {
      setIsAcknowledging(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSubmitGeoCheckIn = async () => {
    if (!checkInItem || !repository.submitGeoCheckIn) {
      return;
    }
    setIsCheckingIn(true);
    try {
      const res = await repository.submitGeoCheckIn(
        checkInItem.eventId,
        16.805,
        96.155,
        15,
      );
      setFeedbackBanner(res.message);
      if (res.success) {
        setCheckInItem(null);
        await fetchAssignments();
      }
    } catch {
      setFeedbackBanner('Geo check-in request failed.');
    } finally {
      setIsCheckingIn(false);
    }
  };

  const handleRedeemQrCheckIn = async () => {
    if (!checkInItem || !repository.redeemQrToken || !qrTokenInput.trim()) {
      return;
    }
    setIsCheckingIn(true);
    try {
      const res = await repository.redeemQrToken(qrTokenInput.trim());
      setFeedbackBanner(res.message);
      if (res.success) {
        setCheckInItem(null);
        setQrTokenInput('');
        await fetchAssignments();
      }
    } catch {
      setFeedbackBanner('QR check-in request failed.');
    } finally {
      setIsCheckingIn(false);
    }
  };

  const handleCheckOut = async (item: VolunteerAssignmentItem) => {
    if (!repository.checkOutAttendance) {
      return;
    }
    setIsLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await repository.checkOutAttendance(item.id);
      setFeedbackBanner(res.message);
      if (res.success) {
        await fetchAssignments();
      }
    } catch {
      setFeedbackBanner('Failed to check out of shift.');
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSubmitFeedback = async () => {
    if (!feedbackItem || !repository.submitEventFeedback) {
      return;
    }
    setIsSubmittingFeedback(true);
    try {
      const res = await repository.submitEventFeedback(
        feedbackItem.eventId,
        feedbackScore,
        feedbackComment,
        ['volunteer_experience'],
        'volunteer_to_event',
      );
      setFeedbackBanner(res.message);
      if (res.success) {
        setFeedbackItem(null);
        setFeedbackComment('');
        await fetchAssignments();
      }
    } catch {
      setFeedbackBanner('Failed to submit event feedback.');
    } finally {
      setIsSubmittingFeedback(false);
    }
  };

  const filteredAssignments = useMemo(() => {
    if (statusFilter === 'all') {
      return assignments;
    }
    if (statusFilter === 'active') {
      return assignments.filter(
        a => a.status === 'assigned' || a.status === 'confirmed',
      );
    }
    if (statusFilter === 'completed') {
      return assignments.filter(
        a => a.status === 'completed' || a.attendanceStatus === 'completed',
      );
    }
    return assignments.filter(a => a.status === statusFilter);
  }, [assignments, statusFilter]);

  const filterTabs: Array<{
    key: AssignmentFilterTab;
    label: string;
  }> = [
    { key: 'all', label: 'All Shifts' },
    { key: 'active', label: 'Active Shifts' },
    { key: 'completed', label: 'Completed' },
    { key: 'cancelled', label: 'Cancelled' },
  ];

  return (
    <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
      <AppHeader
        title="My Assignments"
        subtitle="Confirmed shifts & attendance"
        onMenuPress={navigation ? () => navigation.openDrawer() : undefined}
      />

      {/* Filter tab bar */}
      <View
        style={[
          styles.filtersContainer,
          {
            backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface,
            borderBottomColor: theme.colors.border,
          },
        ]}
      >
        <ScrollView
          horizontal
          showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
          contentContainerStyle={styles.filterChipsRow}
        >
          {filterTabs.map(tab => (
            <FilterChip
              key={tab.key}
              label={tab.label}
              selected={statusFilter === tab.key}
              onPress={() => handleFilterChange(tab.key)}
            />
          ))}
        </ScrollView>
      </View>

      {/* Feedback banner */}
      {feedbackBanner ? (
        <View
          style={[
            styles.feedbackBanner,
            {
              backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
              borderColor: theme.colors.primary,
            },
          ]}
        >
          <AppText
            variant="caption"
            weight="bold"
            color={theme.colors.primary}
          >
            {feedbackBanner}
          </AppText>
        </View>
      ) : null}

      {/* List content */}
      {isLoading ? (
        <LoadingState label="Loading your shift assignments..." />
      ) : errorMessage ? (
        <ErrorState
          title="Could not load assignments"
          description={errorMessage}
          onRetry={fetchAssignments}
        />
      ) : (
        <FlatList
          ref={flatListRef}
          data={filteredAssignments}
          keyExtractor={item => item.id}
          contentContainerStyle={[
            styles.listContent,
            { padding: theme.spacing.md },
          ]}
          onScroll={handleScroll}
          scrollEventThrottle={16}
          refreshControl={
            <RefreshControl
              refreshing={isRefreshing}
              onRefresh={handleRefresh}
              tintColor={theme.colors.primary}
            />
          }
          renderItem={({ item }) => {
            const badge = getAssignmentBadgeConfig(
              item.status,
              item.attendanceStatus,
            );
            const canCancel =
              item.status === 'assigned' || item.status === 'confirmed';
            const isCompleted =
              item.status === 'completed' ||
              item.attendanceStatus === 'completed';

            return (
              <AppCard style={styles.cardSpacing}>
                {/* Header row: Status and Scheduled Date/Time */}
                <View style={styles.cardHeader}>
                  <StatusBadge status={badge.badgeStatus} label={badge.label} />
                  <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                    {formatDateTimeRange(item.startAt, item.endAt)}
                  </AppText>
                </View>

                {/* Main Card Tap Target for Event Detail */}
                <Pressable
                  accessibilityRole="button"
                  accessibilityLabel={`View details for ${item.eventTitle}`}
                  onPress={() => handleOpenDetail(item.eventId)}
                  style={styles.cardContentPressable}
                >
                  <AppText variant="title" weight="bold" style={styles.titleSpacing}>
                    {item.eventTitle}
                  </AppText>

                  <AppText
                    variant="body"
                    color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                    style={styles.roleSpacing}
                  >
                    Role: {item.postTitle}
                  </AppText>

                  <AppText
                    variant="caption"
                    color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                    style={styles.locationSpacing}
                  >
                    📍 {item.location}
                  </AppText>

                  {isCompleted && item.verifiedHours ? (
                    <AppText
                      variant="caption"
                      color={theme.colors.success}
                      weight="semibold"
                      style={styles.verifiedSpacing}
                    >
                      ✓ {item.verifiedHours.toFixed(1)} hrs verified coordinator attendance
                    </AppText>
                  ) : null}

                  <View style={styles.viewDetailLink}>
                    <AppText
                      variant="caption"
                      weight="semibold"
                      color={theme.colors.primary}
                    >
                      View Event Details →
                    </AppText>
                  </View>
                </Pressable>

                {/* Action footer */}
                <View style={styles.cardActions}>
                  {item.status === 'assigned' && repository.confirmAssignment ? (
                    <AppButton
                      label="Confirm Shift"
                      variant="primary"
                      loading={confirmingId === item.id}
                      onPress={() => handleConfirmShift(item)}
                      accessibilityLabel={`Confirm shift for ${item.postTitle}`}
                      style={{ marginRight: 8, marginBottom: 8 }}
                    />
                  ) : null}

                  {repository.getEventOnboarding ? (
                    <AppButton
                      label="Onboarding"
                      variant="secondary"
                      onPress={() => handleOpenOnboarding(item)}
                      accessibilityLabel={`View onboarding for ${item.eventTitle}`}
                      style={{ marginRight: 8, marginBottom: 8 }}
                    />
                  ) : null}

                  {(item.status === 'confirmed' || item.status === 'assigned') &&
                  item.attendanceStatus !== 'checked_in' &&
                  repository.submitGeoCheckIn ? (
                    <AppButton
                      label="Check-In"
                      variant="primary"
                      onPress={() => setCheckInItem(item)}
                      accessibilityLabel={`Check in for ${item.postTitle}`}
                      style={{ marginRight: 8, marginBottom: 8 }}
                    />
                  ) : null}

                  {item.attendanceStatus === 'checked_in' && repository.checkOutAttendance ? (
                    <AppButton
                      label="Check-Out"
                      variant="primary"
                      onPress={() => handleCheckOut(item)}
                      accessibilityLabel={`Check out of ${item.postTitle}`}
                      style={{ marginRight: 8, marginBottom: 8 }}
                    />
                  ) : null}

                  {isCompleted && repository.submitEventFeedback ? (
                    <AppButton
                      label="Feedback"
                      variant="secondary"
                      onPress={() => setFeedbackItem(item)}
                      accessibilityLabel={`Leave feedback for ${item.eventTitle}`}
                      style={{ marginRight: 8, marginBottom: 8 }}
                    />
                  ) : null}

                  {canCancel ? (
                    <AppButton
                      label="Cancel participation"
                      variant="outline"
                      onPress={() => setAssignmentToCancel(item)}
                      accessibilityLabel={`Cancel participation for ${item.postTitle}`}
                      style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}
                    />
                  ) : null}
                </View>
              </AppCard>
            );
          }}
          ListEmptyComponent={
            <EmptyState
              title="No assignments found"
              description={
                statusFilter !== 'all'
                  ? `No shift assignments match the "${statusFilter}" filter.`
                  : 'You have no volunteer shift assignments scheduled.'
              }
              actionLabel={
                statusFilter !== 'all'
                  ? 'Show All Shifts'
                  : 'Discover Volunteer Opportunities'
              }
              onActionPress={
                statusFilter !== 'all'
                  ? () => handleFilterChange('all')
                  : () => navigation?.navigate?.('Events')
              }
            />
          }
        />
      )}

      {/* Cancel participation confirmation modal */}
      <Modal
        visible={!!assignmentToCancel}
        transparent
        animationType="fade"
        onRequestClose={() => setAssignmentToCancel(null)}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalBackdrop}>
          <View
            style={[
              styles.modalCard,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface,
                borderRadius: theme.radius.md,
                borderColor: theme.colors.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <AppText variant="title" weight="bold" style={styles.modalTitle}>
              Cancel Participation
            </AppText>

            <AppText
              variant="body"
              color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
              style={styles.modalBody}
            >
              Are you sure you want to cancel your scheduled participation for "
              {assignmentToCancel?.postTitle}" on "{assignmentToCancel?.eventTitle}"?
              Your shift will be released.
            </AppText>

            {isMockMode() ? (
              <AppText
                variant="caption"
                color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                style={styles.modalDemoNotice}
              >
                Demo only — this cancels your own shift assignment in local preview, not the event.
              </AppText>
            ) : null}

            <View style={styles.modalActions}>
              <View style={styles.modalButtonWrap}>
                <AppButton
                  label="Keep Shift"
                  variant="outline"
                  onPress={() => setAssignmentToCancel(null)}
                  disabled={isCancelling}
                />
              </View>
              <View style={styles.modalButtonWrap}>
                <AppButton
                  label="Cancel Participation"
                  variant="danger"
                  onPress={handleConfirmCancelParticipation}
                  loading={isCancelling}
                />
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* Onboarding Briefing Modal */}
      <Modal
        visible={!!onboardingItem}
        transparent
        animationType="fade"
        onRequestClose={() => setOnboardingItem(null)}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalBackdrop}>
          <View
            style={[
              styles.modalCard,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface,
                borderRadius: theme.radius.md,
                borderColor: theme.colors.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <AppText variant="title" weight="bold" style={styles.modalTitle}>
              Event Onboarding Briefing
            </AppText>
            <AppText
              variant="caption"
              color={theme.colors.primary}
              style={{ marginBottom: 12 }}
            >
              {onboardingItem?.eventTitle}
            </AppText>

            {isOnboardingLoading ? (
              <LoadingState label="Loading onboarding briefing..." />
            ) : (
              <ScrollView style={{ maxHeight: 220, marginBottom: 16 }}>
                <AppText variant="body" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                  {onboardingData?.onboardingText ||
                    'No specific onboarding instructions provided.'}
                </AppText>
                {onboardingData?.acknowledged ? (
                  <View
                    style={{
                      marginTop: 12,
                      flexDirection: 'row',
                      alignItems: 'center',
                    }}
                  >
                    <AppIcon
                      name="check"
                      size={16}
                      color={theme.colors.success}
                    />
                    <AppText
                      variant="caption"
                      color={theme.colors.success}
                      weight="bold"
                      style={{ marginLeft: 6 }}
                    >
                      Onboarding Acknowledged
                    </AppText>
                  </View>
                ) : null}
              </ScrollView>
            )}

            <View style={styles.modalActions}>
              <View style={styles.modalButtonWrap}>
                <AppButton
                  label="Close"
                  variant="outline"
                  onPress={() => setOnboardingItem(null)}
                />
              </View>
              {onboardingData?.requireOnboardingAck &&
              !onboardingData.acknowledged ? (
                <View style={styles.modalButtonWrap}>
                  <AppButton
                    label="Acknowledge Briefing"
                    variant="primary"
                    onPress={handleAcknowledgeOnboarding}
                    loading={isAcknowledging}
                  />
                </View>
              ) : null}
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* Shift Check-In Modal */}
      <Modal
        visible={!!checkInItem}
        transparent
        animationType="fade"
        onRequestClose={() => setCheckInItem(null)}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalBackdrop}>
          <View
            style={[
              styles.modalCard,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface,
                borderRadius: theme.radius.md,
                borderColor: theme.colors.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <AppText variant="title" weight="bold" style={styles.modalTitle}>
              Shift Check-In
            </AppText>
            <AppText
              variant="caption"
              color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
              style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}
            >
              {checkInItem?.eventTitle} • {checkInItem?.postTitle}
            </AppText>

            {/* Option 1: Geo Check-in */}
            <View style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
              <AppText
                variant="caption"
                weight="bold"
                color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
                style={{ marginBottom: 6 }}
              >
                Option 1: Location-Based Check-In
              </AppText>
              <AppButton
                label="Submit Geo Check-In"
                variant="primary"
                onPress={handleSubmitGeoCheckIn}
                loading={isCheckingIn}
              />
            </View>

            {/* Option 2: QR Token Check-in */}
            <View style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
              <AppText
                variant="caption"
                weight="bold"
                color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
                style={{ marginBottom: 6 }}
              >
                Option 2: On-Site QR Token
              </AppText>
              <AppInput
                value={qrTokenInput}
                onChangeText={setQrTokenInput}
                placeholder="Enter or paste QR token..."
                autoCapitalize="none"
              />
              <View style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>
                <AppButton
                  label="Redeem QR Code"
                  variant="secondary"
                  disabled={!qrTokenInput.trim() || isCheckingIn}
                  onPress={handleRedeemQrCheckIn}
                />
              </View>
            </View>

            <View style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>
              <AppButton
                label="Cancel"
                variant="outline"
                onPress={() => {
                  setCheckInItem(null);
                  setQrTokenInput('');
                }}
              />
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* Feedback Modal */}
      <Modal
        visible={!!feedbackItem}
        transparent
        animationType="fade"
        onRequestClose={() => setFeedbackItem(null)}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalBackdrop}>
          <View
            style={[
              styles.modalCard,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface,
                borderRadius: theme.radius.md,
                borderColor: theme.colors.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <AppText variant="title" weight="bold" style={styles.modalTitle}>
              Leave Event Feedback
            </AppText>
            <AppText
              variant="caption"
              color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
              style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}
            >
              {feedbackItem?.eventTitle}
            </AppText>

            {/* Rating Stars 1-5 */}
            <View
              style={{
                flexDirection: 'row',
                justifyContent: 'center',
                marginBottom: 16,
                gap: 8,
              }}
            >
              {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map(score => (
                <Pressable
                  key={score}
                  accessibilityRole="button"
                  accessibilityLabel={`Rate ${score} stars`}
                  onPress={() => setFeedbackScore(score)}
                  style={{
                    paddingHorizontal: 12,
                    paddingVertical: 8,
                    borderRadius: 6,
                    backgroundColor:
                      feedbackScore >= score
                        ? theme.colors.primary
                        : theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                  }}
                >
                  <AppText
                    variant="caption"
                    weight="bold"
                    color={
                      feedbackScore >= score ? '#fff' : theme.colors.textPrimary
                    }
                  >
                    ★ {score}
                  </AppText>
                </Pressable>
              ))}
            </View>

            <AppInput
              value={feedbackComment}
              onChangeText={setFeedbackComment}
              placeholder="Tell us about your shift experience (optional)..."
              multiline
            />

            <View style={[styles.modalActions, { marginTop: 16 }]}>
              <View style={styles.modalButtonWrap}>
                <AppButton
                  label="Cancel"
                  variant="outline"
                  onPress={() => setFeedbackItem(null)}
                />
              </View>
              <View style={styles.modalButtonWrap}>
                <AppButton
                  label="Submit Rating"
                  variant="primary"
                  onPress={handleSubmitFeedback}
                  loading={isSubmittingFeedback}
                />
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  filtersContainer: {
    paddingVertical: 10,
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
  },

FILE: C:\Users\USER\Documents\INTERN\Project\Volunteer_HR_Management\python_stack\mobile\src\features\profile\screens\AvailabilityScreen.tsx
