/**
 * @format
 */

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Modal,
  Pressable,
  StyleSheet,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import type { ProfileRepository } from '../../../data/contracts/profileRepository';
import { defaultProfileRepository } from '../../../data/mock/MockProfileRepository';
import { isMockMode } from '../../../app/configuration/dataMode';
import { useRepositories } from '../../../app/RepositoryProvider';
import type {
  AvailabilityException,
  VolunteerAvailability,
  WeeklyAvailabilityRule,
} from '../../../domain/models/availability';
import type { MainDrawerScreenProps } from '../../../navigation/navigationTypes';
import {
  AppButton,
  AppCard,
  AppHeader,
  AppIcon,
  AppInput,
  AppText,
  EmptyState,
  ErrorState,
  FilterChip,
  LoadingState,
  ScreenContainer,
  SectionHeader,
  StatusBadge,
} from '../../../shared';
import { hexToRgba, useAppTheme } from '../../../theme';

export interface AvailabilityScreenProps
  extends Partial<MainDrawerScreenProps<'Availability'>> {
  repository?: ProfileRepository;
}

const WEEKDAYS = [
  { day: 1, name: 'Monday', letter: 'M' },
  { day: 2, name: 'Tuesday', letter: 'T' },
  { day: 3, name: 'Wednesday', letter: 'W' },
  { day: 4, name: 'Thursday', letter: 'T' },
  { day: 5, name: 'Friday', letter: 'F' },
  { day: 6, name: 'Saturday', letter: 'S' },
  { day: 7, name: 'Sunday', letter: 'S' },
];

export function AvailabilityScreen({
  navigation,
  repository: propRepository,
}: AvailabilityScreenProps): React.JSX.Element {
  const { theme, isDark } = useAppTheme();
  const repositories = useRepositories();
  const repository = propRepository ?? repositories.profileRepository ?? defaultProfileRepository;

  const [availability, setAvailability] =
    useState<VolunteerAvailability | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [feedbackBanner, setFeedbackBanner] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Add Weekly Rule Modal state & field-level validation errors
  const [isRuleModalVisible, setIsRuleModalVisible] = useState(false);
  const [selectedWeekday, setSelectedWeekday] = useState<number>(6); // Default Saturday
  const [ruleStartTime, setRuleStartTime] = useState('08:00');
  const [ruleEndTime, setRuleEndTime] = useState('13:00');
  const [ruleValidFrom, setRuleValidFrom] = useState('');
  const [ruleValidTo, setRuleValidTo] = useState('');
  const [ruleTimeError, setRuleTimeError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [ruleValidityError, setRuleValidityError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Add Exception Modal state & field-level validation errors
  const [isExceptionModalVisible, setIsExceptionModalVisible] = useState(false);
  const [exceptionDate, setExceptionDate] = useState('');
  const [isExceptionAvailable, setIsExceptionAvailable] = useState(false);
  const [exceptionStartTime, setExceptionStartTime] = useState('');
  const [exceptionEndTime, setExceptionEndTime] = useState('');
  const [exceptionNote, setExceptionNote] = useState('');
  const [exceptionDateError, setExceptionDateError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [exceptionTimeError, setExceptionTimeError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const loadAvailability = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setErrorMessage(null);
      const data = await repository.getAvailability();
      setAvailability(data);
    } catch (err) {
      setErrorMessage(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load availability',
      );
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [repository]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadAvailability();
  }, [loadAvailability]);

  // Handle Save Weekly Rule
  const handleSaveWeeklyRule = async () => {
    setRuleTimeError(null);
    setRuleValidityError(null);

    // Validate times
    const timeRegex = /^([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/;
    if (!timeRegex.test(ruleStartTime) || !timeRegex.test(ruleEndTime)) {
      setRuleTimeError('Times must be in 24-hour HH:mm format (e.g. 08:00)');
      return;
    }

    if (ruleStartTime >= ruleEndTime) {
      setRuleTimeError('End time must be after start time (HH:mm).');
      return;
    }

    // Validate dates if supplied
    const dateRegex = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;
    if (ruleValidFrom && !dateRegex.test(ruleValidFrom)) {
      setRuleValidityError('Valid From date must be YYYY-MM-DD');
      return;
    }
    if (ruleValidTo && !dateRegex.test(ruleValidTo)) {
      setRuleValidityError('Valid To date must be YYYY-MM-DD');
      return;
    }
    if (ruleValidFrom && ruleValidTo && ruleValidFrom > ruleValidTo) {
      setRuleValidityError('Valid From cannot be later than Valid To date.');
      return;
    }

    const weekdayObj = WEEKDAYS.find(w => w.day === selectedWeekday);
    const weekdayName = weekdayObj ? weekdayObj.name : 'Saturday';

    const result = await repository.saveWeeklyRule({
      weekday: selectedWeekday,
      weekdayName,
      startTime: ruleStartTime,
      endTime: ruleEndTime,
      validFrom: ruleValidFrom || undefined,
      validTo: ruleValidTo || undefined,
    });

    if (result.success) {
      setIsRuleModalVisible(false);
      setFeedbackBanner(`Weekly schedule rule saved (${result.message})`);
      await loadAvailability();
    } else {
      setRuleTimeError(result.message);
    }
  };

  // Handle Delete Weekly Rule
  const handleDeleteWeeklyRule = async (ruleId: string) => {
    const result = await repository.deleteWeeklyRule(ruleId);
    if (result.success) {
      setFeedbackBanner(`Weekly rule deleted (${result.message})`);
      await loadAvailability();
    }
  };

  // Handle Save Date Exception
  const handleSaveException = async () => {
    setExceptionDateError(null);
    setExceptionTimeError(null);

    if (!exceptionDate.trim()) {
      setExceptionDateError('Date is required (YYYY-MM-DD).');
      return;
    }

    // Validate date format
    const dateRegex = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;
    if (!dateRegex.test(exceptionDate)) {
      setExceptionDateError('Date is required (YYYY-MM-DD).');
      return;
    }

    // Validate times if available slot
    if (isExceptionAvailable) {
      const timeRegex = /^([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/;
      if (!timeRegex.test(exceptionStartTime) || !timeRegex.test(exceptionEndTime)) {
        setExceptionTimeError('Times must be in 24-hour HH:mm format (e.g. 09:00)');
        return;
      }
      if (exceptionStartTime >= exceptionEndTime) {
        setExceptionTimeError('End time must be later than start time.');
        return;
      }
    }

    const result = await repository.saveException({
      date: exceptionDate,
      isAvailable: isExceptionAvailable,
      startTime: isExceptionAvailable ? exceptionStartTime : undefined,
      endTime: isExceptionAvailable ? exceptionEndTime : undefined,
      note: exceptionNote || undefined,
    });

    if (result.success) {
      setIsExceptionModalVisible(false);
      setFeedbackBanner(`Date exception saved (${result.message})`);
      setExceptionDate('');
      setExceptionStartTime('');
      setExceptionEndTime('');
      setExceptionNote('');
      await loadAvailability();
    } else {
      setExceptionDateError(result.message);
    }
  };

  // Handle Delete Exception
  const handleDeleteException = async (exceptionId: string) => {
    const result = await repository.deleteException(exceptionId);
    if (result.success) {
      setFeedbackBanner(`Date exception removed (${result.message})`);
      await loadAvailability();
    }
  };

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
        <AppHeader
          title="Availability"
          onMenuPress={navigation ? () => navigation.openDrawer() : undefined}
        />
        <LoadingState label="Loading availability rules..." />
      </View>
    );
  }

  if (errorMessage || !availability) {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
        <AppHeader
          title="Availability"
          onMenuPress={navigation ? () => navigation.openDrawer() : undefined}
        />
        <ErrorState
          title="Availability Unavailable"
          description={errorMessage ?? 'Could not retrieve availability rules.'}
          onRetry={loadAvailability}
        />
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
      <AppHeader
        title="Availability"
        subtitle="Weekly hours & rules"
        onMenuPress={navigation ? () => navigation.openDrawer() : undefined}
      />

      <ScreenContainer scroll style={styles.scroll}>
        {/* Feedback / Demo confirmation banner */}
        {feedbackBanner ? (
          <View
            style={[
              styles.feedbackBanner,
              {
                backgroundColor: hexToRgba(theme.colors.primary, isDark ? 0.20 : 0.10),
                borderColor: hexToRgba(theme.colors.primary, 0.30),
              },
            ]}
          >
            <AppText
              variant="caption"
              weight="bold"
              color={theme.colors.primary}
            >
              {feedbackBanner}
            </AppText>
          </View>
        ) : null}

        {/* Weekly Recurring Availability Rules Section */}
        <SectionHeader
          title="Weekly Recurring Rules"
          actionLabel="+ Add Rule"
          onActionPress={() => {
            setRuleTimeError(null);
            setRuleValidityError(null);
            setIsRuleModalVisible(true);
          }}
        />

        {availability.weeklyRules.length === 0 ? (
          <AppCard style={styles.emptyCard}>
            <EmptyState
              icon="calendar"
              title="No Weekly Rules"
              description="No recurring weekly availability configured yet."
              actionLabel="+ Add Rule"
              onActionPress={() => {
                setRuleTimeError(null);
                setRuleValidityError(null);
                setIsRuleModalVisible(true);
              }}
            />
          </AppCard>
        ) : (
          <View style={styles.listContainer}>
            {availability.weeklyRules.map((rule: WeeklyAvailabilityRule) => (
              <AppCard key={rule.id} style={styles.ruleCard}>
                <View style={styles.cardHeaderRow}>
                  <StatusBadge status="indigo" label={rule.weekdayName} />

                  <Pressable
                    accessibilityRole="button"
                    accessibilityLabel={`Delete ${rule.weekdayName} rule`}
                    onPress={() => handleDeleteWeeklyRule(rule.id)}
                    hitSlop={8}
                    style={styles.removeAction}
                  >
                    <AppText
                      variant="caption"
                      weight="bold"
                      color={theme.colors.danger}
                    >
                      Remove
                    </AppText>
                  </Pressable>
                </View>

                {/* Big Time Display matching mockup 3 */}
                <AppText
                  variant="hero"
                  weight="extrabold"
                  color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
                  style={styles.timeTitle}
                >
                  {rule.startTime} – {rule.endTime}
                </AppText>

                <AppText
                  variant="caption"
                  color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                  style={styles.validityText}
                >
                  MMT · Weekly · Valid: {rule.validFrom ?? 'Anytime'} to {rule.validTo ?? 'Indefinite'}
                </AppText>
              </AppCard>
            ))}
          </View>
        )}

        {/* Date Exceptions Section (Blackouts & Overrides) */}
        <SectionHeader
          title="Date Exceptions & Blackouts"
          actionLabel="+ Add Exception"
          onActionPress={() => {
            setExceptionDateError(null);
            setExceptionTimeError(null);
            setIsExceptionModalVisible(true);
          }}
        />

        {availability.exceptions.length === 0 ? (
          <AppCard style={styles.emptyCard}>
            <EmptyState
              icon="alert"
              title="No Date Exceptions"
              description="No date overrides or blackouts configured."
              actionLabel="+ Add Exception"
              onActionPress={() => {
                setExceptionDateError(null);
                setExceptionTimeError(null);
                setIsExceptionModalVisible(true);
              }}
            />
          </AppCard>
        ) : (
          <View style={styles.listContainer}>
            {availability.exceptions.map((exc: AvailabilityException) => (
              <AppCard key={exc.id} style={styles.ruleCard}>
                <View style={styles.cardHeaderRow}>
                  <StatusBadge
                    status={exc.isAvailable ? 'sky' : 'rose'}
                    icon={exc.isAvailable ? 'check' : 'close'}
                    label={
                      exc.isAvailable ? 'Available Slot' : 'Unavailable (Blackout)'
                    }
                  />

                  <Pressable
                    accessibilityRole="button"
                    accessibilityLabel={`Delete exception on ${exc.date}`}
                    onPress={() => handleDeleteException(exc.id)}
                    hitSlop={8}
                    style={styles.removeAction}
                  >
                    <AppText
                      variant="caption"
                      weight="bold"
                      color={theme.colors.danger}
                    >
                      Remove
                    </AppText>
                  </Pressable>
                </View>

                <AppText
                  variant="title"
                  weight="extrabold"
                  color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
                  style={styles.exceptionDateText}
                >
                  {exc.date}
                </AppText>

                {exc.isAvailable && exc.startTime && exc.endTime ? (
                  <AppText
                    variant="caption"
                    weight="bold"
                    color={theme.colors.primary}
                    style={styles.exceptionDetails}
                  >
                    Hours: {exc.startTime} – {exc.endTime}
                  </AppText>
                ) : null}

                {exc.note ? (
                  <AppText
                    variant="caption"
                    color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                    style={styles.exceptionDetails}
                  >
                    Note: {exc.note}
                  </AppText>
                ) : null}
              </AppCard>
            ))}
          </View>
        )}
      </ScreenContainer>

      {/* Add Weekly Rule Bottom Sheet */}
      <Modal
        visible={isRuleModalVisible}
        transparent
        animationType="fade"
        onRequestClose={() => setIsRuleModalVisible(false)}
      >
        <View style={styles.scrimBackdrop}>
          <View
            style={[
              styles.sheetCard,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface,
                borderTopColor: theme.colors.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            {/* Handle bar (.hd) */}
            <View
              style={[
                styles.handleBar,
                { backgroundColor: theme.colors.border },
              ]}
            />

            <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
              <View>
                <AppText variant="title" weight="extrabold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                  Add Weekly Availability Rule
                </AppText>
                <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary} style={{ marginTop: 2 }}>
                  Choose a time you can reliably commit.
                </AppText>
              </View>

              <Pressable
                accessibilityRole="button"
                accessibilityLabel="Close rule modal"
                onPress={() => setIsRuleModalVisible(false)}
                hitSlop={8}
                style={[
                  styles.closeIconBtn,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                    borderColor: theme.colors.border,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <AppIcon name="close" size={14} color={theme.colors.textSecondary} />
              </Pressable>
            </View>

            {/* Circular Weekday Picker matching .days */}
            <AppText
              variant="eyebrow"
              color={theme.colors.muted}
              style={styles.fieldSectionLabel}
            >
              DAY OF WEEK
            </AppText>
            <View style={styles.daysRow}>
              {WEEKDAYS.map(w => {
                const isSelected = selectedWeekday === w.day;
                return (
                  <Pressable
                    key={w.day}
                    onPress={() => setSelectedWeekday(w.day)}
                    style={[
                      styles.dayCircle,
                      {
                        backgroundColor: isSelected
                          ? theme.colors.primary
                          : theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                        borderColor: isSelected
                          ? theme.colors.primary
                          : theme.colors.border,
                      },
                      isSelected && styles.dayCircleActive,
                    ]}
                  >
                    <AppText
                      variant="label"
                      weight="bold"
                      color={isSelected ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.colors.textSecondary}
                    >
                      {w.letter}
                    </AppText>
                  </Pressable>
                );
              })}
            </View>

            {/* Time inputs 2-col row (.f2) */}
            <View style={styles.twoColRow}>
              <View style={styles.colHalf}>
                <AppInput
                  label="Start Time (HH:mm)"
                  value={ruleStartTime}
                  rightIcon="clock"
                  onChangeText={text => {
                    setRuleStartTime(text);
                    if (ruleTimeError) {
                      setRuleTimeError(null);
                    }
                  }}
                  placeholder="08:00"
                />
              </View>
              <View style={styles.colHalf}>
                <AppInput
                  label="End Time (HH:mm)"
                  value={ruleEndTime}
                  rightIcon="clock"
                  onChangeText={text => {
                    setRuleEndTime(text);
                    if (ruleTimeError) {
                      setRuleTimeError(null);
                    }
                  }}
                  placeholder="13:00"
                />
              </View>
            </View>

            {ruleTimeError ? (
              <AppText
                variant="caption"
                color={theme.colors.danger}
                style={styles.inlineError}
              >
                {ruleTimeError}
              </AppText>
            ) : null}

            {/* Validity dates 2-col row (.f2) */}
            <View style={styles.twoColRow}>
              <View style={styles.colHalf}>
                <AppInput
                  label="Valid From (Optional)"
                  value={ruleValidFrom}
                  rightIcon="cal"
                  onChangeText={text => {
                    setRuleValidFrom(text);
                    if (ruleValidityError) {
                      setRuleValidityError(null);
                    }
                  }}
                  placeholder="2026-11-01"
                />
              </View>
              <View style={styles.colHalf}>
                <AppInput
                  label="Valid To (Optional)"
                  value={ruleValidTo}
                  rightIcon="cal"
                  onChangeText={text => {
                    setRuleValidTo(text);
                    if (ruleValidityError) {
                      setRuleValidityError(null);
                    }
                  }}
                  placeholder="2026-12-31"
                />
              </View>
            </View>

            {ruleValidityError ? (
              <AppText
                variant="caption"
                color={theme.colors.danger}
                style={styles.inlineError}
              >
                {ruleValidityError}
              </AppText>
            ) : null}

            <View style={styles.sheetActionsRow}>
              <View style={styles.actionBtnWrap}>
                <AppButton
                  label="Cancel"
                  variant="secondary"
                  onPress={() => setIsRuleModalVisible(false)}
                />
              </View>
              <View style={styles.actionBtnWrap}>
                <AppButton
                  label={isMockMode() ? 'Save Rule (Demo)' : 'Save Rule'}
                  variant="primary"
                  leftIcon="check"
                  accessibilityLabel={isMockMode() ? 'Add Rule (Demo)' : 'Add Rule'}
                  onPress={handleSaveWeeklyRule}
                />
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* Add Date Exception Bottom Sheet */}
      <Modal
        visible={isExceptionModalVisible}
        transparent
        animationType="fade"
        onRequestClose={() => setIsExceptionModalVisible(false)}
      >
        <View style={styles.scrimBackdrop}>
          <View
            style={[
              styles.sheetCard,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface,
                borderTopColor: theme.colors.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            {/* Handle bar (.hd) */}
            <View
              style={[
                styles.handleBar,
                { backgroundColor: theme.colors.border },
              ]}
            />

            <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
              <AppText variant="title" weight="extrabold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                Add Date Exception / Blackout
              </AppText>
              <Pressable
                accessibilityRole="button"
                accessibilityLabel="Close exception modal"
                onPress={() => setIsExceptionModalVisible(false)}
                hitSlop={8}
                style={[
                  styles.closeIconBtn,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                    borderColor: theme.colors.border,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <AppIcon name="close" size={14} color={theme.colors.textSecondary} />
              </Pressable>
            </View>

            <AppInput
              label="Date (YYYY-MM-DD) *"
              value={exceptionDate}
              rightIcon="cal"
              onChangeText={text => {
                setExceptionDate(text);
                if (exceptionDateError) {
                  setExceptionDateError(null);
                }
              }}
              placeholder="e.g. 2026-11-25"
              style={styles.modalInputSpacing}
            />

            {exceptionDateError ? (
              <AppText
                variant="caption"
                color={theme.colors.danger}
                style={styles.inlineError}
              >
                {exceptionDateError}
              </AppText>
            ) : null}

            <AppText
              variant="eyebrow"
              color={theme.colors.muted}
              style={styles.fieldSectionLabel}
            >
              AVAILABILITY STATUS FOR THIS DATE
            </AppText>
            <View style={styles.exceptionToggleRow}>
              <FilterChip
                label="Full-Day Blackout"
                selected={!isExceptionAvailable}
                onPress={() => setIsExceptionAvailable(false)}
              />
              <FilterChip
                label="Partial-Day Slot"
                selected={isExceptionAvailable}
                onPress={() => setIsExceptionAvailable(true)}
              />
            </View>

            {isExceptionAvailable ? (
              <>
                <View style={styles.twoColRow}>
                  <View style={styles.colHalf}>
                    <AppInput
                      label="Start Time (HH:mm)"
                      value={exceptionStartTime}
                      rightIcon="clock"
                      onChangeText={text => {
                        setExceptionStartTime(text);
                        if (exceptionTimeError) {
                          setExceptionTimeError(null);
                        }
                      }}
                      placeholder="09:00"
                    />
                  </View>
                  <View style={styles.colHalf}>
                    <AppInput
                      label="End Time (HH:mm)"
                      value={exceptionEndTime}
                      rightIcon="clock"
                      onChangeText={text => {
                        setExceptionEndTime(text);
                        if (exceptionTimeError) {
                          setExceptionTimeError(null);
                        }
                      }}
                      placeholder="12:00"
                    />
                  </View>
                </View>
                {exceptionTimeError ? (
                  <AppText
                    variant="caption"
                    color={theme.colors.danger}
                    style={styles.inlineError}
                  >
                    {exceptionTimeError}
                  </AppText>
                ) : null}
              </>
            ) : null}

            <AppInput
              label="Reason / Note (Optional)"
              value={exceptionNote}
              onChangeText={setExceptionNote}
              placeholder="e.g. Family commitment, out of town"
              style={styles.modalInputSpacing}
            />

            <View style={styles.sheetActionsRow}>
              <View style={styles.actionBtnWrap}>
                <AppButton
                  label="Cancel"
                  variant="secondary"
                  onPress={() => setIsExceptionModalVisible(false)}
                />
              </View>
              <View style={styles.actionBtnWrap}>
                <AppButton
                  label={isMockMode() ? 'Save Exception (Demo)' : 'Save Exception'}
                  variant="primary"
                  leftIcon="check"
                  accessibilityLabel={isMockMode() ? 'Save Exception (Demo)' : 'Save Exception'}
                  onPress={handleSaveException}
                />
              </View>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  scroll: {
    paddingVertical: 12,
    paddingBottom: 40,
  },

================================================================================
FILE: C:\Users\USER\Documents\INTERN\Project\Volunteer_HR_Management\python_stack\mobile\src\features\profile\screens\ProfileEditScreen.tsx

/**
 * @format
 */

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Pressable,
  StyleSheet,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import type { ProfileRepository } from '../../../data/contracts/profileRepository';
import { defaultProfileRepository } from '../../../data/mock/MockProfileRepository';
import { isMockMode } from '../../../app/configuration/dataMode';
import { useRepositories } from '../../../app/RepositoryProvider';
import { CHARITY_CATEGORIES, CharityCategoryCode } from '../../../domain/models/event';
import type {
  BloodType,
  UserLanguage,
  VolunteerProfile,
} from '../../../domain/models/profile';
import type { MainDrawerScreenProps } from '../../../navigation/navigationTypes';
import {
  AppButton,
  AppCard,
  AppHeader,
  AppIcon,
  AppInput,
  AppText,
  ErrorState,
  FilterChip,
  LoadingState,
  ScreenContainer,
  SectionHeader,
  StatusBadge,
} from '../../../shared';
import {
  normalizeSkills,
  validateEmergencyContact,
} from '../../../shared/utils/profileValidation';
import { useAppTheme } from '../../../theme';

export interface ProfileEditScreenProps
  extends Partial<MainDrawerScreenProps<'ProfileEdit'>> {
  repository?: ProfileRepository;
}

const BLOOD_TYPES: BloodType[] = [
  'A+',
  'A-',
  'B+',
  'B-',
  'AB+',
  'AB-',
  'O+',
  'O-',
  'unknown',
];

export function ProfileEditScreen({
  navigation,
  repository: propRepository,
}: ProfileEditScreenProps): React.JSX.Element {
  const { theme } = useAppTheme();
  const repositories = useRepositories();
  const repository = propRepository ?? repositories.profileRepository ?? defaultProfileRepository;

  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<VolunteerProfile | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Form Draft State
  const [displayName, setDisplayName] = useState('');
  const [preferredLanguage, setPreferredLanguage] =
    useState<UserLanguage>('en');
  const [homeArea, setHomeArea] = useState('');
  const [emergencyName, setEmergencyName] = useState('');
  const [emergencyPhone, setEmergencyPhone] = useState('');
  const [skills, setSkills] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [newSkillText, setNewSkillText] = useState('');
  const [charityInterests, setCharityInterests] = useState<
    CharityCategoryCode[]
  >([]);

  // Capabilities
  const [physicalOk, setPhysicalOk] = useState(false);
  const [canLiftHeavy, setCanLiftHeavy] = useState(false);
  const [canWorkOutdoor, setCanWorkOutdoor] = useState(false);
  const [goodEyesight, setGoodEyesight] = useState(true);
  const [needsGlasses, setNeedsGlasses] = useState(false);
  const [hasLeaderExperience, setHasLeaderExperience] = useState(false);
  const [newToVolunteer, setNewToVolunteer] = useState(false);

  // Support & Donation
  const [needsMealSupport, setNeedsMealSupport] = useState(false);
  const [needsTravelSupport, setNeedsTravelSupport] = useState(false);
  const [acceptsUnpaid, setAcceptsUnpaid] = useState(true);
  const [bloodType, setBloodType] = useState<BloodType>('unknown');
  const [isWillingToDonateBlood, setIsWillingToDonateBlood] = useState(false);
  const [isWillingToDonateItems, setIsWillingToDonateItems] = useState(false);

  // UI Feedback
  const [emergencyContactError, setEmergencyContactError] = useState<
    string | null
  >(null);
  const [feedbackBanner, setFeedbackBanner] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);

  const loadData = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setLoadError(null);
      const data = await repository.getProfile();
      setProfile(data);

      // Populate draft fields
      setDisplayName(data.displayName);
      setPreferredLanguage(data.preferredLanguage);
      setHomeArea(data.homeArea);
      setEmergencyName(data.emergencyContactName);
      setEmergencyPhone(data.emergencyContactPhone);
      setSkills(data.skills);
      setCharityInterests(data.charityInterests);

      setPhysicalOk(data.capabilities.physicalOk);
      setCanLiftHeavy(data.capabilities.canLiftHeavy);
      setCanWorkOutdoor(data.capabilities.canWorkOutdoor);
      setGoodEyesight(data.capabilities.goodEyesight);
      setNeedsGlasses(data.capabilities.needsGlasses);
      setHasLeaderExperience(data.capabilities.hasLeaderExperience);
      setNewToVolunteer(data.capabilities.newToVolunteer);

      setNeedsMealSupport(data.supportPreferences.needsMealSupport);
      setNeedsTravelSupport(data.supportPreferences.needsTravelSupport);
      setAcceptsUnpaid(data.supportPreferences.acceptsUnpaid);

      setBloodType(data.donationPreferences.bloodType);
      setIsWillingToDonateBlood(
        data.donationPreferences.isWillingToDonateBlood,
      );
      setIsWillingToDonateItems(
        data.donationPreferences.isWillingToDonateItems,
      );
    } catch (err) {
      setLoadError(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load profile',
      );
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [repository]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, [loadData]);

  const handleAddSkill = () => {
    if (newSkillText.trim().length === 0) {
      return;
    }
    const updated = normalizeSkills([...skills, newSkillText]);
    setSkills(updated);
    setNewSkillText('');
  };

  const handleRemoveSkill = (skillToRemove: string) => {
    setSkills(prev => prev.filter(s => s !== skillToRemove));
  };

  const toggleCharityInterest = (code: CharityCategoryCode) => {
    setCharityInterests(prev =>
      prev.includes(code) ? prev.filter(c => c !== code) : [...prev, code],
    );
  };

  const handleSave = async () => {
    setEmergencyContactError(null);
    setFeedbackBanner(null);

    // Validate emergency contact pairing
    const validation = validateEmergencyContact(emergencyName, emergencyPhone);
    if (!validation.isValid) {
      setEmergencyContactError(
        validation.errorMessage ?? 'Emergency contact validation failed',
      );
      return;
    }

    setIsSaving(true);
    try {
      const result = await repository.updateProfile({
        displayName: displayName.trim(),
        preferredLanguage,
        homeArea: homeArea.trim(),
        emergencyContactName: emergencyName.trim(),
        emergencyContactPhone: emergencyPhone.trim(),
        skills: normalizeSkills(skills),
        charityInterests,
        capabilities: {
          physicalOk,
          goodEyesight,
          needsGlasses,
          canLiftHeavy,
          canWorkOutdoor,
          hasLeaderExperience,
          newToVolunteer,
        },
        supportPreferences: {
          needsMealSupport,
          needsTravelSupport,
          acceptsUnpaid,
        },
        donationPreferences: {
          bloodType,
          isWillingToDonateBlood,
          isWillingToDonateItems,
        },
      });

      if (result.success && result.data) {
        setProfile(result.data);
        setFeedbackBanner(
          isMockMode()
            ? `Profile saved in local mock state (${result.message})`
            : result.message,
        );
      } else {
        setFeedbackBanner(result.message);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setFeedbackBanner(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to update profile',
      );
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false);
    }
  };

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
        <AppHeader
          title="Edit Profile"
          onBackPress={() => navigation?.goBack?.()}
        />
        <LoadingState label="Loading profile details..." />
      </View>
    );
  }

  if (loadError || !profile) {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
        <AppHeader
          title="Edit Profile"
          onBackPress={() => navigation?.goBack?.()}
        />
        <ErrorState
          title="Profile Unavailable"
          description={loadError ?? 'Could not find profile details.'}
          onRetry={loadData}
        />
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
      <AppHeader
        title="Edit Profile"
        subtitle="Update volunteer details & preferences"
        onBackPress={() => navigation?.goBack?.()}
      />

      <ScreenContainer scroll style={styles.scroll}>
        {/* Feedback / Demo confirmation banner */}
        {feedbackBanner ? (
          <View
            style={[
              styles.feedbackBanner,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                borderColor: theme.colors.primary,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <AppText
              variant="caption"
              weight="bold"
              color={theme.colors.primary}
            >
              {feedbackBanner}
            </AppText>
          </View>
        ) : null}

        {/* Read-Only System Identity Card */}
        <AppCard style={styles.cardSpacing}>
          <View style={styles.systemBadgeRow}>
            <StatusBadge
              status={profile.accountStatus === 'active' ? 'success' : 'danger'}
              label={`Account: ${profile.accountStatus}`}
            />
            <StatusBadge
              status={
                profile.verificationStatus === 'verified'
                  ? 'success'
                  : 'warning'
              }
              label={`Identity: ${profile.verificationStatus}`}
            />
            <StatusBadge
              status={
                profile.coordinatorStatus === 'approved' ? 'info' : 'muted'
              }
              label={`Coordinator: ${profile.coordinatorStatus}`}
            />
          </View>

          <AppText variant="title" weight="bold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
            {profile.displayName}
          </AppText>
          <AppText
            variant="body"
            color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
            style={styles.emailSpacing}
          >
            {profile.email}
          </AppText>

          <View
            style={[
              styles.privilegedNoticeBox,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                borderColor: theme.colors.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <AppIcon name="info" size={14} color={theme.colors.textSecondary} />
            <AppText
              variant="caption"
              color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
              style={styles.privilegedNoticeText}
            >
              System privileges and verification statuses are managed by server
              administrators and are read-only.
            </AppText>
          </View>
        </AppCard>

        {/* Basic Allow-listed Profile Fields */}
        <SectionHeader title="Basic Information" />
        <AppCard style={styles.cardSpacing}>
          <AppInput
            label="Display Name *"
            value={displayName}
            onChangeText={setDisplayName}
            placeholder="Your public volunteer name"
            style={styles.fieldSpacing}
          />

          <AppInput
            label="Home Township / Area"
            value={homeArea}
            onChangeText={setHomeArea}
            placeholder="e.g. Kamayut Township"
            style={styles.fieldSpacing}
          />

          <AppText
            variant="caption"
            weight="semibold"
            color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
            style={styles.sectionSublabel}
          >
            Preferred Language
          </AppText>
          <View style={styles.chipsRow}>
            <FilterChip
              label="English (en)"
              selected={preferredLanguage === 'en'}
              onPress={() => setPreferredLanguage('en')}
            />
            <FilterChip
              label="Myanmar (my)"
              selected={preferredLanguage === 'my'}
              onPress={() => setPreferredLanguage('my')}
            />
          </View>
        </AppCard>

        {/* Emergency Contact Pair */}
        <SectionHeader title="Emergency Contact" />
        <AppCard style={styles.cardSpacing}>
          <AppText
            variant="caption"
            color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
            style={styles.emergencyNotice}
          >
            Emergency contact name and phone number must both be provided or both
            cleared.
          </AppText>

          <AppInput
            label="Contact Full Name"
            value={emergencyName}
            onChangeText={text => {
              setEmergencyName(text);
              if (emergencyContactError) {
                setEmergencyContactError(null);
              }
            }}
            placeholder="e.g. Taylor Morgan"
            style={styles.fieldSpacing}
          />

          <AppInput
            label="Contact Phone Number"
            value={emergencyPhone}
            onChangeText={text => {
              setEmergencyPhone(text);
              if (emergencyContactError) {
                setEmergencyContactError(null);
              }
            }}
            placeholder="e.g. +959123456789"
            error={emergencyContactError ?? undefined}
            style={styles.fieldSpacing}
          />
        </AppCard>

        {/* Volunteer Skills */}
        <SectionHeader title="Skills & Competencies" />
        <AppCard style={styles.cardSpacing}>
          <AppText
            variant="caption"
            color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
            style={styles.emergencyNotice}
          >
            Skills are automatically trimmed, lowercased, deduplicated, and
            sorted.
          </AppText>

          {/* Add skill row */}
          <View style={styles.addSkillRow}>
            <View style={styles.addSkillInputWrap}>
              <AppInput
                value={newSkillText}
                onChangeText={setNewSkillText}
                placeholder="Add skill (e.g. first_aid, driving)"
              />
            </View>
            <AppButton
              label="Add"
              variant="outline"
              onPress={handleAddSkill}
              style={styles.addSkillButton}
            />
          </View>

          {/* Current skills chips */}
          <View style={styles.skillsWrap}>
            {skills.map(skill => (
              <Pressable
                key={skill}
                accessibilityRole="button"
                accessibilityLabel={`Remove skill ${skill}`}
                onPress={() => handleRemoveSkill(skill)}
                style={[
                  styles.skillChip,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                    borderColor: theme.colors.border,
                    borderRadius: theme.radius.sm,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <AppText variant="caption" weight="semibold" color={theme.colors.primary}>
                  {skill} ✕
                </AppText>
              </Pressable>
            ))}
          </View>
        </AppCard>

        {/* Charity Interests */}
        <SectionHeader title="Charity Interests" />
        <AppCard style={styles.cardSpacing}>
          <AppText
            variant="caption"
            color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
            style={styles.emergencyNotice}
          >
            Select charity sectors you are interested in supporting:
          </AppText>
          <View style={styles.categoriesWrap}>
            {CHARITY_CATEGORIES.map(category => (
              <FilterChip
                key={category.code}
                label={category.label}
                selected={charityInterests.includes(category.code)}
                onPress={() => toggleCharityInterest(category.code)}
              />
            ))}
          </View>
        </AppCard>

        {/* Volunteer Suitability & Capabilities */}
        <SectionHeader title="Suitability & Work Preferences" />
        <AppCard style={styles.cardSpacing}>
          <View style={styles.togglesWrap}>
            <FilterChip
              label="Physical OK"
              selected={physicalOk}
              onPress={() => setPhysicalOk(!physicalOk)}
            />
            <FilterChip
              label="Can Lift Heavy (>15kg)"
              selected={canLiftHeavy}
              onPress={() => setCanLiftHeavy(!canLiftHeavy)}
            />
            <FilterChip
              label="Can Work Outdoor"
              selected={canWorkOutdoor}
              onPress={() => setCanWorkOutdoor(!canWorkOutdoor)}
            />
            <FilterChip
              label="Good Eyesight"
              selected={goodEyesight}
              onPress={() => setGoodEyesight(!goodEyesight)}
            />
            <FilterChip
              label="Needs Glasses"
              selected={needsGlasses}
              onPress={() => setNeedsGlasses(!needsGlasses)}
            />
            <FilterChip
              label="Team Leader Experience"
              selected={hasLeaderExperience}
              onPress={() => setHasLeaderExperience(!hasLeaderExperience)}
            />
            <FilterChip
              label="New to Volunteering"
              selected={newToVolunteer}
              onPress={() => setNewToVolunteer(!newToVolunteer)}
            />
          </View>

          <AppText
            variant="caption"
            weight="semibold"
            color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
            style={styles.sectionSublabel}
          >
            Support Requirements
          </AppText>
          <View style={styles.togglesWrap}>
            <FilterChip
              label="Requires Meal Support"
              selected={needsMealSupport}
              onPress={() => setNeedsMealSupport(!needsMealSupport)}
            />
            <FilterChip
              label="Requires Travel Support"
              selected={needsTravelSupport}
              onPress={() => setNeedsTravelSupport(!needsTravelSupport)}
            />
            <FilterChip
              label="Accepts Unpaid Roles"
              selected={acceptsUnpaid}
              onPress={() => setAcceptsUnpaid(!acceptsUnpaid)}
            />
          </View>
        </AppCard>

        {/* Blood Donation & Community Giving */}
        <SectionHeader title="Community Contribution" />
        <AppCard style={styles.cardSpacing}>
          <AppText
            variant="caption"
            weight="semibold"
            color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
            style={styles.sectionSublabel}
          >
            Blood Type
          </AppText>
          <View style={styles.togglesWrap}>
            {BLOOD_TYPES.map(bt => (
              <FilterChip
                key={bt}
                label={bt}
                selected={bloodType === bt}
                onPress={() => setBloodType(bt)}
              />
            ))}
          </View>

          <View style={[styles.togglesWrap, styles.givingTopMargin]}>
            <FilterChip
              label="Willing to Donate Blood"
              selected={isWillingToDonateBlood}
              onPress={() => setIsWillingToDonateBlood(!isWillingToDonateBlood)}
            />
            <FilterChip
              label="Willing to Donate Relief Items"
              selected={isWillingToDonateItems}
              onPress={() => setIsWillingToDonateItems(!isWillingToDonateItems)}
            />
          </View>
        </AppCard>

        {/* Save Button */}
        <View style={styles.saveActionContainer}>
          <AppButton
            label="Save Profile Changes"
            variant="primary"
            loading={isSaving}
            onPress={handleSave}
          />
        </View>
      </ScreenContainer>
    </View>
  );
}

/**
 * @format
 */

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Pressable,
  StyleSheet,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { defaultProfileRepository } from '../../../data/mock/MockProfileRepository';
import type {
  CoordinatorEligibilityData,
  ProfileRepository,
} from '../../../data/contracts/profileRepository';
import { useRepositories } from '../../../app/RepositoryProvider';
import { CHARITY_CATEGORIES } from '../../../domain/models/event';
import type { VolunteerProfile } from '../../../domain/models/profile';
import type { MainDrawerScreenProps } from '../../../navigation/navigationTypes';
import {
  AppButton,
  AppCard,
  AppHeader,
  AppIcon,
  AppText,
  ErrorState,
  LoadingState,
  RatingSummary,
  ScreenContainer,
  SectionHeader,
  StatusBadge,
} from '../../../shared';
import { useAppTheme } from '../../../theme';

export interface ProfileScreenProps
  extends Partial<MainDrawerScreenProps<'Profile'>> {
  repository?: ProfileRepository;
}

const CATEGORY_MAP: Record<string, string> = Object.fromEntries(
  CHARITY_CATEGORIES.map(c => [c.code, c.label]),
);

function getInitials(name: string): string {
  const parts = name.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  if (parts.length === 0) {
    return 'V';
  }
  if (parts.length === 1) {
    return parts[0].substring(0, 2).toUpperCase();
  }
  return (parts[0][0] + parts[parts.length - 1][0]).toUpperCase();
}

export function ProfileScreen({
  navigation,
  repository: propRepository,
}: ProfileScreenProps): React.JSX.Element {
  const { theme } = useAppTheme();
  const repositories = useRepositories();
  const repository = propRepository ?? repositories.profileRepository ?? defaultProfileRepository;

  const [profile, setProfile] = useState<VolunteerProfile | null>(null);
  const [eligibility, setEligibility] =
    useState<CoordinatorEligibilityData | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [feedbackBanner, setFeedbackBanner] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isApplying, setIsApplying] = useState(false);

  const loadData = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setLoadError(null);
      const [profileData, eligibilityData] = await Promise.all([
        repository.getProfile(),
        repository.getCoordinatorEligibility(),
      ]);
      setProfile(profileData);
      setEligibility(eligibilityData);
    } catch (err) {
      setLoadError(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load profile details',
      );
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [repository]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, [loadData]);

  const handleApplyCoordinator = async () => {
    if (!eligibility) {
      return;
    }

    const canApply =
      eligibility.isVerified &&
      eligibility.meetsCompletedEventsRequirement &&
      eligibility.meetsGoodRatingRequirement === true;

    if (!canApply) {
      return;
    }

    setIsApplying(true);
    try {
      const result = await repository.submitCoordinatorApplication(
        'Volunteer coordinator application',
      );
      if (result.success) {
        setFeedbackBanner(
          `Coordinator application submitted (${result.message})`,
        );
        // Refresh eligibility data
        const updatedEligibility = await repository.getCoordinatorEligibility();
        setEligibility(updatedEligibility);
      } else {
        setFeedbackBanner(result.message);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setFeedbackBanner(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : 'Failed to submit coordinator application',
      );
    } finally {
      setIsApplying(false);
    }
  };

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
        <AppHeader
          title="Profile"
          onMenuPress={navigation ? () => navigation.openDrawer() : undefined}
        />
        <LoadingState label="Loading profile summary..." />
      </View>
    );
  }

  if (loadError || !profile || !eligibility) {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
        <AppHeader
          title="Profile"
          onMenuPress={navigation ? () => navigation.openDrawer() : undefined}
        />
        <ErrorState
          title="Profile Unavailable"
          description={loadError ?? 'Could not find profile summary.'}
          onRetry={loadData}
        />
      </View>
    );
  }

  const initials = getInitials(profile.displayName);
  const ratingAverage = eligibility.ratingAverage ?? profile.ratingAverage;
  const ratingCount = eligibility.ratingCount ?? profile.ratingCount ?? 0;

  const canApplyCoordinator =
    eligibility.coordinatorStatus === 'none' &&
    eligibility.isVerified &&
    eligibility.meetsCompletedEventsRequirement &&
    eligibility.meetsGoodRatingRequirement === true;

  return (
    <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
      <AppHeader
        title="Profile"
        subtitle="Volunteer summary & verification"
        onMenuPress={navigation ? () => navigation.openDrawer() : undefined}
      />

      <ScreenContainer scroll style={styles.scroll}>
        {/* Feedback / Demo confirmation banner */}
        {feedbackBanner ? (
          <View
            style={[
              styles.feedbackBanner,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                borderColor: theme.colors.primary,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <AppText
              variant="caption"
              weight="bold"
              color={theme.colors.primary}
            >
              {feedbackBanner}
            </AppText>
          </View>
        ) : null}

        {/* Concise Profile Summary Card */}
        <AppCard style={styles.cardSpacing}>
          {/* Card Header Row: Avatar, Name & Badges, Edit Button */}
          <View style={styles.summaryHeaderRow}>
            <View
              style={[
                styles.avatarContainer,
                { backgroundColor: theme.colors.primary },
              ]}
              accessibilityRole="image"
              accessibilityLabel={`Avatar for ${profile.displayName}`}
            >
              <AppText variant="title" weight="bold" color="#ffffff">
                {initials}
              </AppText>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.identityDetails}>
              <AppText
                variant="title"
                weight="bold"
                color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
                numberOfLines={1}
              >
                {profile.displayName}
              </AppText>
              <View style={styles.badgeRow}>
                <StatusBadge
                  status={profile.accountStatus === 'active' ? 'success' : 'danger'}
                  label={
                    profile.accountStatus === 'active'
                      ? 'Active Volunteer'
                      : `Account: ${profile.accountStatus}`
                  }
                />
                <StatusBadge
                  status={
                    profile.verificationStatus === 'verified'
                      ? 'success'
                      : 'warning'
                  }
                  label={
                    profile.verificationStatus === 'verified'
                      ? 'Verified'
                      : `Identity: ${profile.verificationStatus}`
                  }
                />
              </View>
            </View>

            <Pressable
              accessibilityRole="button"
              accessibilityLabel="Edit Profile"
              onPress={() => navigation?.navigate?.('ProfileEdit')}
              style={[
                styles.editButton,
                {
                  borderColor: theme.colors.border,
                  backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <AppIcon name="edit" size={16} color={theme.colors.primary} />
              <AppText
                variant="caption"
                weight="bold"
                color={theme.colors.primary}
                style={styles.editText}
              >
                Edit
              </AppText>
            </Pressable>
          </View>

          {/* Aggregate Volunteer Rating */}
          <View style={styles.ratingSection}>
            <RatingSummary average={ratingAverage} count={ratingCount} />
          </View>

          <View
            style={[styles.divider, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.border }]}
          />

          {/* Selected Summary Fields (Strictly no hash, password, token, admin/staff, or private paths) */}
          <View style={styles.summaryFieldList}>
            <View style={styles.summaryRow}>
              <AppText
                variant="caption"
                weight="semibold"
                color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                style={styles.summaryLabel}
              >
                Email
              </AppText>
              <AppText
                variant="body"
                color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
                style={styles.summaryValue}
              >
                {profile.email}
              </AppText>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.summaryRow}>
              <AppText
                variant="caption"
                weight="semibold"
                color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                style={styles.summaryLabel}
              >
                Home Area
              </AppText>
              <AppText
                variant="body"
                color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
                style={styles.summaryValue}
              >
                {profile.homeArea || 'Not specified'}
              </AppText>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.summaryRow}>
              <AppText
                variant="caption"
                weight="semibold"
                color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                style={styles.summaryLabel}
              >
                Language
              </AppText>
              <AppText
                variant="body"
                color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
                style={styles.summaryValue}
              >
                {profile.preferredLanguage === 'my'
                  ? 'Myanmar (Burmese)'
                  : 'English'}
              </AppText>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.summaryRow}>
              <AppText
                variant="caption"
                weight="semibold"
                color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                style={styles.summaryLabel}
              >
                Emergency Contact
              </AppText>
              <AppText
                variant="body"
                color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
                style={styles.summaryValue}
              >
                {profile.emergencyContactName
                  ? `${profile.emergencyContactName} (${profile.emergencyContactPhone})`
                  : 'Not configured'}
              </AppText>
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* Skills Chips */}
          <View style={styles.tagsContainer}>
            <AppText
              variant="caption"
              weight="semibold"
              color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
              style={styles.tagSectionLabel}
            >
              Skills
            </AppText>
            <View style={styles.tagsRow}>
              {profile.skills.length > 0 ? (
                profile.skills.map(skill => (
                  <View
                    key={skill}
                    style={[
                      styles.tagChip,

                  >
                    <AppText
                      variant="caption"

                      color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
                    >
                      {skill}
                    </AppText>
                  </View>
                ))
              ) : (
                <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                  No skills listed
                </AppText>
              )}
            </View>
          </View>

          {/* Charity Interests Chips */}

              {profile.charityInterests.length > 0 ? (

                  No charity interests selected
                </AppText>
              )}

                  status={
                    eligibility.coordinatorStatus === 'approved'

                      ? 'warning'
                      : 'danger'

                  label={
                    eligibility.coordinatorStatus === 'pending'
                      ? 'Application Pending'
                      : eligibility.coordinatorStatus === 'approved'
                      ? 'Active Coordinator'
                      : eligibility.coordinatorStatus === 'suspended'
                      ? 'Coordinator Suspended'
                      : 'Coordinator Revoked'
                  }
                />
              </View>

              <AppText
                variant="body"
                color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
                style={styles.statusDescriptionText}
              >
                {eligibility.coordinatorStatus === 'pending' &&
                  'Your coordinator application is under review. Our team will verify your qualifications.'}
                {eligibility.coordinatorStatus === 'approved' &&
                  'You are an active coordinator with event management and shift leadership privileges.'}
                {eligibility.coordinatorStatus === 'suspended' &&
                  'Your coordinator privileges are currently suspended. Please contact administration.'}
                {eligibility.coordinatorStatus === 'revoked' &&
                  'Your coordinator privileges have been revoked.'}
              </AppText>
            </View>
          ) : (
            /* Coordinator Status === 'none': Present 3 eligibility checks */
            <View style={styles.checksContainer}>
              {/* Check 1: Account Verified */}
              <View style={styles.checkItem}>
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.checkIconWrap,
                    {
                      backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                      borderColor: eligibility.isVerified
                        ? theme.colors.success
                        : theme.colors.border,
                    },

                  <AppIcon
                    name={eligibility.isVerified ? 'check' : 'alert'}
                    size={14}
                    color={
                      eligibility.isVerified
                        ? theme.colors.success
                        : theme.colors.warning
                    }
                  />
                </View>
                <View style={styles.checkTextWrap}>
                  <AppText
                    variant="body"
                    weight="semibold"
                    color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
                  >
                    Account Verified
                  </AppText>
                  <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                    {eligibility.isVerified
                      ? 'Account verified (Tier 1 identity confirmed)'
                      : `Identity verification required (Status: ${eligibility.verificationStatus})`}
                  </AppText>
                </View>
              </View>

              {/* Check 2: At least 5 completed events (Attendance-backed) */}
              <View style={styles.checkItem}>
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.checkIconWrap,
                    {
                      backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                      borderColor: eligibility.meetsCompletedEventsRequirement
                        ? theme.colors.success
                        : theme.colors.border,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <AppIcon
                    name={
                      eligibility.meetsCompletedEventsRequirement
                        ? 'check'
                        : 'alert'
                    }
                    size={14}
                    color={
                      eligibility.meetsCompletedEventsRequirement
                        ? theme.colors.success
                        : theme.colors.warning
                    }
                  />
                </View>
                <View style={styles.checkTextWrap}>
                  <AppText
                    variant="body"
                    weight="semibold"
                    color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
                  >
                    At least 5 completed events
                  </AppText>
                  <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                    {`${eligibility.completedAttendanceEventsCount}/5 attendance-backed events completed`}
                  </AppText>
                </View>
              </View>

              {/* Check 3: Good Rating (Null represents missing rule configuration / pending confirmation) */}
              <View style={styles.checkItem}>
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.checkIconWrap,
                    {
                      backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                      borderColor:
                        eligibility.meetsGoodRatingRequirement === true
                          ? theme.colors.success
                          : eligibility.meetsGoodRatingRequirement === false
                          ? theme.colors.danger
                          : theme.colors.border,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <AppIcon
                    name={
                      eligibility.meetsGoodRatingRequirement === true
                        ? 'check'
                        : 'alert'
                    }
                    size={14}
                    color={
                      eligibility.meetsGoodRatingRequirement === true
                        ? theme.colors.success
                        : eligibility.meetsGoodRatingRequirement === false
                        ? theme.colors.danger
                        : theme.colors.warning
                    }
                  />
                </View>
                <View style={styles.checkTextWrap}>
                  <AppText
                    variant="body"
                    weight="semibold"
                    color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
                  >
                    Good Volunteer Rating
                  </AppText>
                  <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                    {eligibility.meetsGoodRatingRequirement === true
                      ? 'Good rating requirement met'
                      : eligibility.meetsGoodRatingRequirement === false
                      ? 'Volunteer rating requirement not met'
                      : 'Requirement pending server confirmation (no threshold configured)'}
                  </AppText>
                </View>
              </View>

              {/* Apply Button */}
              <View style={styles.applyActionWrap}>
                <AppButton
                  label="Apply for Coordinator"
                  variant="primary"
                  accessibilityLabel="Apply for Coordinator"
                  disabled={!canApplyCoordinator}
                  loading={isApplying}
                  onPress={handleApplyCoordinator}
                />
                {!canApplyCoordinator ? (
                  <AppText
                    variant="caption"
                    color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                    style={styles.disabledHelperText}
                  >
                    Complete all eligibility requirements above to apply for coordinator status.
                  </AppText>
                ) : null}
              </View>
            </View>
          )}
        </AppCard>

        {/* Schedule & Verification Tools Navigation */}
        <SectionHeader title="Schedule & Verification Tools" />
        <View style={styles.navStack}>
          <AppButton
            label="Manage Weekly Availability"
            variant="secondary"
            leftIcon="calendar"
            onPress={() => navigation?.navigate?.('Availability')}
          />
          <AppButton
            label="Verification & Identity Documents"
            variant="secondary"
            leftIcon="info"
            onPress={() =>
              navigation?.navigate?.('VerificationCoordinatorStatus')
            }
          />
        </View>
      </ScreenContainer>
    </View>
  );
}
