/**
 * @format
 */

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  StyleSheet,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import type { ProfileRepository } from '../../../data/contracts/profileRepository';
import { defaultProfileRepository } from '../../../data/mock/MockProfileRepository';
import { useRepositories } from '../../../app/RepositoryProvider';
import type {
  VerificationCoordinatorData,
  VerificationImageSlot,
} from '../../../domain/models/verification';
import type { MainDrawerScreenProps } from '../../../navigation/navigationTypes';
import {
  AppButton,
  AppCard,
  AppHeader,
  AppIcon,
  AppInput,
  AppText,
  ErrorState,
  LoadingState,
  ScreenContainer,
  SectionHeader,
  StatusBadge,
} from '../../../shared';
import { useAppTheme } from '../../../theme';

export interface VerificationCoordinatorStatusScreenProps
  extends Partial<MainDrawerScreenProps<'VerificationCoordinatorStatus'>> {
  repository?: ProfileRepository;
}

export function VerificationCoordinatorStatusScreen({
  navigation,
  repository: propRepository,
}: VerificationCoordinatorStatusScreenProps): React.JSX.Element {
  const { theme } = useAppTheme();
  const repositories = useRepositories();
  const repository = propRepository ?? repositories.profileRepository ?? defaultProfileRepository;

  const [data, setData] = useState<VerificationCoordinatorData | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Application Draft State
  const [applicationNote, setApplicationNote] = useState('');
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [feedbackBanner, setFeedbackBanner] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const loadVerificationData = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setErrorMessage(null);
      const res = await repository.getVerificationStatus();
      setData(res);
      if (res.coordinatorApplicationNote) {
        setApplicationNote(res.coordinatorApplicationNote);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setErrorMessage(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load verification status',
      );
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [repository]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadVerificationData();
  }, [loadVerificationData]);

  const handleSubmitCoordinatorApplication = async () => {
    if (!data?.canApplyForCoordinator) {
      return;
    }

    setIsSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const result = await repository.submitCoordinatorApplication(
        applicationNote.trim(),
      );
      if (result.success) {
        setFeedbackBanner(
          `Coordinator application submitted (${result.message})`,
        );
        await loadVerificationData();
      } else {
        setFeedbackBanner(result.message);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setFeedbackBanner(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : 'Failed to submit coordinator application',
      );
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
        <AppHeader
          title="Verification & Status"
          onMenuPress={navigation ? () => navigation.openDrawer() : undefined}
        />
        <LoadingState label="Loading identity & coordinator credentials..." />
      </View>
    );
  }

  if (errorMessage || !data) {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
        <AppHeader
          title="Verification & Status"
          onMenuPress={navigation ? () => navigation.openDrawer() : undefined}
        />
        <ErrorState
          title="Credentials Unavailable"
          description={errorMessage ?? 'Could not retrieve verification credentials.'}
          onRetry={loadVerificationData}
        />
      </View>
    );
  }

  const isVerified = data.verificationStatus === 'verified';
  const isApprovedCoordinator = data.coordinatorStatus === 'approved';
  const isPendingCoordinator = data.coordinatorStatus === 'pending';

  return (
    <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
      <AppHeader
        title="Verification & Status"
        subtitle="Identity & coordinator credentials"
        onMenuPress={navigation ? () => navigation.openDrawer() : undefined}
      />

      <ScreenContainer scroll style={styles.scroll}>
        {/* Feedback / Demo confirmation banner */}
        {feedbackBanner ? (
          <View
            style={[
              styles.feedbackBanner,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                borderColor: theme.colors.primary,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <AppText
              variant="caption"
              weight="bold"
              color={theme.colors.primary}
            >
              {feedbackBanner}
            </AppText>
          </View>
        ) : null}

        {/* Section 1: Identity Verification Overview */}
        <SectionHeader title="Tier 1 Identity Verification" />
        <AppCard style={styles.cardSpacing}>
          <View style={styles.cardHeaderRow}>
            <View style={styles.cardHeaderLeft}>
              <AppText variant="title" weight="bold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                Account Verification Level
              </AppText>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                Tier 1 Verified status confirms identity via photo matching.
              </AppText>
            </View>
            <StatusBadge
              status={isVerified ? 'success' : 'warning'}
              label={data.verificationStatus.toUpperCase()}
            />
          </View>

          {/* Visual Upload Placeholders (No device camera/gallery access) */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.uploadNoticeBox,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                borderColor: theme.colors.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <AppIcon name="info" size={16} color={theme.colors.primary} />
            <AppText
              variant="caption"
              color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
              style={styles.uploadNoticeText}
            >
              Hardware camera and file uploads are disabled in this UI preview.
              Document images are encrypted on the server in production.
            </AppText>
          </View>

          <View style={styles.slotsContainer}>
            {data.imageSlots.map((slot: VerificationImageSlot) => (
              <View
                key={slot.imageType}
                style={[
                  styles.slotCard,
                  {
                    backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                    borderColor: theme.colors.border,
                  },
                ]}
              >
                <View style={styles.slotHeaderRow}>
                  <View style={styles.slotTitleGroup}>
                    <AppText
                      variant="body"
                      weight="bold"
                      color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
                    >
                      {slot.label}
                    </AppText>
                    <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                      Format: JPEG • {slot.description}
                    </AppText>
                  </View>
                  <StatusBadge
                    status={
                      slot.status === 'verified'
                        ? 'success'
                        : slot.status === 'pending'
                        ? 'warning'
                        : 'muted'
                    }
                    label={slot.status.toUpperCase()}
                  />
                </View>

                {/* Visual Placeholder Slot */}
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.visualPlaceholderBox,
                    {
                      borderColor: theme.colors.border,
                      backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <AppIcon
                    name={slot.status === 'verified' ? 'check' : 'user'}
                    size={22}
                    color={
                      slot.status === 'verified'
                        ? theme.colors.success
                        : theme.colors.muted
                    }
                  />
                  <AppText
                    variant="caption"
                    color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                    style={styles.placeholderLabel}
                  >
                    {slot.status === 'verified'
                      ? 'Encrypted Document Stored On Server'
                      : 'Visual Upload Slot (Demo Preview)'}
                  </AppText>
                </View>
              </View>
            ))}
          </View>
        </AppCard>

        {/* Section 2: Coordinator Profile Status & Application */}
        <SectionHeader title="Coordinator Authority & Application" />
        <AppCard style={styles.cardSpacing}>
          <View style={styles.cardHeaderRow}>
            <View style={styles.cardHeaderLeft}>
              <AppText variant="title" weight="bold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
                Coordinator Status
              </AppText>
              <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                Governs permission to create and manage volunteer events.
              </AppText>
            </View>
            <StatusBadge
              status={
                isApprovedCoordinator
                  ? 'success'
                  : isPendingCoordinator
                  ? 'warning'
                  : 'info'
              }
              label={data.coordinatorStatus.toUpperCase()}
            />
          </View>

          {/* Explanation Requirement Notice */}
          <View
            style={[
              styles.requirementBox,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                borderColor: theme.colors.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
              ℹ️ Applying for Coordinator authority requires{' '}
              <AppText variant="caption" weight="bold">
                Tier 1 Verified
              </AppText>{' '}
              account status. Only active approved coordinators may publish
              events and manage rosters.
            </AppText>
          </View>

          {/* State 1: Approved Coordinator */}
          {isApprovedCoordinator ? (
            <View
              style={[
                styles.approvedBox,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                  borderColor: theme.colors.success,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <AppIcon name="check" size={20} color={theme.colors.success} />
              <View style={styles.approvedTextCol}>
                <AppText
                  variant="body"
                  weight="bold"
                  color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
                >
                  Approved Coordinator
                </AppText>
                <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                  Your coordinator profile is active. You have full access to the
                  Coordinator Workspace in the navigation menu to publish events
                  and assign volunteers.
                </AppText>
              </View>
            </View>
          ) : null}

          {/* State 2: Pending Coordinator Application */}
          {isPendingCoordinator ? (
            <View
              style={[
                styles.approvedBox,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                  borderColor: theme.colors.warning,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <AppIcon name="alert" size={20} color={theme.colors.warning} />
              <View style={styles.approvedTextCol}>
                <AppText
                  variant="body"
                  weight="bold"
                  color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
                >
                  Application Under Review
                </AppText>
                <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                  Your coordinator application has been received and is pending
                  administrator review. You will be notified once reviewed.
                </AppText>
                {applicationNote ? (
                  <AppText
                    variant="caption"
                    color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                    style={styles.pendingNote}
                  >
                    Submitted note: "{applicationNote}"
                  </AppText>
                ) : null}
              </View>
            </View>
          ) : null}

          {/* State 3: Not applied yet — Show application form if eligible */}
          {!isApprovedCoordinator && !isPendingCoordinator ? (
            <View style={styles.applicationForm}>
              {!isVerified ? (
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.ineligibleBox,
                    {
                      backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                      borderColor: theme.colors.danger,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.danger}>
                    Verification Incomplete: Please complete Tier 1 identity
                    verification before submitting a coordinator application.
                  </AppText>
                </View>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <AppText
                    variant="caption"
                    color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                    style={styles.formPrompt}
                  >
                    You are eligible to apply for Coordinator authority. Provide a
                    brief note describing your leadership or community coordination
                    experience:
                  </AppText>

                  <AppInput
                    label="Coordinator Application Note"
                    value={applicationNote}
                    onChangeText={setApplicationNote}
                    placeholder="e.g. Experienced community organizer with river cleanup background..."
                    multiline
                    numberOfLines={3}
                    style={styles.noteInput}
                  />

                  <AppButton
                    label="Submit Coordinator Application"
                    variant="primary"
                    loading={isSubmitting}
                    onPress={handleSubmitCoordinatorApplication}
                  />
                </>
              )}
            </View>
          ) : null}
        </AppCard>
      </ScreenContainer>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  scroll: {
    paddingVertical: 16,
    paddingBottom: 40,
  },
  feedbackBanner: {
    padding: 12,

    borderRadius: 8,

  },

/**
 * @format
 */

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';
import { useCurrentUser } from '../../../app/UserContext';
import {
  addDataModeListener,
  DataMode,
  getDataMode,
  isHttpMode,
  setDataMode,
} from '../../../app/configuration/dataMode';
import { getApiConfig } from '../../../core/api/apiConfig';
import { eventApi } from '../../../data/endpoints/eventApi';
import {
  AppButton,
  AppCard,
  AppHeader,
  AppText,
  ScreenContainer,
  SectionHeader,
} from '../../../shared';
import { ThemeMode, useAppTheme } from '../../../theme';
import type { MainDrawerScreenProps } from '../../../navigation/navigationTypes';

export interface SettingsScreenProps
  extends Partial<MainDrawerScreenProps<'Settings'>> {}

export function SettingsScreen({
  navigation,
}: SettingsScreenProps): React.JSX.Element {
  const { theme, themeMode, setThemeMode, isDark } = useAppTheme();
  const { logout } = useCurrentUser();
  const [logoutFeedback, setLogoutFeedback] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [currentMode, setCurrentMode] = useState<DataMode>(() => getDataMode());
  const [isPinging, setIsPinging] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [pingResult, setPingResult] = useState<{
    status?: number;
    durationMs?: number;
    success: boolean;
    message: string;
  } | null>(null);

  const apiConfig = getApiConfig();

  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubscribe = addDataModeListener(mode => {
      setCurrentMode(mode);
    });
    return unsubscribe;
  }, []);

  const handleSetDataMode = (mode: DataMode) => {
    setDataMode(mode);
    setCurrentMode(mode);
  };

  const handleTestConnection = async () => {
    setIsPinging(true);
    setPingResult(null);
    const start = Date.now();
    try {
      const res = await eventApi.getCharityFields();
      const durationMs = Date.now() - start;
      if (res.ok) {
        setPingResult({
          status: res.status,
          durationMs,
          success: true,
          message: `BFF Reachable: HTTP ${res.status} (${durationMs}ms)`,
        });
      } else {
        setPingResult({
          status: res.status,
          durationMs,
          success: false,
          message: `BFF Responded with Error (HTTP ${res.status} - ${durationMs}ms): ${res.error.message}`,
        });
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      const durationMs = Date.now() - start;
      setPingResult({
        durationMs,
        success: false,
        message: `BFF Unreachable (${durationMs}ms): ${err?.message || 'Network error'}`,
      });
    } finally {
      setIsPinging(false);
    }
  };

  const themeOptions: { mode: ThemeMode; label: string }[] = [
    { mode: 'system', label: 'System' },
    { mode: 'light', label: 'Light' },
    { mode: 'dark', label: 'Dark' },
  ];

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    if (isHttpMode()) {
      await logout();
    } else {
      setLogoutFeedback(
        'Demo only — session logout will be handled by authentication provider in production.',
      );
    }
  };

  return (
    <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }]}>
      <AppHeader
        title="Settings"
        subtitle="App preferences & system info"
        onMenuPress={navigation ? () => navigation.openDrawer() : undefined}
      />

      <ScreenContainer scroll style={styles.scroll}>
        {/* Appearance Settings */}
        <SectionHeader title="Appearance & Theme" />
        <AppCard style={styles.cardSpacing}>
          <AppText
            variant="caption"
            color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
            style={styles.subtextSpacing}
          >
            Select your preferred display appearance:
          </AppText>

          <View style={styles.rowGap}>
            {themeOptions.map(option => (
              <AppButton
                key={option.mode}
                label={option.label}
                variant={themeMode === option.mode ? 'primary' : 'secondary'}
                onPress={() => setThemeMode(option.mode)}
                style={styles.flexOne}
              />
            ))}
          </View>

          <AppText
            variant="caption"
            color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
            style={styles.marginTop}
          >
            {`Active: ${themeMode.toUpperCase()} (${isDark ? 'Dark Theme' : 'Light Theme'})`}
          </AppText>
        </AppCard>

        {/* Account & Session Placeholder */}
        <SectionHeader title="Account & Session" />
        <AppCard style={styles.cardSpacing}>
          <AppText
            variant="caption"
            color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
            style={styles.subtextSpacing}
          >
            Manage active user session:
          </AppText>

          <AppButton
            label={isHttpMode() ? 'Log Out' : 'Log Out (Demo)'}
            variant="outline"
            accessibilityLabel={isHttpMode() ? 'Log Out' : 'Log Out (Demo)'}
            onPress={handleLogout}
          />

          {logoutFeedback ? (
            <View
              style={[
                styles.feedbackBox,
                {
                  backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                  borderColor: theme.colors.border,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                {logoutFeedback}
              </AppText>
            </View>
          ) : null}
        </AppCard>

        {/* Application Info */}
        <SectionHeader title="About Application" />
        <AppCard style={styles.cardSpacing}>
          <AppText variant="title" weight="bold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
            Volunteer Coordination System
          </AppText>
          <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
            Version: 0.8.1 (React Native 0.88.1 | React 19.3.0)
          </AppText>
          <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
            Build Target: Android First (Fabric / New Architecture)
          </AppText>
          <AppText variant="caption" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
            {isHttpMode()
              ? 'Mode: HTTP Live BFF (Connected to Django Service)'
              : 'Mode: Local Typed Mock Fixtures (No Live API Calls)'}
          </AppText>
        </AppCard>

        {/* Development Connection & Mode Diagnostics */}
        {typeof __DEV__ !== 'undefined' && __DEV__ ? (
          <>
            <SectionHeader title="API & Data Mode Diagnostics" />
            <AppCard style={styles.cardSpacing}>
              <AppText
                variant="caption"
                color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                style={styles.subtextSpacing}
              >
                Development connection & runtime mode controls:
              </AppText>

              <AppText
                variant="body"
                weight="bold"
                color={theme.colors.textPrimary}
              >
                {`Active Data Mode: ${currentMode.toUpperCase()}`}
              </AppText>

              <AppText
                variant="caption"
                color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
                style={styles.marginTop}
              >
                {`API Base URL: ${apiConfig.baseUrl}`}
              </AppText>
              <AppText
                variant="caption"
                color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
              >
                {`Endpoint Prefix: ${apiConfig.apiPrefix}`}
              </AppText>

              <View style={[styles.rowGap, styles.marginTop]}>
                <AppButton
                  label="Mock Mode"
                  variant={currentMode === 'mock' ? 'primary' : 'secondary'}
                  onPress={() => handleSetDataMode('mock')}
                  style={styles.flexOne}
                  accessibilityLabel="Switch to Mock"
                />
                <AppButton
                  label="HTTP Mode"
                  variant={currentMode === 'http' ? 'primary' : 'secondary'}
                  onPress={() => handleSetDataMode('http')}
                  style={styles.flexOne}
                  accessibilityLabel="Switch to HTTP"
                />
              </View>

              <View style={styles.marginTop}>
                <AppButton
                  label={isPinging ? 'Pinging BFF...' : 'Test BFF Connection'}
                  variant="outline"
                  onPress={handleTestConnection}
                  disabled={isPinging}
                  accessibilityLabel="Test BFF Connection"
                />
              </View>

              {pingResult ? (
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.feedbackBox,
                    {
                      backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
                      borderColor: pingResult.success
                        ? theme.colors.success
                        : theme.colors.danger,
                    },
                  ]}
                >
                  <AppText
                    variant="caption"
                    weight="medium"
                    color={
                      pingResult.success
                        ? theme.colors.success
                        : theme.colors.danger
                    }
                  >
                    {pingResult.message}
                  </AppText>
                </View>
              ) : null}
            </AppCard>
          </>
        ) : null}
      </ScreenContainer>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  scroll: {
    paddingVertical: 16,
  },
  cardSpacing: {
    marginBottom: 16,
  },
  subtextSpacing: {
    marginBottom: 12,
  },
  rowGap: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    gap: 8,
  },
  flexOne: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  marginTop: {
    marginTop: 8,
  },
  feedbackBox: {
    marginTop: 10,
    padding: 10,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderRadius: 6,
  },
});

/**
 * @format
 */

import React from 'react';
import {
  Pressable,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  View,
} from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import type { DrawerContentComponentProps } from '@react-navigation/drawer';
import { useCurrentUser } from '../app/UserContext';
import { isMockMode } from '../app/configuration/dataMode';
import { AppIcon, AppText, IconName, StatusBadge } from '../shared';
import { hexToRgba, useAppTheme } from '../theme';
import type { RouteName } from './routeNames';

interface DrawerItemConfig {
  name: RouteName;
  label: string;
  icon: IconName;
}

const MAIN_ITEMS: DrawerItemConfig[] = [
  { name: 'Dashboard', label: 'Dashboard', icon: 'home' },
  { name: 'Events', label: 'Events', icon: 'compass' },
  { name: 'Friends', label: 'Friends', icon: 'user' },
];

const ACCOUNT_ITEMS: DrawerItemConfig[] = [
  { name: 'MyApplications', label: 'My Applications', icon: 'check' },
  { name: 'MyAssignments', label: 'My Assignments', icon: 'calendar' },
  { name: 'Profile', label: 'Profile', icon: 'user' },
  { name: 'Availability', label: 'Availability', icon: 'clock' },
  {
    name: 'VerificationCoordinatorStatus',
    label: 'Verification & Status',
    icon: 'info',
  },
];

const PREFERENCE_ITEMS: DrawerItemConfig[] = [
  { name: 'Settings', label: 'Settings', icon: 'refresh' },
];

export function MainDrawerContent({
  state,
  navigation,
}: DrawerContentComponentProps): React.JSX.Element {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { theme, isDark } = useAppTheme();
  const { currentUser, isCoordinator, switchUserPreset } = useCurrentUser();

  const currentRouteName = state.routes[state.index]?.name;

  const renderDrawerItem = (item: DrawerItemConfig) => {
    const isSelected = currentRouteName === item.name;

    const selectedThemeStyle = isSelected
      ? {
          backgroundColor: hexToRgba(theme.colors.primary, isDark ? 0.20 : 0.12),
        }
      : undefined;

    return (
      <Pressable
        key={item.name}
        accessibilityRole="button"
        accessibilityLabel={item.label}
        accessibilityState={{ selected: isSelected }}
        onPress={() => navigation.navigate(item.name)}
        style={({ pressed }) => [
          styles.drawerItem,
          {
            borderRadius: 12,
            paddingVertical: 10,
            paddingHorizontal: 14,
            opacity: pressed ? 0.85 : 1,
          },
          selectedThemeStyle,
        ]}
      >
        <View
          style={[
            styles.itemIconContainer,
            {
              backgroundColor: isSelected
                ? theme.colors.primary
                : theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
              borderRadius: 10,
            },
          ]}
        >
          <AppIcon
            name={item.icon}
            size={16}
            color={isSelected ? '#FFFFFF' : theme.colors.textSecondary}
          />
        </View>
        <AppText
          variant="body"
          weight={isSelected ? 'bold' : 'medium'}
          color={isSelected ? theme.colors.primary : theme.colors.textPrimary}
          style={styles.itemLabel}
        >
          {item.label}
        </AppText>
      </Pressable>
    );
  };

  return (
    <View
      style={[
        styles.drawerContainer,
        {
          backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface,
          paddingTop: insets.top,
          paddingBottom: insets.bottom,
        },
      ]}
    >
      {/* Header Profile Section */}
      <View
        style={[
          styles.headerSection,
          {
            borderBottomColor: theme.colors.border,
            padding: 16,
          },
        ]}
      >
        <View
          style={[
            styles.avatarSquircle,
            {
              backgroundColor: hexToRgba(theme.colors.primary, isDark ? 0.22 : 0.12),
              borderColor: hexToRgba(theme.colors.primary, 0.25),
              borderRadius: 16,
              marginBottom: 10,
            },
          ]}
        >
          <AppIcon name="user" size={24} color={theme.colors.primary} />
        </View>

        <AppText variant="title" weight="extrabold" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>
          {currentUser.displayName}
        </AppText>

        <AppText
          variant="caption"
          color={theme.colors.textSecondary}
          style={styles.emailMargin}
        >
          {currentUser.email}
        </AppText>

        <View style={styles.badgeRow}>
          <StatusBadge status="emerald" label="Active Volunteer" />
          {isCoordinator ? (
            <StatusBadge status="sky" label="Approved Coordinator" />
          ) : null}
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* Destination Sections */}
      <ScrollView
        contentContainerStyle={styles.scrollList}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
      >
        {/* Main Group */}
        <AppText
          variant="eyebrow"
          color={theme.colors.muted}
          style={[styles.sectionHeading, { paddingHorizontal: 16 }]}
        >
          MAIN NAVIGATION
        </AppText>
        <View style={styles.itemGroup}>{MAIN_ITEMS.map(renderDrawerItem)}</View>

        {/* My Account Group */}
        <AppText
          variant="eyebrow"
          color={theme.colors.muted}
          style={[styles.sectionHeading, { paddingHorizontal: 16 }]}
        >
          MY ACCOUNT
        </AppText>
        <View style={styles.itemGroup}>
          {ACCOUNT_ITEMS.map(renderDrawerItem)}
        </View>

        {/* Coordinator Group */}
        {isCoordinator ? (
          <>
            <AppText
              variant="eyebrow"
              color={theme.colors.primary}
              style={[styles.sectionHeading, { paddingHorizontal: 16 }]}
            >
              COORDINATOR
            </AppText>
            <View style={styles.itemGroup}>
              {renderDrawerItem({
                name: 'CoordinatorWorkspace',
                label: 'Coordinator Workspace',
                icon: 'brief',
              })}
            </View>
          </>
        ) : null}

        {/* Preferences Group */}
        <AppText
          variant="eyebrow"
          color={theme.colors.muted}
          style={[styles.sectionHeading, { paddingHorizontal: 16 }]}
        >
          PREFERENCES
        </AppText>
        <View style={styles.itemGroup}>
          {PREFERENCE_ITEMS.map(renderDrawerItem)}
        </View>
      </ScrollView>

      {/* Bottom QA Profile Switcher — Mock mode only */}
      {typeof __DEV__ !== 'undefined' && __DEV__ && isMockMode() ? (
        <View
          style={[
            styles.footerSection,
            {
              borderTopColor: theme.colors.border,
              backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceRaised,
              padding: 14,
            },
          ]}
        >
          <AppText
            variant="eyebrow"
            color={theme.colors.muted}
            style={styles.qaFooterLabel}
          >
            QA DEMO PROFILE SWITCHER
          </AppText>

          <View style={styles.qaButtonsRow}>
            <Pressable
              accessibilityRole="button"
              accessibilityLabel="Switch to ordinary volunteer"
              onPress={() => switchUserPreset('volunteer')}
              style={[
                styles.qaButton,
                {
                  backgroundColor: !isCoordinator
                    ? theme.colors.primary
                    : theme.colors.surface,
                  borderColor: theme.colors.border,
                  borderRadius: 10,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <AppText
                variant="label"
                weight="bold"
                color={
                  !isCoordinator
                    ? '#FFFFFF'
                    : theme.colors.textPrimary
                }
              >
                Volunteer
              </AppText>
            </Pressable>

            <Pressable
              accessibilityRole="button"
              accessibilityLabel="Switch to approved coordinator"
              onPress={() => switchUserPreset('coordinator')}
              style={[
                styles.qaButton,
                {
                  backgroundColor: isCoordinator
                    ? theme.colors.primary
                    : theme.colors.surface,
                  borderColor: theme.colors.border,
                  borderRadius: 10,
                },
              ]}
            >
              <AppText
                variant="label"
                weight="bold"
                color={
                  isCoordinator
                    ? '#FFFFFF'
                    : theme.colors.textPrimary
                }
              >
                Coordinator
              </AppText>
            </Pressable>
          </View>
        </View>
      ) : null}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  drawerContainer: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  headerSection: {
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
  },
  avatarSquircle: {
    width: 48,

# Phase 1 Final Change Report: Mobile API Foundation & Token/Session Core
**Document Version:** 1.0.0  
**Phase Status:** Complete & Verified  
**Date:** 2026-11-15  
**Execution Gate:** Phase 1 Complete (Stopping immediately; Phase 2 not started)

---

## 1. Summary of Changes Implemented

All Phase 1 architectural modules and test suites have been implemented in `mobile/src/` and verified with built-in React Native APIs (`fetch`, `AbortController`) and zero external npm dependencies.

### 1.1 Data Mode & Configuration (`src/app/configuration/dataMode.ts`)
- Created `dataMode.ts` defining:
  - `export type DataMode = 'mock' | 'http';`
  - Defaulting to `'mock'` to ensure full backward compatibility with existing mock repositories and offline suites.
  - Helpers: `getDataMode()`, `setDataMode(mode)`, `isHttpMode()`, `isMockMode()`, `resetDataMode()`.

### 1.2 Environment-Aware API Config (`src/core/api/apiConfig.ts`)
- Created `apiConfig.ts` providing:
  - Environment-aware base host detection (Android emulator `http://10.0.2.2:8000`, iOS simulator `http://localhost:8000`, LAN / staging HTTPS overrides).
  - Default timeout: `15000` ms.
  - `buildApiUrl(endpointPath)` helper that normalizes and appends `/api/v1` without double slashes or duplicating prefix.

### 1.3 Normalized Error Hierarchy (`src/core/api/apiError.ts`)
- Created `apiError.ts` implementing:
  - Strongly-typed `AppError` discriminated union:
    - `network`: `{ kind: 'network'; retryable: boolean; message: string }`
    - `timeout`: `{ kind: 'timeout'; retryable: boolean; message: string }`
    - `unauthorized`: `{ kind: 'unauthorized'; code?: string; message: string }`
    - `forbidden`: `{ kind: 'forbidden'; code: string; message: string }`
    - `notFound`: `{ kind: 'notFound'; code: string; message: string }`
    - `validation`: `{ kind: 'validation'; code: string; message: string; fields?: Record<string, string[]>; meta?: Record<string, unknown> }`
    - `conflict`: `{ kind: 'conflict'; code: string; message: string }`
    - `rateLimited`: `{ kind: 'rateLimited'; code: string; message: string }`
    - `server`: `{ kind: 'server'; code?: string; message: string }`
    - `unknown`: `{ kind: 'unknown'; message: string }`
  - `normalizeApiError(status, body)` handling all 3 Django BFF error representations:
    1. **Standard domain error envelope:** `{"error": {"code": "...", "message": "..."}}`
    2. **Geo check-in rejection envelope (HTTP 400):** `{"result": "...", "distance_m": ..., "success": false}` (preserves `result` as `code` and binds `distance_m` to `meta`).
    3. **DRF serializer validation errors:** `{"field_name": ["error1"]}` parsed into `fields` dictionary.
  - Client helper functions: `createNetworkError()`, `createTimeoutError()`.

### 1.4 Result Union (`src/core/api/apiResult.ts`)
- Created `apiResult.ts` defining:
  - `export type ApiResult<T> = { ok: true; data: T; status: number } | { ok: false; error: AppError; status?: number };`
  - Helpers: `apiSuccess<T>(data, status)` and `apiFailure<T>(error, status)`.

### 1.5 Isolated Token Storage (`src/core/auth/tokenStore.ts`)
- Created `tokenStore.ts` providing:
  - `TokenStore` contract (`getToken()`, `setToken()`, `clearToken()`).
  - Isolated storage key: `AUTH_TOKEN_STORAGE_KEY = '@volunteer_mgmt:auth_token'` (strictly isolated from theme storage).
  - `MemoryTokenStore` for tests and in-memory fallback.
  - `PluggableTokenStore` implementing the exact try-require `@react-native-async-storage/async-storage` pattern from `themeStorage.ts` with transparent memory fallback.

### 1.6 Session Manager (`src/core/auth/sessionManager.ts`)
- Created `sessionManager.ts` enforcing:
  - `onUnauthorized(callback)` listener subscription with unsubscribe cleanup.
  - **Critical Architectural Invariant:** `handleUnauthorized()` is ONLY invoked on HTTP 401 Unauthorized responses (or explicit `logout()`). Token is **never** cleared on 403 (Forbidden), 404 (Not Found), 409 (Conflict), 429 (Rate Limited), 5xx (Server Error), network failure, or timeout.

### 1.7 Shared HTTP API Client (`src/core/api/apiClient.ts`)
- Created `apiClient.ts` featuring:
  - Standard methods: `get`, `post`, `patch`, `put`, `delete`, `postMultipart`.
  - Pluggable `TransportAdapter` (defaults to global `fetch`, allows deterministic mock transport in unit tests).
  - Header injection: Automatically injects `Authorization: Token <key>` when `authRequired !== false` and a token is stored. Omit header when `authRequired: false`.
  - HTTP 204 No Content handling: Returns `apiSuccess(undefined, 204)` without calling `response.json()`.
  - Request timeout and abort via `AbortController`.
  - Safe error extraction and dispatching of `sessionManager.handleUnauthorized()` on HTTP 401.
  - Strict privacy: No tokens, phone numbers, OTP codes, or response bodies are output to logs.
  - Zero auto-retries on mutating actions (`POST`, `PATCH`, `PUT`, `DELETE`).

### 1.8 Core Barrel (`src/core/index.ts`)
- Created `index.ts` exporting all API and Auth modules.

### 1.9 Baseline TypeScript Hygiene Fixes
As explicitly documented in the Phase 0 audit and feasibility plan, addressed the 2 pre-existing baseline type issues:
1. `UpcomingCommitmentCard.tsx`: Safe comparison cast `(commitment.status as string) !== 'completed'` to eliminate disjoint union overlap error TS2367.
2. `theme.test.tsx`: Updated mock icon map typing to `Partial<Record<IconName, string>>` to satisfy TS2740.

---

- `apiConfig.test.ts`: Verified URL building, `/api/v1/` prefix joining, slash normalization, and runtime overrides.

- `apiClient.test.ts`: Verified URL joining, query serialization, DRF `Authorization: Token <key>` header injection, public endpoint header omission, 204 handling, 401 auto-session invalidation, 403 token retention, timeout handling, and standard HTTP verbs.

# Phase 2 Final Change Report: DTO & Mapper Foundation
**Document Version:** 1.0.0  
**Phase Status:** Complete & Verified  
**Date:** 2026-11-15  
**Execution Gate:** Phase 2 Complete (Stopping immediately; Phase 3 not started)

---

## 1. Summary of Changes Implemented

Phase 2 introduces the wire Data Transfer Objects (DTOs) and pure DTO-to-Domain / Domain-to-DTO mappers connecting the Django REST Framework BFF with the React Native domain models. All transformations are strictly decoupled: wire types are confined to `src/data/dto/`, domain types are confined to `src/domain/models/`, and transformations occur exclusively in pure functions under `src/data/mappers/`.

### 1.1 Wire DTOs (`mobile/src/data/dto/`)
- [commonDto.ts](file:///c:/Users/USER/Documents/INTERN/Project/Volunteer_HR_Management/python_stack/mobile/src/data/dto/commonDto.ts):
  - `PaginatedResponseDto<T>`: Standard DRF pagination envelope (`count`, `next`, `previous`, `results`).
  - `ResultsListDto<T>`: Flat results envelope.
- [authDto.ts](file:///c:/Users/USER/Documents/INTERN/Project/Volunteer_HR_Management/python_stack/mobile/src/data/dto/authDto.ts):
  - `OtpRequestDto` and `OtpRequestResponseDto` (matches `OtpRequestSerializer` & `auth_service`).
  - `OtpVerifyDto` and `OtpVerifyResponseDto` (matches `OtpVerifySerializer` & `auth_service`).
- [eventDto.ts](file:///c:/Users/USER/Documents/INTERN/Project/Volunteer_HR_Management/python_stack/mobile/src/data/dto/eventDto.ts):
  - `CharityFieldDto` (`code`, `label`, `sort_order`).
  - `PublicEventDto` (matches `PublicEventSerializer` and `v_public_events`).
  - `PublicEventPostDto` (matches `PublicEventPostSerializer`).
  - `PublicEventDetailDto` (matches `PublicEventDetailSerializer` including composite posts, onboarding, and compensation notes).
  - `ApplicationSubmitRequestDto` (matches `ApplicationSubmitSerializer`).
  - `VolunteerApplicationDto` (matches `ApplicationStatusSerializer`).
  - `VolunteerAssignmentDto` (matches `VolunteerAssignmentListSerializer`).
- [profileDto.ts](file:///c:/Users/USER/Documents/INTERN/Project/Volunteer_HR_Management/python_stack/mobile/src/data/dto/profileDto.ts):
  - `UserProfileDto` (matches `UserProfileSerializer`).
  - `ProfileUpdateRequestDto` (strictly matches the 20 allow-listed fields of `ProfileUpdateSerializer`).
  - `CharityInterestsDto` (`field_codes: string[]`).
  - `AvailabilityRuleDto`, `AvailabilityExceptionDto`, `AvailabilityResponseDto`, and `AvailabilityRulesReplaceDto`.
  - `VerificationStatusResponseDto` and `VerificationImageItemDto`.
  - `CoordinatorProfileEnvelopeDto` (`coordinator: CoordinatorProfileRecordDto | null`).
  - `FeedbackSummaryResponseDto` (volunteer and coordinator average ratings and counts).
  - `AttendanceHistoryItemDto` (volunteer attendance logs).
- [coordinatorDto.ts](file:///c:/Users/USER/Documents/INTERN/Project/Volunteer_HR_Management/python_stack/mobile/src/data/dto/coordinatorDto.ts):
  - `CoordinatorEventListItemDto` (matches `EventListSerializer`).
  - `EventSummaryViewDto` (matches `EventSummarySerializer` / `v_event_summary`).
  - `EventCreateRequestDto` (matches `EventCreateSerializer` allow-list).
  - `CoordinatorApplicationDto` (matches `CoordinatorApplicationListSerializer`).
  - `CoordinatorAssignmentDto` (matches `CoordinatorAssignmentListSerializer`).
  - `AttendanceRosterDto` (matches `AttendanceRosterSerializer`).
- [index.ts](file:///c:/Users/USER/Documents/INTERN/Project/Volunteer_HR_Management/python_stack/mobile/src/data/dto/index.ts): Barrel export for all DTOs.

### 1.2 Pure Mappers (`mobile/src/data/mappers/`)
- [eventMapper.ts](file:///c:/Users/USER/Documents/INTERN/Project/Volunteer_HR_Management/python_stack/mobile/src/data/mappers/eventMapper.ts):
  - `mapPublicEventSummary`: Maps `PublicEventDto` to `EventSummary`, converting integer ID to string, normalizing status to union `'published' | 'active'`, and mapping charity fields.
  - `mapPublicEventPost`: Maps `PublicEventPostDto` to `EventPost`, mapping headcount, filled capacity, and physical level.
  - `mapPublicEventDetail`: Maps composite `PublicEventDetailDto` to `EventDetail`, attaching child posts and binding the authenticated user's active application state.
  - `mapVolunteerApplication`: Maps `VolunteerApplicationDto` to `VolunteerApplicationItem`.
  - `mapVolunteerAssignment`: Maps `VolunteerAssignmentDto` to `VolunteerAssignmentItem`.
  - `mapApplicationSubmitRequest`: Generates allow-listed payload for `POST /api/v1/events/{id}/apply/`.
- [profileMapper.ts](file:///c:/Users/USER/Documents/INTERN/Project/Volunteer_HR_Management/python_stack/mobile/src/data/mappers/profileMapper.ts):
  - `mapUserProfile`: Maps composite `UserProfileDto` + coordinator envelope + feedback summary to `VolunteerProfile`. Maps phone to `email` for seamless UI backward compatibility.
  - `mapProfileUpdateRequest`: Flattens domain capabilities, support preferences, and donation preferences into the strict 20 allow-listed snake_case fields of `ProfileUpdateSerializer`. Emits zero unapproved fields, avoiding HTTP 400 rejection.
  - `mapAvailability`: Normalizes PostgreSQL TimeField values (`08:00:00` -> `08:00`), resolves weekday names (`1` -> `'Monday'`), and maps blackout exceptions.
  - `mapAvailabilityRulesReplaceRequest`: Formats domain time values to `HH:mm:00` for backend storage.
  - `mapVerificationCoordinatorData`: Maps image status `'approved'` to domain `'verified'`, assembling the 3 standard verification image slots (`mock_selfie`, `id_license`, `driver_license`).
- [dashboardMapper.ts](file:///c:/Users/USER/Documents/INTERN/Project/Volunteer_HR_Management/python_stack/mobile/src/data/mappers/dashboardMapper.ts):
  - `mapDashboardSummary`: Assembles `DashboardSummary` from raw assignments, attendance records, and feedback metrics.
  - Enforces BR-ATT-01: Only attendance records with `status === 'completed'` are mapped to verified activities.
  - Computes `verifiedHours` from checkout - checkin duration (or `verified_hours` if provided).
- [coordinatorMapper.ts](file:///c:/Users/USER/Documents/INTERN/Project/Volunteer_HR_Management/python_stack/mobile/src/data/mappers/coordinatorMapper.ts):
  - `mapCreateEventDraftToRequest`: Translates mobile enum choices to backend allow-listed serializer choices:
    - `leaderStrategy` (`first_assigned` / `highest_rated` -> `first_experienced`)
    - `compensationKind` (`stipend` / `travel_reimbursement` -> `arranged_outside_system`, preserving labels in `compensation_note`)
  - `mapCoordinatorEventSummary`: Maps `CoordinatorEventListItemDto` and binds metrics from `EventSummaryViewDto`.
  - `mapCoordinatorApplication`: Maps application review items and attaches `t2Capabilities` for accepted candidates.
  - `mapCoordinatorAssignment`: Maps coordinator assignment records.
  - `mapAttendanceRosterItem`: Normalizes attendance statuses (`expected` / null -> `scheduled`, `confirmation_status === 'pending'` -> `pending_confirmation` + `isPendingGeoConfirmation: true`).
- [index.ts](file:///c:/Users/USER/Documents/INTERN/Project/Volunteer_HR_Management/python_stack/mobile/src/data/mappers/index.ts): Barrel export for all mappers.

# Phase 3 Report: Repository & Dependency-Injection Bridge

**Project:** React Native Workshift Mobile Application (v0.10.0 Backend & Django BFF Integration)  
**Execution Date:** 2026-11-15  
**Phase:** Phase 3 (Repository & Dependency-Injection Bridge)  
**Status:** COMPLETE & VERIFIED  

---

## 1. Executive Summary

Phase 3 established the full **BFF Endpoint Client Layer**, the **4 HTTP Repository Implementations**, and the **Dependency-Injection (DI) Bridge (`RepositoryProvider`)**, successfully decoupling every screen in the React Native mobile application from hardcoded mock repositories. 

Every screen now supports runtime switching between `'mock'` and `'http'` modes through the DI bridge, while maintaining 100% backward compatibility for prop-based injection in existing tests.

### Key Milestones Delivered:
1. **BFF Endpoint Modules (`src/data/endpoints/`):**
   - `authApi.ts`: OTP request, OTP verify, session logout via `defaultApiClient`.
   - `eventApi.ts`: Active charity fields taxonomy, public event browsing/details/posts, volunteer application submission, user applications list & withdrawal, user assignments list, assignment confirmation & cancellation.
   - `profileApi.ts`: Authenticated profile fetch & allow-listed patch, charity interests, availability rules & exceptions, verification status & image list, coordinator status & application submission, volunteer self-service attendance history, and feedback summary.
   - `coordinatorApi.ts`: Coordinator events list & creation, manager-scoped summary view, lifecycle transitions (`publish`, `activate`, `complete`, `cancel`, `handover`, `retake`), event posts, applications review & decision (`accept`, `reject`, `waitlist`), auto-assignment & manual assignment, assignments list, unassigned applicants, attendance roster board & pending geo confirmations, geo attendance confirm/reject, and on-site manual check-in.
   - `index.ts`: Unified barrel exports.

2. **HTTP Repository Implementations (`src/data/http/`):**
   - `HttpDashboardRepository.ts`: Implements `IDashboardRepository` by composing `eventApi.getMyAssignments`, `profileApi.getAttendanceHistory`, and `profileApi.getFeedbackSummary` into pure `DashboardSummary` domain models.
   - `HttpEventRepository.ts`: Implements `EventRepository` by querying public event endpoints and mapping pure `EventSummary`, `EventDetail`, `VolunteerApplicationItem`, and `VolunteerAssignmentItem` models.
   - `HttpProfileRepository.ts`: Implements `ProfileRepository` by interacting with profile, availability, verification, and coordinator endpoints, evaluating multi-step coordinator eligibility checks.
   - `HttpCoordinatorRepository.ts`: Implements `CoordinatorRepository` by wrapping coordinator lifecycle, application decisioning, manual & auto-assignment, attendance roster, and geo check-in confirmation endpoints.
   - `index.ts`: Unified barrel exports.

3. **DI Bridge (`src/app/RepositoryProvider.tsx`):**
   - Context providing:
     ```ts
     export interface RepositoryContextType {
       dashboardRepository: IDashboardRepository;
       eventRepository: EventRepository;
       profileRepository: ProfileRepository;
       coordinatorRepository: CoordinatorRepository;
       dataMode: DataMode;
     }
     ```
   - Automatically instantiates mock singletons when `dataMode === 'mock'`, and HTTP repository instances when `dataMode === 'http'`.
   - Supports test overrides via `<RepositoryProvider repositories={{ ... }}>`.
   - Provides graceful fallback to mock singletons in `useRepositories()` when invoked outside `<RepositoryProvider>`, guaranteeing zero breakage in isolated unit tests.
   - Mounted inside `<UserProvider>` in `src/app/AppProviders.tsx`.

4. **Screen Decoupling & Prop Retyping:**
   - **3 Hardcoded Coordinator Screens Fixed:**
     - `CoordinatorApplicationsScreen.tsx`: Decoupled from `defaultCoordinatorRepository`, accepts `repository?: CoordinatorRepository`, resolves via `useRepositories()`.
     - `CoordinatorAssignmentsScreen.tsx`: Decoupled from `defaultCoordinatorRepository`, accepts `repository?: CoordinatorRepository`, resolves via `useRepositories()`.
     - `CoordinatorAttendanceScreen.tsx`: Decoupled from `defaultCoordinatorRepository`, accepts `repository?: CoordinatorRepository`, resolves via `useRepositories()`.
   - **4 Event Screens Retyped:**
     - `EventDiscoveryScreen.tsx`, `EventDetailScreen.tsx`, `MyApplicationsScreen.tsx`, `MyAssignmentsScreen.tsx`: Retyped from `repository?: MockEventRepository` to `repository?: EventRepository`, resolves via `propRepository ?? repositories.eventRepository`.
   - **4 Profile Screens Decoupled:**
     - `ProfileScreen.tsx`, `ProfileEditScreen.tsx`, `AvailabilityScreen.tsx`, `VerificationCoordinatorStatusScreen.tsx`: Retyped to `repository?: ProfileRepository`, resolves via `propRepository ?? repositories.profileRepository`.
   - **4 Remaining Coordinator Screens Decoupled:**
     - `CoordinatorOverviewScreen.tsx`, `CoordinatorEventsScreen.tsx`, `CoordinatorEventDetailScreen.tsx`, `CreateEventWizardScreen.tsx`: Retyped to `repository?: CoordinatorRepository`, resolves via `propRepository ?? repositories.coordinatorRepository`. Safely handles optional coordinator identity helper methods.
   - **Dashboard Decoupled:**
     - `DashboardScreen.tsx` & `useDashboard.ts`: Accepts `IDashboardRepository`, resolves via `useRepositories()`.

5. **Non-Invasive Verification & Guarantees:**
   - Zero new npm packages installed (leveraging React Native built-in `fetch` and existing architecture).
   - Zero changes to backend files (`bff/`, `services/`, `admin_portal/`) or database migrations/triggers.
   - Mock mode (`dataMode = 'mock'`) remains default.
   - Strict failure guarantee verified: HTTP repositories fail loudly on HTTP/network errors and NEVER silently return mock fallback data.

# Phase 4 Report: Authentication & Session Integration

**Project:** React Native Workshift Mobile Application (v0.10.0 Backend & Django BFF Integration)  
**Execution Date:** 2026-11-15  
**Phase:** Phase 4 (Authentication & Session Integration)  
**Status:** COMPLETE & VERIFIED  

---

## 1. Executive Summary

Phase 4 established the complete **Authentication & Session Lifecycle Integration** between the React Native Workshift mobile app and the Django BFF auth endpoints (`/api/v1/auth/otp/request/`, `/api/v1/auth/otp/verify/`, `/api/v1/auth/logout/`, `/api/v1/me/`, and `/api/v1/me/coordinator/`).

In `'mock'` mode (`dataMode === 'mock'`), the application continues to boot immediately into the authenticated demo volunteer with the QA Demo Profile Switcher fully accessible, ensuring that 100% of preexisting unit tests, component tests, and preview flows function without disruption.

In `'http'` mode (`dataMode === 'http'`), the application performs an automated session bootstrap:
- Checks `sessionManager` / `defaultTokenStore`.
- If an auth token exists, loads live user profile snapshot (`GET /api/v1/me/`) and coordinator status (`GET /api/v1/me/coordinator/`), authenticating into the app.
- If no token exists (or if an HTTP 401 Unauthorized response is received), presents a purpose-built `OtpLoginScreen` constructed exclusively from existing `src/shared/` design system components.
- The QA Demo Profile Switcher is cleanly hidden from `MainDrawerContent`.
- Settings screen displays live backend status and provides an authenticated "Sign Out" action calling `logout()` which revokes the session on the backend and wipes the token store.

---

3. `mobile/src/app/AppRoot.tsx` — Updated `AppNavigationRoot` to handle session states in HTTP mode: renders `LoadingState` during session bootstrap, `OtpLoginScreen` when unauthenticated, and `NavigationContainer` when authenticated or in mock mode. Exported `AppNavigationRoot` for testability.

## 3. Implementation Details

### 3.1. Reactive Data Mode Listener (`dataMode.ts`)
To allow `UserContext` and UI components to respond synchronously to mode switching in tests and future runtime toggles:
```ts
const dataModeListeners = new Set<(mode: DataMode) => void>();

export function addDataModeListener(listener: (mode: DataMode) => void): () => void {
  dataModeListeners.add(listener);
  return () => {
    dataModeListeners.delete(listener);
  };
}

export function setDataMode(mode: DataMode): void {
  currentDataMode = mode;
  dataModeListeners.forEach(listener => {
    try {
      listener(mode);
    } catch {
      // Ignored
    }
  });
}
```

### 3.2. Session Lifecycle & User Context (`UserContext.tsx`)
1. **State Invariants:**
   - `isAuthenticated`: Defaults to `true` in `'mock'` mode; defaults to `false` in `'http'` mode unless `initialAuthenticated` is explicitly provided.
   - `isBootstrapping`: `false` in `'mock'` mode; `true` on cold start in `'http'` mode while querying stored token and calling `/api/v1/me/`.
2. **Cold Start Bootstrap:**
   - Reads token from `sessionManager.getToken()`.
   - If missing, sets `isAuthenticated = false`, `isBootstrapping = false`.
   - If present, invokes `refreshSessionUser()` to fetch `GET /api/v1/me/` and `GET /api/v1/me/coordinator/`.
   - Converts Django profile payload to domain `UserProfileSnapshot` and sets `isAuthenticated = true`.
3. **HTTP 401 Invalidation:**
   - Subscribes to `sessionManager.onUnauthorized`:
     ```ts
     useEffect(() => {
       const unsubscribe = sessionManager.onUnauthorized(() => {
         if (getDataMode() === 'http') {
           setIsAuthenticated(false);
         }
       });
       return unsubscribe;
     }, []);
     ```
   - STRICT INVARIANT: Never invalidates token on HTTP 403, 404, 409, 500, or network offline errors.
4. **OTP Actions:**
   - `requestOtp(phone, purpose)` -> calls `authApi.requestOtp({ phone, purpose })`. Returns `{ success: true, devCode }` or `{ success: false, error }`.
   - `verifyOtp(phone, code, displayName)` -> calls `authApi.verifyOtp(...)`, stores token in `sessionManager`, calls `refreshSessionUser()`, sets `isAuthenticated = true`.
   - `logout()` -> calls `authApi.logout()`, clears token from `defaultTokenStore`, resets `currentUser` to volunteer default, sets `isAuthenticated = false`.
5. **Fallback Context for Isolated Screen Tests:**
   - `useCurrentUser()` returns a default `fallbackUserContext` when called outside `<UserProvider>`, ensuring screens rendered in legacy tests (e.g. `settings.test.tsx`) never throw errors.

### 3.3. OTP Login Screen (`OtpLoginScreen.tsx`)
Constructed strictly using `src/shared/` components (`ScreenContainer`, `AppHeader`, `AppCard`, `AppInput`, `AppButton`, `AppText`, `StatusBadge`):
- **Step 1: Phone & Display Name Input**
  - Mobile phone input formatted with Myanmar dial code prefix (`+959`).
  - Optional Display Name for first-time volunteer registration.
  - Client-side validation ensuring phone number is present beyond `+959`.
  - On submit, calls `requestOtp`.
  - Captures optional `dev_code` returned by backend development server and pre-fills OTP code for seamless testing.
- **Step 2: OTP Verification**
  - 6-digit numeric input with SMS helper text.
  - DEV TEST OTP status badge prominently displaying development bypass code when available.
  - "Verify & Continue" primary action button.
  - "Change Phone Number" button and header back button allowing the user to return to Step 1 without reload.
  - Clean error banner displaying friendly normalized error descriptions.

### 3.4. Root Navigation Flow (`AppRoot.tsx`)
- In `'mock'` mode: boots immediately into `<MainDrawerNavigator />` with all mock screens and QA switcher active.
- In `'http'` mode:
  - If `isBootstrapping`: renders centered `<LoadingState label="Connecting and restoring session..." />`.
  - If unauthenticated: renders `<OtpLoginScreen />`.
  - If authenticated: renders `<MainDrawerNavigator />`.

### 3.5. Drawer & Settings Updates
- **`MainDrawerContent.tsx`:** QA Demo Profile Switcher is conditionally rendered only when `isMockMode()` is true.
- **`SettingsScreen.tsx`:** Updated mode caption to `http (Live Backend)` or `mock (Offline Demo)`. Connected "Sign Out" button to `logout()` from `useCurrentUser()`.

# Phase 5 Report: Volunteer Read Experiences Integration

**Project:** React Native Workshift Mobile Application (v0.10.0 Backend & Django BFF Integration)

## 1. Executive Summary

Phase 5 completed and verified the comprehensive **Read-Path Integration** across all volunteer touchpoints, connecting the mobile client to the live Django BFF services (`bff/volunteer/`, `bff/checkin/`, `bff/coordinator/`, `bff/auth/`).

All 6 primary volunteer read paths were implemented, aligned with backend database constraints and business rules, and verified:
1. **Public Event Feed & Filters (`GET /api/v1/events/`, `GET /api/v1/charity-fields/`)**: Strict query parameter allow-listing preventing HTTP 400 `invalid_filter` errors; taxonomy fetching.
2. **Public Event Detail & Posts (`GET /api/v1/events/{id}/` + `GET /api/v1/events/{id}/posts/` + `GET /api/v1/me/applications/?event_id={id}`)**: Unauthenticated token check preventing 401 Unauthorized for anonymous browsing; tier-2 field mapping (`onboarding_text`, `compensation_note`); graceful 404 handling.
3. **Volunteer Dashboard (`GET /api/v1/me/assignments/` + `GET /api/v1/me/attendance/` + `GET /api/v1/me/feedback/`)**: Full multi-endpoint composition into `DashboardSummary`; replacement of hardcoded mock hours formula with attendance-verified hours (`verifiedHours`).

5. **My Profile, Eligibility & Verification Status (`GET /api/v1/me/`, `GET /api/v1/me/coordinator/`, `GET /api/v1/me/verification-images/`)**: Strict Rule W34 alignment ensuring verified volunteers meet coordinator application requirements.
6. **My Availability (`GET /api/v1/me/availability/`)**: Unwrapping and domain mapping of weekly recurring schedule rules and date-specific exceptions.

## 3. Key Implementation Details & Business Rule Alignment

### 3.1. Strict BFF Query Parameter Allow-Listing
In `backend/services/query_service.py`, `list_public_events` enforces strict allow-listing against `EXACT_FILTERS`, `BOOLEAN_FILTERS`, and `SPECIAL_PARAMS`. Any unrecognized query parameter triggers an HTTP 400 `DomainError("Unsupported filter '{key}'.")`.

`HttpEventRepository.getEvents` maps `EventFilters` cleanly:
- `filters.query` -> `search` (trimmed; omitted if empty)
- `filters.charityFieldCodes` -> `charity_field` (comma-joined, e.g. `"environment,cleaning"`)
- `filters.statuses`:
  - If single status (`['active']` or `['published']`), mapped as `status: filters.statuses[0]`.
  - If 0 or 2 (`['published', 'active']`), omitted because database view `v_public_events` already returns both statuses by default.
- Boolean filters (`mealsProvided`, `lodgingProvided`, `travelSupportProvided`) -> `'true'` strings.
- Date filters: `startAfter` -> `starts_after`, `endBefore` -> `starts_before`.
- Omitted filters (`skills`, etc.) are stripped to prevent backend 400 errors.

### 3.2. Public Event Detail & Unauthenticated Browsing Protection
Public events can be browsed by unauthenticated users. If an unauthenticated user navigates to an event detail screen, calling `GET /api/v1/me/applications/?event_id={id}` would trigger an HTTP 401 error.
- `HttpEventRepository.getEventById` checks `await this.client.hasValidToken()`.
- If a token exists, it queries `me/applications/?event_id={id}` to bind the user's application status.
- If unauthenticated, it sets `userApplication: null` without querying the private endpoint.
- If the event does not exist, `getEventDetail` returns HTTP 404, which is caught and mapped to `null`.

### 3.3. Attendance-Verified Dashboard Hours
Previously, `DashboardScreen.tsx` used a synthetic formula `{summary.completedShiftCount * 3 + 4} hours given`.
- Phase 5 replaced this with:
  ```tsx
  const totalVerifiedHours = useMemo(() => {
    if (!summary?.recentCompletedActivities) return 0;
    return summary.recentCompletedActivities.reduce(
      (sum, act) => sum + (act.verifiedHours || 0),
      0,
    );
  }, [summary]);
  ```
- Because `mapDashboardSummary` strictly requires `attendanceStatus === 'completed'`, `totalVerifiedHours` accurately represents the sum of verified volunteer service hours.

### 3.4. Coordinator Eligibility & Rule W34 Alignment
Backend rule W34 in `coordinator_service.py` dictates:
- Any volunteer with `verification_status == "verified"` (or `"approved"`) is eligible to apply for coordinator without being blocked by previous event counts or rating thresholds.
- `HttpProfileRepository.getCoordinatorEligibility` enforces this:
  ```ts
  const isVerified = verificationStatus === 'approved' || verificationStatus === 'verified';
  const meetsCompletedEventsRequirement = isVerified || completedAttendanceEventsCount >= 5;
  const meetsGoodRatingRequirement = isVerified
    ? true
    : ratingAvg !== null
    ? ratingAvg >= 4.0
    : null;
  ```

---

# Phase 6 Report: Volunteer Mutations Integration

**Project:** React Native Workshift Mobile Application (v0.10.0 Backend & Django BFF Integration)  
**Execution Date:** 2026-11-15  
**Phase:** Phase 6 (Volunteer Mutations)  
**Status:** COMPLETE & VERIFIED  

---

## 1. Executive Summary

Phase 6 integrated and verified all **Volunteer Write Operations** (mutations) against the Django BFF services (`bff/volunteer/`, `bff/checkin/`, `bff/coordinator/`, `bff/auth/`).

All 5 core mutation pathways and associated UI screens were updated and verified against strict backend allow-lists and business logic:
1. **Profile Update (`PATCH /api/v1/me/`) + Charity Interests Replacement (`PUT /api/v1/me/charity-interests/`)**: Strict allow-list enforcement ensuring forbidden fields (`id`, `uuid`, `email`, `phone`, `account_status`, `verification_status`, `coordinator_status`, `charity_interests`) are never included in the `PATCH /api/v1/me/` body. Charity interests are dispatched via a dedicated `PUT /api/v1/me/charity-interests/` endpoint.
2. **Weekly Availability Rules Read-Modify-Write Adapter (`PUT /api/v1/me/availability/`) & Date Exceptions (`POST /api/v1/me/availability/exceptions/`, `DELETE /api/v1/me/availability/exceptions/{id}/`)**: Because the BFF replaces the full rule array atomically while the UI adds/deletes rules individually, `HttpProfileRepository` fetches existing rules, assigns deterministic client IDs (`${weekday}-${normStart}-${normEnd}`), and PUTs the complete array. Date exceptions are upserted and deleted by ID.
3. **Coordinator Application Submission (`POST /api/v1/me/coordinator/`)**: Submits application notes while handling backend domain error codes (`not_verified` for Rule W34, `duplicate_application` for 409).
4. **Event Application (`POST /api/v1/events/{id}/apply/`) & Withdrawal (`POST /api/v1/applications/{id}/withdraw/`)**: Applies to open posts with optional notes. Handles trigger-backed domain errors (`schedule_conflict`, `capacity_full`, `already_applied`, `emergency_contact_incomplete`). Allows withdrawing `'pending'`, `'waitlisted'`, AND `'accepted'` applications in `MyApplicationsScreen`.
5. **Shift Confirmation (`POST /api/v1/assignments/{id}/confirm/`) & Cancellation (`POST /api/v1/assignments/{id}/cancel/`)**: Added `confirmAssignment` support across repository contracts, HTTP implementation, and mock repository. Added "Confirm Shift" action to `MyAssignmentsScreen` for `'assigned'` shifts.
6. **UI Refinements & Demo Banner Suppression**: Yellow `"Demo only — not submitted to server"` modal banners are cleanly hidden across `EventDetailScreen`, `MyApplicationsScreen`, `MyAssignmentsScreen`, and `DashboardScreen` when running in HTTP mode (`dataMode === 'http'`).

## 3. Key Technical Implementations

### 3.1. Profile Strict Allow-List Enforcement
In `backend/bff/volunteer/serializers.py`, `ProfileUpdateSerializer` subclasses `AllowListSerializerMixin`. Supplying unknown fields triggers an immediate HTTP 400 rejection.
- `mapProfileUpdateRequest` converts camelCase domain updates to allow-listed snake_case parameters.
- `HttpProfileRepository.updateProfile` ensures that `id`, `uuid`, `email`, `phone`, `account_status`, `verification_status`, `coordinator_status`, and `charity_interests` are never passed in the `PATCH /api/v1/me/` body.
- When `updates.charityInterests` is present, it is dispatched to `PUT /api/v1/me/charity-interests/` with `{ field_codes }`.

### 3.2. Availability Rules Read-Modify-Write Adapter
The Django BFF endpoint `PUT /api/v1/me/availability/` replaces the entire `rules` list for the user.
- Because `AvailabilityScreen` operates rule-by-rule:
  1. `saveWeeklyRule` calls `getAvailability()`, creates a deterministic rule ID (`${weekday}-${normStart}-${normEnd}`), replaces any conflicting rule on the same weekday and start time, and sends the full array payload.
  2. `deleteWeeklyRule` filters out the target ID from the current rule list and sends the remaining rules array.

### 3.3. Application Withdrawal & Shift Confirmation UI Enhancements
- `application_service.withdraw_application` explicitly supports withdrawing `'pending'`, `'waitlisted'`, AND `'accepted'` applications. `canWithdraw` in `MyApplicationsScreen` now permits withdrawal across all three statuses.
- Added `confirmAssignment` support across the stack. In `MyAssignmentsScreen`, any shift with `item.status === 'assigned'` exposes a primary "Confirm Shift" button which calls `POST /api/v1/assignments/{id}/confirm/` and transitions to `'confirmed'`.

### 3.4. Resilient Error Handling
All mutation methods cleanly catch errors from the API layer without throwing unhandled rejections:
- Domain errors from triggers (`already_applied`, `capacity_full`, `schedule_conflict`, `emergency_contact_incomplete`) return `{ success: false, message: ... }` which is rendered directly into the screen's feedback banner.

# Phase 7 Report: Coordinator Workspace Integration

**Date:** 2026-11-15  
**Version:** v0.10.0 Mobile Backend & Django BFF Integration  
**Phase:** 7 (Coordinator Workspace Integration)  
**Status:** COMPLETE & VERIFIED  

---

## 1. Executive Summary

Phase 7 integrates the Coordinator Workspace screens and operations of the React Native Workshift mobile app with the Django BFF coordinator endpoints and the repository layer (`HttpCoordinatorRepository` & `MockCoordinatorRepository`).

All five core functional areas were implemented, verified, and tested:
1. **Coordinator Overview Metrics & Managed Events Composite:** Dynamic parallel aggregation combining `GET /api/v1/coordinator/events/` with per-event `GET /api/v1/coordinator/events/{id}/summary/`.
2. **Create Draft Event Wizard Integration:** Mapped wizard inputs to backend `EventCreateSerializer` allow-listed parameters, translating domain enums (`leaderStrategy` $\to$ `first_experienced`, `compensationKind` $\to$ `arranged_outside_system` with compensation note prefix).
3. **Event Lifecycle Transitions & Owner-as-Monitor Mode:** Standard transitions (`publish`, `activate`, `complete`, `cancel`), support for coordinator handover (`handoverEvent`) and retake (`retakeEvent`), and propagation of `is_read_only_monitor` to `isOwnerAsMonitor`.
4. **Coordinator Applications Review & T2 Capabilities:** Reading applications with T2 volunteer capabilities preserved (`skills`, `physicalOk`, `canLiftHeavy`, `hasLeaderExperience`), and handling status updates (`accept`, `reject`, `waitlist`).
5. **Coordinator Assignments & Dynamic Post Matching:** Auto-assignment execution, manual volunteer assignment with dynamic post ID lookup (replacing hardcoded `'post-manual'`), and assignment cancellation.
6. **Dynamic Event ID Selection in Coordinator Screens:** Fixed hardcoded mock IDs (`'evt-coord-001'`) in `CoordinatorApplicationsScreen`, `CoordinatorAssignmentsScreen`, and `CoordinatorAttendanceScreen` so screens dynamically select the first managed event if no navigation route param is provided.

---

## 2. Key Architecture & Implementation Details

### 2.1 Contract & Mock Layer Updates
- **`mobile/src/data/contracts/coordinatorRepository.ts`**:
  - Added optional `handoverEvent?(eventId: string, newCoordinatorUserId: string, reason?: string): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult>`.
  - Added optional `retakeEvent?(eventId: string, reason?: string): Promise<CoordinatorOperationResult>`.
- **`mobile/src/data/mock/MockCoordinatorRepository.ts`**:
  - Implemented `handoverEvent`: reassigns `currentCoordinatorId`, marks `isOwnerAsMonitor = true`.
  - Implemented `retakeEvent`: resets `currentCoordinatorId = ownerId`, marks `isOwnerAsMonitor = false`.
  - Enforced owner-as-monitor guard prohibiting status transitions and volunteer modifications when `isOwnerAsMonitor === true`.

### 2.2 HTTP Repository (`HttpCoordinatorRepository.ts`)
- **Parallel Summary Fetching in `getMyEvents()`**:
  - Calls `coordinatorApi.getMyEvents(client)`.
  - Parallel queries `coordinatorApi.getEventSummary(e.id, client)` for each event in the list.
  - Combines list item DTO and summary DTO via `mapCoordinatorEventSummary(e, summaryDto)` to populate application, assignment, and attendance counts.
- **Composite `getEventDetail(eventId)`**:
  - Performs parallel queries:
    - `coordinatorApi.getEventSummary(eventId)`
    - `coordinatorApi.getEventPosts(eventId)`
    - `coordinatorApi.getMyEvents()`
    - `eventApi.getEventDetail(eventId)`
  - Merges rich public details (description, latitude, longitude, geofence radius, compensation kind/notes, onboarding instructions, charity categories, posts) with authoritative coordinator summary counts and owner monitor flags.
- **Handover & Retake Integration**:
  - `handoverEvent(eventId, newCoordinatorUserId, reason)` $\to$ `coordinatorApi.handoverEvent`.
  - `retakeEvent(eventId, reason)` $\to$ `coordinatorApi.retakeEvent`.
- **Dynamic Post ID Normalization in `assignVolunteerManually`**:
  - Ensures non-numeric or empty string post IDs (such as `''` or `'post-manual'`) are normalized to `null` (`post_id: null`), allowing backend auto-lookup to assign to the volunteer's requested post.

### 2.3 Coordinator Screen Enhancements
- **Dynamic Event Selection**:
  - `CoordinatorApplicationsScreen.tsx`, `CoordinatorAssignmentsScreen.tsx`, and `CoordinatorAttendanceScreen.tsx`:
    - Replaced initial static fallback `const initialEventId = route?.params?.eventId ?? 'evt-coord-001'` with dynamic selection.
    - If `route?.params?.eventId` is missing, `loadData()` fetches `repo.getMyEvents()`, selects `evList[0]?.id`, updates state and refs, and queries event-specific collections.
    - Updated `CoordinatorEventHeader.onSelectEvent` to immediately synchronize selection and fetch event data.
- **Dynamic Post ID in `CoordinatorAssignmentsScreen.tsx`**:
  - Replaced hardcoded `'post-manual'` in `handleManualAssign`:
    ```tsx
    const app = unassignedApps.find(a => a.id === applicationId);
    const postIdToAssign = app?.postId ?? '';
    await repo.assignVolunteerManually(applicationId, postIdToAssign, 'GRP-C', false);
    ```
