# Phase 8 Report: Onboarding, QR, Attendance & Feedback Integration

**Date:** 2026-11-15  
**Version:** v0.10.0 Mobile Backend & Django BFF Integration  
**Phase:** 8 (Onboarding, QR, Attendance & Feedback Integration)  
**Status:** COMPLETE & VERIFIED  

---

## 1. Executive Summary

Phase 8 completes the integration of volunteer on-site operational workflows and coordinator attendance management against the Django BFF (`bff/checkin/urls.py` and `services/checkin/`):

1. **Volunteer Onboarding Briefing & Acknowledgment:**
   - Reading onboarding briefing and acknowledgment status (`GET /api/v1/events/{id}/onboarding/`).
   - Acknowledging onboarding instructions (`POST /api/v1/events/{id}/onboarding/acknowledge/`).
2. **QR Token Lifecycle (Resolve, Redeem, Check-In, Create, Revoke):**
   - Resolving QR tokens non-destructively for metadata/validity inspection (`POST /api/v1/qr/resolve/`).
   - Redeeming QR tokens for shift check-in or walk-in registration (`POST /api/v1/qr/redeem/`).
   - Performing volunteer QR-based check-in (`POST /api/v1/attendance/qr-checkin/`).
   - Coordinator QR token generation (`POST /api/v1/coordinator/events/{id}/qr-tokens/`) and token revocation (`POST /api/v1/coordinator/qr-tokens/{id}/revoke/`).
3. **Volunteer Geo Check-In & Error Telemetry:**
   - Performing location-based check-in with GPS telemetry (`POST /api/v1/attendance/geo-checkin/`).
   - Normalizing backend HTTP 400 responses (`outside_geofence`, `already_checked_in`, etc.) to expose `distance_m`, `result`, and `message` without throwing unhandled exceptions or leaking raw coordinates into application logs.
4. **Shift Checkout (Strict Endpoint Route):**
   - Completing shifts via `POST /api/v1/attendance/{attendance_id}/checkout/` (enforcing the strict route per Constraint 1).
5. **Coordinator Attendance Roster, Geo Approval & Manual Check-In:**
   - Fetching event attendance roster (`GET /api/v1/coordinator/events/{id}/attendance/`).
   - Querying pending geo confirmations (`GET /api/v1/coordinator/events/{id}/attendance/pending/`).
   - Confirming and rejecting pending geo check-ins (`POST /api/v1/coordinator/attendance/{id}/confirm/`, `POST /api/v1/coordinator/attendance/{id}/reject/`).
   - Manual on-site check-in (`POST /api/v1/attendance/manual-checkin/`) with automatic roster lookup for `event_id` and `user_id` (enforcing Constraint 2).
6. **Volunteer Event Feedback:**
   - Submitting post-event feedback (`POST /api/v1/events/{id}/feedback/`) with rating scores, structured feedback tags, and optional comments.
7. **Volunteer Mobile UI Experience:**
   - Seamlessly wired actions into `MyAssignmentsScreen.tsx` with modal dialogs for Onboarding Briefing, Geofenced Check-In, Shift Checkout, and Event Feedback with zero layout redesign.

---

## 2. Strict Constraint Verification & Architecture Decisions

### Constraint 1: Checkout Route Contract
- **Requirement:** Must call `POST /api/v1/attendance/{attendance_id}/checkout/` instead of `/events/{event_id}/checkout/`.
- **Implementation:**
  - In `checkinApi.ts`, `checkoutAttendance(attendanceId, client)` constructs the path `attendance/${attendanceId}/checkout/`.
  - In `HttpEventRepository.ts`, `checkOutAttendance(attendanceId)` delegates directly to `checkinApi.checkoutAttendance(attendanceId, this.client)`.
  - In `MockEventRepository.ts`, `checkOutAttendance(attendanceId)` finds the assignment record and updates its `attendanceStatus` to `'completed'`.

### Constraint 2: Manual Check-In Payload & Roster Lookup
- **Requirement:** Backend view `ManualCheckinView` expects `{ event_id: number, user_id: number, note?: string }`. It does not accept `attendance_id` or string IDs.
- **Implementation:**
  - `HttpCoordinatorRepository.attendanceCache` caches roster items indexed by both `record.id` and `record.assignmentId`.
  - When `manualCheckIn(attendanceId)` is invoked:
    1. Looks up the item in `attendanceCache`.
    2. Extracts `record.eventId` and `record.userId`.
    3. Normalizes both to numeric IDs (`Number(record.eventId)` and `Number(record.userId)`).
    4. Calls `checkinApi.manualCheckIn({ event_id, user_id, note: 'Manual on-site check-in' })`.
  - Re-fetches the roster immediately to keep local state synchronized.

### Constraint 3: Geo Check-In Telemetry & HTTP 400 Result Handling
- **Requirement:** Geofence validation failures return HTTP 400 with `{ success: false, result: 'outside_geofence', distance_m: ..., message: ... }`. The repository must not crash or leak raw GPS coordinates to logs, but must preserve `distance_m`, `result`, `confirmation_status`, and `message` for user feedback.
- **Implementation:**
  - In `HttpEventRepository.ts`:
    ```typescript
    try {
      const response = await checkinApi.submitGeoCheckIn(dto, this.client);
      return {
        success: response.success,
        result: response.result,
        message: response.message,
        distanceM: response.distance_m,
        confirmationStatus: response.confirmation_status,
      };
    } catch (error: any) {
      const meta = 'meta' in error ? (error.meta as Record<string, any>) : undefined;
      const distanceM = meta?.distance_m !== undefined ? Number(meta.distance_m) : undefined;
      const result = meta?.result ?? ('code' in error ? error.code : undefined) ?? 'outside_geofence';
      return {
        success: false,
        result: String(result),
        message: error.message || 'Outside allowed geofence area.',
        distanceM,
        confirmationStatus: 'pending',
      };
    }
    ```
  - Logs are clean of latitude/longitude coordinates; UI receives structured distance telemetry.

### Constraint 4: UI Integration Without Redesign
- **Requirement:** Wire Onboarding, Check-In, Checkout, and Feedback into `MyAssignmentsScreen.tsx` without breaking existing layout or themes.
- **Implementation:**
  - Utilized existing shared design tokens (`AppCard`, `AppText`, `AppButton`, `AppInput`, `StatusBadge`, `colors`, `spacing`, `typography`).

    - `Briefing`: Opens modal to view instructions and acknowledge.
    - `Check In`: Opens modal with current simulated/device GPS telemetry, displaying geofence distance and status.
    - `Check Out`: Opens checkout modal confirming shift completion.
    - `Feedback`: Opens modal to submit 1-5 star rating and feedback comments.
  - All existing tabs, filters, and assignment status badges remain completely intact.

# Phase 9 Report: Stabilization, Diagnostics & Release Readiness

**Date:** 2026-11-15  
**Version:** v0.10.0 Mobile Backend & Django BFF Integration  
**Phase:** 9 (Stabilization, Diagnostics & Release Readiness)  
**Status:** COMPLETE & VERIFIED  

---

## 1. Executive Summary

Phase 9 completes the final stabilization, diagnostic tooling, cache invalidation, and end-to-end regression verification for the React Native Workshift mobile application integration with the Django Backend-for-Frontend (BFF).

Key achievements in Phase 9:
1. **In-Memory Cache Invalidation on Logout & 401 Unauthorized:**
   - Wired `resetDiscoveryCache()`, `resetApplicationsCache()`, and `resetAssignmentsCache()` into `UserContext.logout()` and `sessionManager.onUnauthorized()`.
   - Guarantees that T0–T4 screen states, scroll positions, active filter selections, and cached application/assignment items are purged immediately when the session ends or encounters an authorization failure, preventing any data leakage across user accounts.
2. **Safe Development Connection Diagnostic Panel:**
   - Added an "API & Data Mode Diagnostics" card in `SettingsScreen.tsx`, gated strictly by `typeof __DEV__ !== 'undefined' && __DEV__`.
   - Complied with **Strict Constraint 1**: NEVER displays tokens, phone numbers, passwords, or secrets.
   - Displays current `DataMode` (`MOCK` vs `HTTP`), configured API Base URL (`apiConfig.baseUrl`), and API Prefix (`apiConfig.apiPrefix`).
   - Includes a "Test BFF Connection" button that measures live round-trip latency to the public taxonomy endpoint (`GET /api/v1/charity-fields/`) and displays HTTP status and duration in milliseconds.
   - Includes a safe development-only runtime mode switcher between `Mock Mode` and `HTTP Mode` via `setDataMode()`.
3. **End-to-End Full Mobile Lifecycle Integration Test Suite:**
   - Created `mobile/src/data/__tests__/e2eMobileLifecycle.test.ts`.
   - Exercises the complete mobile lifecycle end-to-end against `HttpEventRepository`, `HttpCoordinatorRepository`, `HttpProfileRepository`, and `authApi`/`sessionManager` using a deterministic mock HTTP transport:
     1. OTP Request & Verification
     2. Session Bootstrap & Profile Hydration
     3. Event Taxonomy & Event Discovery with Search & Filter
     4. Public Event Detail & Posts Lookup
     5. Volunteer Application Submission
     6. Coordinator Application Review & Acceptance
     7. Coordinator Auto-Assignment Execution
     8. Volunteer Shift Assignment Fetch & Confirmation
     9. Onboarding Briefing Read & Acknowledgment
     10. GPS Geofenced Check-In Telemetry
     11. Coordinator Attendance Roster & Geo Confirmation
     12. Volunteer Shift Checkout (`POST /api/v1/attendance/{id}/checkout/`)
     13. Volunteer Event Feedback Submission (`POST /api/v1/events/{id}/feedback/`)
     14. Session Logout & Screen Cache Reset Verification
4. **Zero Backend/Database File Modifications Confirmed:**
   - Strict audit confirmed 0 modifications to `bff/`, `services/`, `admin_portal/`, `common/`, `apps/`, `config/`, SQL scripts, or migrations across the entire integration.

---

## 2. Strict Constraint Verification

### Constraint 1: Safe Diagnostics (No Tokens or Secrets)
- In `SettingsScreen.tsx`:
  - Renders only `currentMode.toUpperCase()`, `apiConfig.baseUrl`, `apiConfig.apiPrefix`, and ping result latency / HTTP status.
  - No authorization headers, session tokens, user phone numbers, or passwords are ever exposed or rendered.
  - Verified by dedicated regex assertions in `__tests__/settings.test.tsx` (`expect(json).not.toMatch(/token/i)`, `expect(json).not.toMatch(/\+?[0-9]{10,}/)`, `expect(json).not.toContain('password')`, `expect(json).not.toContain('secret')`).

### Constraint 2: Session Cache Purge on Logout
- In `src/app/UserContext.tsx`:
  - `logout()` invokes:
    ```typescript
    resetDiscoveryCache();
    resetApplicationsCache();
    resetAssignmentsCache();
    ```
  - `sessionManager.onUnauthorized()` invokes:
    ```typescript
    resetDiscoveryCache();
    resetApplicationsCache();
    resetAssignmentsCache();
    ```
  - Verified in `e2eMobileLifecycle.test.ts` where discovery, application, and assignment caches were inspected after logout and confirmed reset to initial empty states (`searchQuery: ''`, `scrollOffset: 0`, `statusFilter: 'all'`).

### Constraint 3: Zero Backend or Database Modifications
- Git status verified across `bff/`, `services/`, `admin_portal/`, `common/`, `apps/`, `config/`, and database migration files:
  ```text
  git status -- bff services admin_portal common apps config migrations *.sql
  -> no changes added to commit (clean)
  ```

# v0.10.0 Mobile Backend & Django BFF Integration: Final Completion & Verification Summary

**Date:** 2026-11-15  
**Version:** v0.10.0  
**Project:** React Native Workshift Mobile Application & Django Backend-for-Frontend (BFF)  
**Overall Status:** FULLY IMPLEMENTED, INTEGRATED & VERIFIED (Phases 0–9 Complete)  

---

## 1. Executive Summary

The v0.10.0 Mobile Backend & Django BFF Integration has successfully bridged the React Native Workshift mobile application with the production Django BFF services (`services/` and `bff/`). 

Across all 10 phases, the integration was executed with zero breaking changes to existing UI layouts, zero changes to PostgreSQL database schemas/migrations, and zero changes to Django backend files. The application now supports seamless dual-mode execution (`mock` for deterministic previews/isolated tests and `http` for live production API communication).

---

## 2. Phase-by-Phase Delivery Matrix

| Phase | Title | Key Deliverables | Verification Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Phase 0** | Discovery & Baseline Freeze | Clean baseline test run (18 suites, 237 tests); frozen contracts and token safety rules. | COMPLETE & VERIFIED |
| **Phase 1** | Mobile API Foundation & Token/Session Core | `DataMode` configuration (`mock` vs `http`), `TokenStore` with secure fallback, `SessionManager`, `ApiClient` with error normalization, and `ApiConfig`. | COMPLETE & VERIFIED |
| **Phase 2** | DTO & Mapper Foundation | Wire DTOs (`authDto`, `profileDto`, `eventDto`, `coordinatorDto`, `dashboardDto`, `checkinDto`) and pure bidirectional mappers matching Django `AllowListSerializerMixin` serializers. | COMPLETE & VERIFIED |
| **Phase 3** | Repository & DI Bridge | `HttpEventRepository`, `HttpCoordinatorRepository`, `HttpProfileRepository`, `HttpDashboardRepository`, `RepositoryProvider` React context bridge, and backward-compatible screen prop injection. | COMPLETE & VERIFIED |
| **Phase 4** | Auth & Session Integration | OTP request and verification (`/api/v1/auth/otp/`), cold-start profile bootstrap (`/api/v1/me/`, `/api/v1/me/coordinator/`), token lifecycle, and session logout. | COMPLETE & VERIFIED |
| **Phase 5** | Volunteer Read Experiences | Public event feed, search, and taxonomies (`/api/v1/events/`, `/api/v1/charity-fields/`), event detail with posts, volunteer dashboard composites, application history, profile eligibility & verification status. | COMPLETE & VERIFIED |
| **Phase 6** | Volunteer Mutations | Profile updating (`PATCH /api/v1/me/`), charity interests replacement (`PUT /api/v1/me/charity-interests/`), weekly availability & exceptions, coordinator application, event application & withdrawal, assignment confirmation & cancellation. | COMPLETE & VERIFIED |
| **Phase 7** | Coordinator Workspace Integration | Coordinator metrics & managed events aggregation, create draft event wizard, event lifecycle transitions (`publish`, `activate`, `complete`, `cancel`, `handover`, `retake`), applicant review with T2 capabilities, dynamic post auto & manual assignment. | COMPLETE & VERIFIED |
| **Phase 8** | Onboarding, QR, Attendance & Feedback | Onboarding instructions & acknowledgment (`/events/{id}/onboarding/`), QR resolve/redeem/check-in, geo check-in telemetry with error handling, shift checkout (`/attendance/{id}/checkout/`), coordinator roster management, and event feedback submission. | COMPLETE & VERIFIED |
| **Phase 9** | Stabilization & Release Readiness | Safe development connection diagnostic panel in `SettingsScreen.tsx`, session cache clearing on logout/401, full 14-step mobile lifecycle integration test suite (`e2eMobileLifecycle.test.ts`), and final regression verification. | COMPLETE & VERIFIED |

---

## 3. Architecture & Strict Non-Invasive Constraints Audit

1. **Zero Database Modifications:**
   - 0 PostgreSQL migrations, functions, views, or triggers were added or modified.
2. **Zero Backend Modifications:**
   - 0 changes made to `services/`, `bff/`, `admin_portal/`, `common/`, `apps/`, or `config/`.
3. **Zero New Third-Party Dependencies:**
   - No external libraries (such as `axios` or `zod`) were introduced. Uses React Native built-in `fetch`, `AbortController`, and modular functional validators.
4. **Strict UI Layout & Design Token Preservation:**
   - Preserved all theme tokens in `src/theme/`, shared primitives in `src/shared/`, and screen visual hierarchies.
5. **Security & Information Privacy:**
   - Tokens, raw GPS coordinates, phone numbers, and OTP codes are strictly omitted from logs and diagnostics.
   - Development connection panel only displays public taxonomy ping latency and configured hostnames.
6. **Session Cache Hygiene:**
   - Logging out purges in-memory caches across `EventDiscoveryScreen`, `MyApplicationsScreen`, and `MyAssignmentsScreen` to prevent multi-user state bleed.
